== project.godot
==============================================================================
; Engine configuration file.
; It's best edited using the editor UI and not directly,
; since the parameters that go here are not all obvious.
;
; Format:
;   [section] ; section goes between []
;   param=value ; assign values to parameters

config_version=5

[application]

config/name="COMETO"
run/main_scene="res://game/app.tscn"
config/features=PackedStringArray("4.7", "Mobile")
boot_splash/bg_color=Color(0.04, 0.06, 0.08, 1)
config/icon="res://icon.png"

[autoload]

GameState="*res://core/game_state.gd"
Journal="*res://core/journal.gd"
Dialogue="*res://core/dialogue.gd"
SaveManager="*res://core/save_manager.gd"
WorldTime="*res://core/world_time_manager.gd"
Weather="*res://core/weather_manager.gd"
Settings="*res://core/settings.gd"
Audio="*res://core/audio_manager.gd"

[display]

window/stretch/scale_mode="integer"

window/size/viewport_width=480
window/size/viewport_height=270
window/size/window_width_override=1440
window/size/window_height_override=810
window/stretch/mode="canvas_items"
window/stretch/aspect="expand"
window/handheld/orientation=4

[dotnet]

project/assembly_name="COMETO"

[shader_globals]

weather_wet={
"type": "float",
"value": 1.0
}
weather_snow={
"type": "float",
"value": 0.0
}

[rendering]

textures/canvas_textures/default_texture_filter=0
renderer/rendering_method="mobile"
textures/vram_compression/import_etc2_astc=true
scaling_3d/mode.ios=1
environment/defaults/default_clear_color=Color(0.04, 0.06, 0.08, 1)
2d/snap/snap_2d_transforms_to_pixel=true
scaling_3d/scale.ios=0.7
lights_and_shadows/directional_shadow/size.ios=2048
lights_and_shadows/directional_shadow/soft_shadow_filter_quality.ios=1
anti_aliasing/quality/msaa_3d.ios=0

	"width": 1472,
	"x0": -46.0,
	"z0": -46.0
}

==============================================================================
== art/shaders/compass_inlay.gdshader
==============================================================================
shader_type spatial;
// The plaza's landmark inlay: a compass rose of aged brass set into the paving, with
// concentric bands and star points. Drawn on a flat disc in object space (XZ).

render_mode blend_mix, cull_back, diffuse_burley, specular_schlick_ggx;

uniform vec4 stone : source_color = vec4(0.36, 0.32, 0.34, 1.0);
uniform vec4 brass : source_color = vec4(0.72, 0.52, 0.26, 1.0);
uniform vec4 dark : source_color = vec4(0.18, 0.15, 0.17, 1.0);
uniform float radius = 4.0;
global uniform float weather_wet;

varying vec3 lp;

void vertex() {
	lp = VERTEX;
}

float ring(float r, float at, float w) {
	return smoothstep(w, w * 0.4, abs(r - at));
}

void fragment() {
	vec2 p = lp.xz / radius;
	float r = length(p);
	float a = atan(p.y, p.x);
	vec3 col = stone.rgb;
	float metal = 0.0;
	// dark band with ticks
	float band = smoothstep(0.78, 0.8, r) * smoothstep(0.93, 0.91, r);
	col = mix(col, dark.rgb, band);
	float ticks = band * step(0.82, r) * step(r, 0.9) * smoothstep(0.08, 0.03, abs(fract(a / (6.2831853 / 32.0)) - 0.5));
	metal = max(metal, ticks);
	metal = max(metal, ring(r, 0.78, 0.012) + ring(r, 0.93, 0.012) + ring(r, 0.99, 0.01) + ring(r, 0.36, 0.01));
	// eight-point star: long cardinal points, short diagonals
	float k = 6.2831853 / 8.0;
	float sa = mod(a + k * 0.5, k) - k * 0.5;
	float idx = floor((a + k * 0.5) / k);
	float len = mod(idx, 2.0) < 0.5 ? 0.76 : 0.46;
	float width = (1.0 - r / len) * 0.2;
	float point = step(r, len) * smoothstep(width, width * 0.7, abs(sa) * r * 2.6);
	// two-tone points: one half brass, the other dark
	float half = step(0.0, sa);
	col = mix(col, mix(dark.rgb * 1.3, brass.rgb * 0.85, half), point);
	metal = max(metal, point * half);
	metal = clamp(metal, 0.0, 1.0);
	col = mix(col, brass.rgb, metal);
	// centre boss
	float boss = smoothstep(0.075, 0.065, r);
	col = mix(col, brass.rgb * 1.1, boss);
	metal = max(metal, boss);
	ALBEDO = col;
	METALLIC = metal * 0.85;
	ROUGHNESS = mix(mix(0.85, 0.35, metal), 0.15, weather_wet * 0.7);
	SPECULAR = 0.5;
}


==============================================================================
== art/shaders/cosmic_sky.gdshader
==============================================================================
shader_type sky;
// Cometographia's sky: a colossal ringed planet low in the north, a comet, stars and a
// faint nebula over a twilight gradient. No TIME, so the radiance map (reflections in
// the sea, puddles and glass) is only rebuilt when EnvironmentController changes the
// colours. Seen directly only in the arrival shot; everywhere else through reflections.

uniform vec3 zenith : source_color = vec3(0.09, 0.07, 0.2);
uniform vec3 horizon : source_color = vec3(0.55, 0.38, 0.55);
uniform vec3 glow : source_color = vec3(1.0, 0.62, 0.42);
// 0 at night, 1 by day: stars and nebula fade as it rises
uniform float day = 0.0;
// planet: direction (unit), angular radius (radians), colours
uniform vec3 planet_dir = vec3(0.34, 0.2, -0.92);
uniform float planet_r = 0.26;
uniform vec3 planet_a : source_color = vec3(0.95, 0.62, 0.62);
uniform vec3 planet_b : source_color = vec3(0.62, 0.4, 0.55);
uniform vec3 ring_col : source_color = vec3(0.98, 0.8, 0.74);
uniform vec3 sun_dir = vec3(-0.75, 0.3, 0.2);

float hash(vec3 p) {
	return fract(sin(dot(p, vec3(127.1, 311.7, 74.7))) * 43758.5453);
}

float noise(vec3 p) {
	vec3 i = floor(p);
	vec3 f = fract(p);
	f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
	return mix(mix(mix(hash(i), hash(i + vec3(1, 0, 0)), f.x), mix(hash(i + vec3(0, 1, 0)), hash(i + vec3(1, 1, 0)), f.x), f.y),
		mix(mix(hash(i + vec3(0, 0, 1)), hash(i + vec3(1, 0, 1)), f.x), mix(hash(i + vec3(0, 1, 1)), hash(i + vec3(1, 1, 1)), f.x), f.y), f.z);
}

void sky() {
	vec3 d = EYEDIR;
	float up = d.y;
	// gradient: warm glow band at the horizon, deep violet-indigo overhead
	vec3 col = mix(horizon, zenith, smoothstep(-0.05, 0.6, up));
	col += glow * exp(-abs(up) * 9.0) * 0.55;
	col = mix(col, horizon * 0.35, smoothstep(0.0, -0.4, up));
	float night = 1.0 - day;
	// nebula and stars
	float neb = noise(d * 3.0) * 0.6 + noise(d * 7.0) * 0.4;
	col += mix(vec3(0.35, 0.18, 0.5), vec3(0.12, 0.35, 0.45), noise(d * 2.0 + 4.0)) * smoothstep(0.45, 0.85, neb) * 0.22 * night * smoothstep(0.0, 0.3, up);
	vec3 g = d * 220.0;
	float s = hash(floor(g));
	float star = smoothstep(0.992, 1.0, s) * smoothstep(0.35, 0.0, length(fract(g) - 0.5));
	col += vec3(0.9, 0.92, 1.0) * star * night * 1.4 * smoothstep(0.02, 0.2, up);
	// comet: a soft streak with a bright head, upper right
	vec3 ch = normalize(vec3(0.62, 0.62, -0.48));
	vec3 tail = normalize(vec3(0.9, 0.35, -0.25)) - ch;
	vec3 rel = d - ch;
	float along = dot(rel, normalize(tail));
	float across = length(rel - normalize(tail) * along);
	float comet = smoothstep(0.0, 0.02, along) * smoothstep(0.32, 0.0, along) * smoothstep(0.012 + along * 0.05, 0.0, across);
	col += vec3(0.75, 0.88, 1.0) * (comet * 0.7 + smoothstep(0.012, 0.0, length(rel)) * 1.5) * (0.35 + 0.65 * night);
	// the planet: a sphere at distance 1 with radius sin(planet_r), and its rings
	vec3 P = planet_dir;
	float R = sin(planet_r);
	float b = dot(d, P);
	float disc = b * b - (dot(P, P) - R * R);
	float t_sphere = disc > 0.0 ? b - sqrt(disc) : 1e9;
	vec3 n_ring = normalize(vec3(0.25, 0.94, 0.22));
	float denom = dot(d, n_ring);
	float t_ring = abs(denom) > 1e-4 ? dot(P, n_ring) / denom : 1e9;
	float ring_r = t_ring > 0.0 && t_ring < 1e8 ? length(d * t_ring - P) / R : 0.0;
	float ring = smoothstep(1.45, 1.5, ring_r) * smoothstep(2.35, 2.25, ring_r);
	ring *= 0.55 + 0.45 * sin(ring_r * 38.0) * sin(ring_r * 13.0 + 1.0);
	ring *= 1.0 - smoothstep(1.86, 1.9, ring_r) * smoothstep(1.98, 1.94, ring_r) * 0.85;
	if (t_sphere < 1e8) {
		vec3 hit = d * t_sphere;
		vec3 nrm = normalize(hit - P);
		// latitude bands in the planet's frame (tilted with the rings)
		float lat = dot(nrm, n_ring);
		float bands = noise(vec3(lat * 14.0, lat * 3.0, 0.0)) * 0.7 + noise(vec3(lat * 40.0, nrm.x * 2.0, nrm.z * 2.0)) * 0.3;
		vec3 pc = mix(planet_b, planet_a, bands);
		float lit = clamp(dot(nrm, normalize(sun_dir)) * 0.9 + 0.25, 0.0, 1.0);
		pc *= mix(0.12, 1.15, lit);
		// limb glow
		pc += planet_a * pow(1.0 - clamp(dot(nrm, -d), 0.0, 1.0), 3.0) * 0.35;
		float edge = smoothstep(0.0, 0.0006, disc);
		col = mix(col, pc, edge * (0.82 + 0.18 * day));
		// the ring passes in front of the near half
		if (t_ring > 0.0 && t_ring < t_sphere) {
			col = mix(col, ring_col * (0.7 + 0.3 * lit), ring * 0.8);
		} else if (t_ring > t_sphere) {
			// the planet's shadow side darkens the ring behind it: nothing to draw
		}
	} else if (t_ring > 0.0 && t_ring < 1e8) {
		col = mix(col, ring_col, ring * 0.75);
	}
	COLOR = col;
}


==============================================================================
== art/shaders/handheld_grade.gdshader
==============================================================================
shader_type canvas_item;
// The handheld look (ArtStyle.handheld): applied to the low-resolution 3D image as it
// is scaled to the screen. One texture read per pixel, nothing else.
//   1. Colour grade toward the settlement palette: shadows pushed toward indigo and
//      violet, highlights toward warm amber and rose, blacks slightly lifted and faded
//      with a violet cast, a little extra saturation in the mid-tones.
//   2. 15-bit colour (32 levels per channel, as on the DS) with a 4x4 ordered dither,
//      applied per source pixel so the dither pattern is as chunky as the pixels.
//   3. A soft vignette.

// indigo-violet shadows, warm amber-rose light: the settlement palette
uniform vec3 shadow_tint : source_color = vec3(0.4, 0.38, 0.66);
uniform vec3 highlight_tint : source_color = vec3(1.0, 0.82, 0.7);
uniform float split = 0.22;
uniform float lift = 0.035;
uniform float fade = 0.06;
uniform float saturation = 1.12;
uniform float contrast = 1.06;
uniform float levels = 32.0;
uniform float dither = 1.0;
uniform float vignette = 0.28;

float bayer(vec2 p) {
	int x = int(mod(p.x, 4.0));
	int y = int(mod(p.y, 4.0));
	int m[16] = int[16](0, 8, 2, 10, 12, 4, 14, 6, 3, 11, 1, 9, 15, 7, 13, 5);
	return float(m[y * 4 + x]) / 16.0 - 0.5;
}

void fragment() {
	vec3 c = texture(TEXTURE, UV).rgb;
	float l = dot(c, vec3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
	// split toning by luminance
	vec3 tone = mix(shadow_tint, highlight_tint, smoothstep(0.08, 0.7, l));
	c = mix(c, c * tone * 1.25, split);
	// saturation and contrast around mid-grey
	c = mix(vec3(l), c, saturation);
	c = (c - 0.5) * contrast + 0.5;
	// lifted, faded blacks with a violet cast
	c = c * (1.0 - fade) + vec3(lift * 1.0, lift * 0.85, lift * 1.25);
	// 15-bit colour with ordered dither, in source pixels
	vec2 src = floor(UV / TEXTURE_PIXEL_SIZE);
	c += bayer(src) * dither / levels;
	c = floor(clamp(c, 0.0, 1.0) * (levels - 1.0) + 0.5) / (levels - 1.0);
	// vignette
	vec2 q = UV - 0.5;
	c *= 1.0 - dot(q, q) * vignette * 1.6;
	COLOR = vec4(c, 1.0);
}


==============================================================================
== art/shaders/sea.gdshader
==============================================================================
shader_type spatial;
// Storm sea for Anjabi: two drifting noise layers make swell, glossy highlights catch
// the lamps and the afterglow, whitecaps form where the layers peak. World-space, so
// one big plane tiles anywhere. Mobile-friendly: one texture, no screen reads.

render_mode blend_mix, cull_back, diffuse_burley, specular_schlick_ggx;

uniform vec4 deep : source_color = vec4(0.02, 0.06, 0.08, 1.0);
uniform vec4 shallow : source_color = vec4(0.06, 0.17, 0.19, 1.0);
uniform vec4 foam_color : source_color = vec4(0.62, 0.68, 0.7, 1.0);
uniform float speed = 0.02;
uniform float bump_strength = 2.2;
uniform sampler2D noise_tex : hint_default_white, filter_linear_mipmap, repeat_enable;

varying vec3 wpos;

// The shared noise texture is colour-ramped to roughly 0.6..1.05; remap to 0..1.
float nz(vec2 uv) {
	return clamp((texture(noise_tex, uv).r - 0.6) / 0.45, 0.0, 1.0);
}

void vertex() {
	wpos = (MODEL_MATRIX * vec4(VERTEX, 1.0)).xyz;
}

void fragment() {
	vec2 uv = wpos.xz * 0.035;
	float t = TIME * speed;
	float a = nz(uv + vec2(t, t * 0.6));
	float b = nz(uv * 2.7 - vec2(t * 1.4, -t * 0.8));
	float c = nz(uv * 7.0 + vec2(-t * 2.0, t * 1.7));
	float h = a * 0.55 + b * 0.3 + c * 0.15;
	vec3 col = mix(deep.rgb, shallow.rgb, smoothstep(0.3, 0.75, h));
	float foam = smoothstep(0.7, 0.82, a * 0.5 + b * 0.5) * smoothstep(0.45, 0.7, c);
	ALBEDO = mix(col, foam_color.rgb, foam * 0.6);
	ROUGHNESS = mix(0.16, 0.7, foam);
	SPECULAR = 0.3;
	float hh = h * 0.12;
	vec3 dpdx = dFdx(VERTEX);
	vec3 dpdy = dFdy(VERTEX);
	vec3 r1 = cross(dpdy, NORMAL);
	vec3 r2 = cross(NORMAL, dpdx);
	float det = dot(dpdx, r1);
	vec3 grad = sign(det) * (dFdx(hh) * r1 + dFdy(hh) * r2);
	NORMAL = normalize(abs(det) * NORMAL - grad * bump_strength);
}


==============================================================================
== art/shaders/stone_wall.gdshader
==============================================================================
shader_type spatial;
// Weathered ashlar for architecture: world-space courses of dressed stone blocks on
// walls (staggered, per-block tint, recessed mortar, chipped edges, damp at the foot),
// plain worn stone on tops. No UVs needed, so every box built in code tiles cleanly.

render_mode blend_mix, cull_back, diffuse_burley, specular_schlick_ggx;

uniform vec4 base_color : source_color = vec4(0.52, 0.46, 0.44, 1.0);
uniform vec4 mortar_color : source_color = vec4(0.2, 0.17, 0.17, 1.0);
uniform float course = 0.42;
uniform float block = 0.9;
uniform float tint_var = 0.22;
uniform sampler2D noise_tex : hint_default_white, filter_linear_mipmap, repeat_enable;
global uniform float weather_wet;

varying vec3 wpos;
varying vec3 wnrm;

void vertex() {
	wpos = (MODEL_MATRIX * vec4(VERTEX, 1.0)).xyz;
	wnrm = normalize((MODEL_MATRIX * vec4(NORMAL, 0.0)).xyz);
}

float hash(vec2 p) {
	return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453);
}

float nz(vec2 uv) {
	return clamp((texture(noise_tex, uv).r - 0.6) / 0.45, 0.0, 1.0);
}

void fragment() {
	vec3 an = abs(wnrm);
	float wall = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.55, 0.8, an.y);
	// along-wall coordinate: x on walls facing z, z on walls facing x
	float u = an.z > an.x ? wpos.x : wpos.z;
	vec2 p = vec2(u, wpos.y);
	float row = floor(p.y / course);
	float w = block * (0.8 + 0.5 * hash(vec2(row, 3.1)));
	float x = p.x / w + mod(row, 2.0) * 0.5 + hash(vec2(row, 7.7));
	vec2 cell = vec2(floor(x), row);
	vec2 f = vec2(fract(x), fract(p.y / course));
	float edge = min(min(f.x, 1.0 - f.x) * w, min(f.y, 1.0 - f.y) * course);
	float n = nz(wpos.xz * 0.21 + vec2(wpos.y * 0.13));
	float chip = nz(p * 1.7 + cell * 0.37);
	float joint = smoothstep(0.012, 0.035 + chip * 0.02, edge);
	float r = hash(cell);
	vec3 stone = base_color.rgb * (1.0 - tint_var * 0.5 + tint_var * r) * (0.85 + 0.3 * n);
	// a few warmer and a few cooler blocks
	stone = mix(stone, stone * vec3(1.1, 0.98, 0.92), step(0.82, r));
	stone = mix(stone, stone * vec3(0.9, 0.95, 1.04), step(r, 0.12));
	vec3 col = mix(mortar_color.rgb * (0.8 + 0.4 * n), stone, joint);
	// tops: worn flat stone, no courses
	vec3 top = base_color.rgb * (0.9 + 0.25 * n) * (0.9 + 0.2 * nz(wpos.xz * 1.3));
	col = mix(top, col, wall);
	// damp, dark foot of every wall and grime under ledges
	float damp = (1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.7, fract(wpos.y / 3.0) * 3.0)) * 0.25 * wall;
	col *= 1.0 - damp;
	float wet = weather_wet * 0.6;
	ALBEDO = col * mix(1.0, 0.7, wet);
	ROUGHNESS = mix(0.88, 0.45, wet) * mix(1.0, 1.08, 1.0 - joint);
	SPECULAR = 0.35;
	// bump: blocks proud of the mortar
	float h = joint * 0.02 * wall + chip * 0.004;
	vec3 dpdx = dFdx(VERTEX);
	vec3 dpdy = dFdy(VERTEX);
	vec3 r1 = cross(dpdy, NORMAL);
	vec3 r2 = cross(NORMAL, dpdx);
	float det = dot(dpdx, r1);
	vec3 grad = sign(det) * (dFdx(h) * r1 + dFdy(h) * r2);
	NORMAL = normalize(abs(det) * NORMAL - grad * 1.4);
}


==============================================================================
== art/shaders/title_planet.gdshader
==============================================================================
shader_type canvas_item;
// Title screen backdrop: a planet seen from high orbit. One pass, three or four noise
// samples per pixel, no screen reads: fine on phones and in the Compatibility (web)
// renderer. Everything scrolls along the planet's limb at different speeds (ground,
// low cloud, high cloud), which is the parallax; noise repeats, so it loops forever.

uniform sampler2D noise_tex : repeat_enable, filter_linear_mipmap;
// width / height of the rect
uniform float aspect = 1.7778;
// 1 on web and low-end devices: one cloud layer fewer and no light shafts
uniform float lite = 0.0;
uniform float speed = 1.0;
uniform vec3 amber : source_color = vec3(1.0, 0.62, 0.3);
uniform vec3 violet : source_color = vec3(0.36, 0.26, 0.52);
uniform vec3 indigo : source_color = vec3(0.045, 0.045, 0.11);

float nz(vec2 uv) {
	return texture(noise_tex, uv).r;
}

float hash(vec2 p) {
	return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453);
}

void fragment() {
	vec2 p = vec2(UV.x * aspect, UV.y);
	// the limb: a very large circle whose edge crosses the screen from upper left to mid right
	vec2 c = vec2(-0.15, 6.17);
	float r = 6.0;
	vec2 d = p - c;
	float h = length(d) - r;            // > 0 in space, < 0 over the planet
	float breathe = 0.93 + 0.07 * sin(TIME * 0.21) + 0.03 * sin(TIME * 0.057 + 1.3);
	vec3 col;
	if (h > 0.0) {
		// space: indigo to deep violet, stars, the atmosphere's glow
		col = mix(indigo * 1.6, indigo * 0.5, clamp(h * 2.2, 0.0, 1.0));
		vec2 g = p * 90.0;
		vec2 cell = floor(g);
		float s = hash(cell);
		vec2 f = fract(g) - 0.5 - (vec2(hash(cell + 3.1), hash(cell + 7.7)) - 0.5) * 0.6;
		float star = smoothstep(0.985, 1.0, s) * smoothstep(0.12, 0.0, length(f));
		star *= 0.55 + 0.45 * sin(TIME * (0.6 + s * 2.0) + s * 40.0);
		col += vec3(0.85, 0.88, 1.0) * star * smoothstep(0.02, 0.2, h);
		col += amber * exp(-h * 38.0) * 1.15 * breathe;
		col += mix(amber, violet, 0.55) * exp(-h * 9.0) * 0.5 * breathe;
		col += violet * exp(-h * 2.6) * 0.32;
		if (lite < 0.5) {
			// faint shafts of light rising off the limb
			float ang = atan(d.x, -d.y);
			float shaft = smoothstep(0.55, 0.9, nz(vec2(ang * 9.0 + TIME * 0.004 * speed, 0.37)));
			col += amber * shaft * exp(-h * 3.2) * 0.1 * smoothstep(0.0, 0.08, h);
		}
	} else {
		float t = -h;
		// surface coordinates: arc length along the limb, and depth foreshortened near it
		float u = atan(d.x, -d.y) * r;
		float v = sqrt(t) * 1.9;
		vec2 sv = vec2(u, v);
		float spin = TIME * 0.0035 * speed;
		// ground: dark forest and settlements that glow orange
		vec2 gv = sv * vec2(0.55, 0.8) + vec2(spin, 0.0);
		float land = nz(gv * 0.9);
		float fine = nz(gv * 6.0 + 0.31);
		vec3 ground = mix(vec3(0.012, 0.016, 0.03), vec3(0.05, 0.05, 0.085), fine * land);
		float town = smoothstep(0.56, 0.74, land);
		float lights = smoothstep(0.62, 0.8, nz(gv * 15.0 + 0.5)) * smoothstep(0.35, 0.7, fine);
		float flick = 0.8 + 0.2 * sin(TIME * 1.7 + fine * 60.0);
		ground += vec3(1.0, 0.33, 0.1) * lights * town * 1.5 * flick;
		ground += vec3(1.0, 0.45, 0.2) * town * town * 0.05;
		// low cloud deck, then a higher, faster one
		float sun = exp(-t * 4.5);          // how much of the low sun reaches this far in
		vec2 c1 = sv * vec2(0.4, 0.75) + vec2(spin * 1.8, 0.0);
		float low = smoothstep(0.42, 0.72, nz(c1) * 0.72 + nz(c1 * 2.7 + 0.2) * 0.28);
		vec3 low_col = mix(vec3(0.1, 0.09, 0.2), vec3(0.42, 0.36, 0.55), nz(c1 * 2.7 + 0.2));
		low_col = mix(low_col, amber * 1.05, sun * 0.8);
		col = mix(ground, low_col, low * 0.92);
		if (lite < 0.5) {
			vec2 c2 = sv * vec2(0.22, 0.5) + vec2(spin * 3.4 + 0.5, 0.13);
			float high = smoothstep(0.55, 0.85, nz(c2) * 0.8 + nz(c2 * 3.3) * 0.2);
			vec3 high_col = mix(vec3(0.2, 0.18, 0.34), vec3(0.62, 0.55, 0.72), sun + 0.25);
			col = mix(col, mix(high_col, amber, sun * 0.6), high * 0.55);
		}
		// the night side deepens away from the limb; haze gathers at the edge
		col *= mix(0.42, 1.0, exp(-t * 1.6));
		col += amber * exp(-t * 26.0) * 0.95 * breathe;
		col += mix(amber, violet, 0.6) * exp(-t * 5.0) * 0.22 * breathe;
	}
	// soft vignette
	vec2 q = UV - 0.5;
	col *= 1.0 - dot(q, q) * 0.55;
	COLOR = vec4(col, 1.0);
}


==============================================================================
== art/shaders/title_wisps.gdshader
==============================================================================
shader_type canvas_item;
// Thin cloud passing close to the camera, in front of the ship: the fastest parallax layer.

uniform sampler2D noise_tex : repeat_enable, filter_linear_mipmap;
uniform float aspect = 1.7778;
uniform float speed = 1.0;

void fragment() {
	vec2 p = vec2(UV.x * aspect, UV.y);
	float t = TIME * 0.006 * speed;
	float n = texture(noise_tex, p * vec2(0.22, 0.5) + vec2(t, t * 0.15)).r * 0.7
		+ texture(noise_tex, p * vec2(0.6, 1.3) + vec2(t * 1.7, 0.4)).r * 0.3;
	float a = smoothstep(0.52, 0.85, n) * smoothstep(0.25, 0.95, UV.y) * 0.34;
	COLOR = vec4(mix(vec3(0.2, 0.18, 0.36), vec3(0.5, 0.44, 0.62), n), a);
}


==============================================================================
== art/shaders/wet_ground.gdshader
==============================================================================
shader_type spatial;
// Rain-soaked ground for Anjabi. World-space, so any slab or terrain mesh tiles
// seamlessly with no UVs. Up-facing surfaces get cobblestones (staggered rows,
// per-stone tint, mortar joints, rounded bump), a wet sheen and puddles that pool in
// the joints; side faces get plain wet stone. Mobile-friendly: one noise texture,
// no screen reads.

render_mode blend_mix, cull_back, diffuse_burley, specular_schlick_ggx;

uniform vec4 base_color : source_color = vec4(0.36, 0.35, 0.34, 1.0);
uniform vec4 mortar_color : source_color = vec4(0.10, 0.10, 0.11, 1.0);
// 1 = cobbles, 0 = plain wet ground (gravel, mud, moss).
uniform float cobble : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 1.0;
uniform float stone_size = 0.3;
uniform float wetness : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 0.8;
uniform float puddles : hint_range(0.0, 1.0) = 0.55;
uniform float bump_strength = 1.6;
// Walkway slopes steeper than this (normal.y below it) are drawn as stone steps.
uniform float stair_start = 0.99;
uniform float step_height = 0.18;
// Terrain meshes carry their colour in COLOR.rgb (sRGB) and cobble amount in COLOR.a.
uniform bool use_vertex_color = false;
// Set by EnvironmentController: 1 while it rains (dries slowly), and settled snow 0..1.
global uniform float weather_wet;
global uniform float weather_snow;
uniform sampler2D noise_tex : hint_default_white, filter_linear_mipmap, repeat_enable;

varying vec3 wpos;

// The shared noise texture is colour-ramped to roughly 0.6..1.05; remap to 0..1.
float nz(vec2 uv) {
	return clamp((texture(noise_tex, uv).r - 0.6) / 0.45, 0.0, 1.0);
}
varying vec3 wnrm;

void vertex() {
	wpos = (MODEL_MATRIX * vec4(VERTEX, 1.0)).xyz;
	wnrm = normalize((MODEL_MATRIX * vec4(NORMAL, 0.0)).xyz);
}

float hash(vec2 p) {
	return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453);
}

// x: distance to the stone's edge (cells), y: per-stone random.
vec2 cobbles(vec2 p, float warp) {
	p += (warp - 0.5) * vec2(0.22, 0.16);
	p /= stone_size;
	float row = floor(p.y);
	p.x += mod(row, 2.0) * 0.5 + hash(vec2(row, 7.3)) * 0.35;
	p.x *= 0.72;
	// stones of varying width within a row
	p.x += hash(vec2(floor(p.x), row)) * 0.25;
	vec2 cell = floor(p);
	vec2 f = fract(p);
	vec2 d = min(f, 1.0 - f);
	// rounded corners
	float e = min(d.x, d.y);
	float corner = length(max(vec2(0.18) - d, vec2(0.0)));
	e = min(e, 0.18 - corner);
	return vec2(e, hash(cell));
}

void fragment() {
	float up = smoothstep(0.55, 0.85, wnrm.y);
	vec3 col = base_color.rgb;
	float amount = cobble;
	float planks = 0.0;
	if (use_vertex_color) {
		col = pow(COLOR.rgb, vec3(2.2));
		// alpha: 1 = cobbles, ~0.5 = timber boardwalk, 0 = plain
		planks = step(0.3, COLOR.a) * step(COLOR.a, 0.7);
		amount = COLOR.a * (1.0 - planks);
	}
	amount *= up;
	float n = nz(wpos.xz * 0.06);
	float n2 = nz(wpos.xz * 0.5 + vec2(wpos.y * 0.3));

	float warp = nz(wpos.xz * 0.9);
	vec2 c = cobbles(wpos.xz, warp);
	float stone = smoothstep(0.015, 0.07, c.x);
	float dome = smoothstep(0.0, 0.32, c.x);
	vec3 stone_col = col * (0.72 + 0.5 * c.y) * (0.8 + 0.4 * n2);
	stone_col = mix(stone_col, stone_col * vec3(1.08, 0.98, 0.9), step(0.8, c.y));
	vec3 joint_col = mortar_color.rgb * (0.7 + 0.6 * n2);
	vec3 paved = mix(joint_col, stone_col, stone);
	vec3 plain = col * (0.7 + 0.5 * n2) * (0.85 + 0.3 * n);
	// steep faces: layered rock with dark crevices and a little lichen
	float steep = 1.0 - up;
	float strata = nz(vec2((wpos.x + wpos.z) * 0.12, wpos.y * 0.9));
	float crack = smoothstep(0.42, 0.5, nz(vec2((wpos.x - wpos.z) * 0.35, wpos.y * 1.6)));
	vec3 rock = col * vec3(1.0, 0.94, 0.86) * (0.35 + 0.95 * strata) * mix(0.35, 1.0, crack);
	rock = mix(rock, vec3(0.16, 0.22, 0.12), smoothstep(0.62, 0.75, n2) * 0.5);
	plain = mix(plain, rock, steep);
	col = mix(plain, paved, amount);
	// boardwalk: 0.28 m boards running east-west, staggered butt joints, dark gaps
	float bw = wpos.z / 0.28;
	float board = floor(bw);
	float fb = fract(bw);
	float along = fract((wpos.x + hash(vec2(board, 1.7)) * 5.0) / 2.6);
	float gap = smoothstep(0.0, 0.07, min(fb, 1.0 - fb)) * smoothstep(0.0, 0.012, min(along, 1.0 - along));
	vec3 wood = col * (0.7 + 0.5 * hash(vec2(board, 9.1))) * (0.85 + 0.3 * nz(vec2(wpos.x * 0.8, wpos.z * 6.0)));
	col = mix(col, mix(wood * 0.25, wood, gap), planks * up);
	stone = mix(stone, gap, planks * up);
	amount = max(amount, planks * up * 0.6);

	// puddles: low-frequency noise, pooling first in the joints
	float pool = n + (1.0 - stone) * 0.1 * amount - (1.0 - up) * 2.0;
	float puddle = smoothstep(0.52, 0.6, pool) * puddles * mix(0.35, 1.0, max(amount, planks)) * weather_wet;
	float wet = clamp(wetness * (0.65 + 0.35 * n2) + (1.0 - stone) * amount * 0.3, 0.0, 1.0) * mix(0.3, 1.0, weather_wet);

	// stairs: on sloped paved walkways, level treads at fixed risers following contours
	float stairs = smoothstep(stair_start, stair_start - 0.03, wnrm.y) * smoothstep(0.5, 0.62, wnrm.y) * step(0.5, amount + planks * 0.0);
	float sy = wpos.y / step_height;
	float fs = fract(sy);
	float step_id = floor(sy);
	float riser = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.2, fs);
	float nose = smoothstep(0.82, 0.97, fs);
	vec3 tread = base_color.rgb;
	if (use_vertex_color) {
		tread = pow(COLOR.rgb, vec3(2.2));
	}
	tread *= (0.85 + 0.3 * hash(vec2(step_id, 3.3))) * (0.85 + 0.3 * n2);
	float slab_joint = smoothstep(0.0, 0.03, abs(fract((wpos.x + wpos.z) * 0.9 + hash(vec2(step_id, 1.1))) - 0.5) - 0.47);
	tread = mix(tread, tread * 0.5, slab_joint);
	vec3 stair_col = mix(tread, tread * 0.15, riser) + nose * 0.1;
	col = mix(col, stair_col, stairs);
	puddle *= 1.0 - stairs * 0.85;
	stone = mix(stone, 1.0, stairs);

	ALBEDO = col * mix(1.0, 0.5, wet);
	ALBEDO = mix(ALBEDO, ALBEDO * 0.3 + vec3(0.01, 0.015, 0.02), puddle);
	// settled snow: patchy at first, then covering level ground; treads show through a little
	float snow = smoothstep(0.8, 0.95, wnrm.y) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.25, 0.75, n2 * 0.6 + n * 0.4 - weather_snow * 0.9 + 0.4)) * weather_snow * (1.0 - puddle * 0.5);
	ALBEDO = mix(ALBEDO, vec3(0.4, 0.45, 0.53) * (0.9 + 0.2 * n2), snow * 0.85);
	float rough = mix(0.8, 0.18, wet);
	rough = mix(rough, 0.9, snow);
	rough = mix(rough, 0.92, steep);
	rough = mix(rough, 0.95, (1.0 - stone) * amount * (1.0 - wet * 0.6));
	ROUGHNESS = mix(rough, 0.03, puddle);
	SPECULAR = mix(0.55, 0.25, steep);

	// bump: rounded stones, flattened under puddles
	float h = dome * amount * (1.0 - puddle) * 0.03 * (1.0 - stairs);
	// cancel the ramp's slope on each tread so treads read level and risers steep
	h -= fs * step_height * stairs / bump_strength;
	vec3 dpdx = dFdx(VERTEX);
	vec3 dpdy = dFdy(VERTEX);
	float dhdx = dFdx(h);
	float dhdy = dFdy(h);
	vec3 r1 = cross(dpdy, NORMAL);
	vec3 r2 = cross(NORMAL, dpdx);
	float det = dot(dpdx, r1);
	vec3 grad = sign(det) * (dhdx * r1 + dhdy * r2);
	NORMAL = normalize(abs(det) * NORMAL - grad * bump_strength);
}


==============================================================================
== art_provisional/char_sprite.gd
==============================================================================
@tool
class_name CharSprite
extends Node2D
## PROVISIONAL ART. A tiny person drawn in code, origin at the feet.
## To be replaced by authored sprite sheets; keep set_motion() as the interface.

@export var coat := Color(0.35, 0.4, 0.45):
	set(v):
		coat = v
		queue_redraw()
@export var accent := Color(0.7, 0.45, 0.2):
	set(v):
		accent = v
		queue_redraw()
@export var skin := Color(0.78, 0.6, 0.48):
	set(v):
		skin = v
		queue_redraw()
@export var hood := false:
	set(v):
		hood = v
		queue_redraw()

var _dir := 0  # 0 down, 1 up, 2 left, 3 right
var _moving := false
var _t := 0.0
var _frame := 0


func set_motion(facing: Vector2, moving: bool) -> void:
	var d := _dir
	if facing != Vector2.ZERO:
		if absf(facing.x) > absf(facing.y):
			d = 2 if facing.x < 0.0 else 3
		else:
			d = 1 if facing.y < 0.0 else 0
	if d != _dir or moving != _moving:
		_dir = d
		_moving = moving
		if not moving:
			_frame = 0
			_t = 0.0
		queue_redraw()


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if Engine.is_editor_hint() or not _moving:
		return
	_t += delta
	var f := int(_t * 8.0) % 4
	if f != _frame:
		_frame = f
		queue_redraw()


func _draw() -> void:
	var bob := -1.0 if (_moving and _frame % 2 == 1) else 0.0
	var boots := Color(0.1, 0.1, 0.12)
	var dark := coat.darkened(0.22)
	var side := _dir >= 2
	draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, 0.0, Vector2(1.0, 0.45))
	draw_circle(Vector2.ZERO, 6.0, Color(0, 0, 0, 0.3))
	draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, 0.0, Vector2.ONE)
	# legs
	var lift_a := 1.0 if (_moving and _frame == 1) else 0.0
	var lift_b := 1.0 if (_moving and _frame == 3) else 0.0
	if side:
		draw_rect(Rect2(-2 - lift_a * 2.0, -5, 2, 5 - lift_a), boots)
		draw_rect(Rect2(0 + lift_b * 2.0, -5, 2, 5 - lift_b), boots)
	else:
		draw_rect(Rect2(-3, -5, 2, 5 - lift_a), boots)
		draw_rect(Rect2(1, -5, 2, 5 - lift_b), boots)
	# coat
	var bw := 6.0 if side else 8.0
	draw_rect(Rect2(-bw * 0.5, -15 + bob, bw, 11), coat)
	draw_rect(Rect2(-bw * 0.5, -5 + bob, bw, 1), dark)
	draw_rect(Rect2(-bw * 0.5, -11 + bob, bw, 1), accent)
	if side:
		var swing := 0.0
		if _moving:
			swing = 1.0 if _frame < 2 else -1.0
		draw_rect(Rect2(-1 + swing, -14 + bob, 2, 6), dark)
	else:
		draw_rect(Rect2(-5, -14 + bob, 1, 6), dark)
		draw_rect(Rect2(4, -14 + bob, 1, 6), dark)
	# head
	var top := coat if hood else Color(0.14, 0.11, 0.1)
	draw_rect(Rect2(-3, -21 + bob, 6, 6), skin)
	draw_rect(Rect2(-4, -22 + bob, 8, 3), top)
	var eye := Color(0.08, 0.07, 0.08)
	match _dir:
		0:
			if hood:
				draw_rect(Rect2(-4, -20 + bob, 1, 5), top)
				draw_rect(Rect2(3, -20 + bob, 1, 5), top)
			draw_rect(Rect2(-2, -18 + bob, 1, 1), eye)
			draw_rect(Rect2(1, -18 + bob, 1, 1), eye)
		1:
			draw_rect(Rect2(-4, -22 + bob, 8, 7), top)
		2:
			draw_rect(Rect2(0, -22 + bob, 4, 7), top)
			draw_rect(Rect2(-3, -18 + bob, 1, 1), eye)
		3:
			draw_rect(Rect2(-4, -22 + bob, 4, 7), top)
			draw_rect(Rect2(2, -18 + bob, 1, 1), eye)


==============================================================================
== art_provisional/figure_3d.gd
==============================================================================
class_name Figure3D
extends Node3D
## PROVISIONAL ART. A person built from primitive meshes, about 1.65 m and four
## heads tall, facing -Z. Procedural walk and idle animation through limb pivots.
## Keep set_motion() as the interface when an authored, rigged model replaces it.

var coat := Color(0.35, 0.4, 0.45)
var accent := Color(0.7, 0.45, 0.2)
var skin := Color(0.78, 0.6, 0.48)
var hood := false
## Look options (see configure()): hair "short" | "bun" | "long" | "bald";
## hat "" | "cap" | "hardhat" | "brim"; long_coat, apron, satchel.
var hair := "short"
var hair_color := Color(0.12, 0.08, 0.06)
var hat := ""
var long_coat := false
var apron := false
var satchel := true
## Draw a faint silhouette wherever the figure is hidden behind scenery (player only).
var xray := false
## Pull clothing toward the settlement palette. Off for the player, whose colours are
## chosen in character creation and must stay exactly as picked.
var harmonize_clothes := true


func configure(look: Dictionary) -> void:
	for k: String in look:
		set(k, look[k])


var _hips: Node3D
var _leg_l: Node3D
var _leg_r: Node3D
var _arm_l: Node3D
var _arm_r: Node3D
var _torso: Node3D
var _knee_l: Node3D
var _knee_r: Node3D
var _elbow_l: Node3D
var _elbow_r: Node3D
## Neck joint; the head's parts hang under its child so it can nod and turn.
var _neck: Node3D
var _head_parts: Node3D
var _run := 0.0
## Hip height; shorter for the chibi proportions of ArtStyle.handheld.
var _hip_h := 0.82
var _phase := 0.0
var _amount := 0.0
var _speed := 0.0
var _t := 0.0


func _ready() -> void:
	# clothing in the settlement palette's family (skin and hair untouched)
	if harmonize_clothes:
		coat = MapBuilder3D.harmonize(coat, 0.4)
		accent = MapBuilder3D.harmonize(accent, 0.3)
	var coat_m := _mat(coat, 0.85)
	var dark_m := _mat(coat.darkened(0.3), 0.9)
	var skin_m := _mat(skin, 0.7)
	var boot_m := _mat(Color(0.1, 0.09, 0.09), 0.6)
	var accent_m := _mat(accent, 0.6, 0.3)
	_hips = Node3D.new()
	_hip_h = 0.82 * (ArtStyle.LEGS if ArtStyle.handheld else 1.0)
	_hips.position.y = _hip_h
	add_child(_hips)
	_leg_l = _limb(_hips, Vector3(-0.1, 0, 0), 0.075, 0.8, dark_m, boot_m)
	_leg_r = _limb(_hips, Vector3(0.1, 0, 0), 0.075, 0.8, dark_m, boot_m)
	_torso = Node3D.new()
	_hips.add_child(_torso)
	# coat skirt, chest, belt
	var skirt := CylinderMesh.new()
	skirt.top_radius = 0.19
	skirt.bottom_radius = 0.27
	var skirt_h := 0.66 if long_coat else 0.5
	skirt.height = skirt_h
	skirt.bottom_radius = 0.29 if long_coat else 0.27
	skirt.radial_segments = 12
	_part(_torso, skirt, coat_m, Vector3(0, 0.2 - skirt_h * 0.5, 0.01), Vector3(1, 1, 0.85))
	var hem := CylinderMesh.new()
	hem.top_radius = 0.272
	hem.bottom_radius = 0.272
	hem.height = 0.04
	hem.radial_segments = 12
	hem.top_radius = skirt.bottom_radius + 0.003
	hem.bottom_radius = hem.top_radius
	_part(_torso, hem, dark_m, Vector3(0, 0.2 - skirt_h + 0.01, 0.01), Vector3(1, 1, 0.85))
	# lapels and buttons down the front
	var lap := BoxMesh.new()
	lap.size = Vector3(0.07, 0.3, 0.02)
	_part(_torso, lap, dark_m, Vector3(-0.055, 0.45, -0.152)).rotation.z = -0.35
	_part(_torso, lap, dark_m, Vector3(0.055, 0.45, -0.152)).rotation.z = 0.35
	var btn := SphereMesh.new()
	btn.radius = 0.014
	btn.height = 0.028
	var btn_m := _mat(Color(0.75, 0.6, 0.3), 0.4, 0.8)
	for k in 3:
		_part(_torso, btn, btn_m, Vector3(0.0, 0.32 - k * 0.11, -0.158))
	if apron:
		var ap := BoxMesh.new()
		ap.size = Vector3(0.3, 0.55, 0.02)
		_part(_torso, ap, _mat(Color(0.72, 0.66, 0.55), 0.9), Vector3(0, -0.02, -0.2)).rotation.x = 0.1
	var chest := CapsuleMesh.new()
	chest.radius = 0.19
	chest.height = 0.6
	_part(_torso, chest, coat_m, Vector3(0, 0.36, 0), Vector3(1, 1, 0.78))
	var belt := CylinderMesh.new()
	belt.top_radius = 0.2
	belt.bottom_radius = 0.2
	belt.height = 0.05
	_part(_torso, belt, accent_m, Vector3(0, 0.18, 0))
	var collar := CylinderMesh.new()
	collar.top_radius = 0.11
	collar.bottom_radius = 0.17
	collar.height = 0.1
	_part(_torso, collar, accent_m, Vector3(0, 0.6, 0))
	# shoulders, scarf, satchel and strap
	var sh := SphereMesh.new()
	sh.radius = 0.1
	sh.height = 0.16
	_part(_torso, sh, coat_m, Vector3(-0.21, 0.55, 0))
	_part(_torso, sh, coat_m, Vector3(0.21, 0.55, 0))
	var scarf := BoxMesh.new()
	scarf.size = Vector3(0.09, 0.26, 0.04)
	_part(_torso, scarf, accent_m, Vector3(0.06, 0.45, -0.165), Vector3.ONE).rotation.z = 0.12
	var bag_m := _mat(Color(0.3, 0.2, 0.13), 0.8)
	if not satchel:
		bag_m = null
	var bag := BoxMesh.new()
	bag.size = Vector3(0.08, 0.2, 0.24)
	if satchel:
		_part(_torso, bag, bag_m, Vector3(0.27, 0.06, 0.02))
		var strap := BoxMesh.new()
		strap.size = Vector3(0.04, 0.62, 0.02)
		var st := _part(_torso, strap, bag_m, Vector3(0.03, 0.35, -0.155))
		st.rotation.z = -0.62
		var st2 := _part(_torso, strap, bag_m, Vector3(0.03, 0.35, 0.155))
		st2.rotation.z = -0.62
	_arm_l = _limb(_torso, Vector3(-0.25, 0.53, 0), 0.06, 0.56, coat_m, skin_m)
	_arm_r = _limb(_torso, Vector3(0.25, 0.53, 0), 0.06, 0.56, coat_m, skin_m)
	_neck = Node3D.new()
	_neck.position = Vector3(0, 0.64, 0)
	_torso.add_child(_neck)
	_head_parts = Node3D.new()
	_head_parts.position = Vector3(0, -0.64, 0)
	_neck.add_child(_head_parts)
	_head()
	_knee_l = _leg_l.get_node("Lower")
	_knee_r = _leg_r.get_node("Lower")
	_elbow_l = _arm_l.get_node("Lower")
	_elbow_r = _arm_r.get_node("Lower")
	# animate only while on screen
	var vis := VisibleOnScreenNotifier3D.new()
	vis.aabb = AABB(Vector3(-0.6, 0, -0.6), Vector3(1.2, 2.2, 1.2))
	vis.screen_entered.connect(func() -> void: set_process(true))
	vis.screen_exited.connect(func() -> void: set_process(false))
	add_child(vis)
	if ArtStyle.handheld:
		scale = Vector3.ONE * ArtStyle.FIGURE_SCALE
		_neck.scale = Vector3.ONE * ArtStyle.HEAD
		for leg: Node3D in [_leg_l, _leg_r]:
			leg.scale = Vector3(1.1, ArtStyle.LEGS, 1.1)
		for arm: Node3D in [_arm_l, _arm_r]:
			arm.scale = Vector3(1.05, ArtStyle.ARMS, 1.05)
	for pivot: Node3D in [_torso, _head_parts, _leg_l, _leg_r, _arm_l, _arm_r, _knee_l, _knee_r, _elbow_l, _elbow_r]:
		_merge(pivot)


## Collapses the primitive parts under one limb pivot into a single mesh with the
## colours in vertex colours: one surface for cloth and skin, one for metal fittings.
## Emissive parts stay separate. Turns ~50 draws per figure into ~6.
func _merge(pivot: Node3D) -> void:
	var groups := {}
	for c in pivot.get_children():
		var mi := c as MeshInstance3D
		if mi == null:
			continue
		var m := mi.material_override as StandardMaterial3D
		if m == null or m.emission_enabled:
			continue
		var key := "metal" if m.metallic > 0.5 else "matte"
		if not groups.has(key):
			groups[key] = []
		(groups[key] as Array).append(mi)
	if groups.is_empty():
		return
	var mesh := ArrayMesh.new()
	for key: String in groups:
		var verts := PackedVector3Array()
		var norms := PackedVector3Array()
		var cols := PackedColorArray()
		var idx := PackedInt32Array()
		for mi: MeshInstance3D in groups[key]:
			var a := mi.mesh.surface_get_arrays(0)
			var x := mi.transform
			var nb := x.basis.inverse().transposed()
			var col := (mi.material_override as StandardMaterial3D).albedo_color
			var base := verts.size()
			var v: PackedVector3Array = a[Mesh.ARRAY_VERTEX]
			var n: PackedVector3Array = a[Mesh.ARRAY_NORMAL]
			for i in v.size():
				verts.append(x * v[i])
				norms.append((nb * n[i]).normalized())
				cols.append(col)
			var ii: PackedInt32Array = a[Mesh.ARRAY_INDEX]
			if ii.is_empty():
				for i in v.size():
					idx.append(base + i)
			else:
				for i in ii:
					idx.append(base + i)
			mi.queue_free()
			pivot.remove_child(mi)
		var arr := []
		arr.resize(Mesh.ARRAY_MAX)
		arr[Mesh.ARRAY_VERTEX] = verts
		arr[Mesh.ARRAY_NORMAL] = norms
		arr[Mesh.ARRAY_COLOR] = cols
		arr[Mesh.ARRAY_INDEX] = idx
		mesh.add_surface_from_arrays(Mesh.PRIMITIVE_TRIANGLES, arr)
		var mat := _mat(Color.WHITE, 0.4 if key == "metal" else 0.78, 0.7 if key == "metal" else 0.05)
		mat.vertex_color_use_as_albedo = true
		mat.vertex_color_is_srgb = true
		mesh.surface_set_material(mesh.get_surface_count() - 1, mat)
	var merged := MeshInstance3D.new()
	merged.name = "Merged"
	merged.mesh = mesh
	pivot.add_child(merged)


func _head() -> void:
	var skin_m := _mat(skin, 0.7)
	var hair_m := _mat(hair_color, 0.75)
	var neck := CylinderMesh.new()
	neck.top_radius = 0.06
	neck.bottom_radius = 0.07
	neck.height = 0.1
	_part(_head_parts, neck, skin_m, Vector3(0, 0.66, 0))
	var head := SphereMesh.new()
	head.radius = 0.17
	head.height = 0.37
	_part(_head_parts, head, skin_m, Vector3(0, 0.82, 0), Vector3(0.95, 1, 1))
	# face: eye whites and pupils, brows, nose, mouth, a little colour in the cheeks
	var white := SphereMesh.new()
	white.radius = 0.028
	white.height = 0.04
	var pupil := SphereMesh.new()
	pupil.radius = 0.017
	pupil.height = 0.026
	var white_m := _mat(Color(0.92, 0.9, 0.86), 0.5)
	var pupil_m := _mat(Color(0.07, 0.05, 0.05), 0.3)
	var brow := BoxMesh.new()
	brow.size = Vector3(0.055, 0.012, 0.012)
	for sx: float in [-1.0, 1.0]:
		_part(_head_parts, white, white_m, Vector3(sx * 0.058, 0.835, -0.148))
		_part(_head_parts, pupil, pupil_m, Vector3(sx * 0.058, 0.833, -0.166))
		_part(_head_parts, brow, hair_m, Vector3(sx * 0.06, 0.875, -0.152)).rotation.z = -sx * 0.12
		var cheek := SphereMesh.new()
		cheek.radius = 0.03
		cheek.height = 0.02
		_part(_head_parts, cheek, _mat(skin.lerp(Color(0.85, 0.4, 0.38), 0.35), 0.8), Vector3(sx * 0.095, 0.79, -0.135))
	var nose := SphereMesh.new()
	nose.radius = 0.022
	nose.height = 0.04
	_part(_head_parts, nose, _mat(skin.darkened(0.08), 0.7), Vector3(0, 0.8, -0.168))
	var mouth := BoxMesh.new()
	mouth.size = Vector3(0.045, 0.01, 0.01)
	_part(_head_parts, mouth, _mat(skin.darkened(0.35), 0.7), Vector3(0, 0.755, -0.158))
	if hood:
		var h := SphereMesh.new()
		h.radius = 0.2
		h.height = 0.43
		_part(_head_parts, h, _mat(coat, 0.85), Vector3(0, 0.84, 0.04), Vector3(1, 1, 0.95))
		var peak := BoxMesh.new()
		peak.size = Vector3(0.33, 0.045, 0.09)
		_part(_head_parts, peak, _mat(coat, 0.85), Vector3(0, 0.98, -0.14)).rotation.x = 0.35
		return
	if hair != "bald":
		var cap := SphereMesh.new()
		cap.radius = 0.178
		cap.height = 0.24
		cap.is_hemisphere = true
		_part(_head_parts, cap, hair_m, Vector3(0, 0.85, 0.012), Vector3(0.97, 1, 1))
		var back := SphereMesh.new()
		back.radius = 0.17
		back.height = 0.3
		_part(_head_parts, back, hair_m, Vector3(0, 0.81, 0.04), Vector3(0.97, 1, 0.9))
		var fringe := BoxMesh.new()
		fringe.size = Vector3(0.24, 0.05, 0.05)
		_part(_head_parts, fringe, hair_m, Vector3(0, 0.93, -0.13)).rotation.x = -0.4
	if hair == "bun":
		var bun := SphereMesh.new()
		bun.radius = 0.075
		bun.height = 0.15
		_part(_head_parts, bun, hair_m, Vector3(0, 0.93, 0.15))
	elif hair == "long":
		var fall := BoxMesh.new()
		fall.size = Vector3(0.28, 0.32, 0.08)
		_part(_head_parts, fall, hair_m, Vector3(0, 0.66, 0.11))
	match hat:
		"cap":
			var crown := CylinderMesh.new()
			crown.top_radius = 0.16
			crown.bottom_radius = 0.18
			crown.height = 0.1
			var m := _mat(accent.darkened(0.3), 0.85)
			_part(_head_parts, crown, m, Vector3(0, 0.96, 0))
			var bill := BoxMesh.new()
			bill.size = Vector3(0.22, 0.02, 0.12)
			_part(_head_parts, bill, m, Vector3(0, 0.93, -0.2))
		"hardhat":
			var shell := SphereMesh.new()
			shell.radius = 0.2
			shell.height = 0.22
			shell.is_hemisphere = true
			var y := _mat(Color(0.85, 0.62, 0.12), 0.45)
			_part(_head_parts, shell, y, Vector3(0, 0.9, 0))
			var rim := CylinderMesh.new()
			rim.top_radius = 0.235
			rim.bottom_radius = 0.235
			rim.height = 0.025
			_part(_head_parts, rim, y, Vector3(0, 0.9, 0))
			var lamp := CylinderMesh.new()
			lamp.top_radius = 0.035
			lamp.bottom_radius = 0.035
			lamp.height = 0.04
			var lm := _mat(Color(1.0, 0.9, 0.6), 0.3)
			lm.emission_enabled = true
			lm.emission = Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.5)
			lm.emission_energy_multiplier = 2.0
			_part(_head_parts, lamp, lm, Vector3(0, 0.98, -0.19)).rotation.x = PI * 0.5
		"brim":
			var m2 := _mat(Color(0.14, 0.12, 0.11), 0.85)
			var brim := CylinderMesh.new()
			brim.top_radius = 0.28
			brim.bottom_radius = 0.28
			brim.height = 0.02
			_part(_head_parts, brim, m2, Vector3(0, 0.92, 0))
			var crown2 := CylinderMesh.new()
			crown2.top_radius = 0.14
			crown2.bottom_radius = 0.16
			crown2.height = 0.14
			_part(_head_parts, crown2, m2, Vector3(0, 0.99, 0))


## speed_ratio is speed / walk speed: 1 is a walk, about 1.6 and up is a run.
func set_motion(moving: bool, speed_ratio: float = 1.0) -> void:
	_speed = clampf(speed_ratio, 0.0, 2.4) if moving else 0.0


## Procedural gait. One phase drives everything: thighs swing opposite each other, the
## knee folds while its leg travels forward, arms counter-swing with a bent elbow, the
## torso leans and twists against the hips, and the head stays level. `_run` blends the
## walk into a run (longer stride, higher knees, pumping arms, more lean and bounce).
func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	_t += delta
	_amount = move_toward(_amount, 1.0 if _speed > 0.05 else 0.0, delta * 7.0)
	_run = move_toward(_run, smoothstep(1.15, 1.45, _speed), delta * 5.0)
	_phase += delta * lerpf(10.0 * maxf(_speed, 0.4), 14.5, _run) * _amount
	var a := _amount
	var idle := 1.0 - a
	var sw := sin(_phase)
	var cw := cos(_phase)
	# legs
	var hip := lerpf(0.55, 0.95, _run) * a
	var knee := lerpf(0.7, 1.55, _run) * a
	var lift := 0.14 * _run * a
	_leg_l.rotation.x = sw * hip + lift
	_leg_r.rotation.x = -sw * hip + lift
	_knee_l.rotation.x = -(0.06 * a + knee * pow(0.5 + 0.5 * cw, 1.6))
	_knee_r.rotation.x = -(0.06 * a + knee * pow(0.5 - 0.5 * cw, 1.6))
	# arms: opposite the legs, elbows bent more the faster we go
	var arm := lerpf(0.45, 0.9, _run) * a
	var sway := sin(_t * 1.3) * 0.03 * idle
	_arm_l.rotation.x = -sw * arm + sway
	_arm_r.rotation.x = sw * arm - sway
	var splay := lerpf(0.08, 0.16, _run * a)
	_arm_l.rotation.z = -splay
	_arm_r.rotation.z = splay
	var bend := lerpf(0.1, lerpf(0.3, 1.35, _run), a)
	_elbow_l.rotation.x = bend + maxf(-sw, 0.0) * 0.3 * a
	_elbow_r.rotation.x = bend + maxf(sw, 0.0) * 0.3 * a
	# body
	var breathe := sin(_t * 2.0) * 0.012 * idle
	_hips.position.y = _hip_h + absf(cw) * lerpf(0.035, 0.075, _run) * a - 0.03 * _run * a
	_hips.rotation.z = sw * 0.03 * a
	var lean := lerpf(0.06, 0.26, _run) * a
	var twist := sw * lerpf(0.07, 0.17, _run) * a
	_torso.rotation.x = -lean
	_torso.rotation.y = twist
	_torso.scale = Vector3(1.0, 1.0 + breathe, 1.0)
	# head stays level and forward; glances about when idle
	_neck.rotation.x = lean * 0.7
	_neck.rotation.y = -twist * 0.7 + sin(_t * 0.37) * 0.14 * idle
	_neck.rotation.z = sin(_t * 0.23 + 1.0) * 0.03 * idle


## Two-segment limb. Returns the upper pivot (hip or shoulder); the knee or elbow is
## its child "Lower".
func _limb(parent: Node3D, at: Vector3, r: float, length: float, m: Material, end_m: Material) -> Node3D:
	var pivot := Node3D.new()
	pivot.position = at
	parent.add_child(pivot)
	var half := length * 0.5
	var up := CylinderMesh.new()
	up.top_radius = r * 1.15
	up.bottom_radius = r
	up.height = half
	up.radial_segments = 8
	_part(pivot, up, m, Vector3(0, -half * 0.5, 0))
	var joint := SphereMesh.new()
	joint.radius = r * 1.15
	joint.height = r * 2.3
	_part(pivot, joint, m, Vector3.ZERO)
	var lower := Node3D.new()
	lower.name = "Lower"
	lower.position = Vector3(0, -half, 0)
	pivot.add_child(lower)
	var kn := SphereMesh.new()
	kn.radius = r * 1.02
	kn.height = r * 2.04
	_part(lower, kn, m, Vector3.ZERO)
	var lo := CylinderMesh.new()
	lo.top_radius = r
	lo.bottom_radius = r * 0.85
	lo.height = half - r * 0.5
	lo.radial_segments = 8
	_part(lower, lo, m, Vector3(0, -(half - r * 0.5) * 0.5, 0))
	if end_m.get_meta("boot", false):
		var b := BoxMesh.new()
		b.size = Vector3(r * 2.2, r * 1.4, r * 3.6)
		_part(lower, b, end_m, Vector3(0, -half + r * 0.5, -r * 0.7))
		var cuff := CylinderMesh.new()
		cuff.top_radius = r * 1.25
		cuff.bottom_radius = r * 1.15
		cuff.height = r * 2.6
		cuff.radial_segments = 8
		_part(lower, cuff, end_m, Vector3(0, -half + r * 1.9, 0))
	else:
		var sp := SphereMesh.new()
		sp.radius = r * 1.05
		sp.height = r * 2.1
		_part(lower, sp, end_m, Vector3(0, -half + r * 0.6, 0))
	return pivot


func _part(parent: Node3D, mesh: Mesh, m: Material, at: Vector3, s: Vector3 = Vector3.ONE) -> MeshInstance3D:
	_lower_poly(mesh)
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	mi.mesh = mesh
	mi.material_override = m
	mi.position = at
	mi.scale = s
	parent.add_child(mi)
	return mi


## Godot's primitive defaults (64 segments, 32 rings) cost thousands of triangles per
## eye or button; at handheld scale a few segments read the same. Caps what was not set.
func _lower_poly(mesh: Mesh) -> void:
	var big := 0.0
	if mesh is SphereMesh:
		var sm := mesh as SphereMesh
		big = sm.radius
		sm.radial_segments = mini(sm.radial_segments, 16 if big > 0.1 else (10 if big > 0.04 else 6))
		sm.rings = mini(sm.rings, 10 if big > 0.1 else (6 if big > 0.04 else 4))
	elif mesh is CylinderMesh:
		var cm := mesh as CylinderMesh
		big = maxf(cm.top_radius, cm.bottom_radius)
		cm.radial_segments = mini(cm.radial_segments, 14 if big > 0.15 else (10 if big > 0.04 else 6))
		cm.rings = 0
	elif mesh is CapsuleMesh:
		var cp := mesh as CapsuleMesh
		cp.radial_segments = mini(cp.radial_segments, 12)
		cp.rings = mini(cp.rings, 4)


func _mat(c: Color, rough: float, metal: float = 0.0) -> StandardMaterial3D:
	var m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	m.albedo_color = c
	m.roughness = rough
	m.metallic = metal
	if xray:
		m.stencil_mode = BaseMaterial3D.STENCIL_MODE_XRAY
		m.stencil_color = Color(1.0, 0.72, 0.35, 0.5)
	if c.v < 0.12:
		m.set_meta("boot", true)
	return m


==============================================================================
== art_provisional/fog.gdshader
==============================================================================
shader_type canvas_item;
// PROVISIONAL. Drifting sea mist plus a soft vignette. One layer, no screen read.

uniform vec4 fog_color : source_color = vec4(0.6, 0.69, 0.75, 1.0);
uniform float fog_amount = 0.11;
uniform float vignette = 0.5;

float hash(vec2 p) {
	return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453);
}

float noise(vec2 p) {
	vec2 i = floor(p);
	vec2 f = fract(p);
	f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
	return mix(mix(hash(i), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0)), f.x),
			mix(hash(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0)), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0)), f.x), f.y);
}

void fragment() {
	float n = noise(UV * vec2(3.0, 2.0) + vec2(TIME * 0.02, 0.0)) * 0.6
			+ noise(UV * vec2(7.0, 4.0) - vec2(TIME * 0.035, 0.0)) * 0.4;
	float fog = fog_amount * n * 1.6;
	float v = smoothstep(0.35, 0.85, distance(UV, vec2(0.5))) * vignette;
	vec3 col = mix(fog_color.rgb, vec3(0.01, 0.02, 0.04), v / (v + fog + 0.0001));
	COLOR = vec4(col, clamp(fog + v, 0.0, 0.9));
}


==============================================================================
== art_provisional/prov_block.gd
==============================================================================
@tool
class_name ProvBlock
extends StaticBody2D
## PROVISIONAL ART. A solid piece of the world drawn in code, in 3/4 view.
## The node origin is the bottom-centre of the footprint (its front edge), so
## y-sorting works. `size` is the footprint (width x depth); `height` is how far
## the front face rises. Replace with authored art; keep the footprints.

@export var size := Vector2(48, 24):
	set(v):
		size = v
		queue_redraw()
@export var height := 20.0:
	set(v):
		height = v
		queue_redraw()
@export_enum("pod", "hut", "rock", "fence", "gate", "crate", "lamp", "tree", "pit", "adit", "sign", "none") var style := "crate":
	set(v):
		style = v
		queue_redraw()
@export var lit := false:
	set(v):
		lit = v
		queue_redraw()
@export var solid := true
@export var tint := Color(1, 1, 1):
	set(v):
		tint = v
		queue_redraw()

static var _light_tex: GradientTexture2D

var _shape: CollisionShape2D
var _solid_now := true


func _ready() -> void:
	if Engine.is_editor_hint():
		return
	collision_layer = 1
	collision_mask = 0
	if solid:
		var rect := RectangleShape2D.new()
		rect.size = size
		_shape = CollisionShape2D.new()
		_shape.shape = rect
		_shape.position = Vector2(0, -size.y * 0.5)
		_shape.disabled = not _solid_now
		add_child(_shape)
	if lit:
		_add_light()


func set_solid(v: bool) -> void:
	_solid_now = v
	if _shape != null:
		_shape.set_deferred("disabled", not v)


static func light_texture() -> GradientTexture2D:
	if _light_tex == null:
		var g := Gradient.new()
		g.set_color(0, Color(1, 1, 1, 1))
		g.set_color(1, Color(0, 0, 0, 0))
		_light_tex = GradientTexture2D.new()
		_light_tex.gradient = g
		_light_tex.width = 128
		_light_tex.height = 128
		_light_tex.fill = GradientTexture2D.FILL_RADIAL
		_light_tex.fill_from = Vector2(0.5, 0.5)
		_light_tex.fill_to = Vector2(1.0, 0.5)
	return _light_tex


func _add_light() -> void:
	var l := PointLight2D.new()
	l.texture = light_texture()
	l.color = Color(1.0, 0.6, 0.3)
	l.energy = 1.1
	if style == "lamp":
		l.position = Vector2(3, -height + 4)
		l.texture_scale = 1.9
	else:
		l.position = Vector2(size.x * 0.5 - 15, -height * 0.5 + 6)
		l.texture_scale = 1.2
	add_child(l)


func _draw() -> void:
	var w := size.x
	var d := size.y
	var h := height
	var l := -w * 0.5
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = hash(Vector2i(position))
	match style:
		"pod", "hut":
			var wall := (Color(0.4, 0.45, 0.48) if style == "pod" else Color(0.3, 0.36, 0.33)) * tint
			var roof := wall.lightened(0.14)
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -d - h, w, d), roof)
			for i in range(1, int(w / 8.0)):
				draw_line(Vector2(l + i * 8, -d - h), Vector2(l + i * 8, -h), roof.darkened(0.14), 1.0)
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h, w, h), wall)
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h, w, 1), wall.darkened(0.35))
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -2, w, 2), wall.darkened(0.4))
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h + 3, w, 1), Color(0.72, 0.4, 0.2, 0.75))
			draw_rect(Rect2(l + 6, -13, 8, 13), wall.darkened(0.5))
			draw_rect(Rect2(l + w - 23, -h + 6, 15, 8), wall.darkened(0.5))
			draw_rect(Rect2(l + w - 22, -h + 7, 13, 6), Color(1.0, 0.8, 0.45) if lit else Color(0.1, 0.14, 0.18))
			for i in 3:
				var x := l + rng.randf() * w
				draw_line(Vector2(x, -h + 4), Vector2(x, -h + 4 + rng.randf_range(4, 12)), Color(0.45, 0.27, 0.18, 0.5), 1.0)
		"rock":
			var face := Color(0.19, 0.22, 0.25) * tint
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -d - h, w, d), Color(0.24, 0.28, 0.29) * tint)
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h, w, h), face)
			for i in int(w / 12.0):
				var p := Vector2(l + rng.randf() * w, -h + rng.randf() * h)
				draw_line(p, p + Vector2(rng.randf_range(6, 20), rng.randf_range(-2, 2)), face.lightened(0.1), 1.0)
				var q := Vector2(l + rng.randf() * w, -h + rng.randf() * h)
				draw_line(q, q + Vector2(rng.randf_range(-3, 3), rng.randf_range(5, 14)), face.darkened(0.3), 1.0)
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h - 1, w, 3), Color(0.15, 0.26, 0.2))
		"fence":
			var c := Color(0.37, 0.31, 0.27) * tint
			if w >= d:
				var n := maxi(1, int(w / 16.0))
				draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h + 2, w, h - 3), Color(c, 0.16))
				draw_line(Vector2(l, -h + 2), Vector2(l + w, -h + 2), c.lightened(0.15), 1.0)
				draw_line(Vector2(l, -h * 0.5), Vector2(l + w, -h * 0.5), c, 1.0)
				for i in n + 1:
					draw_rect(Rect2(l + i * (w / n) - 1, -h, 2, h), c)
			else:
				var n := maxi(1, int(d / 16.0))
				draw_line(Vector2(0, -h + 2), Vector2(0, -d - h + 2), c.lightened(0.15), 1.0)
				draw_line(Vector2(0, -h * 0.5), Vector2(0, -d - h * 0.5), c, 1.0)
				for i in n + 1:
					draw_rect(Rect2(-1, -i * (d / n) - h, 2, h), c)
		"gate":
			var c := Color(0.3, 0.33, 0.36)
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h, w, h), Color(c, 0.5))
			for i in range(1, int(w / 5.0)):
				draw_line(Vector2(l + i * 5, -h), Vector2(l + i * 5, 0), c, 1.0)
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h, w, 2), c.lightened(0.1))
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -2, w, 2), c)
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h - 3, 3, h + 3), c.darkened(0.2))
			draw_rect(Rect2(l + w - 3, -h - 3, 3, h + 3), c.darkened(0.2))
			for i in int(w / 6.0):
				draw_rect(Rect2(l + i * 6, -h * 0.6, 6, 4), Color(0.8, 0.62, 0.15) if i % 2 == 0 else Color(0.08, 0.08, 0.09))
		"crate":
			var c := Color(0.55, 0.53, 0.47) * tint
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -d - h, w, d), c.lightened(0.1))
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h, w, h), c.darkened(0.15))
			draw_line(Vector2(l, -h), Vector2(l + w, -h), c.darkened(0.4), 1.0)
			for i in range(1, 3):
				draw_line(Vector2(l, -h - d * i / 3.0), Vector2(l + w, -h - d * i / 3.0), c.darkened(0.2), 1.0)
		"lamp":
			var c := Color(0.2, 0.22, 0.24)
			draw_rect(Rect2(-1, -h, 2, h), c)
			draw_rect(Rect2(-1, -h, 6, 2), c)
			draw_rect(Rect2(2, -h + 2, 4, 2), Color(1.0, 0.82, 0.5) if lit else Color(0.12, 0.13, 0.15))
		"tree":
			var s := rng.randf_range(0.85, 1.2)
			var g := Color(0.08, 0.19, 0.16).lightened(rng.randf_range(0.0, 0.06)) * tint
			draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, 0.0, Vector2(1.0, 0.4))
			draw_circle(Vector2.ZERO, 11.0 * s, Color(0, 0, 0, 0.3))
			draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, 0.0, Vector2.ONE)
			draw_rect(Rect2(-2, -12, 4, 12), Color(0.19, 0.14, 0.11))
			var tiers := 4
			var step := (h - 10.0) / tiers
			for i in tiers:
				var y0 := -9.0 - step * i
				var y1 := y0 - step * 1.7
				var hw := 15.0 * s * (1.0 - i * 0.2)
				draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([Vector2(-hw, y0), Vector2(hw, y0), Vector2(0, y1)]), g.lightened(0.05 * i))
				draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([Vector2(1, y0), Vector2(hw, y0), Vector2(1, y1)]), g.darkened(0.28))
		"pit":
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -d, w, d), Color(0.035, 0.045, 0.055))
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -d, w, 7), Color(0.14, 0.13, 0.12))
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -d + 7, w, 2), Color(0.08, 0.08, 0.08))
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -9, w, 5), Color(0.09, 0.15, 0.19))
		"adit":
			draw_rect(Rect2(l - 4, -h - 4, w + 8, h + 4), Color(0.25, 0.19, 0.14))
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h, w, h), Color(0.3, 0.33, 0.35))
			draw_line(Vector2(0, -h), Vector2(0, 0), Color(0.2, 0.22, 0.24), 1.0)
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h, w, h), Color(0.82, 0.84, 0.8), false, 1.0)
			for i in 4:
				draw_rect(Rect2(l + 4 + i * (w - 9) / 3.0, -h + 4, 1, 1), Color(0.15, 0.16, 0.18))
				draw_rect(Rect2(l + 4 + i * (w - 9) / 3.0, -5, 1, 1), Color(0.15, 0.16, 0.18))
		"sign":
			draw_rect(Rect2(l, -h, w, h - 5), Color(0.8, 0.78, 0.7))
			for i in 3:
				draw_line(Vector2(l + 1, -h + 2 + i * 2), Vector2(l + w - 1, -h + 2 + i * 2), Color(0.25, 0.22, 0.2), 1.0)
		_:
			pass

== art_provisional/prov_rail.gd
==============================================================================
@tool
class_name ProvRail
extends Node2D
## PROVISIONAL ART. Narrow-gauge rail drawn along `points`. Also the path data
## that OreCart follows.

@export var points := PackedVector2Array():
	set(v):
		points = v
		queue_redraw()
@export var buffer_start := false
@export var buffer_end := false


func _draw() -> void:
	var tie := Color(0.2, 0.16, 0.13)
	var steel := Color(0.42, 0.43, 0.45)
	for i in points.size() - 1:
		var a := points[i]
		var b := points[i + 1]
		var dir := (b - a).normalized()
		var n := dir.orthogonal()
		var length := a.distance_to(b)
		var s := 0.0
		while s < length:
			var p := a + dir * s
			draw_line(p - n * 6.0, p + n * 6.0, tie, 2.0)
			s += 6.0
		draw_line(a - n * 4.0, b - n * 4.0, steel, 1.0)
		draw_line(a + n * 4.0, b + n * 4.0, steel, 1.0)
	if buffer_start and points.size() > 1:
		_buffer(points[0], (points[0] - points[1]).normalized())
	if buffer_end and points.size() > 1:
		var e := points.size() - 1
		_buffer(points[e], (points[e] - points[e - 1]).normalized())


func _buffer(at: Vector2, out: Vector2) -> void:
	var p := at + out * 12.0
	var n := out.orthogonal()
	draw_line(p - n * 7.0, p + n * 7.0, Color(0.5, 0.2, 0.15), 3.0)

== art_provisional/rain.gd
==============================================================================
class_name Rain
extends Control
## PROVISIONAL ART. Screen-space precipitation for 2D maps: rain streaks, or slow snow.
## EnvironmentController2D sets the amounts through set_precipitation(rain, snow).

@export var count := 220
@export var fall := Vector2(-70.0, 330.0)
@export var streak := 0.035

var rain := 0.5
var snow := 0.0
var _pos := PackedVector2Array()
var _speed := PackedFloat32Array()


func _ready() -> void:
	add_to_group(&"precipitation")
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 1
	for i in count:
		_pos.append(Vector2(rng.randf() * 700.0, rng.randf() * 300.0))
		_speed.append(rng.randf_range(0.75, 1.25))


func set_precipitation(r: float, s: float) -> void:
	rain = r
	snow = s


func clear_particles() -> void:
	pass


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if not is_visible_in_tree() or (rain <= 0.02 and snow <= 0.02):
		if not _pos.is_empty():
			queue_redraw()
		return
	var s := size
	var snowing := snow > rain
	for i in _pos.size():
		var v := fall * _speed[i]
		if snowing:
			v = Vector2(-12.0 + sin(Time.get_ticks_msec() * 0.001 + i) * 10.0, 28.0) * _speed[i]
		var p := _pos[i] + v * delta
		if p.y > s.y:
			p.y -= s.y + 10.0
			p.x += 40.0
		if p.x < -10.0:
			p.x += s.x + 20.0
		elif p.x > s.x + 10.0:
			p.x -= s.x + 20.0
		_pos[i] = p
	queue_redraw()


func _draw() -> void:
	if rain <= 0.02 and snow <= 0.02:
		return
	if snow > rain:
		var n := int(_pos.size() * clampf(snow, 0.0, 1.0))
		for i in n:
			draw_rect(Rect2(_pos[i].round(), Vector2(1, 1)), Color(0.95, 0.97, 1.0, 0.85))
		return
	var k := int(_pos.size() * clampf(rain, 0.0, 1.0))
	for i in k:
		var p := _pos[i]
		draw_line(p, p - fall * _speed[i] * streak, Color(0.75, 0.82, 0.92, 0.3), 1.0)

== art_provisional/rain_3d.gd
==============================================================================
class_name Rain3D
extends GPUParticles3D
## Precipitation around the player: rain streaks that catch the lamps, splashes on the
## ground, and snow. Follows its parent (the player); particles stay in world space.
## One set of emitters is reused for every weather: EnvironmentController calls
## set_precipitation(rain, snow) with 0..1 amounts and the emitters thin out or stop.

var _splash: GPUParticles3D
var _snow: GPUParticles3D


func _init() -> void:
	add_to_group(&"precipitation")
	# the handheld view is smaller and low-res: fewer streaks read the same
	amount = 900 if ArtStyle.handheld else 1600
	lifetime = 0.9
	preprocess = 1.0
	local_coords = false
	visibility_aabb = AABB(Vector3(-20, -14, -20), Vector3(40, 30, 40))
	var pm := ParticleProcessMaterial.new()
	pm.emission_shape = ParticleProcessMaterial.EMISSION_SHAPE_BOX
	pm.emission_box_extents = Vector3(16, 0.5, 16)
	pm.direction = Vector3(-0.15, -1, 0.1)
	pm.spread = 2.0
	pm.initial_velocity_min = 16.0
	pm.initial_velocity_max = 20.0
	pm.gravity = Vector3(0, -6, 0)
	pm.particle_flag_align_y = true
	process_material = pm
	var q := QuadMesh.new()
	q.size = Vector2(0.012, 0.55)
	var m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	m.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
	m.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA
	m.albedo_color = Color(0.8, 0.86, 0.95, 0.14)
	m.billboard_mode = BaseMaterial3D.BILLBOARD_FIXED_Y
	m.billboard_keep_scale = true
	q.material = m
	draw_pass_1 = q
	position = Vector3(0, 12, 0)
	_splash = _splashes()
	add_child(_splash)
	_snow = _snowfall()
	add_child(_snow)
	set_precipitation(0.5, 0.0)


## rain and snow are 0..1: 0 stops the emitter, 1 is the heaviest fall.
func set_precipitation(rain: float, snow: float) -> void:
	_level(self, rain)
	_level(_splash, rain)
	_level(_snow, snow)


## Drop every live particle (used when the weather is set instantly, not blended).
func clear_particles() -> void:
	for p: GPUParticles3D in [self, _splash, _snow]:
		p.restart()


static func _level(p: GPUParticles3D, level: float) -> void:
	var on := level > 0.02
	if p.emitting != on:
		p.emitting = on
	if on:
		p.amount_ratio = clampf(level, 0.05, 1.0)


## Droplets bursting on the ground around the player.
static func _splashes() -> GPUParticles3D:
	var p := GPUParticles3D.new()
	p.amount = 240 if ArtStyle.handheld else 460
	p.lifetime = 0.35
	p.local_coords = false
	p.position = Vector3(0, -11.9, 0)
	p.visibility_aabb = AABB(Vector3(-16, -2, -16), Vector3(32, 4, 32))
	var pm := ParticleProcessMaterial.new()
	pm.emission_shape = ParticleProcessMaterial.EMISSION_SHAPE_BOX
	pm.emission_box_extents = Vector3(11, 0.02, 11)
	pm.direction = Vector3(0, 1, 0)
	pm.spread = 35.0
	pm.initial_velocity_min = 0.6
	pm.initial_velocity_max = 1.3
	pm.gravity = Vector3(0, -9, 0)
	pm.scale_min = 0.6
	pm.scale_max = 1.2
	p.process_material = pm
	var q := QuadMesh.new()
	q.size = Vector2(0.035, 0.035)
	var m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	m.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
	m.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA
	m.albedo_color = Color(0.85, 0.9, 1.0, 0.35)
	m.billboard_mode = BaseMaterial3D.BILLBOARD_ENABLED
	q.material = m
	p.draw_pass_1 = q
	return p


## Slow drifting flakes.
static func _snowfall() -> GPUParticles3D:
	var p := GPUParticles3D.new()
	p.amount = 420 if ArtStyle.handheld else 700
	p.lifetime = 7.0
	p.preprocess = 7.0
	p.local_coords = false
	p.emitting = false
	p.visibility_aabb = AABB(Vector3(-20, -14, -20), Vector3(40, 30, 40))
	var pm := ParticleProcessMaterial.new()
	pm.emission_shape = ParticleProcessMaterial.EMISSION_SHAPE_BOX
	pm.emission_box_extents = Vector3(16, 0.5, 16)
	pm.direction = Vector3(-0.2, -1, 0.1)
	pm.spread = 14.0
	pm.initial_velocity_min = 1.5
	pm.initial_velocity_max = 2.4
	pm.gravity = Vector3(-0.15, -0.1, 0)
	pm.turbulence_enabled = true
	pm.turbulence_noise_strength = 0.5
	pm.turbulence_noise_scale = 2.5
	pm.scale_min = 0.6
	pm.scale_max = 1.4
	p.process_material = pm
	var q := QuadMesh.new()
	q.size = Vector2(0.06, 0.06)
	var m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	m.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
	m.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA
	m.albedo_color = Color(0.95, 0.97, 1.0, 0.8)
	# soft round flake
	var g := Gradient.new()
	g.set_color(0, Color.WHITE)
	g.set_color(1, Color(1, 1, 1, 0))
	var tex := GradientTexture2D.new()
	tex.gradient = g
	tex.width = 16
	tex.height = 16
	tex.fill = GradientTexture2D.FILL_RADIAL
	tex.fill_from = Vector2(0.5, 0.5)
	tex.fill_to = Vector2(1.0, 0.5)
	m.albedo_texture = tex
	m.billboard_mode = BaseMaterial3D.BILLBOARD_ENABLED
	q.material = m
	p.draw_pass_1 = q
	return p

== core/art_style.gd

class_name ArtStyle
## One switch for the HeartGold-style look test (PROJECT_LOG entry 17). With `handheld`
## on, the 3D world renders at the 480x270 base resolution and is scaled up with sharp
## pixels, the camera is steeper and closer, people have chibi proportions and paving
## is chunkier. Set it false to get the previous look back; nothing else changes.

static var handheld := true

## Camera (FollowCamera reads these when handheld is on).
const CAM_PITCH := 52.0
const CAM_DISTANCE := 27.0
const CAM_FOV := 30.0
## People drawn larger than life against the buildings, as handheld RPGs do.
const FIGURE_SCALE := 1.25
## Figure proportions: head size, leg length, arm length.
const HEAD := 1.38
const LEGS := 0.78
const ARMS := 0.88
## Cobble size on wet_ground (metres).
const STONE := 0.45


==============================================================================
== core/audio_manager.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Autoload "Audio": the one place music and interface sounds are requested.
##
## PLACEHOLDERS: no audio assets exist yet (see audio/README.md).
##   Audio.play_music("title")   looks for res://audio/music/title.ogg (or .mp3/.wav);
##                               if the file is missing nothing plays and no error is raised.
##   Audio.ui("move")            looks for res://audio/ui/move.wav (or .ogg); if missing, a
##                               soft synthesised tone stands in so the interface is not silent.
## Dropping correctly named files into those folders is all that is needed to replace them.
## Music plays on the "Music" bus, interface sounds on "SFX" (volumes in Settings).

const MUSIC_DIR := "res://audio/music/"
const UI_DIR := "res://audio/ui/"
## Stand-in tones: [frequency Hz, second frequency (0 = none), seconds, level].
const UI_TONES := {
	"move": [880.0, 0.0, 0.07, 0.10],
	"accept": [660.0, 990.0, 0.16, 0.14],
	"back": [520.0, 390.0, 0.12, 0.11],
	"adjust": [740.0, 0.0, 0.04, 0.07],
}

## Id of the track asked for last ("" = none), whether or not a file exists for it.
var current_music := ""
## Id of the last interface sound asked for (for tests).
var last_ui := ""

var _music: AudioStreamPlayer
var _ui: AudioStreamPlayer
var _fade: Tween
var _cache := {}


func _ready() -> void:
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	_music = AudioStreamPlayer.new()
	_music.bus = "Music"
	add_child(_music)
	_ui = AudioStreamPlayer.new()
	_ui.bus = "SFX"
	_ui.max_polyphony = 4
	add_child(_ui)


## Cross-fades to a looping track. Unknown or missing ids fade to silence.
func play_music(id: String, fade: float = 1.5) -> void:
	if id == current_music:
		return
	current_music = id
	var stream := _find(MUSIC_DIR, id, ["ogg", "mp3", "wav"])
	if _fade != null:
		_fade.kill()
	_fade = create_tween()
	if _music.playing:
		_fade.tween_property(_music, "volume_db", -40.0, fade * 0.5)
	_fade.tween_callback(func() -> void:
		_music.stop()
		if stream != null:
			_music.stream = stream
			_music.volume_db = -40.0
			_music.play())
	if stream != null:
		_fade.tween_property(_music, "volume_db", 0.0, fade)


func stop_music(fade: float = 1.0) -> void:
	play_music("", fade)


## A short interface sound: "move", "accept", "back", "adjust".
func ui(id: String) -> void:
	last_ui = id
	if not _cache.has(id):
		var s := _find(UI_DIR, id, ["wav", "ogg"])
		_cache[id] = s if s != null else _tone(UI_TONES.get(id, UI_TONES["move"]))
	_ui.stream = _cache[id]
	_ui.play()


func _find(dir: String, id: String, exts: Array) -> AudioStream:
	if id == "":
		return null
	for e: String in exts:
		var p := "%s%s.%s" % [dir, id, e]
		if ResourceLoader.exists(p):
			return load(p) as AudioStream
	return null


## A soft sine blip with a quick attack and an exponential tail; optionally glides to a
## second pitch. 16-bit mono.
static func _tone(spec: Array) -> AudioStreamWAV:
	var rate := 22050
	var n := int(float(spec[2]) * rate)
	var data := PackedByteArray()
	data.resize(n * 2)
	var phase := 0.0
	for i in n:
		var t := float(i) / float(n)
		var f: float = spec[0] if float(spec[1]) <= 0.0 else lerpf(spec[0], spec[1], smoothstep(0.25, 0.6, t))
		phase += TAU * f / rate
		var env := minf(t * 30.0, 1.0) * exp(-t * 5.0) * (1.0 - t)
		var v := (sin(phase) + 0.25 * sin(phase * 2.0)) * env * float(spec[3])
		data.encode_s16(i * 2, int(clampf(v, -1.0, 1.0) * 32767.0))
	var w := AudioStreamWAV.new()
	w.format = AudioStreamWAV.FORMAT_16_BITS
	w.mix_rate = rate
	w.data = data
	return w


==============================================================================
== core/data.gd
==============================================================================
class_name Data
## JSON content loading. All narrative content lives under res://data.

static func load_json(path: String) -> Variant:
	if not FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		push_warning("Data: missing %s" % path)
		return null
	var parsed: Variant = JSON.parse_string(FileAccess.get_file_as_string(path))
	if parsed == null:
		push_error("Data: could not parse %s" % path)
	return parsed


static func load_dict(path: String) -> Dictionary:
	var v: Variant = load_json(path)
	if v is Dictionary:
		return v
	return {}


==============================================================================
== core/dialogue.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Data-driven dialogue runner. UI listens to the signals; nothing here draws.
##
## res://data/dialogue/<id>.json:
## {
##   "entry": [ {"if": cond, "goto": "node"}, {"goto": "first"} ],
##   "nodes": {
##     "first": {
##       "do":   [effects on entering the node],
##       "say":  [["Speaker", "line"], ["", "narration"]],
##       "then": [effects once the lines have been read],
##       "choices": [ {"text": "...", "if": cond, "do": [effects], "goto": "node"} ],
##       "goto": "node"            (used when no choice is available; default "end")
##     }
##   }
## }

signal started(id: String)
signal line_shown(speaker: String, text: String)
signal choices_shown(options: Array[String])
signal ended(id: String)

const ROOT := "res://data/dialogue/%s.json"

var active := false
var current_id := ""
var current_choices: Array[Dictionary] = []

var _data: Dictionary = {}
var _node: Dictionary = {}
var _lines: Array = []
var _line_i := 0
var _cache: Dictionary = {}
var _hops := 0


func start(id: String) -> bool:
	if active:
		return false
	if not _cache.has(id):
		_cache[id] = Data.load_dict(ROOT % id)
	_data = _cache[id]
	if _data.is_empty():
		push_warning("Dialogue: nothing to say for '%s'" % id)
		return false
	current_id = id
	active = true
	started.emit(id)
	var entry := "start"
	for e: Dictionary in _data.get("entry", []):
		if GameState.check(e.get("if")):
			entry = str(e.get("goto", "start"))
			break
	_hops = 0
	_enter(entry)
	return true


func advance() -> void:
	if not active or not current_choices.is_empty():
		return
	_line_i += 1
	if _line_i < _lines.size():
		_emit_line()
	else:
		_after_lines()


func choose(index: int) -> void:
	if not active or index < 0 or index >= current_choices.size():
		return
	var c := current_choices[index]
	current_choices = []
	GameState.apply(c.get("do"))
	_hops = 0
	_enter(str(c.get("goto", "end")))


func cancel() -> void:
	if active:
		_finish()


func choice_texts() -> Array[String]:
	var out: Array[String] = []
	for c in current_choices:
		out.append(str(c.get("text", "...")))
	return out


func _enter(node_name: String) -> void:
	var nodes: Dictionary = _data.get("nodes", {})
	_hops += 1
	if node_name == "end" or not nodes.has(node_name) or _hops > 32:
		if node_name != "end":
			push_warning("Dialogue '%s': bad node '%s'" % [current_id, node_name])
		_finish()
		return
	_node = nodes[node_name]
	GameState.apply(_node.get("do"))
	_lines = _node.get("say", [])
	_line_i = 0
	if _lines.is_empty():
		_after_lines()
	else:
		_emit_line()


func _emit_line() -> void:
	var l: Array = _lines[_line_i]
	line_shown.emit(str(l[0]), str(l[1]))


func _after_lines() -> void:
	GameState.apply(_node.get("then"))
	var opts: Array[Dictionary] = []
	for c: Dictionary in _node.get("choices", []):
		if GameState.check(c.get("if")):
			opts.append(c)
	if opts.is_empty():
		_enter(str(_node.get("goto", "end")))
	else:
		current_choices = opts
		choices_shown.emit(choice_texts())


func _finish() -> void:
	var id := current_id
	active = false
	current_id = ""
	current_choices = []
	_lines = []
	ended.emit(id)


==============================================================================
== core/game_state.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Persistent world state: a flat dictionary of flags, plus the tiny condition/effect
## language shared by dialogue, interactables and puzzles.
##
## Conditions (Dictionary):
##   {"flag": "k"}                truthy flag
##   {"flag": "k", "eq": value}   flag equals value
##   {"clue": "id"}               journal holds the clue
##   {"not": cond} {"all": [..]} {"any": [..]}
## Effects (Array of Dictionary):
##   {"set": {"k": value}}  {"clue": "id"}  {"quest": "id"}
##   {"reveal": "quest.objective"}  {"objective": "quest.objective"}  {"complete": "quest"}
##   {"notify": "text"}

signal flag_changed(key: String, value: Variant)
signal notified(text: String)
## Asks the world (game/main.gd) to move the player to another map.
signal travel_requested(map_id: String, spawn: String)
## A named place was entered; the HUD shows it as a title card.
signal location_entered(text: String)

var flags: Dictionary = {}


func _ready() -> void:
	InputSetup.ensure()


func reset() -> void:
	flags = {}


func get_flag(key: String, default: Variant = false) -> Variant:
	return flags.get(key, default)


func set_flag(key: String, value: Variant) -> void:
	if flags.has(key) and _same(flags[key], value):
		return
	flags[key] = value
	flag_changed.emit(key, value)


func notify(text: String) -> void:
	notified.emit(text)


func check(cond: Variant) -> bool:
	if cond == null:
		return true
	if cond is Array:
		for c: Variant in cond:
			if not check(c):
				return false
		return true
	if not cond is Dictionary:
		return bool(cond)
	var d: Dictionary = cond
	if d.is_empty():
		return true
	if d.has("all"):
		return check(d["all"])
	if d.has("any"):
		for c: Variant in d["any"]:
			if check(c):
				return true
		return false
	if d.has("not"):
		return not check(d["not"])
	if d.has("clue"):
		return Journal.has_clue(str(d["clue"]))
	if d.has("flag"):
		var v: Variant = get_flag(str(d["flag"]))
		if d.has("eq"):
			return _same(v, d["eq"])
		return _truthy(v)
	push_warning("GameState.check: unknown condition %s" % [d])
	return true


func apply(effects: Variant) -> void:
	if not effects is Array:
		return
	for e: Variant in effects:
		if not e is Dictionary:
			continue
		var d: Dictionary = e
		if d.has("set"):
			var s: Dictionary = d["set"]
			for k: Variant in s:
				set_flag(str(k), s[k])
		if d.has("clue"):
			Journal.add_clue(str(d["clue"]))
		if d.has("quest"):
			Journal.start_quest(str(d["quest"]))
		if d.has("reveal"):
			Journal.reveal_objective(str(d["reveal"]))
		if d.has("objective"):
			Journal.complete_objective(str(d["objective"]))
		if d.has("complete"):
			Journal.complete_quest(str(d["complete"]))
		if d.has("notify"):
			notify(str(d["notify"]))


func to_dict() -> Dictionary:
	return flags.duplicate(true)


func from_dict(d: Dictionary) -> void:
	flags = d.duplicate(true)


static func _is_num(v: Variant) -> bool:
	return typeof(v) == TYPE_INT or typeof(v) == TYPE_FLOAT


static func _same(a: Variant, b: Variant) -> bool:
	if _is_num(a) and _is_num(b):
		return is_equal_approx(float(a), float(b))
	return typeof(a) == typeof(b) and a == b


static func _truthy(v: Variant) -> bool:
	match typeof(v):
		TYPE_NIL:
			return false
		TYPE_BOOL:
			return v
		TYPE_INT, TYPE_FLOAT:
			return v != 0
		TYPE_STRING:
			return v != ""
	return true
