== core/input_setup.gd
==============================================================================
class_name InputSetup
## Registers gameplay input actions in code so the map lives in one reviewable place.
## Touch input is handled separately by TouchControls.

static func ensure() -> void:
	_add(&"move_left", [KEY_A, KEY_LEFT], JOY_AXIS_LEFT_X, -1.0)
	_add(&"move_right", [KEY_D, KEY_RIGHT], JOY_AXIS_LEFT_X, 1.0)
	_add(&"move_up", [KEY_W, KEY_UP], JOY_AXIS_LEFT_Y, -1.0)
	_add(&"move_down", [KEY_S, KEY_DOWN], JOY_AXIS_LEFT_Y, 1.0)
	_add(&"sprint", [KEY_SHIFT])
	_add_button(&"sprint", JOY_BUTTON_B)
	_add_button(&"sprint", JOY_BUTTON_X)
	_add(&"debug_hour", [KEY_BRACKETRIGHT])
	_add(&"debug_day", [KEY_BRACKETLEFT])
	_add(&"debug_weather", [KEY_BACKSLASH])
	_add(&"interact", [KEY_E, KEY_SPACE, KEY_ENTER])
	_add(&"journal", [KEY_J, KEY_TAB])
	_add(&"menu", [KEY_ESCAPE])
	_add_button(&"interact", JOY_BUTTON_A)
	_add_button(&"journal", JOY_BUTTON_Y)
	_add_button(&"menu", JOY_BUTTON_START)
	# menus: make sure a gamepad can confirm and cancel in the built-in ui actions
	_ensure_button(&"ui_accept", JOY_BUTTON_A)
	_ensure_button(&"ui_cancel", JOY_BUTTON_B)


static func _add(action: StringName, keys: Array, axis: int = -1, axis_value: float = 0.0) -> void:
	if InputMap.has_action(action):
		return
	InputMap.add_action(action, 0.25)
	for k: int in keys:
		var ev := InputEventKey.new()
		ev.physical_keycode = k as Key
		InputMap.action_add_event(action, ev)
	if axis >= 0:
		var jm := InputEventJoypadMotion.new()
		jm.axis = axis as JoyAxis
		jm.axis_value = axis_value
		InputMap.action_add_event(action, jm)


static func _add_button(action: StringName, button: JoyButton) -> void:
	var jb := InputEventJoypadButton.new()
	jb.button_index = button
	InputMap.action_add_event(action, jb)


static func _ensure_button(action: StringName, button: JoyButton) -> void:
	for e in InputMap.action_get_events(action):
		var jb := e as InputEventJoypadButton
		if jb != null and jb.button_index == button:
			return
	_add_button(action, button)


==============================================================================
== core/journal.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Investigations (quests with objectives) and clues. Definitions are data
## (res://data/journal); this node only holds the player's progress.

signal updated(kind: String, text: String)

const HIDDEN := "hidden"
const ACTIVE := "active"
const DONE := "done"

var quest_defs: Dictionary = {}
var clue_defs: Dictionary = {}
## quest id -> {"objectives": {objective id: state}, "complete": bool}
var quests: Dictionary = {}
var clues: Array[String] = []


func _ready() -> void:
	quest_defs = Data.load_dict("res://data/journal/quests.json")
	clue_defs = Data.load_dict("res://data/journal/clues.json")


func reset() -> void:
	quests = {}
	clues.clear()


func start_quest(id: String) -> void:
	if quests.has(id) or not quest_defs.has(id):
		return
	var states := {}
	for o: Dictionary in quest_defs[id].get("objectives", []):
		states[str(o["id"])] = HIDDEN if o.get("hidden", false) else ACTIVE
	quests[id] = {"objectives": states, "complete": false}
	updated.emit("quest", quest_title(id))


func reveal_objective(path: String) -> void:
	var p := _split(path)
	if p.is_empty() or _state(p[0], p[1]) != HIDDEN:
		return
	quests[p[0]]["objectives"][p[1]] = ACTIVE
	updated.emit("objective_new", objective_text(p[0], p[1]))


func complete_objective(path: String) -> void:
	var p := _split(path)
	if p.is_empty() or _state(p[0], p[1]) in ["", DONE]:
		return
	quests[p[0]]["objectives"][p[1]] = DONE
	updated.emit("objective", objective_text(p[0], p[1]))


func complete_quest(id: String) -> void:
	if not quests.has(id) or quests[id]["complete"]:
		return
	quests[id]["complete"] = true
	updated.emit("complete", quest_title(id))


func add_clue(id: String) -> void:
	if clues.has(id) or not clue_defs.has(id):
		return
	clues.append(id)
	updated.emit("clue", str(clue_defs[id].get("title", id)))


func has_clue(id: String) -> bool:
	return clues.has(id)


func objective_state(quest: String, objective: String) -> String:
	return _state(quest, objective)


func is_complete(quest: String) -> bool:
	return quests.has(quest) and quests[quest]["complete"]


func quest_title(id: String) -> String:
	return str(quest_defs.get(id, {}).get("title", id))


func objective_text(quest: String, objective: String) -> String:
	for o: Dictionary in quest_defs.get(quest, {}).get("objectives", []):
		if str(o["id"]) == objective:
			return str(o.get("text", objective))
	return objective


func to_dict() -> Dictionary:
	return {"quests": quests.duplicate(true), "clues": clues.duplicate()}


func from_dict(d: Dictionary) -> void:
	quests = (d.get("quests", {}) as Dictionary).duplicate(true)
	clues.clear()
	for c: Variant in d.get("clues", []):
		clues.append(str(c))


func _split(path: String) -> PackedStringArray:
	var p := path.split(".")
	if p.size() != 2 or not quests.has(p[0]):
		return PackedStringArray()
	return p


func _state(quest: String, objective: String) -> String:
	if not quests.has(quest):
		return ""
	return str(quests[quest]["objectives"].get(objective, ""))


==============================================================================
== core/save_manager.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Save anywhere. One JSON file per slot under user://. "auto" is written whenever
## the app is backgrounded or closed and is resumed on boot, so a five-minute
## session never loses anything.
##
## The active world (game/main.gd) registers itself and provides
## capture_world() -> Dictionary and restore_world(Dictionary).

signal saved(slot: String)
signal loaded(slot: String)

const VERSION := 1

var world: Node = null
var autosave_enabled := true
var boot_from_autosave := true
## Folder the slots live in. Tests point this at a scratch folder.
var dir := "user://"


func slot_path(slot: String) -> String:
	return dir.path_join("save_%s.json" % slot)


func has_save(slot: String) -> bool:
	return FileAccess.file_exists(slot_path(slot))


func save_game(slot: String = "manual") -> bool:
	if world == null or not world.is_inside_tree():
		return false
	var data := {
		"version": VERSION,
		"time": Time.get_datetime_string_from_system(),
		"flags": GameState.to_dict(),
		"journal": Journal.to_dict(),
		"world_time": WorldTime.to_dict(),
		"weather": Weather.to_dict(),
		"world": world.call("capture_world"),
	}
	var path := slot_path(slot)
	var tmp := path + ".tmp"
	var f := FileAccess.open(tmp, FileAccess.WRITE)
	if f == null:
		push_error("SaveManager: cannot write %s" % tmp)
		return false
	f.store_string(JSON.stringify(data, "\t"))
	f.close()
	if FileAccess.file_exists(path):
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(path)
	if DirAccess.rename_absolute(tmp, path) != OK:
		push_error("SaveManager: cannot finalise %s" % path)
		return false
	saved.emit(slot)
	return true


func load_game(slot: String = "manual") -> bool:
	if world == null or not has_save(slot):
		return false
	var data: Dictionary = Data.load_dict(slot_path(slot))
	if data.is_empty() or int(data.get("version", 0)) != VERSION:
		push_warning("SaveManager: save '%s' is unreadable or from another version" % slot)
		return false
	GameState.from_dict(data.get("flags", {}))
	Journal.from_dict(data.get("journal", {}))
	# clock first: the weather schedule is read against it, and the map built by
	# restore_world() lights itself from both straight away
	WorldTime.from_dict(data.get("world_time", {}))
	Weather.from_dict(data.get("weather", {}))
	world.call("restore_world", data.get("world", {}))
	loaded.emit(slot)
	return true


## Slots that hold a readable save from this version, newest first:
## [{slot, saved, map, when}] where `when` is the in-game date and time.
func list_saves() -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var out: Array[Dictionary] = []
	var d := DirAccess.open(dir)
	if d == null:
		return out
	for f in d.get_files():
		if f.begins_with("save_") and f.ends_with(".json"):
			var info := slot_info(f.trim_prefix("save_").trim_suffix(".json"))
			if not info.is_empty():
				out.append(info)
	out.sort_custom(func(a: Dictionary, b: Dictionary) -> bool: return str(a["saved"]) > str(b["saved"]))
	return out


## Summary of one slot, or {} if it is missing, unreadable or from another version.
func slot_info(slot: String) -> Dictionary:
	if not has_save(slot):
		return {}
	# parsed quietly: a damaged file is simply not offered
	var json := JSON.new()
	if json.parse(FileAccess.get_file_as_string(slot_path(slot))) != OK or not json.data is Dictionary:
		return {}
	var data: Dictionary = json.data
	if int(data.get("version", 0)) != VERSION or not data.get("world") is Dictionary:
		return {}
	return {
		"slot": slot,
		"saved": str(data.get("time", "")),
		"map": str((data["world"] as Dictionary).get("map", "")),
		"when": WorldTime.describe(data.get("world_time", {})),
	}


func delete_save(slot: String) -> void:
	if has_save(slot):
		DirAccess.remove_absolute(slot_path(slot))


func _notification(what: int) -> void:
	if what == NOTIFICATION_APPLICATION_PAUSED or what == NOTIFICATION_WM_CLOSE_REQUEST \
			or what == NOTIFICATION_WM_GO_BACK_REQUEST:
		if autosave_enabled and world != null and world.is_inside_tree():
			save_game("auto")


==============================================================================
== core/settings.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Autoload "Settings": player preferences that are not part of a save game. Stored in
## user://settings.cfg and applied at boot. Volumes are 0..1 and drive the audio buses
## Master, Music and SFX (the last two are created here, so no bus layout file is needed).

signal changed

const PATH := "user://settings.cfg"
const BUSES := {"master": "Master", "music": "Music", "sfx": "SFX"}

var volume := {"master": 0.8, "music": 0.7, "sfx": 0.8}
var fullscreen := false
var vsync := true
## Tests point this elsewhere so they never touch the player's file.
var path := PATH


func _ready() -> void:
	for b: String in ["Music", "SFX"]:
		if AudioServer.get_bus_index(b) < 0:
			AudioServer.add_bus()
			var i := AudioServer.bus_count - 1
			AudioServer.set_bus_name(i, b)
			AudioServer.set_bus_send(i, "Master")
	load_settings()
	apply()


func set_volume(key: String, v: float) -> void:
	volume[key] = clampf(v, 0.0, 1.0)
	apply()
	changed.emit()


func set_fullscreen(on: bool) -> void:
	fullscreen = on
	apply()
	changed.emit()


func set_vsync(on: bool) -> void:
	vsync = on
	apply()
	changed.emit()


## Fullscreen and vsync are desktop preferences; phones and browsers manage their own.
func has_display_options() -> bool:
	return OS.has_feature("pc")


func apply() -> void:
	for k: String in BUSES:
		var i := AudioServer.get_bus_index(BUSES[k])
		if i >= 0:
			var v: float = volume[k]
			AudioServer.set_bus_mute(i, v <= 0.001)
			AudioServer.set_bus_volume_db(i, linear_to_db(maxf(v, 0.001)))
	if has_display_options() and DisplayServer.get_name() != "headless":
		var mode := DisplayServer.WINDOW_MODE_FULLSCREEN if fullscreen else DisplayServer.WINDOW_MODE_WINDOWED
		if DisplayServer.window_get_mode() != mode:
			DisplayServer.window_set_mode(mode)
		DisplayServer.window_set_vsync_mode(DisplayServer.VSYNC_ENABLED if vsync else DisplayServer.VSYNC_DISABLED)


func save_settings() -> void:
	var c := ConfigFile.new()
	for k: String in volume:
		c.set_value("audio", k, volume[k])
	c.set_value("display", "fullscreen", fullscreen)
	c.set_value("display", "vsync", vsync)
	c.save(path)


func load_settings() -> void:
	var c := ConfigFile.new()
	if c.load(path) != OK:
		return
	for k: String in volume:
		volume[k] = clampf(float(c.get_value("audio", k, volume[k])), 0.0, 1.0)
	fullscreen = bool(c.get_value("display", "fullscreen", fullscreen))
	vsync = bool(c.get_value("display", "vsync", vsync))


==============================================================================
== core/weather_manager.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Autoload "Weather": a small weather state machine driven by WorldTime.
##
## Weather is planned ahead as a schedule of spells, each lasting min_duration to
## max_duration game minutes and chosen by weighted chance for the season it falls in.
## The schedule always covers the next day, which is the forecast. Nothing is rolled
## per frame: the schedule is only consulted when the clock's minute changes.
##
##   Weather.current                      # "clear" "cloudy" "rain" "heavy_rain" "fog" "snow"
##   Weather.weather_changed.connect(...) # encounters, dialogue, audio
##   Weather.forecast(24)                 # [{type, from, until}] in absolute minutes
##   Weather.set_weather("fog", 120)      # manual override for 120 minutes (-1 = until cleared)
##   Weather.clear_override()
##   Weather.is_wet()
## Debug builds: \ cycles through the weather types as an override.

signal weather_changed(type: String, previous: String)
## Fired at random intervals during heavy rain: hook thunder audio and flashes here.
signal lightning

const TYPES: PackedStringArray = ["clear", "cloudy", "rain", "heavy_rain", "fog", "snow"]

## What each weather does to the world, 0..1. EnvironmentController blends toward these.
## rain/snow: precipitation amount; cloud: how much sun is lost; fog: mist thickness.
var profiles := {
	"clear": {"rain": 0.0, "snow": 0.0, "cloud": 0.0, "fog": 0.0},
	"cloudy": {"rain": 0.0, "snow": 0.0, "cloud": 0.6, "fog": 0.05},
	"rain": {"rain": 0.5, "snow": 0.0, "cloud": 0.75, "fog": 0.15},
	"heavy_rain": {"rain": 1.0, "snow": 0.0, "cloud": 1.0, "fog": 0.3},
	"fog": {"rain": 0.0, "snow": 0.0, "cloud": 0.5, "fog": 1.0},
	"snow": {"rain": 0.0, "snow": 1.0, "cloud": 0.6, "fog": 0.2},
}
## Relative chances per season index (0 Spring .. 3 Winter).
var season_weights := {
	0: {"clear": 35, "cloudy": 20, "rain": 30, "heavy_rain": 8, "fog": 7, "snow": 0},
	1: {"clear": 60, "cloudy": 18, "rain": 8, "heavy_rain": 12, "fog": 2, "snow": 0},
	2: {"clear": 15, "cloudy": 30, "rain": 28, "heavy_rain": 7, "fog": 20, "snow": 0},
	3: {"clear": 14, "cloudy": 30, "rain": 3, "heavy_rain": 0, "fog": 8, "snow": 45},
}
## Length of one spell of weather, in game minutes.
var min_duration := 180
var max_duration := 540
## Weather a new game opens with.
var start_weather := "rain"

var current := "rain"
## Upcoming spells, soonest first: {type, until}; the first is the one in effect.
var schedule: Array[Dictionary] = []
var override := ""
## Absolute minute the override ends; -1 holds it until clear_override().
var override_until := -1

var _rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
var _bolt := 8.0


func _ready() -> void:
	WorldTime.minute_changed.connect(func(_h: int, _m: int) -> void: update())
	WorldTime.time_jumped.connect(update)
	reset()


## Start a fresh schedule. Pass a seed for a repeatable sequence.
func reset(rng_seed: int = -1) -> void:
	if rng_seed >= 0:
		_rng.seed = rng_seed
	else:
		_rng.randomize()
	override = ""
	override_until = -1
	schedule.clear()
	schedule.append({"type": start_weather, "until": WorldTime.total_minutes() + _rng.randi_range(min_duration, max_duration)})
	_fill()
	_apply(start_weather)


## Bring the weather up to date with the clock. Called on every game minute.
func update() -> void:
	var now := WorldTime.total_minutes()
	while not schedule.is_empty() and int(schedule[0]["until"]) <= now:
		schedule.pop_front()
	if schedule.is_empty():
		schedule.append({"type": _roll(now, current), "until": now + _rng.randi_range(min_duration, max_duration)})
	_fill()
	if override != "" and override_until >= 0 and now >= override_until:
		override = ""
		override_until = -1
	_apply(override if override != "" else str(schedule[0]["type"]))


## Force a weather type, for `minutes` game minutes or until cleared.
func set_weather(type: String, minutes: int = -1) -> void:
	if not profiles.has(type):
		push_warning("Weather: unknown type '%s'" % type)
		return
	override = type
	override_until = WorldTime.total_minutes() + minutes if minutes >= 0 else -1
	_apply(type)


func clear_override() -> void:
	override = ""
	override_until = -1
	update()


## The planned weather for the next `hours`: [{type, from, until}] in absolute minutes.
func forecast(hours: int = 24) -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var out: Array[Dictionary] = []
	var from := WorldTime.total_minutes()
	var end := from + hours * 60
	for s in schedule:
		if from >= end:
			break
		out.append({"type": s["type"], "from": from, "until": mini(int(s["until"]), end)})
		from = int(s["until"])
	return out


func profile(type: String = "") -> Dictionary:
	return profiles.get(type if type != "" else current, profiles["clear"])


func is_wet() -> bool:
	return float(profile()["rain"]) > 0.0


func label(type: String = "") -> String:
	return (type if type != "" else current).replace("_", " ").capitalize()


func _apply(type: String) -> void:
	if type == current:
		return
	var prev := current
	current = type
	weather_changed.emit(current, prev)


func _fill() -> void:
	var horizon := WorldTime.total_minutes() + WorldTime.DAY_MINUTES
	while int(schedule[schedule.size() - 1]["until"]) < horizon:
		var last: Dictionary = schedule[schedule.size() - 1]
		var at := int(last["until"])
		schedule.append({"type": _roll(at, str(last["type"])), "until": at + _rng.randi_range(min_duration, max_duration)})


## Weighted pick for the season at absolute minute `at`; avoids repeating `previous`.
func _roll(at: int, previous: String) -> String:
	var season := WorldTime.calendar.season_of_day(at / WorldTime.DAY_MINUTES)
	var weights: Dictionary = season_weights.get(season, season_weights[0])
	var total := 0.0
	for t: String in weights:
		if t != previous:
			total += float(weights[t])
	if total <= 0.0:
		return previous
	var r := _rng.randf() * total
	for t: String in weights:
		if t == previous:
			continue
		r -= float(weights[t])
		if r <= 0.0:
			return t
	return previous


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if current != "heavy_rain" or WorldTime.is_paused():
		return
	_bolt -= delta
	if _bolt <= 0.0:
		_bolt = randf_range(7.0, 20.0)
		lightning.emit()


# --- persistence ------------------------------------------------------------------

func to_dict() -> Dictionary:
	return {
		"current": current,
		"schedule": schedule.duplicate(true),
		"override": override,
		"override_until": override_until,
		# 64-bit values do not survive JSON numbers
		"rng_seed": str(_rng.seed),
		"rng_state": str(_rng.state),
	}


func from_dict(d: Dictionary) -> void:
	if d.is_empty():
		reset()
		return
	schedule.clear()
	for s: Dictionary in d.get("schedule", []):
		schedule.append({"type": str(s["type"]), "until": int(s["until"])})
	override = str(d.get("override", ""))
	override_until = int(d.get("override_until", -1))
	if d.has("rng_seed"):
		_rng.seed = str(d["rng_seed"]).to_int()
		_rng.state = str(d["rng_state"]).to_int()
	if schedule.is_empty():
		schedule.append({"type": str(d.get("current", start_weather)), "until": WorldTime.total_minutes() + min_duration})
	current = str(d.get("current", start_weather))
	update()


func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if OS.is_debug_build() and event.is_action_pressed(&"debug_weather"):
		set_weather(TYPES[(TYPES.find(current) + 1) % TYPES.size()])


==============================================================================
== core/world_calendar.gd
==============================================================================
class_name WorldCalendar
extends RefCounted
## The in-game date: seven-day week, four seasons of 28 days, years. Pure data with
## no clock of its own; WorldTime (core/world_time_manager.gd) owns the one instance
## and advances it at midnight. Everything here is configurable before a game starts.
##
## Scheduled events are a hook for holidays, festivals and quests:
##   WorldTime.calendar.add_event("harvest_fair", 2, 20)        # every Autumn 20
##   WorldTime.calendar.events_today()                          # -> Array[Dictionary]

var season_names := PackedStringArray(["Spring", "Summer", "Autumn", "Winter"])
var weekday_names := PackedStringArray(["Monday", "Tuesday", "Wednesday", "Thursday", "Friday", "Saturday", "Sunday"])
var days_per_season := 28
## Shifts which weekday day 1 of year 1 falls on.
var weekday_offset := 0

var year := 1
## 0-based index into season_names.
var season := 0
## 1-based day of the season.
var day := 1

## {id, season, day, year (-1 = every year), data}
var events: Array[Dictionary] = []


func set_date(y: int, s: int, d: int) -> void:
	year = maxi(y, 1)
	season = posmod(s, season_names.size())
	day = clampi(d, 1, days_per_season)


## Moves to the next day. Returns which larger units rolled over.
func advance_day() -> Dictionary:
	var rolled := {"season": false, "year": false}
	day += 1
	if day > days_per_season:
		day = 1
		season += 1
		rolled["season"] = true
		if season >= season_names.size():
			season = 0
			year += 1
			rolled["year"] = true
	return rolled


func days_per_year() -> int:
	return days_per_season * season_names.size()


## Days since day 1 of year 1.
func total_days() -> int:
	return ((year - 1) * season_names.size() + season) * days_per_season + day - 1


## Season index for an absolute day count (used to plan weather ahead).
func season_of_day(total: int) -> int:
	return posmod(total / days_per_season, season_names.size())


func weekday() -> int:
	return posmod(total_days() + weekday_offset, weekday_names.size())


func weekday_name() -> String:
	return weekday_names[weekday()]


func season_name() -> String:
	return season_names[season]


## "Autumn 12 · Thursday"
func date_text() -> String:
	return "%s %d · %s" % [season_name(), day, weekday_name()]


func add_event(id: String, on_season: int, on_day: int, on_year: int = -1, data: Dictionary = {}) -> void:
	events.append({"id": id, "season": on_season, "day": on_day, "year": on_year, "data": data})


func events_on(on_season: int, on_day: int, on_year: int) -> Array[Dictionary]:
	var out: Array[Dictionary] = []
	for e in events:
		if int(e["season"]) == on_season and int(e["day"]) == on_day and (int(e["year"]) < 0 or int(e["year"]) == on_year):
			out.append(e)
	return out


func events_today() -> Array[Dictionary]:
	return events_on(season, day, year)


func to_dict() -> Dictionary:
	return {"year": year, "season": season, "day": day}


func from_dict(d: Dictionary) -> void:
	set_date(int(d.get("year", 1)), int(d.get("season", 0)), int(d.get("day", 1)))


==============================================================================
== core/world_time_manager.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Autoload "WorldTime": the one authoritative world clock and calendar.
##
## A 24-hour clock that runs while the game runs (1 real second = 1 game minute by
## default), stops with the scene tree (menus, journal), during dialogue, and for any
## reason a system registers with pause(). Midnight advances the WorldCalendar.
##
## Other systems listen to signals or ask; nothing else keeps its own timer:
##   WorldTime.hour_changed.connect(...)          # shops, NPC routines
##   WorldTime.period_changed.connect(...)        # "dawn" "morning" "midday" ...
##   WorldTime.is_between(9, 17)                  # opening hours (wraps past midnight)
##   WorldTime.sleep_until(6)                     # bed
##   WorldTime.advance_minutes(90)                # waiting
##   WorldTime.pause("cutscene") / resume("cutscene")
##   WorldTime.set_time(12, 0)                    # testing
## Debug builds: ] advances an hour, [ advances a day.

signal minute_changed(hour: int, minute: int)
signal hour_changed(hour: int)
signal day_changed(calendar: WorldCalendar)
signal season_changed(season: int)
signal year_changed(year: int)
signal period_changed(period: String, previous: String)
## The clock was set, loaded or skipped ahead: listeners should snap to the new state
## instead of blending toward it.
signal time_jumped
## A calendar event (WorldCalendar.add_event) falls on the day that just began.
signal event_due(event: Dictionary)

const DAY_MINUTES := 1440

## Game minutes per real second. 0 stops the clock.
var minutes_per_second := 1.0
## Day periods, each from its start (minutes after midnight) to the next one's start.
## The last wraps past midnight to the first.
var periods: Array[Dictionary] = [
	{"name": "dawn", "start": 5 * 60},
	{"name": "morning", "start": 7 * 60},
	{"name": "midday", "start": 11 * 60},
	{"name": "afternoon", "start": 15 * 60},
	{"name": "evening", "start": 18 * 60},
	{"name": "night", "start": 20 * 60},
]
## Where a new game begins. season is a 0-based index (2 = Autumn).
var start := {"year": 1, "season": 2, "day": 12, "hour": 18, "minute": 40}
var pause_in_dialogue := true
## Lets cinematics hide the clock HUD without stopping time.
var hud_hidden := false

var calendar := WorldCalendar.new()
var hour := 0
var minute := 0

var _frac := 0.0
var _pauses := {}
var _period := ""


func _ready() -> void:
	# so that Autumn 12 of year 1 is a Thursday
	calendar.weekday_offset = 6
	reset()


func reset() -> void:
	calendar.set_date(int(start["year"]), int(start["season"]), int(start["day"]))
	hour = int(start["hour"])
	minute = int(start["minute"])
	_frac = 0.0
	_pauses.clear()
	_period = period()
	time_jumped.emit()


# --- running ----------------------------------------------------------------------

func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if is_paused():
		return
	_frac += delta * minutes_per_second
	while _frac >= 1.0:
		_frac -= 1.0
		_tick()


## Stop the clock for a named reason; it runs again when every reason is resumed.
func pause(reason: String) -> void:
	_pauses[reason] = true


func resume(reason: String) -> void:
	_pauses.erase(reason)


func is_paused() -> bool:
	return minutes_per_second <= 0.0 or not _pauses.is_empty() or (pause_in_dialogue and Dialogue.active)


func _tick() -> void:
	minute += 1
	if minute >= 60:
		minute = 0
		hour += 1
		if hour >= 24:
			hour = 0
			_next_day()
		hour_changed.emit(hour)
	minute_changed.emit(hour, minute)
	_check_period()


func _next_day() -> void:
	var rolled := calendar.advance_day()
	day_changed.emit(calendar)
	if rolled["season"]:
		season_changed.emit(calendar.season)
	if rolled["year"]:
		year_changed.emit(calendar.year)
	for e in calendar.events_today():
		event_due.emit(e)


func _check_period() -> void:
	var p := period()
	if p != _period:
		var prev := _period
		_period = p
		period_changed.emit(p, prev)


# --- moving the clock -------------------------------------------------------------

## Advance by a number of game minutes. Short waits tick minute by minute so every
## signal fires; long ones jump (one signal per day crossed, then time_jumped).
func advance_minutes(n: int) -> void:
	if n <= 0:
		return
	if n <= 180:
		for i in n:
			_tick()
		return
	var total := minute_of_day() + n
	for i in total / DAY_MINUTES:
		_next_day()
	var rem := total % DAY_MINUTES
	hour = rem / 60
	minute = rem % 60
	_frac = 0.0
	hour_changed.emit(hour)
	minute_changed.emit(hour, minute)
	_check_period()
	time_jumped.emit()


## Minutes from now until the next time the clock reads h:m.
func minutes_until(h: int, m: int = 0) -> int:
	var d := posmod(h * 60 + m - minute_of_day(), DAY_MINUTES)
	return d if d > 0 else DAY_MINUTES


## Skip ahead to the next h:m (sleeping, resting until morning).
func sleep_until(h: int, m: int = 0) -> void:
	var n := minutes_until(h, m)
	advance_minutes(n)
	if n <= 180:
		time_jumped.emit()


## Set the clock directly without advancing the calendar. For testing and scripted scenes.
func set_time(h: int, m: int = 0) -> void:
	hour = posmod(h, 24)
	minute = clampi(m, 0, 59)
	_frac = 0.0
	hour_changed.emit(hour)
	minute_changed.emit(hour, minute)
	_check_period()
	time_jumped.emit()


func set_date(year: int, season: int, day: int) -> void:
	calendar.set_date(year, season, day)
	day_changed.emit(calendar)
	time_jumped.emit()


# --- asking -----------------------------------------------------------------------

func minute_of_day() -> int:
	return hour * 60 + minute


## Minutes since midnight of day 1, year 1. Use for durations and schedules.
func total_minutes() -> int:
	return calendar.total_days() * DAY_MINUTES + minute_of_day()


## 0..1 through the day, continuous between minutes (drives the lighting).
func day_fraction() -> float:
	return (float(minute_of_day()) + _frac) / float(DAY_MINUTES)


func period() -> String:
	var now := minute_of_day()
	var name := str(periods[periods.size() - 1]["name"])
	for p in periods:
		if now >= int(p["start"]):
			name = str(p["name"])
	return name


func is_night() -> bool:
	return period() == "night"


## True from hour a up to hour b; wraps past midnight (is_between(22, 6)).
func is_between(a: float, b: float) -> bool:
	var h := float(minute_of_day()) / 60.0
	return (h >= a and h < b) if a <= b else (h >= a or h < b)


## "6:42 PM"
func time_text() -> String:
	var h := hour % 12
	return "%d:%02d %s" % [12 if h == 0 else h, minute, "AM" if hour < 12 else "PM"]


func date_text() -> String:
	return calendar.date_text()


## "Autumn 12 · 6:40 PM" for a saved clock (to_dict()); "" if empty.
func describe(d: Dictionary) -> String:
	if d.is_empty():
		return ""
	var h := int(d.get("hour", 0))
	var h12 := h % 12
	var season := calendar.season_names[posmod(int(d.get("season", 0)), calendar.season_names.size())]
	return "%s %d · %d:%02d %s" % [season, int(d.get("day", 1)), 12 if h12 == 0 else h12, int(d.get("minute", 0)), "AM" if h < 12 else "PM"]


# --- persistence ------------------------------------------------------------------

func to_dict() -> Dictionary:
	var d := calendar.to_dict()
	d["hour"] = hour
	d["minute"] = minute
	d["frac"] = _frac
	d["rate"] = minutes_per_second
	return d


func from_dict(d: Dictionary) -> void:
	if d.is_empty():
		reset()
		return
	calendar.from_dict(d)
	hour = clampi(int(d.get("hour", 0)), 0, 23)
	minute = clampi(int(d.get("minute", 0)), 0, 59)
	_frac = clampf(float(d.get("frac", 0.0)), 0.0, 0.999)
	minutes_per_second = float(d.get("rate", 1.0))
	_pauses.clear()
	_period = period()
	time_jumped.emit()


func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if not OS.is_debug_build():
		return
	if event.is_action_pressed(&"debug_hour"):
		advance_minutes(60)
		time_jumped.emit()
	elif event.is_action_pressed(&"debug_day"):
		advance_minutes(DAY_MINUTES)

==============================================================================
== game/app.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## The application's root scene (project main scene). Shows the title screen, then runs
## the game (game/main.tscn) underneath it.
##
##   title -> NEW GAME  -> character creation -> [intro hook] -> game
##   title -> LOAD GAME -> game (no character creation, no intro)
##   title -> CONTINUE  -> the game in progress, or the autosave
##   game  -> pause menu "Title screen" -> title; the game is kept, not restarted
##
## While the title is up the world clock is paused (WorldTime.pause("title")) and the
## game, if one is loaded, is taken out of the scene tree: it keeps every bit of its
## state but does not process or draw. Running game/main.tscn directly (tests, tools)
## still works and skips all of this.

const MAIN := preload("res://game/main.tscn")

## Seconds for the fades between title, creation and game. Tests set 0.
var fade_time := 0.6
## False in tests: no title fade-in.
var animate := true
## Where NEW GAME starts: the 2D vertical slice on this branch.
var start_map := "anjabi_steps"

var main: Node
var title: TitleScreen
var creation: CharacterCreation

var _layer: CanvasLayer
var _root: Control
var _shade: ColorRect
var _busy := false


func _ready() -> void:
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	_layer = CanvasLayer.new()
	_layer.layer = 50
	add_child(_layer)
	_root = Control.new()
	_root.theme = UiTheme.build()
	_root.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	_layer.add_child(_root)
	_root.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	title = TitleScreen.new()
	title.animate = animate
	_root.add_child(title)
	title.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	title.continue_requested.connect(_on_continue)
	title.new_game_requested.connect(_on_new_game)
	title.load_requested.connect(_on_load)
	title.quit_requested.connect(_on_quit)
	title.prototype_requested.connect(_on_prototype)
	var top := CanvasLayer.new()
	top.layer = 100
	add_child(top)
	_shade = ColorRect.new()
	_shade.color = Color(0.0, 0.01, 0.03, 1.0)
	_shade.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	top.add_child(_shade)
	_shade.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	_enter_title()
	if animate:
		_fade(0.0, 2.0)
	else:
		_shade.modulate.a = 0.0


## True while the player is in the game rather than at the title or creating a character.
func in_game() -> bool:
	return main != null and main.is_inside_tree()


func _enter_title() -> void:
	WorldTime.pause("title")
	Audio.play_music("title")
	title.session_live = main != null
	_layer.visible = true
	title.visible = true
	title.show_menu()


# --- title choices ------------------------------------------------------------------

func _on_new_game() -> void:
	if _busy:
		return
	_busy = true
	await _fade(1.0)
	creation = CharacterCreation.new()
	_root.add_child(creation)
	creation.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	creation.done.connect(_on_created)
	creation.cancelled.connect(_on_creation_cancelled)
	title.set_process_unhandled_input(false)
	title.set_covered(true)
	await _fade(0.0)
	_busy = false


func _on_creation_cancelled() -> void:
	if _busy:
		return
	_busy = true
	await _fade(1.0)
	_close_creation()
	title.show_menu()
	await _fade(0.0)
	_busy = false


func _on_created(profile: Dictionary) -> void:
	if _busy:
		return
	_busy = true
	await _fade(1.0)
	_close_creation()
	await _play_intro()
	_start("new", profile)
	await _fade(0.0)
	_busy = false


func _on_load(slot: String) -> void:
	if _busy or SaveManager.slot_info(slot).is_empty():
		return
	_busy = true
	await _fade(1.0)
	_start("load:" + slot, {})
	await _fade(0.0)
	_busy = false


func _on_continue() -> void:
	if _busy:
		return
	_busy = true
	await _fade(1.0)
	if main != null:
		add_child(main)
		move_child(main, 0)
		_enter_game()
	else:
		_start("load:auto", {})
	await _fade(0.0)
	_busy = false


## The Golden Sun-style 2D test area: a fresh game started on the 2D Steps.
func _on_prototype() -> void:
	if _busy:
		return
	_busy = true
	await _fade(1.0)
	_start("proto2d", {})
	await _fade(0.0)
	_busy = false


func _on_quit() -> void:
	# a game set aside behind the title was autosaved on the way here
	get_tree().quit()


# --- game ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

## PLACEHOLDER: the introductory cinematic plays here, before a NEW game only. Loading
## and continuing never reach this. Nothing exists yet, so it returns at once.
func _play_intro() -> void:
	await get_tree().process_frame


func _start(boot: String, profile: Dictionary) -> void:
	if main != null:
		if main.is_inside_tree():
			remove_child(main)
		main.free()
	main = MAIN.instantiate()
	main.set("boot", boot)
	main.set("profile", profile)
	main.set("arrival_shot", animate)
	main.set("start_map", start_map)
	add_child(main)
	move_child(main, 0)
	var ui: GameUi = main.get("ui")
	ui.menu.title_enabled = true
	ui.title_requested.connect(return_to_title)
	_enter_game()


func _enter_game() -> void:
	_layer.visible = false
	WorldTime.resume("title")
	Audio.play_music("game")


## Leave the game for the title without ending it: autosave, then set it aside.
func return_to_title() -> void:
	if _busy or not in_game():
		return
	_busy = true
	(main.get("ui") as GameUi).close_panels()
	await _fade(1.0)
	if SaveManager.autosave_enabled:
		SaveManager.save_game("auto")
	remove_child(main)
	_enter_title()
	await _fade(0.0)
	_busy = false


func _notification(what: int) -> void:
	# a game set aside behind the title is not in the tree, so it must be freed by hand
	if what == NOTIFICATION_PREDELETE and is_instance_valid(main) and not main.is_inside_tree():
		main.free()


func _close_creation() -> void:
	if creation != null:
		creation.queue_free()
		creation = null
	title.set_process_unhandled_input(true)
	title.set_covered(false)


func _fade(to: float, seconds: float = -1.0) -> void:
	var t := fade_time if seconds < 0.0 else seconds
	if t <= 0.0 or not animate:
		_shade.modulate.a = to
		return
	var tw := create_tween()
	tw.tween_property(_shade, "modulate:a", to, t).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_SINE)
	await tw.finished


==============================================================================
== game/app.tscn
==============================================================================
[gd_scene load_steps=2 format=3]

[ext_resource type="Script" path="res://game/app.gd" id="1"]

[node name="App" type="Node"]
script = ExtResource("1")


==============================================================================
== game/environment_controller.gd
==============================================================================
class_name EnvironmentController
extends Node
## Lights a 3D map from WorldTime and Weather. MapBuilder3D._environment() creates one
## per map and hands it the Environment, the sun/moon light and the low warm fill light.
##
## Time of day is read as a fraction (0 = midnight, 0.5 = noon) and sampled from the
## Gradients and Curves below, so the look is data: replace or edit any of them
## (controller.sky, controller.sun_energy, ...) to restyle a map. The sample is taken
## every frame from the continuous clock, so lighting drifts instead of stepping.
## Weather is blended on top over `weather_blend` seconds. snap() applies the current
## state at once (used on load, on entering a map and when the clock jumps).
##
## One shadowed DirectionalLight3D serves as sun by day and moon by night; its energy
## passes through zero at dawn and dusk, where it changes sides unseen.
##
## Indoors: set `indoor = true` (GameMap3D.indoor does it) to ignore sky, sun and
## weather and use `indoor_look` instead.

## Fractions of the day where the light changes sides (sun <-> moon).
const SUNRISE := 0.235
const SUNSET := 0.815

var env: Environment
var sun: DirectionalLight3D
var fill: DirectionalLight3D

var sky := Gradient.new()
var ambient := Gradient.new()
var sun_color := Gradient.new()
var fill_color := Gradient.new()
var fog_color := Gradient.new()
var ambient_energy := Curve.new()
var sun_energy := Curve.new()
var fill_energy := Curve.new()
var exposure := Curve.new()
## 0 at night, 1 in full day: scales how much clouds dim and grey the scene.
var daylight := Curve.new()
## 1 when street lamps are lit.
var lamps := Curve.new()

## Seconds for weather to blend in or out.
var weather_blend := 10.0
var indoor := false
var indoor_look := {
	"ambient": Color(0.56, 0.52, 0.62), "ambient_energy": 0.85, "background": Color(0.02, 0.02, 0.03),
	"fog": 0.0, "exposure": 1.2, "lamps": 1.0,
}

## Smoothed weather: rain, snow, cloud, fog, plus ground wetness and snow cover.
var w := {"rain": 0.0, "snow": 0.0, "cloud": 0.0, "fog": 0.0, "wet": 0.0, "cover": 0.0}
var _flash := 0.0
var _lamp_lights: Array[Light3D] = []
var _lamp_level := -1.0
var _lamp_scan := 0.0
## Seconds between lighting updates (the clock moves one game minute per second).
const APPLY_EVERY := 0.1
var _tick := 0.0
var _precip: Array[Node] = []
var _sky_key := Vector3(-1, 0, 0)


func _init() -> void:
	_defaults()


func setup(e: Environment, sun_light: DirectionalLight3D, fill_light: DirectionalLight3D) -> void:
	env = e
	sun = sun_light
	fill = fill_light


func _ready() -> void:
	add_to_group(&"environment_controller")
	WorldTime.time_jumped.connect(snap)
	Weather.lightning.connect(func() -> void: _flash = 1.0)
	snap.call_deferred()


func _enter_tree() -> void:
	# the game is taken out of the tree behind the title screen; relight on return
	if env != null:
		snap.call_deferred()


## Apply the present time and weather immediately, with no blending.
func snap() -> void:
	var p := Weather.profile()
	for k: String in ["rain", "snow", "cloud", "fog"]:
		w[k] = float(p[k])
	w["wet"] = 1.0 if float(p["rain"]) > 0.0 else 0.0
	w["cover"] = 1.0 if float(p["snow"]) > 0.0 else 0.0
	_flash = 0.0
	_lamp_level = -1.0
	if is_inside_tree():
		for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"precipitation"):
			n.call(&"clear_particles")
	_collect_lamps()
	_apply()


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	var p := Weather.profile()
	var k := 1.0 - exp(-delta * 3.0 / maxf(weather_blend, 0.01))
	for key: String in ["rain", "snow", "cloud", "fog"]:
		w[key] = lerpf(w[key], float(p[key]), k)
	# ground soaks quickly and dries slowly; snow settles and melts slowly
	var raining := float(p["rain"]) > 0.0
	w["wet"] = move_toward(w["wet"], 1.0 if raining else 0.0, delta / (8.0 if raining else 60.0))
	var snowing := float(p["snow"]) > 0.0
	w["cover"] = move_toward(w["cover"], 1.0 if snowing else 0.0, delta / (45.0 if snowing else 60.0))
	_flash = move_toward(_flash, 0.0, delta * 4.0)
	# the light drifts slowly; ten updates a second are plenty unless lightning flashes
	_tick -= delta
	if _tick > 0.0 and _flash <= 0.0:
		return
	_tick = APPLY_EVERY
	_lamp_scan -= delta
	if _lamp_scan <= 0.0:
		_lamp_scan = 2.0
		_collect_lamps()
	_apply()


func _apply() -> void:
	if env == null or not is_inside_tree():
		return
	if indoor:
		_apply_indoor()
		return
	var t := WorldTime.day_fraction()
	var day := daylight.sample(t)
	var cloud: float = w["cloud"]
	# clouds take most of the sun by day, less of the moon by night
	var dim := 1.0 - cloud * lerpf(0.25, 0.65, day)
	var flash := _flash * _flash

	var bg := sky.sample(t)
	var grey := bg.get_luminance() * lerpf(1.0, 0.8, day)
	bg = bg.lerp(Color(grey, grey * 1.02, grey * 1.06), cloud * 0.8)
	env.background_color = bg.lightened(flash * 0.5)
	_update_sky(t, day, cloud)
	var amb := ambient.sample(t).lerp(Color(0.7, 0.8, 1.0), float(w["snow"]) * 0.3)
	env.ambient_light_color = amb
	# the handheld style reads brighter and cleaner by day; nights stay moody
	var hh := day if ArtStyle.handheld else 0.0
	env.ambient_light_energy = ambient_energy.sample(t) * (1.0 - cloud * 0.25 * day) * (1.0 + 0.2 * hh) + flash * 1.2
	env.tonemap_exposure = exposure.sample(t) * (1.0 + 0.12 * hh)
	env.adjustment_saturation = 1.18 - cloud * 0.3 * day + 0.08 * hh
	var fog_grey := fog_color.sample(t).get_luminance() * (1.0 - cloud * 0.35)
	env.fog_light_color = fog_color.sample(t).lerp(Color(fog_grey, fog_grey * 1.03, fog_grey * 1.08), cloud * 0.85)
	env.fog_density = (1.0 - 0.45 * hh) * 0.002 + (1.0 - 0.3 * hh) * float(w["rain"]) * 0.007 * (1.0 - 0.4 * float(w["fog"])) + float(w["fog"]) * 0.0065 + float(w["snow"]) * 0.003

	var is_day := t > SUNRISE and t < SUNSET
	if is_day:
		var u := (t - SUNRISE) / (SUNSET - SUNRISE)
		# rises in the east, crosses the south, sets in the west; never lower than 20 degrees
		sun.rotation_degrees = Vector3(-(20.0 + 46.0 * sin(u * PI)), lerpf(100.0, -100.0, u), 0)
	else:
		sun.rotation_degrees = Vector3(-52, -30, 0)
	sun.light_color = sun_color.sample(t).lerp(Color.WHITE, flash)
	sun.light_energy = sun_energy.sample(t) * dim + flash * 2.5
	# the low warm light comes from wherever the sun is near the horizon
	fill.rotation_degrees = Vector3(-12, 100.0 if t < 0.5 else -100.0, 0)
	fill.light_color = fill_color.sample(t)
	fill.light_energy = fill_energy.sample(t) * dim

	_set_lamps(maxf(lamps.sample(t), cloud * 0.6 * day))
	RenderingServer.global_shader_parameter_set(&"weather_wet", float(w["wet"]))
	RenderingServer.global_shader_parameter_set(&"weather_snow", float(w["cover"]))
	for n in _precip:
		if is_instance_valid(n):
			n.call(&"set_precipitation", float(w["rain"]), float(w["snow"]))


## Sky colours follow the same gradients, but only every few seconds and only when they
## have moved: each change rebuilds the reflection map.
func _update_sky(t: float, day: float, cloud: float) -> void:
	if env.sky == null:
		return
	if env.background_mode != Environment.BG_SKY:
		env.background_mode = Environment.BG_SKY
	var m := env.sky.sky_material as ShaderMaterial
	if m == null:
		return
	var key := Vector3(t, cloud, float(w["fog"]))
	if _sky_key.distance_to(key) < 0.01 and _sky_key.x >= 0.0:
		return
	_sky_key = key
	var hz := sky.sample(t).lerp(fog_color.sample(t), 0.35)
	var grey := hz.get_luminance()
	hz = hz.lerp(Color(grey, grey, grey * 1.05), cloud * 0.6)
	m.set_shader_parameter("horizon", hz)
	m.set_shader_parameter("zenith", sky.sample(t).darkened(0.55).lerp(Color(0.09, 0.07, 0.2), 0.5 * (1.0 - day)))
	m.set_shader_parameter("glow", fill_color.sample(t) * (0.35 + fill_energy.sample(t)))
	m.set_shader_parameter("day", day * (1.0 - cloud * 0.3))


func _apply_indoor() -> void:
	env.background_mode = Environment.BG_COLOR
	env.background_color = indoor_look["background"]
	env.ambient_light_color = indoor_look["ambient"]
	env.ambient_light_energy = indoor_look["ambient_energy"]
	env.tonemap_exposure = indoor_look["exposure"]
	env.adjustment_saturation = 1.18
	env.fog_density = indoor_look["fog"]
	sun.light_energy = 0.0
	fill.light_energy = 0.0
	_set_lamps(float(indoor_look["lamps"]))
	RenderingServer.global_shader_parameter_set(&"weather_wet", 0.0)
	RenderingServer.global_shader_parameter_set(&"weather_snow", 0.0)
	for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"precipitation"):
		n.call(&"set_precipitation", 0.0, 0.0)


## Lamps register themselves in the "lamp_lights" group with their lit energy in meta.
func _collect_lamps() -> void:
	if not is_inside_tree():
		return
	_precip = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"precipitation")
	var found: Array[Light3D] = []
	for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"lamp_lights"):
		found.append(n as Light3D)
	if found.size() != _lamp_lights.size():
		_lamp_level = -1.0
	_lamp_lights = found


func _set_lamps(level: float) -> void:
	if absf(level - _lamp_level) < 0.01:
		return
	_lamp_level = level
	for l in _lamp_lights:
		if is_instance_valid(l):
			l.light_energy = float(l.get_meta(&"lit_energy", 1.0)) * level
			l.visible = level > 0.02


# --- the default look ---------------------------------------------------------------

func _defaults() -> void:
	var n_sky := Color(0.045, 0.06, 0.1)
	_grad(sky, [[0.0, n_sky], [0.2, Color(0.06, 0.08, 0.15)], [0.25, Color(0.5, 0.4, 0.45)], [0.31, Color(0.5, 0.64, 0.8)],
		[0.5, Color(0.44, 0.63, 0.86)], [0.7, Color(0.47, 0.62, 0.8)], [0.775, Color(0.72, 0.5, 0.6)],
		[0.83, Color(0.3, 0.22, 0.42)], [0.875, n_sky], [1.0, n_sky]])
	var n_amb := Color(0.36, 0.42, 0.55)
	_grad(ambient, [[0.0, n_amb], [0.2, Color(0.36, 0.42, 0.58)], [0.25, Color(0.52, 0.54, 0.74)], [0.33, Color(0.74, 0.78, 0.84)],
		[0.5, Color(0.8, 0.83, 0.86)], [0.7, Color(0.82, 0.8, 0.76)], [0.775, Color(0.62, 0.56, 0.78)],
		[0.83, Color(0.48, 0.44, 0.72)], [0.875, n_amb], [1.0, n_amb]])
	var moon := Color(0.52, 0.62, 0.86)
	_grad(sun_color, [[0.0, moon], [SUNRISE - 0.005, moon], [SUNRISE + 0.005, Color(1.0, 0.76, 0.74)], [0.3, Color(1.0, 0.84, 0.72)],
		[0.4, Color(1.0, 0.96, 0.9)], [0.66, Color(1.0, 0.95, 0.86)], [0.76, Color(1.0, 0.8, 0.66)],
		[SUNSET - 0.005, Color(1.0, 0.66, 0.58)], [SUNSET + 0.005, moon], [1.0, moon]])
	var glow := Color(1.0, 0.55, 0.35)
	_grad(fill_color, [[0.0, glow], [0.2, Color(1.0, 0.6, 0.62)], [0.27, Color(1.0, 0.68, 0.6)], [0.5, Color(1.0, 0.9, 0.8)],
		[0.72, Color(1.0, 0.72, 0.55)], [0.8, Color(0.95, 0.55, 0.55)], [1.0, glow]])
	var n_fog := Color(0.2, 0.25, 0.33)
	_grad(fog_color, [[0.0, n_fog], [0.2, n_fog], [0.26, Color(0.56, 0.5, 0.6)], [0.33, Color(0.64, 0.72, 0.8)],
		[0.5, Color(0.66, 0.74, 0.82)], [0.7, Color(0.68, 0.72, 0.76)], [0.775, Color(0.58, 0.46, 0.6)],
		[0.83, Color(0.3, 0.26, 0.42)], [0.875, n_fog], [1.0, n_fog]])
	_curve(ambient_energy, [[0.0, 0.75], [0.2, 0.75], [SUNRISE, 1.3], [0.28, 1.0], [0.5, 1.0], [0.7, 0.95], [0.78, 1.0], [SUNSET, 1.3], [0.85, 0.95], [0.875, 0.75], [1.0, 0.75]])
	_curve(sun_energy, [[0.0, 0.86], [SUNRISE - 0.012, 0.7], [SUNRISE, 0.0], [SUNRISE + 0.012, 0.45], [0.27, 0.7], [0.36, 1.05], [0.5, 1.2], [0.68, 1.1],
		[0.75, 0.85], [0.77, 0.7], [SUNSET - 0.012, 0.45], [SUNSET, 0.0], [SUNSET + 0.012, 0.6], [0.86, 0.8], [1.0, 0.86]])
	_curve(fill_energy, [[0.0, 0.35], [0.17, 0.2], [0.2, 0.0], [0.25, 0.32], [0.31, 0.2], [0.4, 0.0], [0.62, 0.0],
		[0.72, 0.18], [0.785, 0.32], [0.83, 0.3], [0.875, 0.3], [1.0, 0.35]])
	_curve(exposure, [[0.0, 1.45], [0.2, 1.45], [0.27, 1.25], [0.36, 1.05], [0.5, 1.0], [0.7, 1.02], [0.78, 1.25], [0.84, 1.4], [0.875, 1.45], [1.0, 1.45]])
	_curve(daylight, [[0.0, 0.0], [0.2, 0.0], [0.3, 1.0], [0.74, 1.0], [0.85, 0.0], [1.0, 0.0]])
	_curve(lamps, [[0.0, 1.0], [0.225, 1.0], [0.255, 0.0], [0.74, 0.0], [0.79, 1.0], [1.0, 1.0]])


static func _grad(g: Gradient, keys: Array) -> void:
	var offsets := PackedFloat32Array()
	var colors := PackedColorArray()
	for k: Array in keys:
		offsets.append(k[0])
		colors.append(k[1])
	g.offsets = offsets
	g.colors = colors


static func _curve(c: Curve, keys: Array) -> void:
	c.clear_points()
	c.min_value = 0.0
	c.max_value = 4.0
	for k: Array in keys:
		c.add_point(Vector2(k[0], k[1]), 0, 0, Curve.TANGENT_LINEAR, Curve.TANGENT_LINEAR)


==============================================================================
== game/main.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Boots the game, owns the current map and the player, and is the "world" that
## SaveManager captures and restores.

const MAPS := {
	"anjabi_mine": "res://maps/anjabi/mine_3d.tscn",
	"anjabi_town": "res://maps/anjabi/town_3d.tscn",
	# interiors (InteriorMap3D)
	"anjabi_shop": "res://maps/anjabi/interiors/shop.tscn",
	# Golden Sun-style 2D prototype baked from the 3D district (tools/bake_2d.gd)
	"anjabi_steps_2d": "res://maps/anjabi/steps_2d.tscn",
	# the 2D tile-based rebuild (branch 2d): the Steps vertical slice
	"anjabi_steps": "res://world2d/maps/steps.tscn",
	"anjabi_steps_shop": "res://world2d/maps/shop.tscn",
}
const START_MAP := "anjabi_town"
const START_QUEST := "quiet_mine"
const PLAYER_SCENE := preload("res://game/player/player.tscn")

## GameMap (2D) or GameMap3D. The player is Player or Player3D to match.
var map
var player
var map_id := ""
## How to start, set by game/app.gd before this enters the tree:
## "" = resume the autosave or start new (running this scene directly, tests),
## "new" = new game with `profile`, "load:<slot>" = load that save.
var boot := ""
## Play the arrival shot the next time a game starts or resumes in the town (set by
## game/app.gd when leaving the title; tests leave it off).
var arrival_shot := false
## The map a new game starts on (game/app.gd sets the 2D slice; tests that build
## main.tscn directly keep the 3D town).
var start_map := START_MAP
## Tile maps render here at their native pixel size (240x135 at the reference size)
## and are scaled up by a whole number, so pixels stay square and crisp.
var view2d: SubViewport
## Flags a new game starts with (the created character: player_name, player_look).
var profile := {}

@onready var world: Node2D = $World
## 3D maps render here: at the base resolution with sharp upscaling when
## ArtStyle.handheld is on, otherwise straight to the screen.
var view3d: Node
@onready var ui: GameUi = $UI


func _ready() -> void:
	view3d = world
	if ArtStyle.handheld:
		# a 480x270 image needs far less shadow resolution and filtering
		RenderingServer.directional_shadow_atlas_set_size(2048, true)
		RenderingServer.directional_soft_shadow_filter_set_quality(RenderingServer.SHADOW_QUALITY_SOFT_VERY_LOW)
		RenderingServer.positional_soft_shadow_filter_set_quality(RenderingServer.SHADOW_QUALITY_SOFT_VERY_LOW)
		var layer := CanvasLayer.new()
		layer.layer = -1
		add_child(layer)
		var box := SubViewportContainer.new()
		box.stretch = true
		box.texture_filter = CanvasItem.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
		var grade := ShaderMaterial.new()
		grade.shader = preload("res://art/shaders/handheld_grade.gdshader")
		box.material = grade
		box.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
		layer.add_child(box)
		box.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
		var vp := SubViewport.new()
		vp.own_world_3d = true
		vp.audio_listener_enable_3d = true
		box.add_child(vp)
		view3d = vp
	var layer2 := CanvasLayer.new()
	layer2.layer = -1
	add_child(layer2)
	var box2 := SubViewportContainer.new()
	box2.stretch = true
	box2.stretch_shrink = 2
	box2.texture_filter = CanvasItem.TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	box2.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	layer2.add_child(box2)
	box2.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	view2d = SubViewport.new()
	view2d.snap_2d_transforms_to_pixel = true
	view2d.snap_2d_vertices_to_pixel = true
	view2d.canvas_item_default_texture_filter = Viewport.DEFAULT_CANVAS_ITEM_TEXTURE_FILTER_NEAREST
	box2.add_child(view2d)
	SaveManager.world = self
	ui.new_game_requested.connect(new_game)
	ui.touch.tapped.connect(_on_tapped)
	GameState.travel_requested.connect(_on_travel_requested)
	$Weather/Fog.add_to_group(&"fog_overlay")
	if boot == "proto2d":
		new_game("anjabi_steps_2d")
		return
	if boot.begins_with("load:"):
		if SaveManager.load_game(boot.trim_prefix("load:")):
			return
	elif boot == "" and SaveManager.boot_from_autosave and SaveManager.has_save("auto") and SaveManager.load_game("auto"):
		return
	new_game()


func new_game(first_map: String = "") -> void:
	GameState.reset()
	Journal.reset()
	for k: String in profile:
		GameState.set_flag(k, profile[k])
	WorldTime.reset()
	Weather.reset()
	load_map(first_map if first_map != "" else start_map, "default")
	Journal.start_quest(START_QUEST)


func load_map(id: String, spawn: String = "default") -> void:
	_enter_map(id)
	player.global_position = map.spawn_position(spawn)
	if player is GridPlayer:
		player.place(map.tile_of(player.position), map.spawn_facing(spawn))
	if player is Player2D:
		player.global_position = map.nearest_standable(player.global_position)
	if player is Player and player.walk_mask != null:
		player.global_position = player.walk_mask.nearest_walkable(player.global_position)
	player.camera.reset_smoothing()
	_arrive()


func _arrive() -> void:
	if arrival_shot and player is Player3D and map_id == "anjabi_town":
		arrival_shot = false
		(player as Player3D).camera.play_intro()


func capture_world() -> Dictionary:
	if player is Player3D:
		var p: Vector3 = player.global_position
		return {"map": map_id, "dim": 3, "x": p.x, "y": p.y, "z": p.z, "fx": player.facing.x, "fz": player.facing.z}
	return {
		"map": map_id,
		"x": player.global_position.x,
		"y": player.global_position.y,
		"fx": player.facing.x,
		"fy": player.facing.y,
	}


func restore_world(d: Dictionary) -> void:
	var id := str(d.get("map", START_MAP))
	var known := MAPS.has(id)
	if not known:
		# e.g. saves from the retired separate district map: resume at the Steps
		id = START_MAP
	_enter_map(id)
	if player is Player3D:
		if known and int(d.get("dim", 2)) == 3:
			player.global_position = Vector3(float(d.get("x", 0.0)), float(d.get("y", 0.0)), float(d.get("z", 0.0)))
			var f := Vector3(float(d.get("fx", 0.0)), 0.0, float(d.get("fz", 1.0)))
			player.facing = f.normalized() if f.length() > 0.01 else Vector3(0, 0, 1)
			player.face_now()
		else:
			player.global_position = map.spawn_position("default")
	else:
		player.global_position = Vector2(float(d.get("x", 0.0)), float(d.get("y", 0.0)))
		player.facing = Vector2(float(d.get("fx", 0.0)), float(d.get("fy", 1.0)))
	player.camera.reset_smoothing()
	_arrive()


func _enter_map(id: String) -> void:
	Dialogue.cancel()
	get_tree().paused = false
	if map != null:
		map.get_parent().remove_child(map)
		map.queue_free()
	map_id = id
	map = (load(MAPS[id]) as PackedScene).instantiate()
	if map is TileWorld:
		view2d.add_child(map)
	else:
		(view3d if map is GameMap3D else world).add_child(map)
	if map is TileWorld:
		player = GridPlayer.new()
		map.entities.add_child(player)
		player.setup(ui.touch, map)
	elif map is Map2D:
		player = Player2D.new()
		map.entities.add_child(player)
		player.setup(ui.touch, map)
	elif map is GameMap3D:
		player = Player3D.new()
		map.entities.add_child(player)
		player.setup(ui.touch)
	else:
		player = PLAYER_SCENE.instantiate() as Player
		map.entities.add_child(player)
		player.setup(ui.touch, map.bounds)
		player.walk_mask = map.get_node_or_null("WalkMask") as WalkMask
	map.player = player
	ui.bind_player(player)
	# the 2D mist overlay is for 2D maps; 3D maps carry their own fog
	$Weather/Fog.visible = not (map is GameMap3D)
	$Weather/Rain.visible = not (map is GameMap3D)
	(get_node("Weather/Fog") as CanvasItem).z_index = 0
	ui.hud.show_location(map.display_name)


func _on_travel_requested(id: String, spawn: String) -> void:
	if MAPS.has(id):
		load_map.call_deferred(id, spawn)


func _on_tapped() -> void:
	if player != null:
		player.try_interact()

class_name Npc
extends StaticBody2D
## A person who stands somewhere and can be talked to. Routines, schedules and
## relationships are future work; they belong here, not in dialogue data.

@export var display_name := "Someone"
@export var dialogue_id := ""
@export var coat := Color(0.35, 0.4, 0.45)
@export var accent := Color(0.7, 0.45, 0.2)
@export var skin := Color(0.78, 0.6, 0.48)
@export var hood := false
@export var facing := Vector2.DOWN

@onready var sprite: CharSprite = $Sprite
@onready var interactable: Interactable = $Interactable


func _ready() -> void:
	sprite.coat = coat
	sprite.accent = accent
	sprite.skin = skin
	sprite.hood = hood
	sprite.set_motion(facing, false)
	interactable.label = display_name
	interactable.prompt = "Talk"
	interactable.dialogue_id = dialogue_id
	interactable.interacted.connect(_on_interacted)


func _on_interacted(by: Node2D) -> void:
	sprite.set_motion((by.global_position - global_position).normalized(), false)

== game/actors/npc_3d.gd

class_name Npc3D
extends StaticBody3D
## A person standing in a 3D map who can be talked to. Same data as Npc
## (display_name, dialogue_id, colours); turns to face whoever speaks to them.

@export var display_name := "Someone"
@export var dialogue_id := ""
@export var coat := Color(0.35, 0.4, 0.45)
@export var accent := Color(0.7, 0.45, 0.2)
@export var skin := Color(0.78, 0.6, 0.48)
@export var hood := false
## Facing in degrees around Y; 0 faces north, 180 faces south (toward the camera).
@export var yaw_deg := 180.0
## Figure3D look options (hair, hat, long_coat, apron, satchel, hair_color).
@export var look: Dictionary = {}

var figure: Figure3D
var interactable: Interactable3D


func _ready() -> void:
	collision_layer = 1
	collision_mask = 0
	var cs := CollisionShape3D.new()
	var cap := CapsuleShape3D.new()
	cap.radius = 0.3
	cap.height = 1.6
	cs.shape = cap
	cs.position.y = 0.8
	add_child(cs)
	figure = Figure3D.new()
	figure.coat = coat
	figure.accent = accent
	figure.skin = skin
	figure.hood = hood
	figure.configure(look)
	figure.rotation.y = deg_to_rad(yaw_deg)
	add_child(figure)
	interactable = Interactable3D.new()
	interactable.label = display_name
	interactable.prompt = "Talk"
	interactable.dialogue_id = dialogue_id
	interactable.radius = 1.0
	interactable.position.y = 0.8
	add_child(interactable)
	interactable.interacted.connect(_on_interacted)


func _on_interacted(by: Node3D) -> void:
	var d := by.global_position - global_position
	d.y = 0.0
	if d.length() > 0.01:
		create_tween().tween_property(figure, "rotation:y", atan2(-d.x, -d.z), 0.2)


==============================================================================
== game/actors/townsfolk.gd
==============================================================================
class_name Townsfolk
extends Node3D
## Background inhabitant: walks a loop of points, pausing at each, or stands idle if
## given one point. Not interactive and not solid, so it never blocks the player.
## Lives in the map's Entities; built entirely from data set before entering the tree.

var points := PackedVector3Array()
var speed := 1.1
var coat := Color(0.3, 0.32, 0.35)
var accent := Color(0.6, 0.45, 0.25)
var skin := Color(0.7, 0.53, 0.42)
var hood := false
## Facing in degrees when standing still with a single point.
var yaw_deg := 180.0
var look := {}

var _fig: Figure3D
var _i := 0
var _wait := 0.0


func _ready() -> void:
	_fig = Figure3D.new()
	_fig.coat = coat
	_fig.accent = accent
	_fig.skin = skin
	_fig.hood = hood
	_fig.configure(look)
	_fig.rotation.y = deg_to_rad(yaw_deg)
	add_child(_fig)
	if not points.is_empty():
		global_position = points[0]
	_wait = randf_range(0.0, 3.0)


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if points.size() < 2:
		return
	if _wait > 0.0:
		_wait -= delta
		_fig.set_motion(false)
		return
	var target := points[(_i + 1) % points.size()]
	var to := target - global_position
	var flat := Vector3(to.x, 0, to.z)
	if flat.length() < 0.08:
		_i = (_i + 1) % points.size()
		_wait = randf_range(1.5, 5.0)
		return
	var step := minf(speed * delta, flat.length())
	global_position += flat.normalized() * step
	global_position.y = lerpf(global_position.y, target.y, clampf(step / maxf(flat.length(), 0.01), 0.0, 1.0))
	_fig.rotation.y = lerp_angle(_fig.rotation.y, atan2(-flat.x, -flat.z), 1.0 - exp(-10.0 * delta))
	_fig.set_motion(true, speed / 4.2)


==============================================================================
== game/camera/follow_camera.gd
==============================================================================
class_name FollowCamera
extends Camera3D
## Fixed-orientation elevated RPG camera. Never rotates: north is always up the
## screen. Narrow-lens perspective by default (orthographic optional).

@export var pitch_deg := 40.0
@export var distance := 30.0
@export var ortho_size := 14.0
## Perspective with a narrow lens reads like the handheld reference (facades visible,
## gentle depth); set false for the old orthographic view.
@export var perspective := true
@export var fov_deg := 32.0
@export var follow_speed := 6.0
## Aim slightly ahead of the feet so the player sits a little below centre.
@export var look_height := 1.0

## Set by the player: widens the lens a touch and leads the view while running.
var sprint := false
@export var sprint_fov_add := 2.5
@export var sprint_lead := 1.6

var target: Node3D
var _lead := Vector3.ZERO
## Arrival shot (play_intro): 0..1 progress, < 0 when not playing.
var _intro := -1.0
var _intro_len := 6.0
const INTRO_PITCH := 3.0
const INTRO_DISTANCE := 9.0
const INTRO_FOV := 56.0
const INTRO_HEIGHT := 2.4


func _ready() -> void:
	if ArtStyle.handheld:
		pitch_deg = ArtStyle.CAM_PITCH
		distance = ArtStyle.CAM_DISTANCE
		fov_deg = ArtStyle.CAM_FOV
	top_level = true
	if perspective:
		projection = PROJECTION_PERSPECTIVE
		fov = fov_deg
	else:
		projection = PROJECTION_ORTHOGONAL
		size = ortho_size
	near = 0.5
	far = 220.0
	rotation_degrees = Vector3(-pitch_deg, 0, 0)
	current = true
	reset_smoothing()


func _offset() -> Vector3:
	var v := _view()
	var p := deg_to_rad(v.x)
	return Vector3(0, sin(p), cos(p)) * v.y + Vector3(0, v.w, 0)


## Current (pitch, distance, fov, look height), blended during the arrival shot.
func _view() -> Vector4:
	if _intro < 0.0:
		return Vector4(pitch_deg, distance, fov_deg, look_height)
	# hold on the sky, then sweep down to the play view
	var k := smoothstep(0.3, 1.0, _intro)
	return Vector4(lerpf(INTRO_PITCH, pitch_deg, k), lerpf(INTRO_DISTANCE, distance, k), lerpf(INTRO_FOV, fov_deg, k), lerpf(INTRO_HEIGHT, look_height, k))


## The arrival shot: starts low behind the player looking out over the settlement to
## the horizon and the ringed planet, holds, then sweeps down to the play camera.
## Any input hurries it along. Only the arrival shot draws past `far` 220 m.
func play_intro(seconds := 6.0) -> void:
	_intro = 0.0
	_intro_len = seconds
	far = 1600.0
	reset_smoothing()


func is_playing_intro() -> bool:
	return _intro >= 0.0


func reset_smoothing() -> void:
	_lead = Vector3.ZERO
	if target != null and target.is_inside_tree():
		global_position = target.global_position + _offset()


func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	if target == null:
		return
	var ahead := Vector3.ZERO
	if sprint and target is CharacterBody3D:
		var v := (target as CharacterBody3D).velocity
		ahead = Vector3(v.x, 0, v.z).limit_length(1.0) * sprint_lead
	_lead = _lead.lerp(ahead, 1.0 - exp(-3.0 * delta))
	if _intro >= 0.0:
		_intro = minf(_intro + delta / _intro_len, 1.0)
		var v := _view()
		rotation_degrees = Vector3(-v.x, 0, 0)
		fov = v.z
		global_position = target.global_position + _offset()
		if _intro >= 1.0:
			_intro = -1.0
			far = 220.0
		return
	var want := target.global_position + _offset() + _lead
	global_position = global_position.lerp(want, 1.0 - exp(-follow_speed * delta))
	if perspective:
		fov = lerpf(fov, fov_deg + (sprint_fov_add if sprint else 0.0), 1.0 - exp(-4.0 * delta))


func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if _intro >= 0.0 and _intro < 0.3 and event.is_pressed() and not event.is_echo():
		_intro = 0.3

== game/interaction/interactable.gd

class_name Interactable
extends Area2D
## Reusable "the player can do something here" component. Drop it on anything.
## Either set dialogue_id (examine text and conversations are both dialogue data)
## or connect to `interacted` from a parent script.

signal interacted(by: Node2D)

@export var prompt := "Look"
@export var label := ""
@export var dialogue_id := ""
@export var radius := 14.0
## GameState condition; the interactable is ignored while it is false.
@export var enabled_if: Dictionary = {}

var enabled := true


func _ready() -> void:
	add_to_group(&"interactables_2d")
	collision_layer = 4
	collision_mask = 0
	monitoring = false
	if get_child_count() == 0:
		var shape := CircleShape2D.new()
		shape.radius = radius
		var cs := CollisionShape2D.new()
		cs.shape = shape
		add_child(cs)


func is_available() -> bool:
	return enabled and GameState.check(enabled_if)


func interact(by: Node2D) -> void:
	interacted.emit(by)
	if dialogue_id != "":
		Dialogue.start(dialogue_id)

== game/player/player.gd

class_name Player
extends CharacterBody2D

signal target_changed(target: Interactable)

@export var walk_speed := 64.0

var touch: TouchControls
var facing := Vector2.DOWN
var target: Interactable
## Optional painted walkable area (maps without collision bodies).
var walk_mask: WalkMask

@onready var sprite: CharSprite = $Sprite
@onready var reach: Area2D = $Reach
@onready var camera: Camera2D = $Camera


func setup(touch_controls: TouchControls, bounds: Rect2) -> void:
	touch = touch_controls
	camera.limit_left = int(bounds.position.x)
	camera.limit_top = int(bounds.position.y)
	camera.limit_right = int(bounds.end.x)
	camera.limit_bottom = int(bounds.end.y)
	camera.reset_smoothing()


func _physics_process(_delta: float) -> void:
	var dir := Vector2.ZERO
	if not Dialogue.active:
		dir = Input.get_vector(&"move_left", &"move_right", &"move_up", &"move_down")
		if dir == Vector2.ZERO and touch != null:
			dir = touch.vector
	var moving := dir.length() > 0.1
	velocity = _allowed(dir.limit_length(1.0) * walk_speed, _delta) if moving else Vector2.ZERO
	move_and_slide()
	if moving:
		facing = dir.normalized()
	sprite.set_motion(facing, moving)
	_update_target()


func _allowed(v: Vector2, delta: float) -> Vector2:
	if walk_mask == null or not _clear(global_position):
		return v
	if _clear(global_position + v * delta):
		return v
	var vx := Vector2(v.x, 0.0)
	if v.x != 0.0 and _clear(global_position + vx * delta):
		return vx
	var vy := Vector2(0.0, v.y)
	if v.y != 0.0 and _clear(global_position + vy * delta):
		return vy
	return Vector2.ZERO


func _clear(p: Vector2) -> bool:
	return walk_mask.is_walkable(p) and walk_mask.is_walkable(p + Vector2(3, 0)) \
			and walk_mask.is_walkable(p + Vector2(-3, 0)) and walk_mask.is_walkable(p + Vector2(0, -3))


func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event.is_action_pressed(&"interact") and try_interact():
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()


func try_interact() -> bool:
	if Dialogue.active or target == null or not target.is_available():
		return false
	target.interact(self)
	return true


func _update_target() -> void:
	var best: Interactable = null
	var best_d := INF
	for a in reach.get_overlapping_areas():
		var it := a as Interactable
		if it == null or not it.is_available():
			continue
		var d := global_position.distance_squared_to(it.global_position)
		if d < best_d:
			best_d = d
			best = it
	if best != target:
		target = best
		target_changed.emit(target)
