== game/player/player_3d.gd
==============================================================================
class_name Player3D
extends CharacterBody3D
## The player in 3D maps. Same outward interface as Player (target, facing,
## try_interact, setup, camera.reset_smoothing) so main, HUD and tests can drive
## either. Screen-up is north (-Z); the camera never rotates.

signal target_changed(target: Interactable3D)

@export var walk_speed := 4.2
## Held sprint (Shift, pad B/X, or the HUD's RUN toggle on touch).
@export var run_speed := 7.4
@export var accel := 28.0
@export var decel := 36.0
@export var gravity := 22.0

var touch: TouchControls
var facing := Vector3(0, 0, 1)
var target: Interactable3D
## Not used in 3D; kept so code written for 2D maps can test it safely.
var walk_mask: Variant = null
var figure: Figure3D
var reach: Area3D
var camera: FollowCamera
## Last place the player stood; used if they ever fall out of the world.
## True while the sprint input is held and the player is moving.
var sprinting := false
var _dust: GPUParticles3D
var _safe := Vector3.ZERO
var _has_safe := false


func _init() -> void:
	collision_layer = 2
	collision_mask = 1
	floor_snap_length = 0.45
	floor_max_angle = deg_to_rad(52.0)
	floor_constant_speed = true
	var cs := CollisionShape3D.new()
	var cap := CapsuleShape3D.new()
	cap.radius = 0.28
	cap.height = 1.6
	cs.shape = cap
	cs.position.y = 0.8
	add_child(cs)
	figure = Figure3D.new()
	figure.coat = Color(0.5, 0.13, 0.11)
	figure.accent = Color(0.62, 0.42, 0.2)
	figure.hood = false
	figure.configure({"hair": "bun", "hair_color": Color(0.1, 0.06, 0.05), "long_coat": true})
	apply_look(figure, GameState.get_flag("player_look", {}))
	figure.xray = true
	figure.harmonize_clothes = false
	add_child(figure)
	reach = Area3D.new()
	reach.collision_layer = 0
	reach.collision_mask = 4
	reach.monitorable = false
	var rs := CollisionShape3D.new()
	var sphere := SphereShape3D.new()
	sphere.radius = 1.4
	rs.shape = sphere
	rs.position = Vector3(0, 0.8, 0)
	reach.add_child(rs)
	add_child(reach)
	add_child(Rain3D.new())
	# soft ring under the feet, as in the key art
	var ring := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var tm := TorusMesh.new()
	tm.inner_radius = 0.42
	tm.outer_radius = 0.47
	tm.rings = 32
	tm.ring_segments = 4
	ring.mesh = tm
	ring.scale = Vector3(1, 0.05, 1)
	ring.position.y = 0.04
	var rm := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	rm.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
	rm.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA
	rm.albedo_color = Color(0.95, 0.92, 0.85, 0.55)
	ring.material_override = rm
	ring.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
	add_child(ring)
	_dust = _make_dust()
	add_child(_dust)
	camera = FollowCamera.new()
	camera.target = self
	add_child(camera)


## Applies a created character's look (ui/title/character_creation.gd) to a figure.
## Colours are stored as HTML strings so they survive the JSON save.
static func apply_look(f: Figure3D, look: Variant) -> void:
	if not look is Dictionary:
		return
	var d: Dictionary = look
	for k: String in ["hair", "long_coat"]:
		if d.has(k):
			f.set(k, d[k])
	for k: String in ["hair_color", "skin", "coat"]:
		if d.has(k):
			f.set(k, Color.html(str(d[k])))


func setup(touch_controls: TouchControls, _bounds: Variant = null) -> void:
	touch = touch_controls
	camera.reset_smoothing()


func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	var dir := Vector2.ZERO
	if not Dialogue.active:
		dir = Input.get_vector(&"move_left", &"move_right", &"move_up", &"move_down")
		if dir == Vector2.ZERO and touch != null:
			dir = touch.vector
	var moving := dir.length() > 0.1
	sprinting = moving and (Input.is_action_pressed(&"sprint") or (touch != null and touch.sprint))
	var top := run_speed if sprinting else walk_speed
	var want := Vector3(dir.x, 0, dir.y).limit_length(1.0) * top if moving else Vector3.ZERO
	var h := Vector3(velocity.x, 0, velocity.z).move_toward(want, (accel if moving else decel) * delta)
	velocity.x = h.x
	velocity.z = h.z
	if is_on_floor():
		velocity.y = 0.0
	else:
		velocity.y -= gravity * delta
	move_and_slide()
	if is_on_floor():
		_safe = global_position
		_has_safe = true
	elif global_position.y < -40.0 and _has_safe:
		global_position = _safe + Vector3(0, 0.3, 0)
		velocity = Vector3.ZERO
	if moving:
		facing = Vector3(dir.x, 0, dir.y).normalized()
	var yaw := atan2(-facing.x, -facing.z)
	figure.rotation.y = lerp_angle(figure.rotation.y, yaw, 1.0 - exp(-14.0 * delta))
	figure.set_motion(h.length() > 0.3, h.length() / walk_speed)
	_dust.emitting = is_on_floor() and h.length() > walk_speed * 1.25
	camera.sprint = sprinting
	_update_target()


## Small kicks of spray at the heels while running.
func _make_dust() -> GPUParticles3D:
	var p := GPUParticles3D.new()
	p.amount = 14
	p.lifetime = 0.45
	p.local_coords = false
	p.emitting = false
	p.position = Vector3(0, 0.06, 0)
	p.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
	var pm := ParticleProcessMaterial.new()
	pm.emission_shape = ParticleProcessMaterial.EMISSION_SHAPE_SPHERE
	pm.emission_sphere_radius = 0.15
	pm.direction = Vector3(0, 1, 0)
	pm.spread = 50.0
	pm.initial_velocity_min = 0.5
	pm.initial_velocity_max = 1.2
	pm.gravity = Vector3(0, -3.5, 0)
	pm.scale_min = 0.5
	pm.scale_max = 1.3
	p.process_material = pm
	var q := QuadMesh.new()
	q.size = Vector2(0.07, 0.07)
	var m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	m.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
	m.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA
	m.albedo_color = Color(0.8, 0.86, 0.95, 0.3)
	m.billboard_mode = BaseMaterial3D.BILLBOARD_ENABLED
	q.material = m
	p.draw_pass_1 = q
	return p


func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event.is_action_pressed(&"interact") and try_interact():
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()


func try_interact() -> bool:
	if Dialogue.active or target == null or not target.is_available():
		return false
	target.interact(self)
	return true


func face_now() -> void:
	figure.rotation.y = atan2(-facing.x, -facing.z)


func _update_target() -> void:
	var best: Interactable3D = null
	var best_d := INF
	for a in reach.get_overlapping_areas():
		var it := a as Interactable3D
		if it == null or not it.is_available():
			continue
		var d := global_position.distance_squared_to(it.global_position)
		if d < best_d:
			best_d = d
			best = it
	if best != target:
		target = best
		target_changed.emit(target)


==============================================================================
== game/puzzle/ore_cart.gd
==============================================================================
class_name OreCart
extends StaticBody2D
## A cart on a rail spur. Pushed from its start it rolls to the junction and takes
## whichever branch the points are set to: branch A ends at a buffer (haul it back
## and try again), branch B ends in the washout, where the cart wedges and becomes
## a bridge. State lives in GameState so it survives save/load.
## PROVISIONAL ART (drawn in code).

@export var state_key := "anjabi.cart"
@export var switch_key := "anjabi.spur_switch"
@export var rail_main: NodePath
@export var rail_a: NodePath
@export var rail_b: NodePath
## ProvBlock pit segment whose collision is removed once the cart bridges it.
@export var gap: NodePath
@export var bridge_position := Vector2.ZERO
@export var roll_speed := 70.0
@export var dialogue_buffer := ""
@export var dialogue_bridge := ""
@export var on_bridged: Array[Dictionary] = []

var moving := false

var _interactable: Interactable
var _shape: CollisionShape2D


func _ready() -> void:
	collision_layer = 1
	collision_mask = 0
	var rect := RectangleShape2D.new()
	rect.size = Vector2(18, 10)
	_shape = CollisionShape2D.new()
	_shape.shape = rect
	_shape.position = Vector2(0, -5)
	add_child(_shape)
	_interactable = Interactable.new()
	_interactable.label = "Ore cart"
	_interactable.radius = 16.0
	_interactable.position = Vector2(0, -5)
	add_child(_interactable)
	_interactable.interacted.connect(_on_interacted)
	_apply_state()


func state() -> String:
	return str(GameState.get_flag(state_key, "start"))


func _points(path: NodePath) -> PackedVector2Array:
	var rail := get_node(path) as ProvRail
	var out := PackedVector2Array()
	for p in rail.points:
		out.append(rail.to_global(p))
	return out


func _apply_state() -> void:
	var s := state()
	var bridged := s == "trench"
	match s:
		"buffer":
			var a := _points(rail_a)
			global_position = a[a.size() - 1]
		"trench":
			global_position = bridge_position
		_:
			global_position = _points(rail_main)[0]
	_shape.set_deferred("disabled", bridged)
	z_index = -4 if bridged else 0
	_interactable.enabled = not bridged
	_interactable.prompt = "Haul back" if s == "buffer" else "Push"
	var pit := get_node_or_null(gap) as ProvBlock
	if pit != null:
		pit.set_solid(not bridged)
	queue_redraw()


func _on_interacted(_by: Node2D) -> void:
	if moving:
		return
	var route := PackedVector2Array()
	var next := "start"
	var main := _points(rail_main)
	if state() == "start":
		var to_trench := str(GameState.get_flag(switch_key, "a")) == "b"
		route.append_array(main.slice(1))
		route.append_array(_points(rail_b if to_trench else rail_a).slice(1))
		if to_trench:
			route.append(bridge_position)
		next = "trench" if to_trench else "buffer"
	elif state() == "buffer":
		var a := _points(rail_a)
		a.reverse()
		route.append_array(a.slice(1))
		main.reverse()
		route.append_array(main.slice(1))
	else:
		return
	_roll(route, next)


func _roll(route: PackedVector2Array, next: String) -> void:
	moving = true
	_interactable.enabled = false
	var tween := create_tween()
	var from := global_position
	for i in route.size():
		var t := from.distance_to(route[i]) / roll_speed
		var step := tween.tween_property(self, "global_position", route[i], maxf(t, 0.05))
		if i == 0:
			step.set_trans(Tween.TRANS_QUAD).set_ease(Tween.EASE_IN)
		from = route[i]
	await tween.finished
	moving = false
	GameState.set_flag(state_key, next)
	_apply_state()
	if next == "trench":
		GameState.apply(on_bridged)
		if dialogue_bridge != "":
			Dialogue.start(dialogue_bridge)
	elif next == "buffer" and dialogue_buffer != "":
		Dialogue.start(dialogue_buffer)


func _draw() -> void:
	var rust := Color(0.5, 0.29, 0.18)
	if state() == "trench" and not moving:
		# Wedged in the washout, bed up: a short steel deck.
		draw_rect(Rect2(-13, -42, 26, 44), Color(0.23, 0.2, 0.19))
		draw_rect(Rect2(-12, -41, 24, 42), rust.darkened(0.2))
		for i in 6:
			draw_line(Vector2(-12, -38 + i * 7), Vector2(12, -38 + i * 7), rust.darkened(0.45), 1.0)
		return
	draw_rect(Rect2(-8, -3, 4, 3), Color(0.1, 0.1, 0.11))
	draw_rect(Rect2(4, -3, 4, 3), Color(0.1, 0.1, 0.11))
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
		Vector2(-11, -15), Vector2(11, -15), Vector2(8, -3), Vector2(-8, -3)]), rust)
	draw_rect(Rect2(-11, -17, 22, 3), rust.lightened(0.12))
	draw_rect(Rect2(-9, -16, 18, 1), Color(0.08, 0.08, 0.09))
	draw_line(Vector2(-3, -13), Vector2(-4, -5), rust.darkened(0.3), 1.0)
	draw_line(Vector2(5, -13), Vector2(4, -6), rust.darkened(0.3), 1.0)


==============================================================================
== game/puzzle/ore_cart_3d.gd
==============================================================================
class_name OreCart3D
extends Node3D
## 3D version of OreCart, same rules and the same GameState keys. Pushed from its
## start it rolls to the junction and takes whichever branch the points are set to:
## branch A ends at a buffer (haul it back), branch B ends in the washout, where the
## cart wedges bed-up and becomes a bridge. PROVISIONAL ART (primitives).

@export var state_key := "anjabi.cart"
@export var switch_key := "anjabi.spur_switch"
@export var roll_speed := 3.5
@export var dialogue_buffer := ""
@export var dialogue_bridge := ""
@export var on_bridged: Array[Dictionary] = []

## Rail polylines in world space, set by the map before the cart enters the tree.
var rail_main := PackedVector3Array()
var rail_a := PackedVector3Array()
var rail_b := PackedVector3Array()
var bridge_position := Vector3.ZERO
## Invisible wall across the washout; removed once the cart bridges it.
var gap_blocker: CollisionShape3D

var moving := false

var _body: StaticBody3D
var _shape: CollisionShape3D
var _deck_body: StaticBody3D
var _deck_shape: CollisionShape3D
var _visual: Node3D
var _deck_visual: Node3D
var _interactable: Interactable3D


func _ready() -> void:
	var rust := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	rust.albedo_color = Color(0.5, 0.29, 0.18)
	rust.roughness = 0.65
	rust.metallic = 0.5
	var dark := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	dark.albedo_color = Color(0.1, 0.1, 0.11)
	dark.roughness = 0.5
	dark.metallic = 0.6
	_body = StaticBody3D.new()
	_body.collision_layer = 1
	_body.collision_mask = 0
	add_child(_body)
	_shape = CollisionShape3D.new()
	var bs := BoxShape3D.new()
	bs.size = Vector3(1.3, 1.0, 2.2)
	_shape.shape = bs
	_shape.position.y = 0.5
	_body.add_child(_shape)
	_visual = Node3D.new()
	add_child(_visual)
	_mesh(_visual, Vector3(1.2, 0.75, 2.1), rust, Vector3(0, 0.62, 0))
	_mesh(_visual, Vector3(1.0, 0.05, 1.9), dark, Vector3(0, 1.0, 0))
	_mesh(_visual, Vector3(1.3, 0.08, 2.2), rust, Vector3(0, 1.0, 0))
	for x: float in [-0.5, 0.5]:
		for z: float in [-0.65, 0.65]:
			var w := MeshInstance3D.new()
			var cm := CylinderMesh.new()
			cm.top_radius = 0.2
			cm.bottom_radius = 0.2
			cm.height = 0.1
			cm.radial_segments = 12
			cm.rings = 0
			w.mesh = cm
			w.material_override = dark
			w.position = Vector3(x, 0.2, z)
			w.rotation.z = PI * 0.5
			_visual.add_child(w)
	# wedged in the washout: the tub upside down, its bed level with the ground
	_deck_body = StaticBody3D.new()
	_deck_body.collision_layer = 1
	_deck_body.collision_mask = 0
	_deck_body.top_level = true
	add_child(_deck_body)
	_deck_shape = CollisionShape3D.new()
	var ds := BoxShape3D.new()
	ds.size = Vector3(1.6, 0.3, 2.6)
	_deck_shape.shape = ds
	_deck_shape.position.y = -0.15
	_deck_body.add_child(_deck_shape)
	_deck_visual = Node3D.new()
	_deck_body.add_child(_deck_visual)
	_mesh(_deck_visual, Vector3(1.3, 0.85, 2.4), rust, Vector3(0, -0.45, 0))
	_mesh(_deck_visual, Vector3(1.32, 0.04, 2.42), rust.duplicate(), Vector3(0, -0.01, 0))
	for i in 5:
		_mesh(_deck_visual, Vector3(1.32, 0.03, 0.06), dark, Vector3(0, 0.01, -1.0 + i * 0.5))
	_interactable = Interactable3D.new()
	_interactable.label = "Ore cart"
	_interactable.radius = 1.3
	_interactable.position = Vector3(0, 0.6, 0)
	add_child(_interactable)
	_interactable.interacted.connect(_on_interacted)
	_apply_state()


func state() -> String:
	return str(GameState.get_flag(state_key, "start"))


func _apply_state() -> void:
	var s := state()
	var bridged := s == "trench"
	match s:
		"buffer":
			global_position = rail_a[rail_a.size() - 1]
		"trench":
			global_position = bridge_position
		_:
			global_position = rail_main[0]
	_deck_body.global_position = bridge_position
	_shape.set_deferred("disabled", bridged)
	_deck_shape.set_deferred("disabled", not bridged)
	_visual.visible = not bridged
	_deck_visual.visible = bridged
	_interactable.enabled = not bridged
	_interactable.prompt = "Haul back" if s == "buffer" else "Push"
	if gap_blocker != null:
		gap_blocker.set_deferred("disabled", bridged)


func _on_interacted(_by: Node3D) -> void:
	if moving:
		return
	var route := PackedVector3Array()
	var next := "start"
	if state() == "start":
		var to_trench := str(GameState.get_flag(switch_key, "a")) == "b"
		route.append_array(rail_main.slice(1))
		route.append_array((rail_b if to_trench else rail_a).slice(1))
		if to_trench:
			route.append(bridge_position)
		next = "trench" if to_trench else "buffer"
	elif state() == "buffer":
		var a := rail_a.duplicate()
		a.reverse()
		route.append_array(a.slice(1))
		var m := rail_main.duplicate()
		m.reverse()
		route.append_array(m.slice(1))
	else:
		return
	_roll(route, next)


func _roll(route: PackedVector3Array, next: String) -> void:
	moving = true
	_interactable.enabled = false
	var tween := create_tween()
	var from := global_position
	for i in route.size():
		var t := from.distance_to(route[i]) / roll_speed
		var step := tween.tween_property(self, "global_position", route[i], maxf(t, 0.05))
		if i == 0:
			step.set_trans(Tween.TRANS_QUAD).set_ease(Tween.EASE_IN)
		from = route[i]
	if next == "trench":
		tween.tween_property(self, "global_position", bridge_position + Vector3(0, -0.9, 0), 0.25)
	await tween.finished
	moving = false
	GameState.set_flag(state_key, next)
	_apply_state()
	if next == "trench":
		GameState.apply(on_bridged)
		if dialogue_bridge != "":
			Dialogue.start(dialogue_bridge)
	elif next == "buffer" and dialogue_buffer != "":
		Dialogue.start(dialogue_buffer)


func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
	if moving:
		var d := Vector3.ZERO
		# face the direction of travel along the rails
		d = global_position - get_meta("last", global_position)
		set_meta("last", global_position)
		d.y = 0.0
		if d.length() > 0.001:
			_visual.rotation.y = atan2(d.x, d.z)


func _mesh(parent: Node3D, size: Vector3, m: Material, at: Vector3) -> void:
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var b := BoxMesh.new()
	b.size = size
	mi.mesh = b
	mi.material_override = m
	mi.position = at
	parent.add_child(mi)

== game/puzzle/track_switch.gd

class_name TrackSwitch
extends StaticBody2D
## Points lever. Flips a GameState flag between "a" and "b"; anything on the
## rails reads that flag. PROVISIONAL ART (drawn in code).

@export var switch_key := "anjabi.spur_switch"
@export var notify_a := "The points grind across, toward the buffer."
@export var notify_b := "The points grind across, toward the washout."

var _interactable: Interactable


func _ready() -> void:
	collision_layer = 1
	collision_mask = 0
	var shape := CircleShape2D.new()
	shape.radius = 3.0
	var cs := CollisionShape2D.new()
	cs.shape = shape
	add_child(cs)
	_interactable = Interactable.new()
	_interactable.prompt = "Throw"
	_interactable.label = "Points lever"
	add_child(_interactable)
	_interactable.interacted.connect(_on_interacted)
	GameState.flag_changed.connect(_on_flag_changed)


func branch() -> String:
	return str(GameState.get_flag(switch_key, "a"))


func _on_interacted(_by: Node2D) -> void:
	var next := "b" if branch() == "a" else "a"
	GameState.set_flag(switch_key, next)
	GameState.notify(notify_b if next == "b" else notify_a)


func _on_flag_changed(key: String, _value: Variant) -> void:
	if key == switch_key:
		queue_redraw()


func _draw() -> void:
	var lean := -5.0 if branch() == "a" else 5.0
	draw_rect(Rect2(-4, -3, 8, 4), Color(0.2, 0.2, 0.22))
	draw_line(Vector2(0, -2), Vector2(lean, -14), Color(0.55, 0.3, 0.2), 2.0)
	draw_circle(Vector2(lean, -15), 2.0, Color(0.85, 0.82, 0.7) if branch() == "a" else Color(0.85, 0.3, 0.2))

== game/puzzle/track_switch_3d.gd

class_name TrackSwitch3D
extends StaticBody3D
## 3D points lever. Flips a GameState flag between "a" and "b"; anything on the
## rails reads that flag. PROVISIONAL ART (primitives).

@export var switch_key := "anjabi.spur_switch"
@export var notify_a := "The points grind across, toward the buffer."
@export var notify_b := "The points grind across, toward the washout."

var _lever: Node3D
var _knob: MeshInstance3D
var _knob_a: StandardMaterial3D
var _knob_b: StandardMaterial3D


func _ready() -> void:
	collision_layer = 1
	collision_mask = 0
	var cs := CollisionShape3D.new()
	var s := BoxShape3D.new()
	s.size = Vector3(0.6, 0.5, 0.4)
	cs.shape = s
	cs.position.y = 0.25
	add_child(cs)
	var base_m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	base_m.albedo_color = Color(0.2, 0.2, 0.22)
	base_m.metallic = 0.6
	base_m.roughness = 0.5
	var arm_m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	arm_m.albedo_color = Color(0.55, 0.3, 0.2)
	arm_m.metallic = 0.4
	_knob_a = StandardMaterial3D.new()
	_knob_a.albedo_color = Color(0.85, 0.82, 0.7)
	_knob_b = StandardMaterial3D.new()
	_knob_b.albedo_color = Color(0.85, 0.3, 0.2)
	_knob_b.emission_enabled = true
	_knob_b.emission = Color(0.8, 0.2, 0.1)
	var base := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var bm := BoxMesh.new()
	bm.size = Vector3(0.6, 0.35, 0.4)
	base.mesh = bm
	base.material_override = base_m
	base.position.y = 0.17
	add_child(base)
	_lever = Node3D.new()
	_lever.position.y = 0.3
	add_child(_lever)
	var arm := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var am := CylinderMesh.new()
	am.top_radius = 0.035
	am.bottom_radius = 0.045
	am.height = 1.0
	am.radial_segments = 8
	am.rings = 0
	arm.mesh = am
	arm.material_override = arm_m
	arm.position.y = 0.5
	_lever.add_child(arm)
	_knob = MeshInstance3D.new()
	var km := SphereMesh.new()
	km.radius = 0.08
	km.height = 0.16
	km.radial_segments = 10
	km.rings = 6
	_knob.mesh = km
	_knob.position.y = 1.02
	_lever.add_child(_knob)
	var it := Interactable3D.new()
	it.prompt = "Throw"
	it.label = "Points lever"
	it.radius = 1.0
	it.position.y = 0.6
	add_child(it)
	it.interacted.connect(_on_interacted)
	GameState.flag_changed.connect(_on_flag_changed)
	_show()


func branch() -> String:
	return str(GameState.get_flag(switch_key, "a"))


func _on_interacted(_by: Node3D) -> void:
	var next := "b" if branch() == "a" else "a"
	GameState.set_flag(switch_key, next)
	GameState.notify(notify_b if next == "b" else notify_a)


func _on_flag_changed(key: String, _value: Variant) -> void:
	if key == switch_key:
		_show()


func _show() -> void:
	var b := branch() == "b"
	_lever.rotation.z = 0.5 if b else -0.5
	_knob.material_override = _knob_b if b else _knob_a

class_name GameMap
extends Node2D
## Base for every walkable location. A map scene needs an "Entities" child
## (y-sorted; the player is added there) and Marker2D spawn points under "Spawns".

@export var map_id := ""
@export var display_name := ""
@export var bounds := Rect2(0, 0, 960, 704)

## Set by Main once the player has been placed.
var player: Node2D

@onready var entities: Node2D = $Entities


func spawn_position(spawn: String) -> Vector2:
	var m := get_node_or_null("Spawns/" + spawn) as Node2D
	if m == null:
		m = get_node_or_null("Spawns/default") as Node2D
	return m.global_position if m != null else bounds.get_center()
