== maps/interior_map_3d.gd
==============================================================================
class_name InteriorMap3D
extends MapBuilder3D
## Base for interiors in the Pokemon manner: a single room (or a few) entered through
## a door on the overworld, drawn as a cut-away box under the same fixed camera, with
## black around it. The front wall is left out so the camera sees in; a mat at the
## front edge leads back out. Lighting is indoor (EnvironmentController.indoor_look):
## the clock runs on, but sky, sun and weather do not reach inside.
##
## A subclass sets `exit_map` / `exit_spawn` (where the mat leads) and builds its room
## in _build() with room() and the MapBuilder3D kit.

## Where the exit mat leads.
@export var exit_map := "anjabi_town"
@export var exit_spawn := "default"


func _ready() -> void:
	indoor = true
	_materials()
	SettlementKit.materials(self)
	_environment()
	_build()
	var spawns := Node3D.new()
	spawns.name = "Spawns"
	add_child(spawns)
	var m := Marker3D.new()
	m.name = "default"
	m.position = entry_point()
	spawns.add_child(m)


## Override: the room's contents.
func _build() -> void:
	pass


## Override: where the player appears on entering (just inside the door).
func entry_point() -> Vector3:
	return Vector3(0, 0.05, 2.6)


## A room `size` (width, height, depth) centred on the origin, floor at y = 0, door in
## the middle of the open front (+Z). Walls are solid; the front has a low sill either
## side of the door so the room reads as closed.
func room(size: Vector3, floor_m: Material, wall_m: Material, door_w := 1.6) -> void:
	var hx := size.x * 0.5
	var hz := size.z * 0.5
	slab(Vector3(-hx - 0.3, -0.4, -hz - 0.3), Vector3(hx + 0.3, 0.0, hz + 0.3), floor_m)
	box(Vector3(0, size.y * 0.5, -hz - 0.15), Vector3(size.x + 0.6, size.y, 0.3), wall_m)
	box(Vector3(-hx - 0.15, size.y * 0.5, 0), Vector3(0.3, size.y, size.z), wall_m)
	box(Vector3(hx + 0.15, size.y * 0.5, 0), Vector3(0.3, size.y, size.z), wall_m)
	# skirting along the walls
	box(Vector3(0, 0.08, -hz + 0.03), Vector3(size.x, 0.16, 0.06), M.timber, false)
	# low front sill either side of the door, and an invisible wall above it
	var side := (size.x - door_w) * 0.5
	for s: float in [-1.0, 1.0]:
		var cx := s * (door_w * 0.5 + side * 0.5)
		box(Vector3(cx, 0.25, hz + 0.15), Vector3(side, 0.5, 0.3), wall_m)
		blocker(Vector3(cx - side * 0.5, 0, hz), Vector3(cx + side * 0.5, 3, hz + 0.3))
	# the door mat and the way out
	box(Vector3(0, 0.01, hz - 0.35), Vector3(door_w * 0.8, 0.02, 0.6), M.canvas, false)
	blocker(Vector3(-door_w * 0.5, 0, hz + 0.8), Vector3(door_w * 0.5, 3, hz + 1.1))
	var out := MapExit3D.new()
	out.name = "ExitMat"
	out.prompt = "Leave"
	out.label = display_name.get_slice("·", display_name.get_slice_count("·") - 1).strip_edges().capitalize()
	out.radius = 1.0
	out.target_map = exit_map
	out.target_spawn = exit_spawn
	out.position = Vector3(0, 0.6, hz - 0.2)
	add_child(out)


## A hanging lamp: a small lit shade and a warm light under the ceiling.
func ceiling_lamp(at: Vector3) -> void:
	box(at, Vector3(0.5, 0.18, 0.5), M.lamp, false)
	warm_light(at + Vector3(0, -0.4, 0), 2.2, 7.0)


==============================================================================
== maps/map_builder_3d.gd
==============================================================================
class_name MapBuilder3D
extends GameMap3D
## Shared toolkit for 3D maps built in code: the material palette `M`, primitives
## (box, slab, cyl, dome, beam, stairs, railing, fence, lamp, tree, sign_text) and the
## Anjabi night-rain environment. PROVISIONAL: authored meshes replace the pieces,
## the footprints and colliders stay.

var M := {}
## Drives this map's light, sky, fog and precipitation from WorldTime and Weather.
var environment: EnvironmentController

static var _detail: NoiseTexture2D
static var _detail_n: NoiseTexture2D


## Shared surface detail: a seamless noise for albedo variation and a matching
## normal map, applied world-space triplanar so every box and slab lines up.
static func detail_textures() -> Array:
	if _detail == null:
		var n := FastNoiseLite.new()
		n.noise_type = FastNoiseLite.TYPE_SIMPLEX_SMOOTH
		n.frequency = 0.03
		n.fractal_octaves = 5
		n.fractal_gain = 0.55
		var g := Gradient.new()
		g.set_color(0, Color(0.6, 0.6, 0.6))
		g.set_color(1, Color(1.05, 1.05, 1.05))
		_detail = NoiseTexture2D.new()
		_detail.width = 256
		_detail.height = 256
		_detail.seamless = true
		_detail.noise = n
		_detail.color_ramp = g
		_detail_n = NoiseTexture2D.new()
		_detail_n.width = 256
		_detail_n.height = 256
		_detail_n.seamless = true
		_detail_n.as_normal_map = true
		_detail_n.bump_strength = 4.0
		_detail_n.noise = n
	return [_detail, _detail_n]


static var _detail_soft: NoiseTexture2D


## The detail noise with a narrower range, for the handheld style's flatter surfaces.
## (The ground and sea shaders keep the full-range texture: their thresholds expect it.)
static func soft_detail() -> NoiseTexture2D:
	if _detail_soft == null:
		_detail_soft = detail_textures()[0].duplicate() as NoiseTexture2D
		var g := Gradient.new()
		g.set_color(0, Color(0.8, 0.8, 0.8))
		g.set_color(1, Color(1.02, 1.02, 1.02))
		_detail_soft.color_ramp = g
	return _detail_soft


static func add_detail(m: StandardMaterial3D, scale := 0.4) -> void:
	var t := detail_textures()
	m.albedo_texture = soft_detail() if ArtStyle.handheld else t[0]
	m.normal_enabled = true
	m.normal_texture = t[1]
	m.normal_scale = 0.8 if not ArtStyle.handheld else 0.35
	m.uv1_triplanar = true
	m.uv1_world_triplanar = true
	m.uv1_scale = Vector3(scale, scale, scale)


# --- materials ------------------------------------------------------------------

const WET_GROUND := preload("res://art/shaders/wet_ground.gdshader")


## Wet ground material (art/shaders/wet_ground.gdshader).
static func ground_mat(c: Color, cobble := 1.0, wet := 0.8, puddles := 0.55, vertex_color := false) -> ShaderMaterial:
	var m := ShaderMaterial.new()
	m.shader = WET_GROUND
	m.set_shader_parameter("base_color", c)
	m.set_shader_parameter("cobble", cobble)
	m.set_shader_parameter("wetness", wet)
	m.set_shader_parameter("puddles", puddles)
	m.set_shader_parameter("use_vertex_color", vertex_color)
	if ArtStyle.handheld:
		m.set_shader_parameter("stone_size", ArtStyle.STONE)
	m.set_shader_parameter("noise_tex", detail_textures()[0])
	return m


func _materials() -> void:
	M.paving = ground_mat(Color(0.46, 0.41, 0.42))
	M.paving_light = ground_mat(Color(0.55, 0.49, 0.48), 1.0, 0.7, 0.3)
	M.stone = _mat(Color(0.36, 0.35, 0.33), 0.8)
	M.rock = _mat(Color(0.25, 0.22, 0.27), 0.95)
	M.rock_dark = _mat(Color(0.14, 0.12, 0.17), 0.95)
	M.moss = ground_mat(Color(0.17, 0.27, 0.23), 0.0, 0.5, 0.15)
	M.concrete = _mat(Color(0.5, 0.45, 0.45), 0.85)
	M.steel = _mat(Color(0.38, 0.33, 0.33), 0.45, 0.6)
	M.steel_dark = _mat(Color(0.17, 0.15, 0.19), 0.5, 0.6)
	M.copper = _mat(Color(0.3, 0.53, 0.48), 0.5, 0.45)
	M.copper_raw = _mat(Color(0.62, 0.36, 0.22), 0.4, 0.75)
	M.panel = _mat(Color(0.36, 0.45, 0.46), 0.7, 0.2)
	M.panel_violet = _mat(Color(0.33, 0.29, 0.36), 0.75, 0.15)
	M.timber = _mat(Color(0.28, 0.17, 0.12), 0.85)
	M.canvas = _mat(Color(0.52, 0.2, 0.24), 0.95)
	M.canvas_teal = _mat(Color(0.19, 0.38, 0.4), 0.95)
	M.window = _mat(Color(1.0, 0.72, 0.38), 0.3, 0.0, Color(1.0, 0.62, 0.28), 2.2)
	M.lamp = _mat(Color(1.0, 0.8, 0.5), 0.3, 0.0, Color(1.0, 0.7, 0.35), 4.0)
	M.warning = _mat(Color(0.8, 0.6, 0.12), 0.6)
	M.sand = _mat(Color(0.58, 0.51, 0.45), 0.9)
	M.sea = ShaderMaterial.new()
	M.sea.shader = preload("res://art/shaders/sea.gdshader")
	M.sea.set_shader_parameter("noise_tex", detail_textures()[0])
	M.foliage = _mat(Color(0.09, 0.21, 0.19), 0.9)
	M.foliage_b = _mat(Color(0.12, 0.25, 0.2), 0.9)
	M.trunk = _mat(Color(0.18, 0.13, 0.1), 0.9)
	M.mesh_fence = _mat(Color(0.25, 0.27, 0.3, 0.45), 0.6, 0.4)
	M.mesh_fence.transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TRANSPARENCY_ALPHA
	M.mud = ground_mat(Color(0.32, 0.26, 0.25), 0.0, 0.95, 0.75)
	M.gravel = ground_mat(Color(0.44, 0.4, 0.41), 0.0, 0.75, 0.45)
	M.rust = _mat(Color(0.48, 0.27, 0.17), 0.7, 0.5)
	M.blue_light = _mat(Color(0.4, 0.6, 1.0), 0.3, 0.0, Color(0.35, 0.55, 1.0), 3.0)


func _mat(c: Color, rough: float, metal: float = 0.0, emit: Color = Color.BLACK, emit_e: float = 0.0) -> StandardMaterial3D:
	var m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	m.albedo_color = c
	m.roughness = rough
	m.metallic = metal
	if emit_e > 0.0:
		m.emission_enabled = true
		m.emission = emit
		m.emission_energy_multiplier = emit_e
	elif rough >= 0.6 and c.a >= 0.99:
		add_detail(m)
	return m


# --- primitives -------------------------------------------------------------------

func _body() -> StaticBody3D:
	var b := StaticBody3D.new()
	b.collision_layer = 1
	b.collision_mask = 0
	return b


## An authored model from res://art/models (exported from art/source/*.blend).
## Models face +Z (south, toward the camera). Visual only; add colliders separately.
## Repeated small models in BATCHED are not instanced: they are gathered and drawn as
## one MultiMesh per model part per 24 m cell (see _flush_batches), and model() returns null.
func model(model_name: String, at: Vector3, yaw := 0.0, s: Variant = 1.0) -> Node3D:
	if model_name in BATCHED and not bake_mode:
		var sv: Vector3 = s if s is Vector3 else Vector3(s, s, s)
		batch(model_name, Transform3D(Basis(Vector3.UP, yaw) * Basis.from_scale(sv), at))
		return null
	var n := (load("res://art/models/%s.glb" % model_name) as PackedScene).instantiate() as Node3D
	n.position = at
	n.rotation.y = yaw
	n.scale = s if s is Vector3 else Vector3(s, s, s)
	weather_model(n)
	add_child(n)
	return n


## Set by tools/bake_2d.gd: every model stays a separate node so it can be rendered alone.
static var bake_mode := false
const BATCHED := ["pine", "lantern_post", "barrel", "planter", "crate_stack"]
## Models whose batches cast shadows (the rest are too small to matter).
const BATCH_SHADOWS := ["pine", "lantern_post"]
const BATCH_CELL := 24.0
var _batches := {}


## Queue one copy of a model; all queued copies are built at the end of the frame.
func batch(model_name: String, x: Transform3D) -> void:
	if _batches.is_empty():
		_flush_batches.call_deferred()
	var key := "%s|%d|%d" % [model_name, floori(x.origin.x / BATCH_CELL), floori(x.origin.z / BATCH_CELL)]
	if not _batches.has(key):
		_batches[key] = [] as Array[Transform3D]
	(_batches[key] as Array[Transform3D]).append(x)


func _flush_batches() -> void:
	for key: String in _batches:
		var model_name := key.get_slice("|", 0)
		var xs: Array[Transform3D] = _batches[key]
		for part: Array in model_parts(model_name):
			var mm := MultiMesh.new()
			mm.transform_format = MultiMesh.TRANSFORM_3D
			mm.mesh = part[0]
			mm.instance_count = xs.size()
			for i in xs.size():
				mm.set_instance_transform(i, xs[i] * (part[1] as Transform3D))
			var mmi := MultiMeshInstance3D.new()
			mmi.name = "Batch_" + model_name
			mmi.multimesh = mm
			mmi.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_ON if model_name in BATCH_SHADOWS else GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
			mmi.visibility_range_end = 90.0
			add_child(mmi)
	_batches.clear()


func _notification(what: int) -> void:
	if what == NOTIFICATION_READY and not bake_mode:
		merge_static.call_deferred()


## Static batching: once the map is built, every primitive mesh (box, slab, beam, cyl,
## dome) that belongs to plain scenery is merged into one mesh per material per 24 m
## cell, so hundreds of walls, beams and steps cost a few draws. Anything under a
## scripted node (NPCs, doors, puzzle pieces, sub-maps) or an imported model is left alone.
func merge_static() -> void:
	if not is_inside_tree():
		return
	var inv := global_transform.affine_inverse()
	var groups := {}
	var stack: Array[Node] = []
	for c in get_children():
		stack.append(c)
	while not stack.is_empty():
		var n: Node = stack.pop_back()
		if n.get_script() != null or n.scene_file_path != "" or n.name == "Entities":
			continue
		for c in n.get_children():
			stack.append(c)
		var mi := n as MeshInstance3D
		if mi == null or not (mi.mesh is PrimitiveMesh) or mi.material_override == null or not mi.visible:
			continue
		var x := inv * mi.global_transform
		var key := [mi.material_override, mi.cast_shadow, floori(x.origin.x / BATCH_CELL), floori(x.origin.z / BATCH_CELL)]
		if not groups.has(key):
			groups[key] = []
		(groups[key] as Array).append([mi, x])
	for key: Array in groups:
		var items: Array = groups[key]
		if items.size() < 2:
			continue
		var st := SurfaceTool.new()
		for it: Array in items:
			st.append_from((it[0] as MeshInstance3D).mesh, 0, it[1])
		var merged := MeshInstance3D.new()
		merged.name = "Static"
		merged.mesh = st.commit()
		merged.material_override = key[0]
		merged.cast_shadow = key[1]
		add_child(merged)
		for it: Array in items:
			(it[0] as Node).queue_free()


static var _parts_cache := {}


## Every mesh in a model with its transform relative to the model root: [[Mesh, Transform3D]].
static func model_parts(model_name: String) -> Array:
	if not _parts_cache.has(model_name):
		var n := (load("res://art/models/%s.glb" % model_name) as PackedScene).instantiate() as Node3D
		weather_model(n)
		var out := []
		for mi: MeshInstance3D in n.find_children("*", "MeshInstance3D", true, false):
			if mi.mesh == null:
				continue
			var x := mi.transform
			var p := mi.get_parent() as Node3D
			while p != null and p != n:
				x = p.transform * x
				p = p.get_parent() as Node3D
			out.append([mi.mesh, x])
		_parts_cache[model_name] = out
		n.free()
	return _parts_cache[model_name]


## The shed model (native 4 m wide, 3 m tall, 3 m deep) stretched to `size`
## (width, height, depth), standing on `base`, door and window facing +Z, with a box collider.
func shed(base: Vector3, size: Vector3, yaw := 0.0, solid := true) -> Node3D:
	var n := model("shed", base, yaw, Vector3(size.x / 4.0, size.y / 3.0, size.z / 3.0))
	if solid:
		box(base + Vector3(0, size.y * 0.5, 0), size, null, true, yaw)
	return n


static var _weathered := {}


## Gives imported model materials the shared surface detail (once per material) and
## makes foliage double-sided. Emissive glass and smooth metal are left alone.
static func weather_model(root: Node) -> void:
	for mi: MeshInstance3D in root.find_children("*", "MeshInstance3D", true, false):
		if mi.mesh == null:
			continue
		for i in mi.mesh.get_surface_count():
			var m := mi.mesh.surface_get_material(i) as StandardMaterial3D
			if m == null or _weathered.has(m):
				continue
			_weathered[m] = true
			var nm := m.resource_name.to_lower()
			if "leaf" in nm or "pine" in nm or "petal" in nm:
				m.cull_mode = BaseMaterial3D.CULL_DISABLED
			if not m.emission_enabled:
				m.albedo_color = harmonize(m.albedo_color)
			if m.emission_enabled or m.roughness < 0.55:
				continue
			add_detail(m, 0.6)


## The settlement palette (PROJECT_LOG entry 21): dusty rose, warm stone, ochre,
## muted teal, verdigris, indigo slate, aged brass, deep violet. Shared by everything
## that is coloured in code.
const PALETTE: Array[Color] = [
	Color(0.62, 0.46, 0.46), Color(0.54, 0.47, 0.45), Color(0.66, 0.53, 0.38),
	Color(0.3, 0.45, 0.46), Color(0.3, 0.53, 0.48), Color(0.22, 0.2, 0.32),
	Color(0.62, 0.46, 0.25), Color(0.32, 0.24, 0.38), Color(0.52, 0.2, 0.24),
	Color(0.14, 0.24, 0.2), Color(0.3, 0.19, 0.13),
]


## Pulls a colour part of the way toward the nearest palette swatch (by hue and
## value), keeping its own lightness, so imported and code-built things share a family.
static func harmonize(c: Color, amount := 0.35) -> Color:
	var best := PALETTE[0]
	var best_d := INF
	for p in PALETTE:
		var dh := absf(fposmod(c.h - p.h + 0.5, 1.0) - 0.5) * (c.s + p.s)
		var d := dh * 2.0 + absf(c.v - p.v) + absf(c.s - p.s) * 0.5
		if d < best_d:
			best_d = d
			best = p
	var target := Color.from_hsv(best.h, lerpf(c.s, best.s, 0.6), c.v, c.a)
	return c.lerp(target, amount)


static var _mesh_cache := {}


## The first mesh of a model, for batching with scatter().
static func model_mesh(model_name: String) -> Mesh:
	if not _mesh_cache.has(model_name):
		var n := (load("res://art/models/%s.glb" % model_name) as PackedScene).instantiate()
		weather_model(n)
		var mi := n.find_children("*", "MeshInstance3D", true, false)[0] as MeshInstance3D
		_mesh_cache[model_name] = mi.mesh
		n.free()
	return _mesh_cache[model_name]


## Many copies of one model as MultiMesh draws, one per 24 m cell so off-screen
## cells are culled (ferns, rocks, clutter, flowers).
func scatter(model_name: String, xforms: Array[Transform3D], shadows := false) -> void:
	multimesh_cells(model_mesh(model_name), xforms, shadows, model_name)


func multimesh_cells(mesh: Mesh, xforms: Array[Transform3D], shadows: bool, label: String) -> void:
	var cells := {}
	for x in xforms:
		var k := Vector2i(floori(x.origin.x / BATCH_CELL), floori(x.origin.z / BATCH_CELL))
		if not cells.has(k):
			cells[k] = [] as Array[Transform3D]
		(cells[k] as Array[Transform3D]).append(x)
	for k: Vector2i in cells:
		var xs: Array[Transform3D] = cells[k]
		var mm := MultiMesh.new()
		mm.transform_format = MultiMesh.TRANSFORM_3D
		mm.mesh = mesh
		mm.instance_count = xs.size()
		for i in xs.size():
			mm.set_instance_transform(i, xs[i])
		var mmi := MultiMeshInstance3D.new()
		mmi.name = "Cells_" + label
		mmi.multimesh = mm
		mmi.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_ON if shadows else GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
		mmi.visibility_range_end = 90.0
		add_child(mmi)


static var _rail_mesh: ArrayMesh


## One metre of iron railing (post at the start, top and mid rails) along +X.
static func railing_mesh() -> ArrayMesh:
	if _rail_mesh == null:
		var st := SurfaceTool.new()
		st.begin(Mesh.PRIMITIVE_TRIANGLES)
		var parts := [
			[Vector3(0.05, 1.0, 0.05), Vector3(0.0, 0.5, 0)],
			[Vector3(1.0, 0.05, 0.05), Vector3(0.5, 0.97, 0)],
			[Vector3(1.0, 0.035, 0.035), Vector3(0.5, 0.5, 0)],
		]
		for p: Array in parts:
			var b := BoxMesh.new()
			b.size = p[0]
			st.append_from(b, 0, Transform3D(Basis(), p[1]))
		st.generate_normals()
		_rail_mesh = st.commit()
		var m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
		m.albedo_color = Color(0.05, 0.05, 0.055)
		m.metallic = 0.8
		m.roughness = 0.4
		_rail_mesh.surface_set_material(0, m)
	return _rail_mesh


## Batched railings, one transform per metre segment.
func railings(xforms: Array[Transform3D]) -> void:
	multimesh_cells(railing_mesh(), xforms, false, "railing")


static func xform(at: Vector3, yaw: float, s: float) -> Transform3D:
	return Transform3D(Basis(Vector3.UP, yaw).scaled(Vector3(s, s, s)), at)


## A background inhabitant walking `points` (one point = standing).
func townsfolk(points: Array, coat: Color, accent: Color, skin: Color, hood := false, yaw := 180.0, look := {}) -> void:
	var t := Townsfolk.new()
	t.points = PackedVector3Array(points)
	t.coat = coat
	t.accent = accent
	t.skin = skin
	t.hood = hood
	t.yaw_deg = yaw
	t.look = look
	entities.add_child(t)


## Invisible cylinder collider standing on `base`.
func collider_cyl(base: Vector3, r: float, h: float) -> void:
	var b := _body()
	b.position = base + Vector3(0, h * 0.5, 0)
	var cs := CollisionShape3D.new()
	var shape := CylinderShape3D.new()
	shape.radius = r
	shape.height = h
	cs.shape = shape
	b.add_child(cs)
	add_child(b)


## Box by centre and size. solid adds a matching collider.
func box(center: Vector3, size: Vector3, m: Material, solid := true, yaw := 0.0, parent: Node3D = self) -> Node3D:
	var root: Node3D = _body() if solid else Node3D.new()
	root.position = center
	root.rotation.y = yaw
	parent.add_child(root)
	if m != null:
		var mesh := BoxMesh.new()
		mesh.size = size
		var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
		mi.mesh = mesh
		mi.material_override = m
		root.add_child(mi)
	if solid:
		var cs := CollisionShape3D.new()
		var s := BoxShape3D.new()
		s.size = size
		cs.shape = s
		root.add_child(cs)
	return root


## Box spanning min..max corners.
func slab(a: Vector3, b: Vector3, m: Material, solid := true) -> Node3D:
	var lo := Vector3(minf(a.x, b.x), minf(a.y, b.y), minf(a.z, b.z))
	var hi := Vector3(maxf(a.x, b.x), maxf(a.y, b.y), maxf(a.z, b.z))
	return box((lo + hi) * 0.5, hi - lo, m, solid)


func cyl(base: Vector3, r_top: float, r_bot: float, h: float, m: Material, solid := true, sides := 16) -> Node3D:
	var root: Node3D = _body() if solid else Node3D.new()
	root.position = base + Vector3(0, h * 0.5, 0)
	add_child(root)
	var mesh := CylinderMesh.new()
	mesh.top_radius = r_top
	mesh.bottom_radius = r_bot
	mesh.height = h
	mesh.radial_segments = sides
	mesh.rings = 1
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	mi.mesh = mesh
	mi.material_override = m
	root.add_child(mi)
	if solid:
		var cs := CollisionShape3D.new()
		var s := CylinderShape3D.new()
		s.radius = maxf(r_top, r_bot)
		s.height = h
		cs.shape = s
		root.add_child(cs)
	return root


func dome(center: Vector3, r: float, m: Material, squash := 1.0) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var mesh := SphereMesh.new()
	mesh.radius = r
	mesh.height = r
	mesh.is_hemisphere = true
	mesh.radial_segments = 24
	mesh.rings = 8
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	mi.mesh = mesh
	mi.material_override = m
	mi.position = center
	mi.scale = Vector3(1, squash, 1)
	add_child(mi)
	return mi


## Thin oriented box from a to b (walls, beams, rails). Height measured up from a.y.
func beam(a: Vector3, b: Vector3, h: float, t: float, m: Material, solid := false) -> Node3D:
	var d := b - a
	var flat := Vector3(d.x, 0, d.z)
	var n := box((a + b) * 0.5 + Vector3(0, h * 0.5, 0), Vector3(t, h, flat.length()), m, solid, atan2(d.x, d.z))
	return n


## Stairs: bottom front-centre `start`, climbing along `dir` by `rise` over `run`.
## Visible solid steps plus one smooth invisible ramp collider for traversal.
func stairs(start: Vector3, dir: Vector3, rise: float, run: float, width: float, m: Material) -> void:
	dir = dir.normalized()
	var n := maxi(2, int(round(absf(rise) / 0.2)))
	var step_h := rise / n
	var step_d := run / n
	var yaw := atan2(dir.x, dir.z)
	for i in n:
		var top := start.y + step_h * (i + 1)
		var c := start + dir * step_d * (i + 0.5)
		var depth := top - (start.y - 0.4)
		box(Vector3(c.x, top - depth * 0.5, c.z), Vector3(width, depth, step_d + 0.01), m, false, yaw)
	# ramp collider whose surface runs through the step nosings
	var fwd := (dir * run + Vector3.UP * rise).normalized()
	var side := dir.cross(Vector3.UP).normalized()
	var up := side.cross(fwd).normalized()
	var length := Vector3(run, rise, 0).length() + 0.6
	var thick := 0.3
	var body := _body()
	# surface runs exactly from the bottom edge to the top edge; extra length only below
	body.transform = Transform3D(Basis(side, up, -fwd), start + dir * run * 0.5 + Vector3.UP * rise * 0.5 - fwd * 0.3 - up * thick * 0.5)
	var cs := CollisionShape3D.new()
	var s := BoxShape3D.new()
	s.size = Vector3(width, thick, length)
	cs.shape = s
	body.add_child(cs)
	add_child(body)


## Railing from a to b (feet level): posts, top rail, and an invisible 1.3 m wall.
func railing(a: Vector3, b: Vector3, m: Material = null) -> void:
	if m == null:
		m = M.steel_dark
	var len := a.distance_to(b)
	var posts := maxi(1, int(len / 1.6))
	for i in posts + 1:
		var p := a.lerp(b, float(i) / posts)
		box(p + Vector3(0, 0.5, 0), Vector3(0.08, 1.0, 0.08), m, false)
	beam(a + Vector3(0, 0.95, 0), b + Vector3(0, 0.95, 0), 0.06, 0.07, m)
	beam(a + Vector3(0, 0.5, 0), b + Vector3(0, 0.5, 0), 0.04, 0.05, m)
	beam(a, b, 1.3, 0.12, null, true)


func lamp(at: Vector3, with_light := true, _h := 2.6) -> void:
	model("lantern_post", at)
	collider_cyl(at, 0.15, 3.0)
	if with_light:
		var l := OmniLight3D.new()
		l.position = at + Vector3(0, 3.0, 0)
		l.light_color = Color(1.0, 0.6, 0.28)
		l.light_energy = 2.4
		l.omni_range = 8.5
		l.omni_attenuation = 1.6
		_street(l)
		_fade(l)
		add_child(l)


func warm_light(at: Vector3, energy := 1.4, rng := 6.0) -> void:
	var l := OmniLight3D.new()
	l.position = at
	l.light_color = Color(1.0, 0.6, 0.3)
	l.light_energy = energy * 1.3
	l.omni_range = rng
	_street(l)
	_fade(l)
	add_child(l)


## Street lighting: lit from dusk to dawn and under heavy cloud (EnvironmentController).
static func _street(l: Light3D) -> void:
	l.set_meta(&"lit_energy", l.light_energy)
	l.add_to_group(&"lamp_lights")


## Lights far from the camera are switched off (the follow camera sits ~30 m back).
static func _fade(l: Light3D) -> void:
	l.distance_fade_enabled = true
	l.distance_fade_begin = 42.0
	l.distance_fade_length = 8.0


func tree(at: Vector3, s := 1.0) -> void:
	model("pine", at, fmod(at.x * 1.7 + at.z * 0.9, TAU), s * 0.9)


func sign_text(at: Vector3, text: String, yaw := 0.0, px := 48, col := Color(0.95, 0.8, 0.55)) -> Label3D:
	var l := Label3D.new()
	l.text = text
	l.position = at
	l.rotation.y = yaw
	l.pixel_size = 0.01
	l.font_size = px
	l.modulate = col
	l.outline_size = 8
	l.outline_modulate = Color(0.05, 0.05, 0.06)
	add_child(l)
	return l


# --- world ----------------------------------------------------------------------

func _environment() -> void:
	var env := Environment.new()
	env.background_mode = Environment.BG_COLOR
	env.background_color = Color(0.06, 0.08, 0.11)
	env.ambient_light_source = Environment.AMBIENT_SOURCE_COLOR
	env.ambient_light_color = Color(0.36, 0.42, 0.55)
	env.ambient_light_energy = 0.75
	# the cosmic sky (art/shaders/cosmic_sky.gdshader): seen in the arrival shot and in
	# every reflection; a small radiance map rebuilt only when its colours change
	var sky := Sky.new()
	var sm := ShaderMaterial.new()
	sm.shader = preload("res://art/shaders/cosmic_sky.gdshader")
	sky.sky_material = sm
	sky.radiance_size = Sky.RADIANCE_SIZE_64
	sky.process_mode = Sky.PROCESS_MODE_INCREMENTAL
	env.background_mode = Environment.BG_SKY
	env.sky = sky
	env.reflected_light_source = Environment.REFLECTION_SOURCE_SKY
	env.tonemap_mode = Environment.TONE_MAPPER_ACES
	env.tonemap_exposure = 1.45
	env.tonemap_white = 6.0
	env.glow_enabled = true
	env.glow_intensity = 0.85
	env.glow_strength = 1.1
	env.glow_bloom = 0.08
	env.glow_hdr_threshold = 0.85
	env.glow_blend_mode = Environment.GLOW_BLEND_MODE_SOFTLIGHT
	env.fog_enabled = true
	env.fog_light_color = Color(0.2, 0.25, 0.33)
	env.fog_light_energy = 1.0
	env.fog_density = 0.0055
	env.fog_sky_affect = 0.0
	env.adjustment_enabled = true
	env.adjustment_contrast = 1.08
	env.adjustment_saturation = 1.18
	var we := WorldEnvironment.new()
	we.environment = env
	add_child(we)
	# one shadowed light is the sun by day and the moon by night (EnvironmentController)
	var moon := DirectionalLight3D.new()
	moon.rotation_degrees = Vector3(-52, -30, 0)
	moon.light_color = Color(0.52, 0.62, 0.86)
	moon.light_energy = 0.7
	moon.shadow_enabled = true
	# two splits instead of four halves the shadow passes; the camera never sees past ~45 m
	moon.directional_shadow_mode = DirectionalLight3D.SHADOW_PARALLEL_2_SPLITS
	moon.directional_shadow_max_distance = 45.0
	moon.directional_shadow_fade_start = 0.85
	add_child(moon)
	# low warm light from the horizon, no shadows: dawn and golden-hour rims
	var glow := DirectionalLight3D.new()
	glow.rotation_degrees = Vector3(-12, -100, 0)
	glow.light_color = Color(1.0, 0.55, 0.35)
	glow.light_energy = 0.4
	glow.light_specular = 0.6
	add_child(glow)
	environment = EnvironmentController.new()
	environment.name = "EnvironmentController"
	environment.setup(env, moon, glow)
	environment.indoor = indoor
	add_child(environment)


## A once-baked reflection probe so wet ground and glass catch the lamps.
func reflection_probe(center: Vector3, size: Vector3) -> void:
	var p := ReflectionProbe.new()
	p.position = center
	p.size = size
	p.box_projection = true
	p.update_mode = ReflectionProbe.UPDATE_ONCE
	p.intensity = 0.8
	p.max_distance = 60.0
	add_child(p)


## Chain-link fence from a to b: posts, rails, a dark mesh panel, a 2 m invisible wall.
func fence(a: Vector3, b: Vector3, h := 1.8) -> void:
	var len := a.distance_to(b)
	var posts := maxi(1, int(len / 2.0))
	for i in posts + 1:
		var p := a.lerp(b, float(i) / posts)
		box(p + Vector3(0, h * 0.5, 0), Vector3(0.1, h, 0.1), M.steel_dark, false)
	beam(a + Vector3(0, h - 0.05, 0), b + Vector3(0, h - 0.05, 0), 0.06, 0.08, M.steel)
	beam(a + Vector3(0, 0.15, 0), b + Vector3(0, 0.15, 0), h - 0.25, 0.03, M.mesh_fence)
	beam(a, b, 2.0, 0.14, null, true)


## Invisible collider spanning min..max corners.
func blocker(a: Vector3, b: Vector3) -> StaticBody3D:
	return slab(a, b, null, true) as StaticBody3D


==============================================================================
== maps/settlement_kit.gd
==============================================================================
class_name SettlementKit
## The settlement art direction as reusable pieces (PROJECT_LOG entry 20): weathered
## ashlar, dusty-rose and ochre plaster, indigo slate, verdigris and aged brass, warm
## windows, striped canvas, climbing plants, and the quiet technology of an old
## spacefaring culture (armillary instruments, star-chart terminals, brass inlays).
## Every function builds into a MapBuilder3D `b`; plain primitives are merged by
## b.merge_static(), so a whole street costs a handful of draws.

const STONE_WALL := preload("res://art/shaders/stone_wall.gdshader")
const INLAY := preload("res://art/shaders/compass_inlay.gdshader")


## Adds the kit's materials to b.M (once per map).
static func materials(b: MapBuilder3D) -> void:
	if b.M.has("ashlar"):
		return
	var M: Dictionary = b.M
	M.ashlar = wall_mat(Color(0.54, 0.47, 0.45))
	M.ashlar_dark = wall_mat(Color(0.4, 0.35, 0.36))
	M.plaster_rose = _m(Color(0.66, 0.46, 0.44), 0.9)
	M.plaster_ochre = _m(Color(0.68, 0.53, 0.36), 0.9)
	M.plaster_teal = _m(Color(0.36, 0.5, 0.5), 0.9)
	M.slate = _m(Color(0.2, 0.19, 0.3), 0.7, 0.1)
	M.verdigris = _m(Color(0.3, 0.55, 0.5), 0.55, 0.35)
	M.brass = _m(Color(0.66, 0.48, 0.24), 0.35, 0.85)
	M.brass_dark = _m(Color(0.36, 0.25, 0.14), 0.45, 0.8)
	M.copper_pipe = _m(Color(0.52, 0.3, 0.2), 0.45, 0.7)
	M.wood_warm = _m(Color(0.34, 0.2, 0.13), 0.8)
	M.canvas_rose = _m(Color(0.56, 0.2, 0.24), 0.95)
	M.canvas_cream = _m(Color(0.82, 0.72, 0.6), 0.95)
	M.canvas_teal2 = _m(Color(0.18, 0.4, 0.42), 0.95)
	M.rug = _m(Color(0.45, 0.16, 0.18), 1.0)
	M.interior = _m(Color(0.16, 0.09, 0.07), 0.9, 0.0, Color(1.0, 0.55, 0.25), 0.55)
	M.window_warm = _m(Color(1.0, 0.75, 0.42), 0.3, 0.0, Color(1.0, 0.64, 0.3), 2.6)
	M.window_dark = _m(Color(0.1, 0.1, 0.16), 0.2, 0.3)
	M.lantern_glass = _m(Color(1.0, 0.82, 0.5), 0.3, 0.0, Color(1.0, 0.72, 0.36), 3.0)
	M.screen = _m(Color(0.2, 0.8, 0.85), 0.3, 0.0, Color(0.25, 0.85, 0.95), 2.4)
	M.core = _m(Color(1.0, 0.7, 0.45), 0.2, 0.0, Color(1.0, 0.62, 0.35), 5.0)
	M.leaf = _m(Color(0.2, 0.36, 0.2), 0.9)
	M.blossom = _m(Color(0.92, 0.9, 0.86), 0.8, 0.0, Color(0.9, 0.88, 0.85), 0.25)
	M.lavender = _m(Color(0.55, 0.4, 0.78), 0.85)
	M.pot = _m(Color(0.6, 0.36, 0.24), 0.8)
	b.M.stone = M.ashlar


static func wall_mat(c: Color) -> ShaderMaterial:
	var m := ShaderMaterial.new()
	m.shader = STONE_WALL
	m.set_shader_parameter("base_color", c)
	m.set_shader_parameter("noise_tex", MapBuilder3D.detail_textures()[0])
	return m


static func _m(c: Color, rough: float, metal := 0.0, emit := Color.BLACK, emit_e := 0.0) -> StandardMaterial3D:
	var m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	m.albedo_color = c
	m.roughness = rough
	m.metallic = metal
	if emit_e > 0.0:
		m.emission_enabled = true
		m.emission = emit
		m.emission_energy_multiplier = emit_e
	return m


## A primitive mesh placed with a transform, visual only.
static func mesh(b: Node3D, m: Mesh, mat: Material, x: Transform3D) -> MeshInstance3D:
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	mi.mesh = m
	mi.material_override = mat
	mi.transform = x
	b.add_child(mi)
	return mi


static func torus(r: float, t: float) -> TorusMesh:
	var tm := TorusMesh.new()
	tm.inner_radius = r - t
	tm.outer_radius = r + t
	tm.rings = 24
	tm.ring_segments = 6
	return tm


# --- landmarks ------------------------------------------------------------------

## The plaza's brass compass rose set into the paving (walkable, no collider).
static func compass_inlay(b: MapBuilder3D, at: Vector3, radius: float) -> void:
	var c := CylinderMesh.new()
	c.top_radius = radius
	c.bottom_radius = radius
	c.height = 0.02
	c.radial_segments = 40
	c.rings = 0
	var m := ShaderMaterial.new()
	m.shader = INLAY
	m.set_shader_parameter("radius", radius)
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	mi.mesh = c
	mi.material_override = m
	mi.position = at + Vector3(0, 0.012, 0)
	mi.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
	b.add_child(mi)


## An armillary sphere of aged brass on a fluted stone column; its rings turn slowly
## round a glowing core. `s` scales the whole instrument.
static func armillary(b: MapBuilder3D, at: Vector3, column_h := 2.4, s := 1.0) -> void:
	b.cyl(at, 0.55, 0.7, 0.35, b.M.ashlar)
	b.cyl(at + Vector3(0, 0.35, 0), 0.3, 0.36, column_h - 0.6, b.M.ashlar)
	b.cyl(at + Vector3(0, column_h - 0.25, 0), 0.5, 0.34, 0.25, b.M.brass, false, 12)
	var top := at + Vector3(0, column_h + 0.85 * s, 0)
	var spin := Spinner.new()
	spin.position = top
	b.add_child(spin)
	for k in 3:
		var r := (0.82 - k * 0.12) * s
		var ring := MeshInstance3D.new()
		ring.mesh = torus(r, 0.035 * s)
		ring.material_override = b.M.brass
		ring.rotation = [Vector3(PI * 0.5, 0, 0), Vector3(PI * 0.5, PI * 0.5, 0), Vector3(0.41, 0, 0)][k]
		spin.add_child(ring)
	var band := MeshInstance3D.new()
	band.mesh = torus(0.95 * s, 0.05 * s)
	band.material_override = b.M.brass_dark
	band.position = top
	band.rotation = Vector3(0.41, 0, 0.2)
	b.add_child(band)
	var core := SphereMesh.new()
	core.radius = 0.2 * s
	core.height = 0.4 * s
	core.radial_segments = 12
	core.rings = 6
	mesh(b, core, b.M.core, Transform3D(Basis(), top))
	b.warm_light(top + Vector3(0, 0.2, 0), 1.6, 7.0)


# --- architecture ---------------------------------------------------------------

## Stone balustrade from a to c (any slope): base course, pillars every ~1.8 m, a brass
## handrail, and a 1.3 m collider. Lantern caps on the end posts if `lanterns`.
static func balustrade(b: MapBuilder3D, a: Vector3, c: Vector3, lanterns := false) -> void:
	var d := c - a
	var flat := Vector2(d.x, d.z).length()
	var n := maxi(1, roundi(flat / 1.8))
	var yaw := atan2(d.x, d.z)
	for i in n + 1:
		var p := a.lerp(c, float(i) / n)
		b.box(p + Vector3(0, 0.5, 0), Vector3(0.34, 1.0, 0.34), b.M.ashlar, false, yaw)
		b.box(p + Vector3(0, 1.05, 0), Vector3(0.42, 0.1, 0.42), b.M.ashlar_dark, false, yaw)
		if lanterns and (i == 0 or i == n):
			lantern(b, p + Vector3(0, 1.1, 0))
	for i in n:
		var p := a.lerp(c, (i + 0.5) / n)
		var q0 := a.lerp(c, float(i) / n)
		var q1 := a.lerp(c, float(i + 1) / n)
		# balusters
		for k in 4:
			var bp := q0.lerp(q1, (k + 1.0) / 5.0)
			b.cyl(bp, 0.06, 0.08, 0.85, b.M.ashlar, false, 6)
		b.beam(q0 + Vector3(0, 0.85, 0), q1 + Vector3(0, 0.85, 0), 0.08, 0.2, b.M.ashlar_dark)
		b.beam(q0 + Vector3(0, 0.93, 0), q1 + Vector3(0, 0.93, 0), 0.05, 0.06, b.M.brass)
		b.beam(q0, q1, 0.12, 0.3, b.M.ashlar)
	b.beam(a, c, 1.3, 0.3, null, true)


## A small lantern: brass cage, warm glass, cap. Visual only.
static func lantern(b: MapBuilder3D, at: Vector3, s := 1.0) -> void:
	b.box(at + Vector3(0, 0.05, 0) * s, Vector3(0.22, 0.06, 0.22) * s, b.M.brass_dark, false)
	b.box(at + Vector3(0, 0.22, 0) * s, Vector3(0.17, 0.26, 0.17) * s, b.M.lantern_glass, false)
	var cap := PrismMesh.new()
	cap.size = Vector3(0.26, 0.14, 0.26) * s
	mesh(b, cap, b.M.brass_dark, Transform3D(Basis(), at + Vector3(0, 0.42, 0) * s))


## A lantern on an iron bracket out from a wall facing `out` (unit, horizontal).
static func wall_lantern(b: MapBuilder3D, at: Vector3, out: Vector3, light := true) -> void:
	b.beam(at, at + out * 0.45, 0.04, 0.04, b.M.brass_dark)
	lantern(b, at + out * 0.45 + Vector3(0, -0.45, 0))
	if light:
		b.warm_light(at + out * 0.6 + Vector3(0, -0.2, 0), 0.9, 5.0)


## One arcade bay on a wall face: stone pilasters, a voussoir ring, a warm shopfront
## recessed behind (door and lit windows), a striped awning and a lantern. `x` is the
## bay centre on a wall facing +Z at z = `face`, standing on `y`.
static func arcade_bay(b: MapBuilder3D, x: float, face: float, y: float, w: float, stripes: Array, lit := true) -> void:
	var open_w := w * 0.62
	var r := open_w * 0.5
	var spring := y + 1.75
	# shopfront panel behind the arch: dark warm interior with glowing windows
	b.box(Vector3(x, y + 1.2, face + 0.03), Vector3(open_w, 2.4, 0.06), b.M.interior, false)
	var disc := CylinderMesh.new()
	disc.top_radius = r
	disc.bottom_radius = r
	disc.height = 0.06
	disc.radial_segments = 16
	disc.rings = 0
	mesh(b, disc, b.M.interior, Transform3D(Basis(Vector3.RIGHT, PI * 0.5), Vector3(x, spring, face + 0.03)))
	b.box(Vector3(x - open_w * 0.22, y + 1.0, face + 0.07), Vector3(open_w * 0.28, 1.0, 0.04), b.M.window_warm if lit else b.M.window_dark, false)
	b.box(Vector3(x + open_w * 0.2, y + 0.95, face + 0.08), Vector3(open_w * 0.3, 1.9, 0.05), b.M.wood_warm, false)
	b.box(Vector3(x + open_w * 0.31, y + 0.95, face + 0.11), Vector3(0.05, 0.05, 0.04), b.M.brass, false)
	# arch ring and pilasters
	var ring := torus(r + 0.12, 0.13)
	mesh(b, ring, b.M.ashlar_dark, Transform3D(Basis(Vector3.RIGHT, PI * 0.5), Vector3(x, spring, face + 0.1)))
	b.box(Vector3(x, spring + r + 0.18, face + 0.14), Vector3(0.26, 0.34, 0.12), b.M.ashlar, false)
	for s: float in [-1.0, 1.0]:
		b.box(Vector3(x + s * w * 0.5, y + 1.5, face + 0.16), Vector3(0.36, 3.0, 0.32), b.M.ashlar, false)
	# striped awning sloping out over the shopfront
	var n := stripes.size() * 2
	for i in n:
		var sx := x - open_w * 0.6 + (i + 0.5) * open_w * 1.2 / n
		var strip := b.box(Vector3(sx, spring + 0.25, face + 0.6), Vector3(open_w * 1.2 / n + 0.01, 0.04, 1.1), stripes[i % stripes.size()], false)
		strip.rotation.x = 0.38
	if lit:
		wall_lantern(b, Vector3(x, spring + r + 0.5, face + 0.2), Vector3(0, 0, 1), false)


## A market stall facing +Z (turned by yaw): counter with goods, four poles, striped
## canopy, a rug in front, a hanging lantern. Solid counter.
static func stall(b: MapBuilder3D, at: Vector3, yaw: float, stripes: Array, goods := "wares") -> void:
	var basis := Basis(Vector3.UP, yaw)
	var P := func(v: Vector3) -> Vector3: return at + basis * v
	b.box(P.call(Vector3(0, 0.45, 0)), Vector3(2.6, 0.9, 1.0), b.M.wood_warm, true, yaw)
	b.box(P.call(Vector3(0, 0.92, 0.05)), Vector3(2.7, 0.06, 1.1), b.M.timber, false, yaw)
	for sx: float in [-1.25, 1.25]:
		for sz: float in [-0.6, 0.55]:
			b.cyl(P.call(Vector3(sx, 0, sz)), 0.045, 0.05, 2.4 if sz < 0 else 2.1, b.M.timber, false, 6)
	var n := stripes.size() * 3
	for i in n:
		var sx := -1.4 + (i + 0.5) * 2.8 / n
		var strip := b.box(P.call(Vector3(sx, 2.28, 0.0)), Vector3(2.8 / n + 0.01, 0.04, 1.6), stripes[i % stripes.size()], false, yaw)
		strip.rotation = Vector3(0.2, yaw, 0)
	b.box(P.call(Vector3(0, 0.01, 1.4)), Vector3(2.4, 0.02, 1.4), b.M.rug, false, yaw)
	lantern(b, P.call(Vector3(0.9, 1.75, 0.6)), 0.9)
	b.warm_light(P.call(Vector3(0.9, 1.9, 1.0)), 1.2, 5.0)
	match goods:
		"instruments":
			# a small armillary on the counter, brass dishes, a chart case
			for k in 2:
				var tr := MeshInstance3D.new()
				tr.mesh = torus(0.22, 0.015)
				tr.material_override = b.M.brass
				tr.position = P.call(Vector3(-0.7, 1.22, 0))
				tr.rotation = Vector3(PI * 0.5 * k, yaw + 0.6 * k, 0.3)
				b.add_child(tr)
			b.cyl(P.call(Vector3(-0.7, 0.95, 0)), 0.06, 0.09, 0.05, b.M.brass_dark, false, 8)
			b.cyl(P.call(Vector3(0.1, 0.95, 0.1)), 0.18, 0.18, 0.04, b.M.brass, false, 12)
			b.box(P.call(Vector3(0.6, 1.0, -0.15)), Vector3(0.5, 0.12, 0.35), b.M.wood_warm, false, yaw)
		_:
			for k in 4:
				b.cyl(P.call(Vector3(-1.0 + k * 0.6, 0.95, 0.1 * (k % 2))), 0.14, 0.1, 0.24, b.M.pot, false, 8)
			b.box(P.call(Vector3(0.9, 1.06, -0.2)), Vector3(0.5, 0.28, 0.4), b.M.timber, false, yaw + 0.2)
	# crates beside it
	b.box(P.call(Vector3(1.75, 0.3, -0.1)), Vector3(0.6, 0.6, 0.6), b.M.timber, true, yaw + 0.3)
	b.box(P.call(Vector3(1.7, 0.75, -0.1)), Vector3(0.45, 0.3, 0.45), b.M.wood_warm, false, yaw + 0.1)


## A terraced townhouse facing +Z: an ashlar ground floor with an arched door, plaster
## upper floors with arched windows (some lit), an optional balcony, and a roof
## ("pitched" slate, or "dome" of verdigris). Solid. Returns the door point.
static func townhouse(b: MapBuilder3D, base: Vector3, w: float, d: float, floors: int, plaster: Material, roof := "pitched", seed := 1) -> Vector3:
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = seed
	var fh := 2.6
	var h := fh * floors
	var front := base.z + d * 0.5
	b.box(base + Vector3(0, fh * 0.5, 0), Vector3(w, fh, d), b.M.ashlar)
	if floors > 1:
		b.box(base + Vector3(0, fh + (h - fh) * 0.5, 0), Vector3(w - 0.1, h - fh, d - 0.1), plaster)
	# string course between the floors
	for f in range(1, floors):
		b.box(base + Vector3(0, f * fh, d * 0.5 + 0.06), Vector3(w + 0.1, 0.14, 0.14), b.M.ashlar_dark, false)
	# door: timber in an arched stone surround
	var dx := -w * 0.25 + rng.randf_range(-0.3, 0.3)
	b.box(Vector3(base.x + dx, base.y + 1.05, front + 0.03), Vector3(1.1, 2.1, 0.06), b.M.wood_warm, false)
	var ring := torus(0.62, 0.1)
	mesh(b, ring, b.M.ashlar_dark, Transform3D(Basis(Vector3.RIGHT, PI * 0.5), Vector3(base.x + dx, base.y + 2.05, front + 0.06)))
	b.box(Vector3(base.x + dx + 0.38, base.y + 1.05, front + 0.08), Vector3(0.05, 0.05, 0.04), b.M.brass, false)
	b.box(Vector3(base.x + dx, base.y + 0.06, front + 0.4), Vector3(1.4, 0.12, 0.7), b.M.ashlar_dark, false)
	# ground-floor window
	_window(b, Vector3(base.x + w * 0.22, base.y + 1.3, front), 0.9, 1.2, rng.randf() < 0.75)
	# upper windows
	for f in range(1, floors):
		var count := maxi(1, int(w / 2.0))
		for i in count:
			var wx := base.x - w * 0.5 + (i + 0.5) * w / count
			var wy := base.y + f * fh + 1.2
			_window(b, Vector3(wx, wy, front), 0.8, 1.25, rng.randf() < 0.6)
			# window box with flowers
			if rng.randf() < 0.6:
				b.box(Vector3(wx, wy - 0.7, front + 0.18), Vector3(0.9, 0.18, 0.28), b.M.pot, false)
				b.box(Vector3(wx, wy - 0.55, front + 0.18), Vector3(0.8, 0.16, 0.2), b.M.lavender if rng.randf() < 0.5 else b.M.leaf, false)
	# balcony on the second floor
	if floors >= 3 and rng.randf() < 0.8:
		var by := base.y + fh * 2
		b.box(Vector3(base.x, by + 0.05, front + 0.55), Vector3(w * 0.6, 0.12, 1.1), b.M.ashlar_dark, false)
		b.beam(Vector3(base.x - w * 0.3, by, front + 1.08), Vector3(base.x + w * 0.3, by, front + 1.08), 0.95, 0.04, b.M.brass_dark)
		b.box(Vector3(base.x, by + 0.95, front + 1.08), Vector3(w * 0.6, 0.05, 0.08), b.M.brass, false)
	# roof
	var top := base.y + h
	if roof == "dome":
		b.box(Vector3(base.x, top + 0.2, base.z), Vector3(w + 0.2, 0.4, d + 0.2), b.M.ashlar_dark, false)
		var dm := b.dome(Vector3(base.x, top + 0.4, base.z), minf(w, d) * 0.42, b.M.verdigris, 1.0)
		dm.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_ON
		# ribs and a brass finial
		for k in 4:
			var rib := torus(minf(w, d) * 0.42, 0.04)
			var mi := mesh(b, rib, b.M.brass_dark, Transform3D(Basis(Vector3.UP, k * PI * 0.25) * Basis(Vector3.RIGHT, PI * 0.5), Vector3(base.x, top + 0.4, base.z)))
			mi.scale = Vector3(1, 1, 1)
		b.cyl(Vector3(base.x, top + 0.4 + minf(w, d) * 0.42, base.z), 0.03, 0.08, 0.7, b.M.brass, false, 6)
	else:
		var pr := PrismMesh.new()
		pr.size = Vector3(w + 0.5, 1.8, d + 0.4)
		# gable end to the street, slopes to the sides
		mesh(b, pr, b.M.slate, Transform3D(Basis(), Vector3(base.x, top + 0.9, base.z)))
		b.box(Vector3(base.x + w * 0.3, top + 1.3, base.z - d * 0.15), Vector3(0.5, 1.4, 0.5), b.M.ashlar, false)
	# copper downpipe
	b.box(Vector3(base.x + w * 0.5 - 0.15, base.y + h * 0.5, front + 0.12), Vector3(0.1, h, 0.1), b.M.copper_pipe, false)
	return Vector3(base.x + dx, base.y, front + 0.9)


static func _window(b: MapBuilder3D, c: Vector3, w: float, h: float, lit: bool) -> void:
	b.box(c + Vector3(0, 0, 0.03), Vector3(w, h, 0.05), b.M.window_warm if lit else b.M.window_dark, false)
	var ring := torus(w * 0.5, 0.06)
	mesh(b, ring, b.M.ashlar_dark, Transform3D(Basis(Vector3.RIGHT, PI * 0.5), c + Vector3(0, h * 0.5 - w * 0.1, 0.05)))
	b.box(c + Vector3(0, 0, 0.07), Vector3(0.04, h, 0.03), b.M.brass_dark, false)
	b.box(c + Vector3(0, -h * 0.5 - 0.05, 0.1), Vector3(w + 0.2, 0.08, 0.16), b.M.ashlar_dark, false)


## Things left by a front door: a pot of lavender, boots, a crate or a broom.
static func doorstep(b: MapBuilder3D, at: Vector3, seed: int) -> void:
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = seed
	pot(b, at + Vector3(-0.9, 0, -0.1), "lavender" if rng.randf() < 0.5 else "leaf")
	if rng.randf() < 0.6:
		for s: float in [-0.08, 0.08]:
			b.box(at + Vector3(0.75 + s, 0.09, -0.2), Vector3(0.1, 0.18, 0.24), b.M.wood_warm, false, 0.2)
	if rng.randf() < 0.5:
		b.box(at + Vector3(1.05, 0.22, -0.35), Vector3(0.45, 0.44, 0.4), b.M.timber, false, rng.randf())
	else:
		b.cyl(at + Vector3(1.0, 0, -0.45), 0.02, 0.02, 1.2, b.M.timber, false, 4)


## A clay pot with lavender or a leafy plant.
static func pot(b: MapBuilder3D, at: Vector3, kind := "lavender", s := 1.0) -> void:
	b.cyl(at, 0.24 * s, 0.17 * s, 0.4 * s, b.M.pot, false, 10)
	if kind == "lavender":
		for k in 5:
			var a := k * TAU / 5.0
			b.cyl(at + Vector3(cos(a) * 0.1, 0.4, sin(a) * 0.1) * s, 0.025 * s, 0.01 * s, 0.5 * s, b.M.lavender, false, 4)
	else:
		var sp := SphereMesh.new()
		sp.radius = 0.3 * s
		sp.height = 0.45 * s
		sp.radial_segments = 8
		sp.rings = 4
		mesh(b, sp, b.M.leaf, Transform3D(Basis(), at + Vector3(0, 0.55 * s, 0)))


## A bench of timber slats on stone feet, facing +Z turned by yaw. Solid.
static func bench(b: MapBuilder3D, at: Vector3, yaw: float) -> void:
	var bs := Basis(Vector3.UP, yaw)
	for sx: float in [-0.85, 0.85]:
		b.box(at + bs * Vector3(sx, 0.22, 0), Vector3(0.25, 0.44, 0.5), b.M.ashlar, false, yaw)
	for k in 3:
		b.box(at + bs * Vector3(0, 0.47, -0.16 + k * 0.16), Vector3(2.1, 0.05, 0.13), b.M.wood_warm, false, yaw)
	b.box(at + bs * Vector3(0, 0.8, -0.3), Vector3(2.1, 0.35, 0.06), b.M.wood_warm, false, yaw)
	b.box(at + Vector3(0, 0.4, 0), Vector3(2.2, 0.8, 0.6), null, true, yaw)


## A public terminal: a stone housing with a brass frame and a softly glowing star
## chart screen. Examinable.
static func terminal(b: MapBuilder3D, at: Vector3, yaw: float, dialogue_id: String) -> void:
	var bs := Basis(Vector3.UP, yaw)
	b.box(at + Vector3(0, 0.7, 0), Vector3(0.9, 1.4, 0.6), b.M.ashlar, true, yaw)
	b.box(at + bs * Vector3(0, 1.45, 0.05), Vector3(1.0, 0.12, 0.7), b.M.ashlar_dark, false, yaw)
	var scr := b.box(at + bs * Vector3(0, 1.05, 0.32), Vector3(0.66, 0.5, 0.04), b.M.screen, false, yaw)
	scr.rotation.x = -0.25
	b.box(at + bs * Vector3(0, 1.05, 0.3), Vector3(0.78, 0.62, 0.04), b.M.brass_dark, false, yaw)
	b.box(at + bs * Vector3(0, 0.62, 0.34), Vector3(0.6, 0.06, 0.18), b.M.brass, false, yaw)
	var l := OmniLight3D.new()
	l.position = at + bs * Vector3(0, 1.1, 0.7)
	l.light_color = Color(0.4, 0.85, 1.0)
	l.light_energy = 0.8
	l.omni_range = 3.0
	b.add_child(l)
	var it := Interactable3D.new()
	it.prompt = "Look"
	it.label = "Terminal"
	it.dialogue_id = dialogue_id
	it.radius = 1.3
	it.position = at + bs * Vector3(0, 0.9, 0.7)
	b.add_child(it)


## A string of small lights hanging between two points with a sag. One MultiMesh.
static func string_lights(b: MapBuilder3D, a: Vector3, c: Vector3, sag: float, count := 11) -> void:
	var xs: Array[Transform3D] = []
	var prev := a
	for i in count + 1:
		var t := float(i) / count
		var p := a.lerp(c, t) + Vector3(0, -sag * 4.0 * t * (1.0 - t), 0)
		if i > 0:
			b.beam(prev - Vector3(0, 0.01, 0), p - Vector3(0, 0.01, 0), 0.015, 0.015, b.M.brass_dark)
		if i > 0 and i < count:
			xs.append(Transform3D(Basis(), p + Vector3(0, -0.08, 0)))
		prev = p
	var sp := SphereMesh.new()
	sp.radius = 0.035
	sp.height = 0.07
	sp.radial_segments = 6
	sp.rings = 3
	sp.material = b.M.lantern_glass
	b.multimesh_cells(sp, xs, false, "string_lights")


## Climbing and trailing plants along a wall top from a to c, hanging down the face
## (facing `out`): leaf clusters and white blossom. Two MultiMeshes.
static func vines(b: MapBuilder3D, a: Vector3, c: Vector3, out: Vector3, drop: float, seed: int) -> void:
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = seed
	var leaves: Array[Transform3D] = []
	var blooms: Array[Transform3D] = []
	var n := int(a.distance_to(c) * 6.0)
	for i in n:
		var p := a.lerp(c, rng.randf()) + out * rng.randf_range(0.02, 0.2)
		var hang := rng.randf() * drop * rng.randf()
		var q := p + Vector3(0, -hang, 0)
		var s := rng.randf_range(0.12, 0.26)
		leaves.append(Transform3D(Basis(Vector3(rng.randf(), rng.randf(), rng.randf()).normalized(), rng.randf() * TAU).scaled(Vector3(s, s * 0.6, s)), q))
		if rng.randf() < 0.45:
			blooms.append(Transform3D(Basis().scaled(Vector3.ONE * rng.randf_range(0.5, 0.9)), q + out * 0.08))
	var leaf := SphereMesh.new()
	leaf.radius = 0.5
	leaf.height = 1.0
	leaf.radial_segments = 5
	leaf.rings = 3
	leaf.material = b.M.leaf
	b.multimesh_cells(leaf, leaves, false, "vine_leaves")
	var bloom := SphereMesh.new()
	bloom.radius = 0.05
	bloom.height = 0.1
	bloom.radial_segments = 5
	bloom.rings = 2
	bloom.material = b.M.blossom
	b.multimesh_cells(bloom, blooms, false, "vine_blooms")


# --- the wider world --------------------------------------------------------------

## The world beyond the settlement, far to the north: a plateau with gothic spires and
## lit windows, an aqueduct across the valley, a citadel on a far ridge. Unshaded
## silhouettes already hazed by distance, so fog and light cost nothing; beyond the
## gameplay camera's far plane, so only the arrival shot ever draws them.
static func skyline(b: MapBuilder3D, center: Vector3) -> void:
	var near_m := _flat(Color(0.12, 0.1, 0.17))
	var mid_m := _flat(Color(0.22, 0.18, 0.28))
	var far_m := _flat(Color(0.34, 0.27, 0.38))
	var lit := _flat(Color(1.0, 0.72, 0.4))
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 77
	var root := Node3D.new()
	root.name = "Skyline"
	b.add_child(root)
	# valley floor and the plateau edges
	_sb(root, center + Vector3(0, -30, -330), Vector3(1400, 2, 520), near_m)
	_sb(root, center + Vector3(-260, 0, -300), Vector3(300, 70, 120), mid_m)
	_sb(root, center + Vector3(240, 10, -420), Vector3(360, 90, 140), far_m)
	_sb(root, center + Vector3(-10, -10, -470), Vector3(900, 60, 80), far_m)
	# spires on the near plateau
	for i in 9:
		var x := -380.0 + rng.randf() * 260.0
		var h := rng.randf_range(30.0, 85.0)
		var p := center + Vector3(x, 35, -300 + rng.randf_range(-40, 40))
		_sb(root, p + Vector3(0, h * 0.5, 0), Vector3(rng.randf_range(6, 12), h, rng.randf_range(6, 12)), mid_m)
		_cone(root, p + Vector3(0, h, 0), rng.randf_range(3, 6), rng.randf_range(12, 26), mid_m)
		for k in 4:
			if rng.randf() < 0.7:
				_sb(root, p + Vector3(rng.randf_range(-2, 2), rng.randf_range(4, h - 4), 7), Vector3(1.2, 1.8, 0.5), lit)
	# the citadel on the far ridge, right
	for i in 7:
		var p := center + Vector3(180 + i * 22 + rng.randf_range(-6, 6), 55, -400)
		var h := rng.randf_range(20.0, 70.0) * (1.6 if i == 3 else 1.0)
		_sb(root, p + Vector3(0, h * 0.5, 0), Vector3(14, h, 14), far_m)
		_cone(root, p + Vector3(0, h, 0), 7, 22 if i == 3 else 12, far_m)
		_sb(root, p + Vector3(0, h * 0.6, 8), Vector3(2, 3, 0.5), lit)
	# the aqueduct across the valley
	var ay := 22.0
	for i in 16:
		var x := -120.0 + i * 18.0
		_sb(root, center + Vector3(x, ay * 0.5 - 30, -250), Vector3(4, ay + 30, 6), near_m)
	_sb(root, center + Vector3(15, ay, -250), Vector3(300, 5, 7), near_m)
	_sb(root, center + Vector3(15, ay - 8, -250), Vector3(300, 2, 6.5), near_m)
	# scattered lights of a far settlement in the valley
	for i in 40:
		_sb(root, center + Vector3(rng.randf_range(-300, 320), -26 + rng.randf() * 6, rng.randf_range(-420, -220)), Vector3(1.5, 1.5, 1.5), lit)


static func _flat(c: Color) -> StandardMaterial3D:
	var m := StandardMaterial3D.new()
	m.shading_mode = BaseMaterial3D.SHADING_MODE_UNSHADED
	m.albedo_color = c
	m.disable_fog = true
	return m


static func _sb(root: Node3D, c: Vector3, s: Vector3, m: Material) -> void:
	var bm := BoxMesh.new()
	bm.size = s
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	mi.mesh = bm
	mi.material_override = m
	mi.position = c
	mi.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
	root.add_child(mi)


static func _cone(root: Node3D, base: Vector3, r: float, h: float, m: Material) -> void:
	var cm := CylinderMesh.new()
	cm.top_radius = 0.0
	cm.bottom_radius = r
	cm.height = h
	cm.radial_segments = 6
	cm.rings = 0
	var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
	mi.mesh = cm
	mi.material_override = m
	mi.position = base + Vector3(0, h * 0.5, 0)
	mi.cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_OFF
	root.add_child(mi)


## Turns its children slowly about Y (the armillary's rings).
class Spinner:
	extends Node3D

	func _process(delta: float) -> void:
		rotate_y(delta * 0.12)
		rotate_object_local(Vector3.RIGHT, delta * 0.05)


==============================================================================
== maps/walk_mask.gd
==============================================================================
class_name WalkMask
extends Node2D
## Walkable area stored as a BitMap, for maps laid out from a painted plan
## rather than from collision bodies. `pixel_size` is world pixels per mask pixel.

@export var mask: BitMap
@export var pixel_size := 2.0


func is_walkable(world: Vector2) -> bool:
	if mask == null:
		return true
	var p := Vector2i(((world - global_position) / pixel_size).floor())
	var s := mask.get_size()
	if p.x < 0 or p.y < 0 or p.x >= s.x or p.y >= s.y:
		return false
	return mask.get_bitv(p)


func nearest_walkable(world: Vector2, max_radius: float = 240.0) -> Vector2:
	if is_walkable(world):
		return world
	var r := pixel_size
	while r <= max_radius:
		for i in 16:
			var p := world + Vector2.from_angle(TAU * i / 16.0) * r
			if is_walkable(p):
				return p
		r += pixel_size
	return world


==============================================================================
== maps/anjabi/district.gd
==============================================================================
extends MapBuilder3D
## Anjabi central district in 3D, built in code from reusable pieces (PROVISIONAL
## geometry and materials, to be replaced by authored meshes on the same footprints).
##
## Units are metres. +X east, -Z north, +Y up. Levels:
##   pods 6  ·  tavern terrace 3  ·  the Steps plaza 0  ·  mine approach -3  ·  beach -6  ·  sea -7
## Layout follows Greybox Study 01 around the Steps: tavern up and to the north-west,
## living pods further north over a ravine (reached by a raised walkway), shop to the
## east, the drop to the beach and sea south, the way to the mine going down west.

const PLAZA := 0.0
const TERRACE := 3.0
const PODS := 6.0
const MINE := -3.0
const BEACH := -6.0
const SEA := -7.0



## When true this is the detailed Steps insert inside the town map: only the plaza,
## the tavern terrace, their stairs, buildings, props and people are built.
@export var insert := false


func _ready() -> void:
	if insert:
		_materials()
		SettlementKit.materials(self)
		slab(Vector3(-12, -12, -10), Vector3(20, PLAZA, 10), M.paving)
		_plaza_feature()
		slab(Vector3(-24, -12, -28), Vector3(12, TERRACE, -10), M.stone)
		slab(Vector3(-24, TERRACE - 0.02, -28), Vector3(12, TERRACE + 0.01, -10), M.paving, false)
		stairs(Vector3(0, PLAZA, -4), Vector3(0, 0, -1), TERRACE - PLAZA, 6.0, 4.0, M.stone)
		SettlementKit.balustrade(self, Vector3(-11.8, TERRACE, -10.25), Vector3(-2.2, TERRACE, -10.25), true)
		SettlementKit.balustrade(self, Vector3(2.2, TERRACE, -10.25), Vector3(11.8, TERRACE, -10.25), true)
		_tavern()
		_shop()
		_props(true)
		_people()
		_insert_dressing()
		_market_quarter()
		return
	_materials()
	_environment()
	_ground()
	_stairs_and_walkways()
	_railings()
	_tavern()
	_shop()
	_pods()
	_mine_approach()
	_coast()
	_backdrop()
	_props()
	_people()


func _ground() -> void:
	# the Steps plaza
	slab(Vector3(-12, -12, -10), Vector3(20, PLAZA, 10), M.paving)
	_plaza_feature()
	# tavern terrace, retaining wall faces the plaza
	slab(Vector3(-24, -12, -28), Vector3(12, TERRACE, -10), M.stone)
	slab(Vector3(-24, TERRACE - 0.02, -28), Vector3(12, TERRACE + 0.01, -10), M.paving, false)
	# pod level beyond the ravine
	slab(Vector3(-20, -12, -50), Vector3(16, PODS, -34), M.stone)
	slab(Vector3(-20, PODS - 0.02, -50), Vector3(16, PODS + 0.01, -34), M.moss, false)
	# ravine floor
	slab(Vector3(-24, -12, -34), Vector3(16, -4, -28), M.rock_dark)
	# mine approach, a shelf below the plaza
	slab(Vector3(-44, -14, -8), Vector3(-12, MINE, 12), M.concrete)
	# beach
	slab(Vector3(-14, -14, 16), Vector3(40, BEACH, 36), M.sand)
	# cliff face below the plaza
	slab(Vector3(-12, -14, 10), Vector3(22, -1.5, 16), M.rock)


func _plaza_feature() -> void:
	if insert:
		# the compass rose of the Steps and the armillary that stands on its boss
		SettlementKit.compass_inlay(self, Vector3(2, 0, 1), 4.2)
		SettlementKit.armillary(self, Vector3(2, 0, 1), 2.3, 1.15)
		return
	var ring := CylinderMesh.new()
	ring.top_radius = 4.5
	ring.bottom_radius = 4.5
	ring.height = 0.04
	ring.radial_segments = 32
	ring.rings = 0
	var rm := MeshInstance3D.new()
	rm.mesh = ring
	rm.material_override = M.paving_light
	rm.position = Vector3(2, 0.02, 1)
	add_child(rm)
	cyl(Vector3(2, 0, 1), 1.2, 1.4, 0.5, M.stone, true, 12)  # plinth
	cyl(Vector3(2, 0.5, 1), 0.25, 0.3, 2.2, M.copper, true, 10)  # beacon column
	box(Vector3(2, 2.85, 1), Vector3(0.6, 0.3, 0.6), M.lamp, false)
	warm_light(Vector3(2, 3.2, 1), 1.8, 9.0)


func _stairs_and_walkways() -> void:
	# plaza -> tavern terrace (north)
	stairs(Vector3(0, PLAZA, -4), Vector3(0, 0, -1), TERRACE - PLAZA, 6.0, 4.0, M.stone)
	# terrace -> raised walkway (north), then the bridge over the ravine to the pods
	stairs(Vector3(8.5, TERRACE, -20), Vector3(0, 0, -1), PODS - TERRACE, 6.0, 3.0, M.stone)
	slab(Vector3(7, PODS - 0.4, -34.2), Vector3(10, PODS, -26.0), M.steel)
	for z: float in [-27.0, -30.5, -33.5]:
		box(Vector3(8.5, (PODS + -4) * 0.5 - 0.2, z), Vector3(0.5, PODS + 4 - 0.4, 0.5), M.steel_dark, false)
	# plaza -> mine approach (down to the west)
	stairs(Vector3(-18, MINE, 2), Vector3(1, 0, 0), PLAZA - MINE, 6.0, 4.0, M.stone)
	slab(Vector3(-18, -14, -8), Vector3(-12, MINE, 12), M.concrete)


func _railings() -> void:
	var P := PLAZA
	# plaza: south over the cliff, east end, west edge (gap for the mine stairs)
	railing(Vector3(-12, P, 9.8), Vector3(20, P, 9.8))
	railing(Vector3(19.8, P, -10), Vector3(19.8, P, 10))
	railing(Vector3(-11.8, P, -10), Vector3(-11.8, P, 0))
	railing(Vector3(-11.8, P, 4), Vector3(-11.8, P, 10))
	# terrace: south edge (gap for the plaza stairs), north edge, east edge
	var T := TERRACE
	railing(Vector3(-24, T, -10.2), Vector3(-2, T, -10.2))
	railing(Vector3(2, T, -10.2), Vector3(12, T, -10.2))
	railing(Vector3(-24, T, -27.8), Vector3(12, T, -27.8))
	railing(Vector3(11.8, T, -28), Vector3(11.8, T, -10))
	# raised stair sides and bridge
	railing(Vector3(7, T, -20), Vector3(7, PODS, -26))
	railing(Vector3(10, T, -20), Vector3(10, PODS, -26))
	railing(Vector3(7, PODS, -26), Vector3(7, PODS, -34))
	railing(Vector3(10, PODS, -26), Vector3(10, PODS, -34))
	# pods: south edge (gap for the bridge), east and west
	railing(Vector3(-20, PODS, -34.2), Vector3(7, PODS, -34.2))
	railing(Vector3(10, PODS, -34.2), Vector3(16, PODS, -34.2))
	railing(Vector3(15.8, PODS, -50), Vector3(15.8, PODS, -34))
	railing(Vector3(-19.8, PODS, -50), Vector3(-19.8, PODS, -34))
	# mine approach: south over the drop, north under the plaza wall
	railing(Vector3(-44, MINE, 11.8), Vector3(-12, MINE, 11.8))
	# west of the terrace: rock wall
	slab(Vector3(-30, -12, -28), Vector3(-24, 9, -10), M.rock)
	# walls the player must not walk off: plaza north beside stairs is the terrace face (solid)
	slab(Vector3(-44, MINE, -10), Vector3(-12, PLAZA + 0.0, -8), M.rock)


func _tavern() -> void:
	var c := Vector3(-10, TERRACE, -20.5)
	model("tavern_v2", c)
	collider_cyl(c, 6.2, 5.0)
	collider_cyl(c + Vector3(4.6, 0, -3.6), 1.95, 9.5)  # silo
	box(c + Vector3(0, 1.8, 6.1), Vector3(3.4, 3.6, 1.4), null, true)  # entrance bay
	sign_text(c + Vector3(0, 4.45, 7.08), "MAIN TAVERN", 0.0, 52, Color(0.98, 0.82, 0.5))
	warm_light(c + Vector3(0, 2.4, 7.6), 2.4, 9.0)
	warm_light(c + Vector3(2.6, 2.2, 8.4), 1.4, 6.0)
	lamp(c + Vector3(-3.4, 0, 8.6))
	lamp(c + Vector3(6.4, 0, 8.6), false)
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(1.6, 0, 8.4), Vector3(3.8, 0, 8.2)]:
		model("cafe_table", c + p, 0.4)
		collider_cyl(c + p, 0.5, 0.8)
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(-2.8, 0, 7.8), Vector3(-2.2, 0, 8.5)]:
		model("barrel", c + p)
		collider_cyl(c + p, 0.38, 1.0)
	model("planter", c + Vector3(-4.8, 0, 7.6))
	box(c + Vector3(-4.8, 0.3, 7.6), Vector3(1.2, 0.6, 0.6), null, true)
	for x: float in [-2.4, 2.4]:
		model("banner", c + Vector3(x, 0, 7.2), PI if x < 0 else 0.0)


func _shop() -> void:
	var c := Vector3(15, PLAZA, -5)
	model("shop", c)
	box(c + Vector3(0, 2.0, 0), Vector3(7, 4, 6), null)
	sign_text(c + Vector3(0, 3.35, 3.2), "SHOP", 0.0, 56)
	warm_light(c + Vector3(0, 2.0, 4.2), 1.6, 6.0)
	for i in 3:
		box(c + Vector3(-2.8 + i * 0.7, 0.35, 4.4), Vector3(0.6, 0.7, 0.6), M.timber, true)
	# the shop door: the first interior (maps/anjabi/interiors/shop.tscn)
	var door := MapExit3D.new()
	door.name = "ShopDoor"
	door.prompt = "Enter"
	door.label = "Shop"
	door.radius = 1.2
	door.target_map = "anjabi_shop"
	door.target_spawn = "default"
	door.position = c + Vector3(0, 0.9, 3.4)
	add_child(door)


func _pod(at: Vector3, tag := "", _lit := true) -> void:
	model("pod", at, PI if tag == "" and int(at.x) % 2 == 0 else 0.0)
	collider_cyl(at, 2.25, 3.4)
	if tag != "":
		sign_text(at + Vector3(0, 3.0, 2.3), tag, 0.0, 40)


func _pods() -> void:
	var y := PODS
	_pod(Vector3(4, y, -39.5), "B-17")
	_pod(Vector3(-3, y, -41), "", true)
	_pod(Vector3(-10, y, -39), "", false)
	_pod(Vector3(-15, y, -45.5), "", true)
	_pod(Vector3(11, y, -45), "", true)
	_pod(Vector3(2, y, -47), "", false)
	lamp(Vector3(6.6, y, -36))
	lamp(Vector3(-6.5, y, -36), false)
	# walkway paving from the bridge to B-17's door
	slab(Vector3(3.2, y, -38), Vector3(9.8, y + 0.03, -34.2), M.paving, false)
	# B-17 door interactable
	var door := Interactable3D.new()
	door.prompt = "Knock"
	door.label = "Pod B-17"
	door.dialogue_id = "anjabi/pod_b17"
	door.radius = 1.3
	door.position = Vector3(4, y + 0.9, -36.9)
	add_child(door)


func _mine_approach() -> void:
	var y := MINE
	# cliff wall with the mine mouth cut into it
	slab(Vector3(-56, -14, -12), Vector3(-44, 12, 16), M.rock)
	slab(Vector3(-44.01, y, -1.6), Vector3(-43.6, y + 3.6, 5.6), M.rock_dark, false)
	# timber headframe and steel door
	for z: float in [-1.4, 5.4]:
		box(Vector3(-43.4, y + 1.9, z), Vector3(0.4, 3.8, 0.4), M.timber, true)
	box(Vector3(-43.4, y + 3.9, 2), Vector3(0.5, 0.4, 7.4), M.timber, false)
	box(Vector3(-43.75, y + 1.6, 2), Vector3(0.12, 3.2, 6.2), M.steel, true)
	for z: float in [-1.0, 0.6, 2.2, 3.8, 5.0]:
		box(Vector3(-43.66, y + 1.6, z), Vector3(0.06, 3.2, 0.08), M.steel_dark, false)
	# warning stripes across the door
	for i in 6:
		box(Vector3(-43.64, y + 2.1, -0.6 + i * 1.1), Vector3(0.05, 0.3, 0.5), M.warning if i % 2 == 0 else M.steel_dark, false)
	sign_text(Vector3(-43.5, y + 4.5, 2), "NO. 2 WORKING", PI * 0.5, 44)
	# rails running into the mine
	for z: float in [1.3, 2.7]:
		beam(Vector3(-43.6, y, z), Vector3(-30, y, z), 0.08, 0.08, M.steel)
	for i in 14:
		box(Vector3(-43 + i, y + 0.02, 2), Vector3(0.25, 0.05, 2.0), M.timber, false)
	# fence and gate across the approach
	railing(Vector3(-38, y, -8), Vector3(-38, y, 0.0), M.steel)
	railing(Vector3(-38, y, 4.0), Vector3(-38, y, 12), M.steel)
	box(Vector3(-38, y + 1.0, 2), Vector3(0.12, 2.0, 4.0), M.steel_dark, true)
	for i in 5:
		box(Vector3(-37.92, y + 1.2, 0.4 + i * 0.8), Vector3(0.05, 0.25, 0.4), M.warning if i % 2 == 0 else M.steel_dark, false)
	sign_text(Vector3(-37.85, y + 2.4, 2), "DANGER  ·  NO ENTRY", PI * 0.5, 36, Color(0.95, 0.75, 0.3))
	# guard hut
	shed(Vector3(-33, y, -5), Vector3(3.0, 2.8, 2.6))
	warm_light(Vector3(-33, y + 2.0, -3.0), 1.2, 5.0)
	lamp(Vector3(-36.5, y, 5.5))
	lamp(Vector3(-24, y, 9.5))
	# crates and a winch
	box(Vector3(-28, y + 0.45, -6), Vector3(1.2, 0.9, 1.2), M.timber)
	box(Vector3(-26.7, y + 0.35, -6.2), Vector3(0.9, 0.7, 0.9), M.timber)
	cyl(Vector3(-30, y, 9), 0.6, 0.6, 1.2, M.copper_raw, true, 10)
	# notice and the way through to the mine yard
	var notice := Interactable3D.new()
	notice.prompt = "Read"
	notice.label = "Warning sign"
	notice.dialogue_id = "anjabi/mine_approach_sign"
	notice.position = Vector3(-37, y + 1.0, -1.2)
	add_child(notice)
	box(Vector3(-37.85, y + 1.3, -1.2), Vector3(0.05, 0.6, 0.8), M.concrete, false)
	var exit := MapExit3D.new()
	exit.prompt = "Enter"
	exit.label = "Mine yard"
	exit.radius = 1.4
	exit.target_map = "anjabi_mine"
	exit.target_spawn = "default"
	exit.position = Vector3(-36.6, y + 1.0, 2)
	add_child(exit)


func _coast() -> void:
	var sea := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var pm := PlaneMesh.new()
	pm.size = Vector2(500, 500)
	sea.mesh = pm
	sea.material_override = M.sea
	sea.position = Vector3(40, SEA, 80)
	add_child(sea)
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 4
	for i in 14:
		var p := Vector3(rng.randf_range(-6, 46), SEA - 0.5, rng.randf_range(30, 60))
		var s := rng.randf_range(1.0, 3.2)
		var r := box(p + Vector3(0, s * 0.4, 0), Vector3(s, s * 1.3, s * 0.9), M.rock, false, rng.randf() * TAU)
		r.rotation.x = rng.randf_range(-0.2, 0.2)
	# headland and the cliffs east of the cove
	slab(Vector3(-60, -14, 12), Vector3(-14, -2, 40), M.rock)
	slab(Vector3(40, -14, -20), Vector3(70, 4, 20), M.rock)
	# docks boardwalk east of the plaza, below it
	slab(Vector3(22, -1.4, -6), Vector3(40, -1.0, 6), M.timber, false)
	for x in range(23, 40, 3):
		for z: float in [-5.5, 5.5]:
			box(Vector3(x, -4.2, z), Vector3(0.3, 6.2, 0.3), M.timber, false)
	# Imperial Tower far to the south-east, and its tram line
	var t := Vector3(64, SEA, 82)
	cyl(t, 6, 8, 3, M.concrete, false, 16)
	cyl(t + Vector3(0, 3, 0), 3.2, 4.0, 44, M.steel_dark, false, 12)
	for i in 6:
		box(t + Vector3(0, 8 + i * 6.5, 3.6), Vector3(1.6, 0.4, 0.2), M.blue_light, false)
	cyl(t + Vector3(0, 47, 0), 4.6, 3.2, 3, M.steel, false, 12)
	box(t + Vector3(0, 51, 0), Vector3(1, 3, 1), M.blue_light, false)
	var top := Vector3(24, 9, -8)
	beam(top, t + Vector3(0, 40, 0), 0.12, 0.12, M.steel_dark)
	beam(top + Vector3(0, -0.6, 0), t + Vector3(0, 39.4, 0), 0.08, 0.08, M.steel_dark)
	for f: float in [0.3, 0.6]:
		var p := top.lerp(t + Vector3(0, 40, 0), f)
		box(Vector3(p.x, (p.y + SEA) * 0.5, p.z), Vector3(0.8, p.y - SEA, 0.8), M.steel_dark, false)
		box(p + Vector3(0, 0.3, 0), Vector3(0.4, 0.3, 0.4), M.blue_light, false)
	cyl(Vector3(24, -1.0, -8), 0.5, 0.7, 10.0, M.steel, false, 8)  # tram pylon at the docks


func _backdrop() -> void:
	# forested hill north of the pods, and the west ridge
	slab(Vector3(-70, -14, -90), Vector3(60, 12, -50), M.rock)
	slab(Vector3(-70, 12, -90), Vector3(60, 12.1, -52), M.moss, false)
	slab(Vector3(-70, -14, -50), Vector3(-20, 9, -28), M.rock)
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 11
	for i in 40:
		tree(Vector3(rng.randf_range(-60, 55), 12, rng.randf_range(-80, -53)), rng.randf_range(1.2, 2.0))
	for i in 10:
		tree(Vector3(rng.randf_range(-60, -24), 9, rng.randf_range(-48, -30)), rng.randf_range(1.0, 1.6))
	# watchtower silhouette north-east (guard tower direction)
	cyl(Vector3(30, 4, -46), 4, 5, 9, M.concrete, false, 8)
	cyl(Vector3(30, 13, -46), 4.6, 4.2, 2, M.panel_violet, false, 8)
	box(Vector3(30, 13.5, -41.7), Vector3(3, 0.8, 0.2), M.window, false)


func _props(core_only := false) -> void:
	# plaza lamps, benches, planters, crates
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(-8, 0, 8), Vector3(12, 0, 8), Vector3(-8, 0, -8), Vector3(8, 0, -3)]:
		if insert and p.z < 0.0:
			continue
		lamp(p)
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(-4, 0, 7), Vector3(8, 0, 7)]:
		if insert:
			SettlementKit.bench(self, p, PI)
		else:
			box(p + Vector3(0, 0.25, 0), Vector3(2.2, 0.5, 0.6), M.timber)
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(-10, 0, -6), Vector3(17, 0, 6)]:
		if insert and p.x < 0.0:
			continue
		box(p + Vector3(0, 0.4, 0), Vector3(1.8, 0.8, 1.8), M.stone)
		tree(p + Vector3(0, 0.8, 0), 0.7)
	box(Vector3(10.5, 0.4, 4), Vector3(0.9, 0.8, 0.9), M.timber)
	box(Vector3(11.3, 0.3, 4.6), Vector3(0.7, 0.6, 0.7), M.timber)
	cyl(Vector3(-6, 0, 3), 0.45, 0.45, 1.0, M.copper_raw, true, 10)
	# terrace lamps, planters
	lamp(Vector3(5, TERRACE, -12.5))
	lamp(Vector3(-20, TERRACE, -12.5), false)
	tree(Vector3(-21, TERRACE, -25), 1.0)
	if not insert:
		tree(Vector3(2, TERRACE, -25.5), 0.9)
	if core_only:
		return
	# coastal vegetation along the plaza's southern lip
	tree(Vector3(-10.8, -1.5, 13), 1.0)
	tree(Vector3(18, -1.5, 14), 1.2)


func _people() -> void:
	var keeper := Npc3D.new()
	keeper.display_name = "Tavern keeper"
	keeper.dialogue_id = "anjabi/tavern_keeper"
	keeper.coat = Color(0.4, 0.22, 0.2)
	keeper.accent = Color(0.85, 0.7, 0.4)
	keeper.skin = Color(0.6, 0.43, 0.33)
	keeper.position = Vector3(-7.2, TERRACE, -12.2)
	keeper.yaw_deg = 180.0
	keeper.look = {"apron": true, "satchel": false, "hair": "short", "hair_color": Color(0.35, 0.3, 0.27)}
	entities.add_child(keeper)


func _insert_dressing() -> void:
	# ferns and flowers along the terrace wall foot and plaza edges; clutter by the shop
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 12
	var ferns: Array[Transform3D] = []
	for i in 6:
		ferns.append(xform(Vector3(rng.randf_range(-2.3, -1.9) * (1 if i % 2 == 0 else -1), PLAZA, -4.0 + rng.randf() * 0.4), rng.randf() * TAU, rng.randf_range(0.8, 1.2)))
	for i in 14:
		ferns.append(xform(Vector3(rng.randf_range(-23.5, -17.0), TERRACE, -27.4 + rng.randf() * 0.8), rng.randf() * TAU, rng.randf_range(0.9, 1.4)))
	scatter("fern", ferns)
	var fl: Array[Transform3D] = []
	for i in 22:
		fl.append(xform(Vector3(rng.randf_range(-23.5, -17.0), TERRACE, -27.3 + rng.randf() * 0.7), rng.randf() * TAU, rng.randf_range(0.9, 1.3)))
	scatter("flowers", fl)
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(-11, PLAZA, 9.2), Vector3(18.5, PLAZA, 9.2)]:
		model("planter", p)
	model("crate_stack", Vector3(19, PLAZA, -1.6), 0.3)
	model("barrel", Vector3(18.6, PLAZA, -3.4))
	model("barrel", Vector3(19.3, PLAZA, -4.1))
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(-11, PLAZA, -9.0), Vector3(19, PLAZA, -9.0)]:
		model("banner", p)


## The market quarter of the Steps (entry 20): an arcade of shopfronts in the terrace
## wall, market stalls, townhouses along the top of the terrace, a garden with a sun
## instrument, a terminal, string lights, climbing plants and doorstep life.
## Paths kept clear: the stairs (x -2..2), the plaza's east and west exits, the terrace
## west exit (z -22..-18) and the walk in front of the tavern.
func _market_quarter() -> void:
	var K := SettlementKit
	var rose: Array = [M.canvas_rose, M.canvas_cream]
	var teal: Array = [M.canvas_teal2, M.canvas_cream]
	# arcade: three bays either side of the stairs in the terrace wall (face z = -10)
	var bays := [[-10.4, rose, true], [-7.2, teal, false], [-4.0, rose, true], [4.0, teal, true], [7.2, rose, false], [10.4, teal, true]]
	for bay: Array in bays:
		K.arcade_bay(self, bay[0], -10.0, PLAZA, 3.1, bay[1], bay[2])
	warm_light(Vector3(-8.8, 2.2, -8.6), 1.4, 6.0)
	warm_light(Vector3(8.8, 2.2, -8.6), 1.4, 6.0)
	# vines trailing from the terrace balustrade down over the arcade
	K.vines(self, Vector3(-11.8, TERRACE + 0.1, -9.95), Vector3(-2.4, TERRACE + 0.1, -9.95), Vector3(0, 0, 1), 1.6, 3)
	K.vines(self, Vector3(2.4, TERRACE + 0.1, -9.95), Vector3(11.8, TERRACE + 0.1, -9.95), Vector3(0, 0, 1), 1.6, 4)
	# stair side balustrades with lantern newels, lavender at the foot
	for sx: float in [-2.25, 2.25]:
		K.balustrade(self, Vector3(sx, PLAZA, -4.1), Vector3(sx, TERRACE, -9.9), true)
		K.pot(self, Vector3(sx * 1.25, PLAZA, -3.6), "lavender", 1.2)
	# market stalls in front of the arcade, out of the stairs' way
	K.stall(self, Vector3(-8.6, PLAZA, -6.9), 0.0, rose, "instruments")
	K.stall(self, Vector3(7.4, PLAZA, -7.0), 0.0, teal, "wares")
	K.pot(self, Vector3(-11.2, PLAZA, -8.6), "lavender", 1.3)
	K.pot(self, Vector3(11.0, PLAZA, -8.7), "leaf", 1.3)
	# the public terminal beside the foot of the stairs
	K.terminal(self, Vector3(3.6, PLAZA, -4.5), 0.0, "anjabi/public_terminal")
	# string lights from the balustrade to the plaza lamps
	K.string_lights(self, Vector3(-11.8, TERRACE + 1.15, -10.25), Vector3(-8, 3.0, 8), 0.9)
	K.string_lights(self, Vector3(11.8, TERRACE + 1.15, -10.25), Vector3(12, 3.0, 8), 0.9)
	# townhouses along the top of the terrace, east of the tavern, facing the Steps
	var d1 := K.townhouse(self, Vector3(1.2, TERRACE, -26.3), 5.4, 3.4, 2, M.plaster_rose, "pitched", 5)
	var d2 := K.townhouse(self, Vector3(7.6, TERRACE, -26.3), 6.8, 3.4, 3, M.plaster_ochre, "dome", 9)
	for i in 2:
		var d: Vector3 = [d1, d2][i]
		K.doorstep(self, d, 20 + i)
		var door := Interactable3D.new()
		door.prompt = "Knock"
		door.label = "Townhouse"
		door.dialogue_id = "anjabi/townhouse_door"
		door.radius = 1.1
		door.position = d + Vector3(0, 0.9, -0.4)
		add_child(door)
	K.wall_lantern(self, Vector3(1.2 - 2.7, TERRACE + 2.4, -24.55), Vector3(0, 0, 1))
	K.wall_lantern(self, Vector3(7.6 + 1.5, TERRACE + 2.4, -24.55), Vector3(0, 0, 1))
	K.vines(self, Vector3(-1.4, TERRACE + 5.2, -24.55), Vector3(3.8, TERRACE + 5.2, -24.55), Vector3(0, 0, 1), 2.6, 6)
	# west terrace garden: bench, planting and a brass sun instrument
	K.bench(self, Vector3(-20.5, TERRACE, -25.2), 0.0)
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(-23.3, TERRACE, -24.8), Vector3(-17.6, TERRACE, -25.0), Vector3(-23.2, TERRACE, -15.6)]:
		K.pot(self, p, "lavender" if p.z < -20 else "leaf", 1.3)
	K.armillary(self, Vector3(-19.2, TERRACE, -15.4), 1.0, 0.6)
	# people living here: two merchants at the stalls, two talking by the terminal
	townsfolk([to_global(Vector3(-8.6, PLAZA, -7.9))], Color(0.42, 0.2, 0.22), Color(0.7, 0.55, 0.3), Color(0.7, 0.52, 0.4), false, 180.0, {"hat": "brim", "satchel": false})
	townsfolk([to_global(Vector3(7.4, PLAZA, -8.0))], Color(0.2, 0.32, 0.34), Color(0.75, 0.6, 0.35), Color(0.55, 0.4, 0.3), false, 180.0, {"hair": "long", "apron": true, "satchel": false})
	townsfolk([to_global(Vector3(5.2, PLAZA, -3.6))], Color(0.36, 0.3, 0.44), Color(0.7, 0.52, 0.3), Color(0.76, 0.58, 0.46), false, 250.0, {"hair": "bun"})
	townsfolk([to_global(Vector3(6.4, PLAZA, -3.9))], Color(0.48, 0.4, 0.3), Color(0.5, 0.35, 0.2), Color(0.6, 0.45, 0.35), true, 110.0, {})
