==============================================================================
== maps/anjabi/mine_3d.gd
==============================================================================
extends MapBuilder3D
## Anjabi / Collapsed Mine (location 04) in 3D. Replaces the 2D mine.tscn with the
## same layout at 20 px = 1 m: stairs down from the Estate in the north-west (6 m
## drop), a fenced inner yard under the cliff holding the sealed mine door, an outer
## yard with wash-house and sheds, the old spur line and its washout to the east,
## the path east toward the airpad, and the cliff drop to the south.
## Units: metres, +X east, -Z north (2D y -> Z), ground at y 0.

const TOP := 6.0
const TRENCH_FLOOR := -2.5
## Washout: x range and z range of the cut in the ground.
const TR_X0 := 37.2
const TR_X1 := 44.8
const TR_Z0 := 15.0
const TR_Z1 := 17.0


func _ready() -> void:
	_materials()
	SettlementKit.materials(self)
	_environment()
	_ground()
	_walls_and_fences()
	_mine_door()
	_buildings()
	_rails_and_puzzle()
	_props()
	_people()
	_interactables()
	reflection_probe(Vector3(25, 5, 18), Vector3(44, 16, 36))
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 4
	var ferns: Array[Transform3D] = []
	for i in 60:
		var side := rng.randi() % 3
		var p := Vector3(rng.randf_range(10.5, 44.5), 0, 34.0 + rng.randf() * 0.4)
		if side == 1:
			p = Vector3(rng.randf_range(10.5, 21.0), 0, 5.6 + rng.randf() * 0.6)
		elif side == 2:
			p = Vector3(5.9 + rng.randf() * 0.4, 0, rng.randf_range(13, 33))
		ferns.append(xform(p, rng.randf() * TAU, rng.randf_range(0.8, 1.4)))
	scatter("fern", ferns)
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(14.6, 0, 29.6), Vector3(31.0, 0, 20.4), Vector3(9.0, 0, 24.0)]:
		model("barrel", p)
	model("crate_stack", Vector3(16.5, 0, 25.6), 0.4)


func _ground() -> void:
	# yard floor, with the washout cut out of it
	slab(Vector3(5.6, -1, 0), Vector3(45, 0, TR_Z0), M.gravel)
	slab(Vector3(5.6, -1, TR_Z1), Vector3(45, 0, 35.2), M.paving)
	slab(Vector3(5.6, -1, TR_Z0), Vector3(TR_X0, 0, TR_Z1), M.paving)
	slab(Vector3(TR_X1, -1, TR_Z0), Vector3(45, 0, TR_Z1), M.paving)
	# the washout itself
	slab(Vector3(TR_X0, TRENCH_FLOOR - 1, TR_Z0), Vector3(TR_X1, TRENCH_FLOOR, TR_Z1), M.rock_dark)
	slab(Vector3(TR_X0, TRENCH_FLOOR, TR_Z0 - 0.05), Vector3(TR_X1, 0, TR_Z0), M.rock, false)
	slab(Vector3(TR_X0, TRENCH_FLOOR, TR_Z1), Vector3(TR_X1, 0, TR_Z1 + 0.05), M.rock, false)
	var water := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var pm := PlaneMesh.new()
	pm.size = Vector2(TR_X1 - TR_X0, TR_Z1 - TR_Z0)
	water.mesh = pm
	water.material_override = M.sea
	water.position = Vector3((TR_X0 + TR_X1) * 0.5, TRENCH_FLOOR + 0.3, (TR_Z0 + TR_Z1) * 0.5)
	add_child(water)
	# mud paths: haul road, wash-house apron, path east, stair foot
	slab(Vector3(22.7, 0, 12.5), Vector3(25.3, 0.02, 34.5), M.mud, false)
	slab(Vector3(22.8, 0, 5.3), Vector3(25.2, 0.02, 12.5), M.mud, false)
	slab(Vector3(7.5, 0, 29.2), Vector3(22.6, 0.02, 31.2), M.mud, false)
	slab(Vector3(25.4, 0, 31.3), Vector3(45, 0.02, 33.1), M.mud, false)
	slab(Vector3(5.8, 0, 12.5), Vector3(22.6, 0.02, 14.6), M.mud, false)
	# the cliff drop beyond the south fence, and mist below
	slab(Vector3(0, -30, 35.2), Vector3(50, -0.5, 36.5), M.rock_dark)
	var mist := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var mp := PlaneMesh.new()
	mp.size = Vector2(300, 200)
	mist.mesh = mp
	mist.material_override = M.sea
	mist.position = Vector3(24, -24, 120)
	add_child(mist)


func _walls_and_fences() -> void:
	# rock: north cliff over the yard, west wall, the Estate ledge and stair cut
	slab(Vector3(-10, -1, -6), Vector3(60, 12, 0), M.rock)
	slab(Vector3(10, -1, 0), Vector3(60, 9, 5.3), M.rock)
	slab(Vector3(-10, -1, 0), Vector3(6.1, 10, 12.6), M.rock)
	slab(Vector3(-10, -1, 12.6), Vector3(5.6, 8, 36), M.rock)
	slab(Vector3(45, -1, 5.3), Vector3(60, 8, 36), M.rock)
	slab(Vector3(45, 8, 5.3), Vector3(60, 8.1, 36), M.moss, false)
	slab(Vector3(-10, 10, 0), Vector3(6.1, 10.1, 12.6), M.moss, false)
	# the Estate landing at the top of the stairs, then the stairs down
	slab(Vector3(6.1, -1, 0), Vector3(9.95, TOP, 2.0), M.stone)
	stairs(Vector3(8.0, 0, 12.6), Vector3(0, 0, -1), TOP, 10.6, 3.85, M.stone)
	slab(Vector3(9.95, -1, 2.0), Vector3(10.2, TOP + 1.0, 5.3), M.rock)
	blocker(Vector3(9.95, 0, 5.3), Vector3(10.05, TOP + 2.0, 12.6))
	# inner yard fence: west side, front with the gate, east to the corner
	fence(Vector3(10.1, 0, 5.3), Vector3(10.1, 0, 12.5))
	fence(Vector3(10.1, 0, 12.5), Vector3(22.5, 0, 12.5))
	fence(Vector3(25.5, 0, 12.5), Vector3(37.0, 0, 12.5))
	fence(Vector3(37.0, 0, 12.5), Vector3(37.0, 0, 15.0))
	# the gate: closed, solid
	box(Vector3(24, 1.0, 12.5), Vector3(3.0, 2.0, 0.14), M.steel_dark, true)
	for i in 6:
		box(Vector3(22.75 + i * 0.5, 1.15, 12.42), Vector3(0.5, 0.28, 0.04), M.warning if i % 2 == 0 else M.steel_dark, false)
	for x: float in [22.4, 25.6]:
		box(Vector3(x, 1.2, 12.5), Vector3(0.2, 2.4, 0.2), M.steel, false)
	# south fence at the cliff edge, east wall
	railing(Vector3(5.6, 0, 34.5), Vector3(45, 0, 34.5), M.timber)
	# washout: blockers so nobody walks into it; the middle one goes when bridged
	blocker(Vector3(36.9, 0, TR_Z0), Vector3(39.2, 2.0, TR_Z1))
	blocker(Vector3(40.8, 0, TR_Z0), Vector3(TR_X1, 2.0, TR_Z1))


func _mine_door() -> void:
	# timber frame set into the cliff face, steel door welded shut
	var z := 5.3
	slab(Vector3(21.5, 0, z - 0.3), Vector3(26.5, 4.4, z), M.rock_dark, false)
	# timber portal, barred gate and sign (director's key art) in front of the welded door
	model("mine_portal", Vector3(24, 0, z + 0.55))
	warm_light(Vector3(22.05, 3.5, z + 1.3), 1.0, 5.0)
	warm_light(Vector3(25.95, 3.5, z + 1.3), 1.0, 5.0)
	sign_text(Vector3(24, 5.75, z + 1.5), "ANJABI MINE", 0.0, 46, Color(0.9, 0.78, 0.55))
	sign_text(Vector3(24, 5.25, z + 1.5), "LEVELS I–IV", 0.0, 28, Color(0.85, 0.72, 0.5))
	sign_text(Vector3(24.75, 4.75, z + 1.62), "CLOSED", 0.0, 36, Color(1.0, 0.85, 0.75))
	box(Vector3(24, 1.8, z + 0.05), Vector3(4.0, 3.6, 0.12), M.steel, true)
	box(Vector3(24, 1.8, z + 0.12), Vector3(0.05, 3.6, 0.04), M.steel_dark, false)
	# the bright weld bead round the frame
	var weld := _mat(Color(0.9, 0.85, 0.7), 0.3, 0.8, Color(0.6, 0.55, 0.4), 0.4)
	box(Vector3(24, 3.62, z + 0.13), Vector3(4.0, 0.05, 0.03), weld, false)
	box(Vector3(24, 0.05, z + 0.13), Vector3(4.0, 0.05, 0.03), weld, false)
	for x: float in [22.02, 25.98]:
		box(Vector3(x, 1.8, z + 0.13), Vector3(0.05, 3.6, 0.03), weld, false)
	# stacked rescue stretchers, still wrapped
	var wrap := _mat(Color(0.82, 0.8, 0.72), 0.7)
	for i in 3:
		box(Vector3(26.9, 0.12 + i * 0.16, 6.2), Vector3(0.7, 0.14, 2.0), wrap, i == 0, 0.08 * i)


func _shed(center: Vector3, size: Vector3, _m: Material, lit := false, _door_side := 1.0) -> void:
	shed(center, size)
	if lit:
		warm_light(Vector3(center.x + size.x * 0.22, size.y * 0.6, center.z + size.z * 0.5 + 1.0), 1.3, 6.0)


func _buildings() -> void:
	_shed(Vector3(11.5, 0, 27.4), Vector3(6.0, 3.0, 3.6), M.panel, true)  # wash-house
	box(Vector3(11.5, 2.6, 30.0), Vector3(6.4, 0.08, 1.8), M.canvas_teal, false)  # awning
	for x: float in [8.6, 14.4]:
		box(Vector3(x, 1.3, 30.8), Vector3(0.08, 2.6, 0.08), M.steel_dark, false)
	_shed(Vector3(10.0, 0, 21.0), Vector3(3.0, 2.6, 3.0), M.panel_violet)  # store shed
	_shed(Vector3(32.0, 0, 22.4), Vector3(2.8, 2.6, 3.0), M.panel_violet)  # winch house
	cyl(Vector3(33.8, 0, 22.4), 0.5, 0.5, 1.0, M.rust, true, 10)
	_shed(Vector3(29.5, 0, 14.4), Vector3(2.5, 2.7, 2.6), M.panel, true)  # guard hut
	_shed(Vector3(12.5, 0, 9.0), Vector3(2.6, 2.6, 2.6), M.concrete)  # pump shed
	# junction box on the pump shed's east side, breaker pulled
	box(Vector3(13.86, 1.3, 10.0), Vector3(0.12, 0.7, 0.5), M.steel, false)
	box(Vector3(13.95, 1.35, 10.0), Vector3(0.05, 0.12, 0.2), M.warning, false)


func _rail_line(points: PackedVector3Array) -> void:
	for i in points.size() - 1:
		var a := points[i]
		var b := points[i + 1]
		var d := (b - a)
		var side := d.cross(Vector3.UP).normalized() * 0.35
		beam(a + side + Vector3(0, 0.02, 0), b + side + Vector3(0, 0.02, 0), 0.08, 0.07, M.steel)
		beam(a - side + Vector3(0, 0.02, 0), b - side + Vector3(0, 0.02, 0), 0.08, 0.07, M.steel)
		var n := int(a.distance_to(b) / 0.6)
		for j in n:
			var p := a.lerp(b, (j + 0.5) / n)
			box(p + Vector3(0, 0.02, 0), Vector3(1.1, 0.05, 0.22), M.timber, false, atan2(d.x, d.z))


func _rails_and_puzzle() -> void:
	var main := PackedVector3Array([Vector3(40, 0, 28.75), Vector3(40, 0, 22)])
	var a := PackedVector3Array([Vector3(40, 0, 22), Vector3(41.3, 0, 20.6), Vector3(43.4, 0, 19.2), Vector3(43.4, 0, 18.1)])
	var b := PackedVector3Array([Vector3(40, 0, 22), Vector3(40, 0, TR_Z1 + 0.1)])
	_rail_line(main)
	_rail_line(a)
	_rail_line(b)
	# rails still reach a little way out over the gap from the north side
	_rail_line(PackedVector3Array([Vector3(40, 0, TR_Z0 - 0.2), Vector3(40, 0, 12.6)]))
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(40, 0, 29.9), Vector3(43.4, 0, 17.4)]:
		box(p + Vector3(0, 0.35, 0), Vector3(1.2, 0.3, 0.25), M.canvas, true)  # buffers
	var gap := blocker(Vector3(39.2, 0, TR_Z0), Vector3(40.8, 2.0, TR_Z1))
	var lever := TrackSwitch3D.new()
	lever.name = "Lever"
	lever.position = Vector3(38.6, 0, 22.4)
	entities.add_child(lever)
	var cart := OreCart3D.new()
	cart.name = "OreCart"
	cart.rail_main = main
	cart.rail_a = a
	cart.rail_b = b
	cart.bridge_position = Vector3(40, 0, (TR_Z0 + TR_Z1) * 0.5)
	cart.gap_blocker = gap.get_child(0) as CollisionShape3D
	cart.dialogue_buffer = "anjabi/cart_buffer"
	cart.dialogue_bridge = "anjabi/cart_bridge"
	cart.on_bridged = [{"objective": "quiet_mine.way_in"}, {"reveal": "quiet_mine.door"}]
	entities.add_child(cart)


func _props() -> void:
	lamp(Vector3(21.9, 0, 13.4))  # gate
	lamp(Vector3(18.5, 0, 29.8))  # wash-house
	lamp(Vector3(10.6, 0, 13.6))  # stair foot
	lamp(Vector3(19.6, 0, 8.8), false)  # yard lamp, dark since the power cut
	lamp(Vector3(42.0, 0, 31.0))  # path east
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(28.0, 0, 29.7), Vector3(15.0, 0, 23.2), Vector3(33.0, 0, 7.2)]:
		box(p + Vector3(0, 0.4, 0), Vector3(1.1, 0.8, 1.1), M.timber)
		box(p + Vector3(0.9, 0.3, 0.2), Vector3(0.7, 0.6, 0.7), M.timber)
	# puddles
	var water := _mat(Color(0.2, 0.27, 0.33), 0.05, 0.4)
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(23.6, 0, 23.5), Vector3(24.6, 0, 18), Vector3(23.5, 0, 31), Vector3(32, 0, 32.2),
			Vector3(16.5, 0, 30.3), Vector3(15, 0, 8.5), Vector3(32, 0, 10), Vector3(41, 0, 13.5), Vector3(8, 0, 13.6)]:
		var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
		var cm := CylinderMesh.new()
		cm.top_radius = 0.6
		cm.bottom_radius = 0.6
		cm.height = 0.02
		cm.radial_segments = 16
		cm.rings = 0
		mi.mesh = cm
		mi.material_override = water
		mi.position = p + Vector3(0, 0.03, 0)
		mi.scale = Vector3(1.0, 1.0, 0.6)
		add_child(mi)
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(7.5, 0, 21), Vector3(6.8, 0, 33), Vector3(30, 0, 20.2), Vector3(35, 0, 19.2),
			Vector3(36, 0, 29.8), Vector3(42.8, 0, 30.3), Vector3(46, 8, 7.5), Vector3(46.5, 8, 11.2),
			Vector3(46, 8, 15), Vector3(47.2, 8, 19), Vector3(46, 8, 22.6), Vector3(47, 8, 26.2),
			Vector3(46, 8, 30), Vector3(47.2, 8, 33.6), Vector3(2, 10, 4), Vector3(3, 10, 9), Vector3(1, 8, 20), Vector3(2.5, 8, 28)]:
		tree(p, 1.1 if p.y == 0 else 1.4)
		if p.y == 0:
			blocker(p + Vector3(-0.2, 0, -0.2), p + Vector3(0.2, 1.5, 0.2))


func _npc(n: String, d: String, at: Vector3, coat: Color, accent: Color, skin: Color, hood := false, look := {}) -> void:
	var npc := Npc3D.new()
	npc.name = n.replace(" ", "")
	npc.display_name = n
	npc.dialogue_id = d
	npc.coat = coat
	npc.accent = accent
	npc.skin = skin
	npc.hood = hood
	npc.position = at
	npc.yaw_deg = 180.0
	npc.look = look
	entities.add_child(npc)


func _people() -> void:
	_npc("Driller", "anjabi/driller", Vector3(16.8, 0, 30.0), Color(0.42, 0.33, 0.27), Color(0.8, 0.5, 0.18), Color(0.62, 0.45, 0.36), false,
			{"hat": "hardhat", "satchel": false, "hair_color": Color(0.2, 0.18, 0.16)})
	_npc("Gate guard", "anjabi/guard", Vector3(26.4, 0, 13.3), Color(0.24, 0.3, 0.36), Color(0.75, 0.75, 0.7), Color(0.8, 0.64, 0.52), true,
			{"satchel": false, "long_coat": true})
	# two miners at the portal, as in the key art
	townsfolk([Vector3(21.6, 0, 7.4)], Color(0.4, 0.36, 0.28), Color(0.7, 0.55, 0.2), Color(0.66, 0.5, 0.4), false, 120.0,
			{"hat": "hardhat", "satchel": false})
	townsfolk([Vector3(23.0, 0, 8.1)], Color(0.3, 0.33, 0.36), Color(0.6, 0.6, 0.55), Color(0.5, 0.36, 0.27), false, 250.0,
			{"hat": "hardhat", "satchel": false})


func _look(n: String, prompt: String, label: String, dialogue: String, at: Vector3, enabled_if := {}) -> Interactable3D:
	var it := Interactable3D.new()
	it.name = n
	it.prompt = prompt
	it.label = label
	it.dialogue_id = dialogue
	it.enabled_if = enabled_if
	it.position = at
	add_child(it)
	return it


func _interactables() -> void:
	# the closure notice, cable-tied to the fence by the gate
	box(Vector3(21.25, 1.2, 12.42), Vector3(0.7, 0.5, 0.03), M.concrete, false)
	_look("Notice", "Read", "Notice", "anjabi/notice", Vector3(21.25, 1.0, 12.9))
	_look("MineDoor", "Examine", "Mine door", "anjabi/door", Vector3(24, 1.0, 5.9))
	_look("JunctionBox", "Examine", "Junction box", "anjabi/junction", Vector3(14.3, 1.0, 10.0))
	_look("Trench", "Look", "Washout", "anjabi/trench", Vector3(40, 0.6, 17.6), {"not": {"flag": "anjabi.cart", "eq": "trench"}})
	_look("CliffEdge", "Look", "Cliff edge", "anjabi/cliff_edge", Vector3(24, 1.0, 34.1))
	var up := MapExit3D.new()
	up.name = "StairsToSteps"
	up.prompt = "Climb"
	up.label = "Stairs up to the Steps"
	up.target_map = "anjabi_town"
	up.target_spawn = "mine"
	up.position = Vector3(8, TOP + 0.8, 0.6)
	add_child(up)
	var east := MapExit3D.new()
	east.name = "PathToAirpad"
	east.prompt = "Walk"
	east.label = "Path to the airpad"
	east.target_map = "anjabi_town"
	east.target_spawn = "airpad"
	east.position = Vector3(44.4, 0.8, 32.2)
	add_child(east)

== maps/anjabi/mine_ground.gd
==============================================================================
@tool
extends Node2D
## PROVISIONAL ART. Ground painting for Anjabi / Collapsed Mine (location 04), drawn once.
## Layout follows Greybox Study 01: stairs down from the Estate (north-west), a walled
## yard on a cliff ledge, a path east toward the airpad, and a drop to the south.

func _draw() -> void:
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 7
	var earth := Color(0.17, 0.22, 0.2)
	var gravel := Color(0.27, 0.29, 0.3)
	var mud := Color(0.29, 0.26, 0.23)
	var rock := Color(0.17, 0.2, 0.22)
	draw_rect(Rect2(0, 0, 960, 704), earth)
	draw_rect(Rect2(900, 0, 60, 704), Color(0.08, 0.14, 0.13))
	draw_rect(Rect2(0, 0, 112, 704), rock)
	# inner yard and the corridor behind the washout
	draw_rect(Rect2(200, 106, 700, 144), gravel)
	draw_rect(Rect2(742, 250, 158, 50), gravel)
	draw_rect(Rect2(456, 106, 48, 144), mud.darkened(0.12))
	# stairs down from the Estate
	var stone := Color(0.4, 0.4, 0.38)
	draw_rect(Rect2(122, 0, 76, 252), stone)
	for i in 38:
		draw_line(Vector2(122, 16 + i * 6), Vector2(198, 16 + i * 6), stone.darkened(0.3), 1.0)
	draw_rect(Rect2(122, 0, 2, 252), stone.darkened(0.45))
	draw_rect(Rect2(196, 0, 2, 252), stone.darkened(0.45))
	# landing, path to the gate, haul road, wash-house apron, path east to the airpad
	draw_rect(Rect2(116, 250, 90, 44), mud)
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([Vector2(200, 258), Vector2(454, 254), Vector2(454, 292), Vector2(200, 294)]), mud)
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([Vector2(450, 690), Vector2(510, 690), Vector2(506, 250), Vector2(454, 250)]), mud)
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([Vector2(150, 596), Vector2(452, 584), Vector2(452, 620), Vector2(150, 628)]), mud.darkened(0.08))
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([Vector2(508, 626), Vector2(900, 630), Vector2(900, 662), Vector2(508, 658)]), mud.darkened(0.05))
	draw_rect(Rect2(762, 340, 126, 46), Color(0.21, 0.25, 0.23))
	for x: float in [466.0, 494.0]:
		draw_line(Vector2(x, 690), Vector2(x, 250), mud.darkened(0.22), 2.0)
	# the drop to the south: rock lip, then mist
	draw_rect(Rect2(0, 690, 960, 14), rock.darkened(0.25))
	for i in 5:
		draw_rect(Rect2(0, 694 + i * 2, 960, 2), Color(0.45, 0.52, 0.58, 0.1 + i * 0.07))
	# puddles
	for p: Vector3 in [Vector3(472, 470, 9), Vector3(492, 360, 7), Vector3(470, 620, 11), Vector3(640, 644, 8),
			Vector3(330, 606, 7), Vector3(300, 170, 10), Vector3(640, 200, 8), Vector3(820, 270, 6),
			Vector3(480, 180, 9), Vector3(160, 272, 8)]:
		draw_set_transform(Vector2(p.x, p.y), 0.0, Vector2(1.0, 0.4))
		draw_circle(Vector2.ZERO, p.z, Color(0.33, 0.42, 0.5, 0.85))
		draw_circle(Vector2(-p.z * 0.3, -p.z * 0.2), p.z * 0.35, Color(0.5, 0.6, 0.68, 0.5))
	draw_set_transform(Vector2.ZERO, 0.0, Vector2.ONE)
	# gravel flecks, weeds, rock cracks
	for i in 900:
		var p := Vector2(rng.randf_range(114, 898), rng.randf_range(108, 688))
		if p.x < 200 and p.y < 252:
			continue
		var on_road := p.x > 448 and p.x < 512
		if p.y < 250 or (p.x > 742 and p.y < 300):
			draw_rect(Rect2(p, Vector2(1, 1)), gravel.lightened(rng.randf_range(-0.15, 0.2)))
		elif not on_road:
			var c := earth.lightened(rng.randf_range(0.02, 0.14))
			draw_line(p, p + Vector2(rng.randf_range(-1, 1), -rng.randf_range(2, 4)), c, 1.0)
	for i in 60:
		var p := Vector2(rng.randf_range(4, 104), rng.randf_range(4, 700))
		draw_line(p, p + Vector2(rng.randf_range(-6, 6), rng.randf_range(6, 18)), rock.lightened(0.08), 1.0)

== maps/anjabi/town.gd
==============================================================================
extends GameMap
## Anjabi, whole settlement, greybox. Ground art and walkable area both come from
## docs/reference/anjabi_greybox_01.png (see tools/build_town_mask.gd).
## Announces the district the player is standing in.

## Image-pixel rectangles on the greybox; first match wins.
const DISTRICTS := [
	["THE STEPS", Rect2(820, 370, 125, 75)],
	["TRAM STATION", Rect2(940, 440, 180, 210)],
	["SHOPS", Rect2(890, 220, 170, 160)],
	["MAIN TAVERN", Rect2(670, 190, 215, 175)],
	["TION TOWER", Rect2(425, 300, 180, 175)],
	["GUARD TOWER", Rect2(930, 40, 210, 165)],
	["WORKER PODS", Rect2(440, 40, 325, 205)],
	["ESTATE GARDENS", Rect2(95, 320, 215, 100)],
	["ESTATE", Rect2(120, 420, 290, 165)],
	["COLLAPSED MINE", Rect2(60, 630, 250, 180)],
	["PRIVATE AIRPAD", Rect2(320, 735, 200, 130)],
	["BEACH", Rect2(500, 560, 430, 175)],
	["DOCKS AND BOARDWALK", Rect2(1060, 360, 320, 215)],
	["RAINFOREST APPROACH", Rect2(1255, 220, 165, 150)],
	["FOREST", Rect2(40, 40, 330, 215)],
]

var _district := ""
var _acc := 0.0

@onready var _mask: WalkMask = $WalkMask


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	_acc += delta
	if _acc < 0.25 or player == null:
		return
	_acc = 0.0
	var p := (player.global_position - _mask.global_position) / _mask.pixel_size
	var found := ""
	for d: Array in DISTRICTS:
		if (d[1] as Rect2).has_point(p):
			found = d[0]
			break
	if found != _district:
		_district = found
		if found != "":
			GameState.location_entered.emit("ANJABI  ·  " + found)

==============================================================================
== maps/anjabi/town_3d.gd
==============================================================================
extends MapBuilder3D
## Anjabi, the whole settlement, in 3D. Terrain, cliffs, water and colliders come
## from maps/anjabi/town_3d_cells.res (built from Greybox Study 01 by
## tools/build_town_3d.gd): one cell per metre, walkway heights interpolated between
## the districts so the paths climb and fall between levels. Landmark buildings are
## placed on their greybox footprints. Announces the district the player is in.
## Greybox pixel p maps to world (p - ORIGIN) * PX metres.

const CELLS := "res://maps/anjabi/town_3d_cells.res"
const ORIGIN := Vector2(34, 34)
const PX := 0.12
const SEA := -7.0
const CHUNK := 16
const WATER := 0
const PATH := 1
const GRASS := 2
const SAND := 3
const SLAB := 4
const CLIFF := 5
const INSERT := 6
## Where the detailed Steps insert (district.tscn) sits; matches tools/build_town_3d.gd.
const INSERT_AT := Vector3(101.52, 0, 45.72)
const INSERT_SCENE := preload("res://maps/anjabi/district.tscn")

## Greybox-pixel rectangles; first match wins (same as the 2D town).
const DISTRICTS := [
	["THE STEPS", Rect2(820, 370, 125, 75)],
	["TRAM STATION", Rect2(940, 440, 180, 210)],
	["SHOPS", Rect2(890, 220, 170, 160)],
	["MAIN TAVERN", Rect2(670, 190, 215, 175)],
	["TION TOWER", Rect2(425, 300, 180, 175)],
	["GUARD TOWER", Rect2(930, 40, 210, 165)],
	["WORKER PODS", Rect2(440, 40, 325, 205)],
	["ESTATE GARDENS", Rect2(95, 320, 215, 100)],
	["ESTATE", Rect2(120, 420, 290, 165)],
	["COLLAPSED MINE", Rect2(60, 630, 250, 180)],
	["PRIVATE AIRPAD", Rect2(320, 735, 200, 130)],
	["BEACH", Rect2(500, 560, 430, 175)],
	["DOCKS AND BOARDWALK", Rect2(1060, 360, 320, 215)],
	["RAINFOREST APPROACH", Rect2(1255, 220, 165, 150)],
	["FOREST", Rect2(40, 40, 330, 215)],
]
## Greybox-pixel rectangle drawn as timber boardwalk.
const DOCKS := Rect2(1040, 360, 360, 215)
## Spawn points and exits, in greybox pixels (the 2D town's positions / 2).
const SPAWNS := {"default": Vector2(880, 415), "mine": Vector2(212, 676), "airpad": Vector2(336, 746)}

var w := 0
var h := 0
var cls := PackedByteArray()
var hv := PackedFloat32Array()
var hc := PackedFloat32Array()
var _district := ""
var _acc := 0.0


func _ready() -> void:
	_load_cells()
	_materials()
	SettlementKit.materials(self)
	_environment()
	SettlementKit.skyline(self, INSERT_AT)
	_terrain()
	_collision()
	_sea()
	_landmarks()
	_vegetation_and_lamps()
	_spawns_and_exits()
	reflection_probe(INSERT_AT + Vector3(-2, 6, -8), Vector3(70, 24, 60))
	_dressing()


# --- grid ------------------------------------------------------------------------

func _load_cells() -> void:
	var img: Image = load(CELLS)
	w = img.get_width()
	h = img.get_height()
	cls.resize(w * h)
	hv.resize(w * h)
	hc.resize(w * h)
	for z in h:
		for x in w:
			var c := img.get_pixel(x, z)
			var i := z * w + x
			hv[i] = c.r
			cls[i] = int(round(c.g))
			hc[i] = c.b


static func walkable(t: int) -> bool:
	return t == PATH or t == GRASS or t == SAND or t == INSERT


## World position of a greybox pixel, on the ground.
func at_px(p: Vector2) -> Vector3:
	var m := (p - ORIGIN) * PX
	return Vector3(m.x, ground(m.x, m.y), m.y)


func px_of(world: Vector3) -> Vector2:
	return Vector2(world.x, world.z) / PX + ORIGIN


## Visual height of the cell under (x, z).
func ground(x: float, z: float) -> float:
	var cx := clampi(int(x), 0, w - 1)
	var cz := clampi(int(z), 0, h - 1)
	return hv[cz * w + cx]


func nearest_walk(p: Vector2) -> Vector3:
	var m := (p - ORIGIN) * PX
	var c := Vector2i(int(m.x), int(m.y))
	for r in 12:
		for dz in range(-r, r + 1):
			for dx in range(-r, r + 1):
				if maxi(absi(dx), absi(dz)) != r:
					continue
				var q := c + Vector2i(dx, dz)
				if q.x < 0 or q.y < 0 or q.x >= w or q.y >= h:
					continue
				var i := q.y * w + q.x
				if walkable(cls[i]):
					return Vector3(q.x + 0.5, hv[i], q.y + 0.5)
	return Vector3(m.x, ground(m.x, m.y), m.y)


## Vertex heights: a vertex touching any walkable cell takes their mean, so walk
## surfaces stay exactly where the collider puts them; walls rise inside their cells.
func _vertex(x: int, z: int, collide: bool) -> float:
	var ws := 0.0
	var wn := 0
	var os := 0.0
	var on := 0
	var top := -INF
	for dz in [-1, 0]:
		for dx in [-1, 0]:
			var cx: int = x + dx
			var cz: int = z + dz
			if cx < 0 or cz < 0 or cx >= w or cz >= h:
				continue
			var i := cz * w + cx
			if cls[i] == INSERT:
				if collide:
					top = maxf(top, -30.0)
					continue
				os += hv[i] - 0.5
				on += 1
			elif walkable(cls[i]):
				ws += hv[i]
				wn += 1
			elif collide:
				top = maxf(top, hc[i])
			elif cls[i] != WATER:
				os += hv[i]
				on += 1
			else:
				os += SEA - 2.5
				on += 1
	if wn > 0:
		return ws / wn
	if collide:
		return top if top > -INF else 0.0
	return os / on if on > 0 else SEA - 2.5


func _terrain() -> void:
	var vh := PackedFloat32Array()
	vh.resize((w + 1) * (h + 1))
	for z in h + 1:
		for x in w + 1:
			vh[z * (w + 1) + x] = _vertex(x, z, false)
	var mat := ground_mat(Color.WHITE, 1.0, 0.8, 0.5, true)
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 21
	for cz0 in range(0, h, CHUNK):
		for cx0 in range(0, w, CHUNK):
			var st := SurfaceTool.new()
			st.begin(Mesh.PRIMITIVE_TRIANGLES)
			st.set_smooth_group(0)
			var any := false
			for z in range(cz0, mini(cz0 + CHUNK, h)):
				for x in range(cx0, mini(cx0 + CHUNK, w)):
					var i := z * w + x
					var t := cls[i]
					if t == INSERT:
						continue
					any = true
					var col := _colour(t, rng.randf())
					if t == PATH and hv[i] < -0.3 and DOCKS.has_point(Vector2(x + 0.5, z + 0.5) / PX + ORIGIN):
						col = Color(0.5, 0.36, 0.24, 0.5)
					var a := Vector3(x, vh[z * (w + 1) + x], z)
					var b := Vector3(x + 1, vh[z * (w + 1) + x + 1], z)
					var c := Vector3(x + 1, vh[(z + 1) * (w + 1) + x + 1], z + 1)
					var d := Vector3(x, vh[(z + 1) * (w + 1) + x], z + 1)
					_quad(st, a, b, c, d, col)
			if not any:
				continue
			st.index()
			st.generate_normals()
			var mi := MeshInstance3D.new()
			mi.mesh = st.commit()
			mi.material_override = mat
			add_child(mi)


func _quad(st: SurfaceTool, a: Vector3, b: Vector3, c: Vector3, d: Vector3, col: Color) -> void:
	st.set_color(col)
	st.add_vertex(a)
	st.add_vertex(b)
	st.add_vertex(c)
	st.add_vertex(a)
	st.add_vertex(c)
	st.add_vertex(d)


func _colour(t: int, n: float) -> Color:
	# rgb: sRGB colour; a: cobble amount for the wet-ground shader
	var j := (n - 0.5) * (0.04 if t == PATH or t == SLAB else 0.012)
	match t:
		# the settlement palette: rose-grey paving, teal-green grass, rose sand,
		# violet-grey rock
		PATH:
			return Color(0.64 + j, 0.56 + j, 0.56 + j, 1.0)
		GRASS:
			return Color(0.22 + j, 0.35 + j * 1.5, 0.3 + j, 0.0)
		WATER:
			return Color(0.3, 0.27, 0.28, 0.0)
		SAND:
			return Color(0.72 + j, 0.64 + j, 0.56 + j, 0.0)
		SLAB:
			return Color(0.62 + j, 0.56 + j, 0.56 + j, 1.0)
	return Color(0.38 + j, 0.33 + j, 0.38 + j, 0.0)


func _collision() -> void:
	var data := PackedFloat32Array()
	data.resize((w + 1) * (h + 1))
	for z in h + 1:
		for x in w + 1:
			data[z * (w + 1) + x] = _vertex(x, z, true)
	var shape := HeightMapShape3D.new()
	shape.map_width = w + 1
	shape.map_depth = h + 1
	shape.map_data = data
	var body := _body()
	var cs := CollisionShape3D.new()
	cs.shape = shape
	cs.position = Vector3(w * 0.5, 0, h * 0.5)
	body.add_child(cs)
	add_child(body)
	# frame the map so nothing walks off its edge
	blocker(Vector3(-2, -10, -2), Vector3(0, 20, h + 2))
	blocker(Vector3(w, -10, -2), Vector3(w + 2, 20, h + 2))
	blocker(Vector3(-2, -10, -2), Vector3(w + 2, 20, 0))
	blocker(Vector3(-2, -10, h), Vector3(w + 2, 20, h + 2))


func _sea() -> void:
	var sea := MeshInstance3D.new()
	var pm := PlaneMesh.new()
	pm.size = Vector2(600, 500)
	sea.mesh = pm
	sea.material_override = M.sea
	sea.position = Vector3(w * 0.5, SEA + 0.4, h * 0.5)
	add_child(sea)


# --- buildings ---------------------------------------------------------------------

func _pod(p: Vector2, tag := "", _lit := true) -> void:
	var at := at_px(p)
	model("quonset", at)
	if tag != "":
		sign_text(at + Vector3(1.35, 2.15, 3.68), tag, 0.0, 40)


func _shed_px(a: Vector2, b: Vector2, h_m: float) -> void:
	var lo := at_px(Vector2(minf(a.x, b.x), minf(a.y, b.y)))
	var hi := at_px(Vector2(maxf(a.x, b.x), maxf(a.y, b.y)))
	var base := Vector3((lo.x + hi.x) * 0.5, minf(lo.y, hi.y), (lo.z + hi.z) * 0.5)
	shed(base, Vector3(hi.x - lo.x, h_m, hi.z - lo.z), 0.0, false)


func _block(a: Vector2, b: Vector2, h_m: float, m: Material, roof: Material = null, lit := true) -> void:
	var lo := at_px(Vector2(minf(a.x, b.x), minf(a.y, b.y)))
	var hi := at_px(Vector2(maxf(a.x, b.x), maxf(a.y, b.y)))
	var base := minf(lo.y, hi.y)
	var size := Vector3(hi.x - lo.x, h_m, hi.z - lo.z)
	var c := Vector3((lo.x + hi.x) * 0.5, base + h_m * 0.5, (lo.z + hi.z) * 0.5)
	box(c, size, m, false)
	box(c + Vector3(0, h_m * 0.5 + 0.12, 0), size + Vector3(0.5, 0.24, 0.5), roof if roof != null else M.steel_dark, false)
	if lit:
		for i in maxi(1, int(size.x / 4.0)):
			box(Vector3(lo.x + 2.0 + i * 4.0, base + h_m * 0.55, hi.z + 0.04), Vector3(1.2, 0.8, 0.08), M.window, false)


func _landmarks() -> void:
	# 08 Main Tavern and 12 Shop are part of the Steps insert
	var steps := INSERT_SCENE.instantiate()
	steps.set("insert", true)
	steps.position = INSERT_AT
	add_child(steps)
	# 07 Tion Tower
	var tion := at_px(Vector2(515, 390))
	model("tion_tower", tion)
	# 10 Guard Tower
	var gt := at_px(Vector2(1035, 118))
	model("guard_tower", gt)
	warm_light(gt + Vector3(0, 13.5, 7.0), 2.0, 10.0)
	# 09 Living pods
	_pod(Vector2(530, 68), "", true)
	_pod(Vector2(622, 72), "", false)
	_pod(Vector2(508, 128), "", true)
	_pod(Vector2(660, 132), "B-17", true)
	_pod(Vector2(546, 182), "", true)
	for b: Array in [[Vector2(480, 95), Vector2(505, 118)], [Vector2(690, 75), Vector2(730, 108)], [Vector2(718, 112), Vector2(745, 135)],
			[Vector2(592, 272), Vector2(622, 296)]]:
		_shed_px(b[0], b[1], 3.0)
	# 03 Estate and its walls
	model("estate", at_px(Vector2(240, 480)), 0.0, 0.85)
	# 04 Mine yard: headframe over the slab
	var mine := at_px(Vector2(215, 700))
	model("headframe", mine + Vector3(0, 0, -6))
	_shed_px(Vector2(150, 760), Vector2(205, 790), 3.0)
	_shed_px(Vector2(255, 640), Vector2(300, 668), 3.0)
	# 05 Private airpad: pad ring and a parked craft
	var pad := at_px(Vector2(382, 782))
	cyl(pad + Vector3(0, 0.02, 0), 3.6, 3.6, 0.05, M.warning, false, 24)
	cyl(pad + Vector3(0, 0.04, 0), 3.2, 3.2, 0.05, M.concrete, false, 24)
	var craft := at_px(Vector2(450, 790))
	box(craft + Vector3(0, 1.2, 0), Vector3(3.0, 1.6, 7.0), M.steel, false)
	box(craft + Vector3(0, 1.4, 0), Vector3(9.0, 0.25, 2.4), M.steel_dark, false)
	box(craft + Vector3(0, 2.1, -2.2), Vector3(2.0, 0.6, 1.6), M.blue_light, false)
	# 13 Tram / subway station, angled toward the docks
	var tram := at_px(Vector2(1030, 505))
	model("tram_station", tram, deg_to_rad(-33), 0.8)
	warm_light(tram + Vector3(-3, 3, 6), 1.6, 8.0)
	# tram line north-south, and 16 Imperial Tower
	var north := at_px(Vector2(1160, 10)) + Vector3(0, 0, 0)
	var tower := at_px(Vector2(1195, 880))
	north.y = 9.0
	var deck := tower + Vector3(0, 0, 0)
	deck.y = 9.0
	beam(north, deck, 0.15, 0.15, M.steel_dark)
	beam(north + Vector3(0.6, -0.4, 0), deck + Vector3(0.6, -0.4, 0), 0.1, 0.1, M.steel_dark)
	var along := deck - north
	for f: float in [0.18, 0.47, 0.76]:
		var g := north.lerp(deck, f) + Vector3(0.3, -2.7, 0)
		model("gondola", g, atan2(along.x, along.z) + PI * 0.5)
	for f in 7:
		var p := north.lerp(deck, (f + 0.5) / 7.0)
		for o: Vector3 in [Vector3(-0.45, 0, -0.45), Vector3(0.45, 0, 0.45), Vector3(-0.45, 0, 0.45), Vector3(0.45, 0, -0.45)]:
			box(Vector3(p.x, (p.y + SEA) * 0.5, p.z) + o, Vector3(0.14, p.y - SEA, 0.14), M.steel_dark, false)
		for k in 6:
			var yk := SEA + (p.y - SEA) * (k + 0.5) / 6.0
			box(Vector3(p.x, yk, p.z), Vector3(1.0, 0.08, 1.0), M.steel_dark, false, PI * 0.25 * (k % 2))
		box(p + Vector3(0, 0.8, 0), Vector3(0.4, 0.3, 0.4), M.blue_light, false)
	var it := Vector3(tower.x, SEA, tower.z)
	model("imperial_tower", it)
	# docks: posts and crates on the boardwalk
	for p: Vector2 in [Vector2(1080, 380), Vector2(1150, 380), Vector2(1240, 400), Vector2(1300, 470), Vector2(1250, 520)]:
		box(at_px(p) + Vector3(0, 0.45, 0), Vector3(1.0, 0.9, 1.0), M.timber, true)


func _vegetation_and_lamps() -> void:
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 33
	var lamps := 0
	for z in range(1, h - 1):
		for x in range(1, w - 1):
			var i := z * w + x
			var t := cls[i]
			var r := rng.randf()
			var forest := Rect2(40, 40, 330, 560).has_point(Vector2(x + 0.5, z + 0.5) / PX + ORIGIN) or z < 8
			if t == CLIFF and r < (0.16 if forest else 0.05) and hv[i] > -2.0 and cls[i - 1] == CLIFF and cls[i + 1] == CLIFF \
					and cls[i - w] == CLIFF and cls[i + w] == CLIFF and _clear_of_paths(x, z, 2):
				tree(Vector3(x + rng.randf(), hv[i], z + rng.randf()), rng.randf_range(0.8, 1.4))
			elif t == GRASS and r < 0.012:
				var clear := true
				for n: int in [i - 1, i + 1, i - w, i + w, i - 2, i + 2, i - 2 * w, i + 2 * w]:
					if n < 0 or n >= w * h or cls[n] != GRASS:
						clear = false
				if clear:
					var p := Vector3(x + 0.5, hv[i], z + 0.5)
					tree(p, rng.randf_range(0.9, 1.4))
					blocker(p + Vector3(-0.25, 0, -0.25), p + Vector3(0.25, 1.6, 0.25))

	# lamps: evenly spaced along path edges (against a wall), every other one lit
	var placed: Array[Vector3] = []
	for z in range(1, h - 1):
		for x in range(1, w - 1):
			var i := z * w + x
			if cls[i] != PATH or placed.size() >= 90:
				continue
			var edge := false
			for n: int in [i - 1, i + 1, i - w, i + w]:
				if cls[n] == CLIFF or cls[n] == SLAB:
					edge = true
			if not edge:
				continue
			var p := Vector3(x + 0.5, hv[i], z + 0.5)
			var near := false
			for q in placed:
				if q.distance_squared_to(p) < 100.0:
					near = true
					break
			if not near:
				placed.append(p)
				lamp(p, placed.size() % 2 == 0)


## True when no walkable cell lies within r cells (keeps trees off paths and boardwalks).
func _clear_of_paths(x: int, z: int, r: int) -> bool:
	for dz in range(-r, r + 1):
		for dx in range(-r, r + 1):
			var cx := clampi(x + dx, 0, w - 1)
			var cz := clampi(z + dz, 0, h - 1)
			var t := cls[cz * w + cx]
			if t == PATH or t == SAND or t == INSERT:
				return false
	return true


func _spawns_and_exits() -> void:
	var spawns := Node3D.new()
	spawns.name = "Spawns"
	add_child(spawns)
	for k: String in SPAWNS:
		var m := Marker3D.new()
		m.name = k
		m.position = nearest_walk(SPAWNS[k]) + Vector3(0, 0.1, 0)
		if k == "default":
			m.position = INSERT_AT + Vector3(2, 0.05, 5)
		spawns.add_child(m)
	# back out of the shop, on the step in front of its door
	var shop := Marker3D.new()
	shop.name = "shop"
	shop.position = INSERT_AT + Vector3(15, 0.05, -0.6)
	spawns.add_child(shop)
	var door := Interactable3D.new()
	door.name = "PodB17Door"
	door.prompt = "Knock"
	door.label = "Pod B-17"
	door.dialogue_id = "anjabi/pod_b17"
	door.radius = 1.4
	door.position = at_px(Vector2(660, 132)) + Vector3(0, 0.9, 4.2)
	add_child(door)
	var sign := Interactable3D.new()
	sign.name = "MineSign"
	sign.prompt = "Read"
	sign.label = "Warning sign"
	sign.dialogue_id = "anjabi/mine_approach_sign"
	sign.position = nearest_walk(Vector2(232, 676)) + Vector3(0, 0.9, 0)
	add_child(sign)
	box(sign.position + Vector3(0, 0.2, -0.4), Vector3(0.8, 0.6, 0.06), M.concrete, false)
	_exit("ToMineRoad", "Enter", "Mine yard", Vector2(212, 692), "anjabi_mine", "default")
	_exit("ToMineFromAirpad", "Walk", "Mine yard", Vector2(322, 745), "anjabi_mine", "east")


func _exit(n: String, prompt: String, label: String, p: Vector2, target: String, spawn: String) -> void:
	var e := MapExit3D.new()
	e.name = n
	e.prompt = prompt
	e.label = label
	e.radius = 1.6
	e.target_map = target
	e.target_spawn = spawn
	e.position = nearest_walk(p) + Vector3(0, 0.8, 0)
	add_child(e)


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	_acc += delta
	if _acc < 0.25 or player == null:
		return
	_acc = 0.0
	var p := px_of(player.global_position)
	var found := ""
	for d: Array in DISTRICTS:
		if (d[1] as Rect2).has_point(p):
			found = d[0]
			break
	if found != _district:
		_district = found
		if found != "":
			GameState.location_entered.emit("ANJABI  ·  " + found)


## Pass 3 set dressing: ferns on grass by paths and cliff feet, clutter at porches,
## shop and docks, and townsfolk going about the Steps, docks and pods.
func _dressing() -> void:
	var rng := RandomNumberGenerator.new()
	rng.seed = 77
	var ferns: Array[Transform3D] = []
	for z in range(1, h - 1):
		for x in range(1, w - 1):
			var i := z * w + x
			if cls[i] != GRASS:
				continue
			var edge := false
			for nb: int in [i - 1, i + 1, i - w, i + w]:
				if cls[nb] == PATH or cls[nb] == CLIFF or cls[nb] == SLAB:
					edge = true
			if rng.randf() < (0.55 if edge else 0.12):
				for k in 2:
					var p := Vector3(x + rng.randf(), hv[i], z + rng.randf())
					ferns.append(xform(p, rng.randf() * TAU, rng.randf_range(0.8, 1.5)))
	scatter("fern", ferns)
	# iron railings where a walkway or boardwalk drops to the water
	var rails: Array[Transform3D] = []
	for z in range(1, h - 1):
		for x in range(1, w - 1):
			var i := z * w + x
			if not (cls[i] == PATH or cls[i] == SAND) or hv[i] < SEA + 1.5:
				continue
			var y := hv[i]
			if cls[i - w] == WATER:
				rails.append(Transform3D(Basis(), Vector3(x, y, z + 0.05)))
			if cls[i + w] == WATER:
				rails.append(Transform3D(Basis(), Vector3(x, y, z + 0.95)))
			if cls[i - 1] == WATER:
				rails.append(Transform3D(Basis(Vector3.UP, -PI * 0.5), Vector3(x + 0.05, y, z)))
			if cls[i + 1] == WATER:
				rails.append(Transform3D(Basis(Vector3.UP, -PI * 0.5), Vector3(x + 0.95, y, z)))
	railings(rails)
	# flowers along the grass edges of paths
	var flowers: Array[Transform3D] = []
	for z in range(1, h - 1):
		for x in range(1, w - 1):
			var i := z * w + x
			if cls[i] != GRASS:
				continue
			for nb: int in [i - 1, i + 1, i - w, i + w]:
				if cls[nb] == PATH and rng.randf() < 0.35:
					flowers.append(xform(Vector3(x + rng.randf(), hv[i], z + rng.randf()), rng.randf() * TAU, rng.randf_range(0.8, 1.4)))
					break
	scatter("flowers", flowers)
	# lone water cells on the beach and shore are the map's rocks: boulders
	for z in range(1, h - 1):
		for x in range(1, w - 1):
			var i := z * w + x
			if cls[i] != WATER:
				continue
			var land := 0
			for nb: int in [i - 1, i + 1, i - w, i + w]:
				if cls[nb] != WATER:
					land += 1
			if land >= 3 or (land == 0 and rng.randf() < 0.004):
				var s := rng.randf_range(0.9, 2.2)
				var b := box(Vector3(x + 0.5, SEA + s * 0.3, z + 0.5), Vector3(s, s * 1.4, s * 0.9), M.rock, false, rng.randf() * TAU)
				b.rotation.x = rng.randf_range(-0.3, 0.3)
	# clutter by the Quonset porches, the shop and the docks
	for p: Vector2 in [Vector2(530, 68), Vector2(622, 72), Vector2(508, 128), Vector2(660, 132), Vector2(546, 182)]:
		var at := at_px(p)
		model("planter", at + Vector3(-1.9, 0, 4.1))
		model("barrel", at + Vector3(2.0, 0, 3.8))
	for p: Vector2 in [Vector2(1080, 380), Vector2(1150, 380), Vector2(1240, 400), Vector2(1300, 470), Vector2(1250, 520)]:
		var d := at_px(p)
		model("crate_stack", d + Vector3(1.2, 0, 0.6), rng.randf() * TAU)
		model("barrel", d + Vector3(-0.8, 0, 0.9))
	# townsfolk
	var O := INSERT_AT
	var coats := [Color(0.24, 0.3, 0.34), Color(0.38, 0.3, 0.22), Color(0.3, 0.22, 0.28), Color(0.2, 0.27, 0.22)]
	var skins := [Color(0.78, 0.6, 0.48), Color(0.55, 0.38, 0.28), Color(0.68, 0.5, 0.38)]
	var hc := [Color(0.1, 0.07, 0.05), Color(0.35, 0.22, 0.12), Color(0.55, 0.5, 0.45), Color(0.18, 0.12, 0.1)]
	townsfolk([O + Vector3(-6, 0, 6), O + Vector3(10, 0, 6), O + Vector3(10, 0, -3)], coats[0], Color(0.7, 0.6, 0.4), skins[0], true, 180.0, {"long_coat": true})
	townsfolk([O + Vector3(14, 0, 2), O + Vector3(-4, 0, 1)], coats[1], Color(0.5, 0.15, 0.12), skins[1], false, 180.0, {"hat": "brim", "hair_color": hc[0]})
	townsfolk([O + Vector3(1.5, 0, -2.5), O + Vector3(4, 0, 4)], coats[2], Color(0.75, 0.65, 0.45), skins[2], false, 180.0, {"hair": "long", "hair_color": hc[1], "satchel": false})
	townsfolk([O + Vector3(-8.4, 3, -11.0)], coats[3], Color(0.6, 0.4, 0.2), skins[0], false, 160.0, {"hat": "cap", "hair_color": hc[3]})
	townsfolk([O + Vector3(-6.6, 3, -10.6)], coats[1], Color(0.3, 0.3, 0.3), skins[2], false, 210.0, {"hair": "bun", "hair_color": hc[2], "long_coat": true})
	townsfolk([nearest_walk(Vector2(1120, 430)), nearest_walk(Vector2(1260, 430)), nearest_walk(Vector2(1230, 480))], coats[0], Color(0.8, 0.7, 0.3), skins[1], false, 180.0, {"hat": "cap", "satchel": false})
	townsfolk([nearest_walk(Vector2(600, 110)), nearest_walk(Vector2(640, 200))], coats[2], Color(0.5, 0.2, 0.15), skins[0], false, 180.0, {"hair": "long", "hair_color": hc[0]})
	townsfolk([nearest_walk(Vector2(230, 690))], coats[1], Color(0.7, 0.55, 0.2), skins[2], false, 200.0, {"hat": "hardhat", "satchel": false})

== maps/anjabi/interiors/shop.gd

extends InteriorMap3D
## Interior of 12 Shop on the Steps (greybox landmark 12). PROVISIONAL dressing; what
## the shop sells and who keeps it are for the director. One room, counter at the back.


func _build() -> void:
	room(Vector3(10, 3.2, 7), ground_mat(Color(0.4, 0.33, 0.3), 0.0, 0.2, 0.0), M.plaster_teal)
	# counter along the back, a till on it
	box(Vector3(1.5, 0.55, -2.2), Vector3(4.2, 1.1, 0.8), M.timber)
	box(Vector3(2.6, 1.2, -2.2), Vector3(0.6, 0.2, 0.4), M.steel_dark, false)
	# shelving on both side walls
	for s: float in [-1.0, 1.0]:
		var x := s * 4.55
		box(Vector3(x, 1.1, -0.4), Vector3(0.7, 2.2, 4.4), M.timber)
		for k in 3:
			box(Vector3(x - s * 0.05, 0.45 + k * 0.7, -0.4), Vector3(0.6, 0.06, 4.2), M.steel_dark, false)
		var shelf := Interactable3D.new()
		shelf.prompt = "Look"
		shelf.label = "Shelves"
		shelf.dialogue_id = "anjabi/shop_shelves"
		shelf.radius = 1.4
		shelf.position = Vector3(x - s * 0.8, 0.9, -0.4)
		add_child(shelf)
	model("crate_stack", Vector3(-3.0, 0, -2.6), 0.3)
	model("barrel", Vector3(-1.6, 0, -2.8))
	model("barrel", Vector3(3.9, 0, 2.6))
	model("planter", Vector3(-3.9, 0, 2.7))
	# a rug in the middle
	box(Vector3(0, 0.01, 0.4), Vector3(3.6, 0.02, 2.4), M.rug, false)
	SettlementKit.pot(self, Vector3(-4.0, 0, 2.2), "lavender", 1.2)
	SettlementKit.lantern(self, Vector3(2.0, 1.2, -2.2), 0.8)
	ceiling_lamp(Vector3(-1.5, 2.9, -0.5))
	ceiling_lamp(Vector3(2.0, 2.9, -1.0))
