==============================================================================
== maps/proto2d/environment_controller_2d.gd
==============================================================================
class_name EnvironmentController2D
extends EnvironmentController
## Day, night and weather for a Map2D. Same clock, weather and gradients as the 3D
## EnvironmentController; drawn with a CanvasModulate tint, PointLight2D lamps, the
## baked glow layers (windows, signs) and the screen-space rain, snow and mist in
## game/main.tscn's Weather layer.

var _tint: CanvasModulate
var _lamps2d: Array[PointLight2D] = []
static var _lamp_tex: GradientTexture2D


func _init() -> void:
	super._init()
	_tint = CanvasModulate.new()


func _ready() -> void:
	super._ready()
	get_parent().add_child.call_deferred(_tint)


func add_lamp(at: Vector2, energy: float, range_px: float) -> void:
	if _lamp_tex == null:
		var g := Gradient.new()
		g.offsets = PackedFloat32Array([0.0, 0.25, 1.0])
		g.colors = PackedColorArray([Color(1, 1, 1, 1), Color(1, 1, 1, 0.45), Color(1, 1, 1, 0)])
		_lamp_tex = GradientTexture2D.new()
		_lamp_tex.gradient = g
		_lamp_tex.width = 128
		_lamp_tex.height = 128
		_lamp_tex.fill = GradientTexture2D.FILL_RADIAL
		_lamp_tex.fill_from = Vector2(0.5, 0.5)
		_lamp_tex.fill_to = Vector2(1.0, 0.5)
	var l := PointLight2D.new()
	l.texture = _lamp_tex
	l.texture_scale = range_px / 64.0 * 0.9
	l.position = at
	l.color = Color(1.0, 0.66, 0.36)
	l.set_meta(&"lit_energy", clampf(energy * 0.35, 0.3, 1.0))
	l.blend_mode = Light2D.BLEND_MODE_ADD
	get_parent().add_child.call_deferred(l)
	_lamps2d.append(l)


func _apply() -> void:
	if not is_inside_tree():
		return
	if indoor:
		# interiors: steady lamplight, no sky or weather, glow on
		_tint.color = Color(0.82, 0.74, 0.8)
		for l in _lamps2d:
			l.energy = float(l.get_meta(&"lit_energy"))
			l.visible = true
		for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"night_glow"):
			(n as CanvasItem).modulate = Color(1, 1, 1, 0.5)
			(n as CanvasItem).visible = true
		for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"precipitation"):
			n.call(&"set_precipitation", 0.0, 0.0)
		for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"fog_overlay"):
			var fm := (n as CanvasItem).material as ShaderMaterial
			if fm != null:
				fm.set_shader_parameter("fog_amount", 0.0)
		return
	var t := WorldTime.day_fraction()
	var day := daylight.sample(t)
	var cloud: float = w["cloud"]
	var dim := 1.0 - cloud * lerpf(0.25, 0.65, day)
	var flash := _flash * _flash
	var sunc := sun_color.sample(t) * sun_energy.sample(t) * dim * 0.5
	var amb := ambient.sample(t) * ambient_energy.sample(t) * 0.55
	var c := Color(sunc.r + amb.r, sunc.g + amb.g, sunc.b + amb.b)
	var grey := c.get_luminance()
	c = c.lerp(Color(grey, grey * 1.02, grey * 1.08), cloud * 0.5 * day)
	c = c.lerp(Color(0.78, 0.84, 0.95), float(w["snow"]) * 0.15)
	c = Color(minf(c.r + flash, 1.0), minf(c.g + flash, 1.0), minf(c.b + flash, 1.0))
	_tint.color = c
	var lit := maxf(lamps.sample(t), cloud * 0.6 * day)
	for l in _lamps2d:
		l.energy = float(l.get_meta(&"lit_energy")) * lit
		l.visible = lit > 0.02
	# glow layers bypass the tint, so they shine against the dark
	for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"night_glow"):
		(n as CanvasItem).modulate = Color(1, 1, 1, lit * 0.9)
		(n as CanvasItem).visible = lit > 0.02
	for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"precipitation"):
		n.call(&"set_precipitation", float(w["rain"]), float(w["snow"]))
	for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"fog_overlay"):
		var m := (n as CanvasItem).material as ShaderMaterial
		if m != null:
			m.set_shader_parameter("fog_amount", 0.03 + float(w["fog"]) * 0.32 + float(w["rain"]) * 0.05 + float(w["snow"]) * 0.05)


==============================================================================
== maps/proto2d/map_2d.gd
==============================================================================
class_name Map2D
extends GameMap
## A 2D map in the Golden Sun-style 3/4 view, built at runtime from a bake made by
## tools/bake_2d.gd (a pre-rendered ground, y-sorted object sprites, a height grid).
##
## Two coordinate spaces, both in pixels (S per metre):
##   ground (x, z): where things stand. Node positions use it, so y-sort is by z.
##   screen (x, z - h): where they are drawn. Visuals are offset up by their height h.
## Walking is limited by the height grid: no cell without ground, no step taller than
## MAX_STEP between neighbours, nothing below the sea. That gives cliffs, terraces,
## stairs and walls the way the 3D colliders defined them.

const MAX_STEP := 0.35

@export_dir var bake_dir := ""

var S := 16
var layout: Dictionary = {}
var lighting: EnvironmentController2D
var _heights: Image
var _grid := 4
var _x0 := 0.0
var _z0 := 0.0
var _sea := -1000.0


func _ready() -> void:
	layout = Data.load_dict(bake_dir.path_join("layout.json"))
	S = int(layout["S"])
	_grid = int(layout["grid"])
	_x0 = float(layout["x0"])
	_z0 = float(layout["z0"])
	_sea = float(layout["sea"])
	_heights = load(bake_dir.path_join("heights.res")) as Image
	var v0 := float(layout["v0"])
	var origin := Vector2(_x0 * S, v0 * S)
	bounds = Rect2(origin, Vector2(float(layout["width"]), float(layout["height"])))
	var bg := ColorRect.new()
	bg.color = Color(0.05, 0.06, 0.09)
	bg.position = origin - Vector2(400, 400)
	bg.size = bounds.size + Vector2(800, 800)
	bg.z_index = -20
	add_child(bg)
	var ground := Sprite2D.new()
	ground.texture = load(bake_dir.path_join("ground.png"))
	ground.centered = false
	ground.position = origin
	ground.z_index = -10
	add_child(ground)
	var gglow := bake_dir.path_join("ground_glow.png")
	if ResourceLoader.exists(gglow):
		add_child(_glow_sprite(load(gglow), origin, -9))
	entities.y_sort_enabled = true
	for o: Dictionary in layout["objects"]:
		var r: Array = o["rect"]
		var screen := origin + Vector2(float(r[0]), float(r[1]))
		var node := Node2D.new()
		node.name = "Obj_" + str(o["model"])
		# sort by where the object stands; the sprite hangs from there to its screen spot
		var base := Vector2(screen.x + float(r[2]) * 0.5, float(o["sort_z"]) * S)
		node.position = base
		var sp := Sprite2D.new()
		sp.texture = load(bake_dir.path_join("objects/%s.png" % o["file"]))
		sp.centered = false
		sp.position = screen - base
		node.add_child(sp)
		if o.get("glow", false):
			node.add_child(_glow_sprite(load(bake_dir.path_join("objects/%s_glow.png" % o["file"])), screen - base, 1))
		entities.add_child(node)
	var spawns := Node2D.new()
	spawns.name = "Spawns"
	add_child(spawns)
	for k: String in layout["spawns"]:
		var p: Array = layout["spawns"][k]
		var m := Marker2D.new()
		m.name = k
		m.position = Vector2(float(p[0]), float(p[2])) * S
		spawns.add_child(m)
	for e: Dictionary in layout["interactables"]:
		var it := Interactable.new()
		it.prompt = str(e["prompt"])
		it.label = str(e["label"])
		it.dialogue_id = str(e["dialogue_id"])
		it.radius = float(e["radius"]) * S
		it.position = Vector2(float(e["x"]), float(e["z"])) * S
		if e.has("target_map"):
			var tm := str(e["target_map"])
			var ts := str(e["target_spawn"])
			it.interacted.connect(func(_by: Node2D) -> void: GameState.travel_requested.emit(tm, ts))
		add_child(it)
	for p: Dictionary in layout["people"]:
		var person := Person2D.new()
		person.sheet = load("res://art/baked2d/chars/%s.png" % p["sheet"])
		person.map = self
		var pts := PackedVector2Array()
		for q: Array in p["points"]:
			pts.append(Vector2(float(q[0]), float(q[2])) * S)
		person.points = pts
		var yaw := deg_to_rad(float(p["yaw"]))
		person.facing = Vector2(-sin(yaw), -cos(yaw))
		if str(p["dialogue_id"]) != "":
			person.make_talkable(str(p["name"]), str(p["dialogue_id"]))
		entities.add_child(person)
	lighting = EnvironmentController2D.new()
	lighting.name = "Lighting"
	add_child(lighting)
	for l: Dictionary in layout["lamps"]:
		lighting.add_lamp(Vector2(float(l["x"]), float(l["z"]) - float(l["y"])) * S, float(l["energy"]), float(l["range"]) * S)


func _glow_sprite(tex: Texture2D, at: Vector2, z: int) -> Sprite2D:
	var g := Sprite2D.new()
	g.texture = tex
	g.centered = false
	g.position = at
	g.z_index = z
	var m := CanvasItemMaterial.new()
	m.blend_mode = CanvasItemMaterial.BLEND_MODE_ADD
	m.light_mode = CanvasItemMaterial.LIGHT_MODE_UNSHADED
	g.material = m
	g.add_to_group(&"night_glow")
	return g


## Ground height in metres under a ground-space pixel position; -1000 off the grid.
func height_at(p: Vector2) -> float:
	var i := int(floor((p.x / S - _x0) * _grid))
	var j := int(floor((p.y / S - _z0) * _grid))
	if i < 0 or j < 0 or i >= _heights.get_width() or j >= _heights.get_height():
		return -1000.0
	return _heights.get_pixel(i, j).r


func is_ground(h: float) -> bool:
	return h > _sea + 0.5


## True if a walker at `from` may stand at `to` (ground px). Checks the footprint.
func can_step(from: Vector2, to: Vector2) -> bool:
	var h0 := height_at(from)
	for o: Vector2 in [Vector2.ZERO, Vector2(0.22, 0), Vector2(-0.22, 0), Vector2(0, 0.12), Vector2(0, -0.12)]:
		var h := height_at(to + o * S)
		if not is_ground(h) or absf(h - h0) > MAX_STEP:
			return false
	return true


## Nearest standable point to a ground px position (spawns, loads).
func nearest_standable(p: Vector2) -> Vector2:
	if is_ground(height_at(p)):
		return p
	for r in range(1, 40):
		for k in 16:
			var q := p + Vector2.from_angle(TAU * k / 16.0) * r * 4.0
			if is_ground(height_at(q)):
				return q
	return p


==============================================================================
== maps/proto2d/person_2d.gd
==============================================================================
class_name Person2D
extends Node2D
## A character in a Map2D drawn from a baked sheet (tools/bake_2d.gd): 8 directions,
## columns idle, 8 walk frames, 8 run frames. Stands in ground space; the sprite is
## lifted by the ground height. Optionally walks a loop of points (townsfolk) and can
## be talked to (make_talkable).

const CELL := Vector2i(32, 42)
const FEET := 35

var sheet: Texture2D
var map: Map2D
var points := PackedVector2Array()
var facing := Vector2(0, 1)
var speed := 1.1
var interactable: Interactable

var visual: Node2D
var _sprite: Sprite2D
var _phase := 0.0
var _height := 0.0
var _i := 0
var _wait := 0.0


func _ready() -> void:
	visual = Node2D.new()
	add_child(visual)
	var shadow := Shadow.new()
	visual.add_child(shadow)
	_sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	_sprite.texture = sheet
	_sprite.hframes = 17
	_sprite.vframes = 8
	_sprite.centered = false
	_sprite.offset = Vector2(-CELL.x * 0.5, -FEET)
	visual.add_child(_sprite)
	if not points.is_empty():
		position = points[0]
	_height = map.height_at(position) if map != null else 0.0
	_wait = randf_range(0.0, 3.0)
	show_pose(false, false, 0.0)


func make_talkable(display_name: String, dialogue_id: String) -> void:
	interactable = Interactable.new()
	interactable.prompt = "Talk"
	interactable.label = display_name
	interactable.dialogue_id = dialogue_id
	interactable.radius = 18.0
	add_child(interactable)
	interactable.interacted.connect(func(by: Node2D) -> void:
		var d := by.position - position
		if d.length() > 0.5:
			facing = d.normalized()
			show_pose(false, false, 0.0))


## Pick the frame for the facing and gait; delta advances the cycle.
func show_pose(moving: bool, running: bool, delta: float) -> void:
	var d := posmod(roundi(facing.angle() / (TAU / 8.0)), 8)
	var col := 0
	if moving:
		_phase += delta * (14.5 if running else 10.0)
		var f := posmod(int(floor(_phase / (TAU / 8.0))), 8)
		col = (9 if running else 1) + f
	else:
		_phase = 0.0
	_sprite.frame = d * 17 + col


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if map != null:
		var h := map.height_at(position)
		if map.is_ground(h):
			_height = lerpf(_height, h, 1.0 - exp(-20.0 * delta))
	visual.position = Vector2(0, -roundf(_height * map.S)) if map != null else Vector2.ZERO
	if points.size() < 2 or Dialogue.active:
		return
	if _wait > 0.0:
		_wait -= delta
		show_pose(false, false, delta)
		return
	var target := points[(_i + 1) % points.size()]
	var to := target - position
	if to.length() < 1.0:
		_i = (_i + 1) % points.size()
		_wait = randf_range(1.5, 5.0)
		return
	facing = to.normalized()
	position += facing * minf(speed * map.S * delta, to.length())
	show_pose(true, false, delta)


## Soft oval under the feet.
class Shadow:
	extends Node2D

	func _draw() -> void:
		draw_set_transform(Vector2(0, -1), 0.0, Vector2(1.0, 0.42))
		draw_circle(Vector2.ZERO, 7.0, Color(0.02, 0.02, 0.05, 0.35))
		draw_circle(Vector2.ZERO, 5.0, Color(0.02, 0.02, 0.05, 0.25))


==============================================================================
== maps/proto2d/player_2d.gd
==============================================================================
class_name Player2D
extends Person2D
## The player on a Map2D. Same outward interface as Player and Player3D (target,
## facing, try_interact, setup, camera, sprinting) so main, HUD and saves drive it alike.
## Moves in ground space, blocked by the map's height grid; slides along edges.

signal target_changed(target: Interactable)

@export var walk_speed := 4.2
@export var run_speed := 7.4
## Camera zoom: 2 shows about 15 x 8 m, close to a handheld screen.
@export var zoom := 2.0

var touch: TouchControls
var target: Interactable
var sprinting := false
var camera: Camera2D
var _vel := Vector2.ZERO
var _cam_pos := Vector2.ZERO


func _init() -> void:
	sheet = load("res://art/baked2d/chars/player.png")


func setup(touch_controls: TouchControls, m: Map2D) -> void:
	touch = touch_controls
	map = m
	camera = Camera2D.new()
	camera.zoom = Vector2(zoom, zoom)
	camera.top_level = true
	camera.limit_left = int(m.bounds.position.x)
	camera.limit_top = int(m.bounds.position.y)
	camera.limit_right = int(m.bounds.end.x)
	camera.limit_bottom = int(m.bounds.end.y)
	add_child(camera)
	camera.make_current()
	_height = m.height_at(position)
	_cam_pos = _screen()
	camera.position = _cam_pos


func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	if map == null:
		return
	var dir := Vector2.ZERO
	if not Dialogue.active:
		dir = Input.get_vector(&"move_left", &"move_right", &"move_up", &"move_down")
		if dir == Vector2.ZERO and touch != null:
			dir = touch.vector
	var moving := dir.length() > 0.1
	sprinting = moving and (Input.is_action_pressed(&"sprint") or (touch != null and touch.sprint))
	var top := (run_speed if sprinting else walk_speed) * map.S
	var want := dir.limit_length(1.0) * top if moving else Vector2.ZERO
	_vel = _vel.move_toward(want, (28.0 if moving else 36.0) * map.S * delta)
	var step := _vel * delta
	if step != Vector2.ZERO:
		var to := position + step
		if map.can_step(position, to):
			position = to
		elif step.x != 0.0 and map.can_step(position, position + Vector2(step.x, 0)):
			position.x += step.x
			_vel.y = 0.0
		elif step.y != 0.0 and map.can_step(position, position + Vector2(0, step.y)):
			position.y += step.y
			_vel.x = 0.0
		else:
			_vel = Vector2.ZERO
	if moving:
		facing = dir.normalized()
	show_pose(_vel.length() > 0.3 * map.S, _vel.length() > walk_speed * 1.25 * map.S, delta * _vel.length() / (walk_speed * map.S) if _vel.length() < walk_speed * 1.25 * map.S else delta)
	_update_target()


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if map == null:
		return
	var h := map.height_at(position)
	if map.is_ground(h):
		_height = lerpf(_height, h, 1.0 - exp(-20.0 * delta))
	visual.position = Vector2(0, -roundf(_height * map.S))
	if camera == null:
		return
	var want := _screen()
	if _cam_pos.distance_to(want) > 120.0:
		_cam_pos = want
	_cam_pos = _cam_pos.lerp(want, 1.0 - exp(-8.0 * delta))
	# whole art pixels only, so the pixel art never shimmers
	camera.position = _cam_pos.round()


func _screen() -> Vector2:
	return position + Vector2(0, -_height * map.S - 12.0)


func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event.is_action_pressed(&"interact") and try_interact():
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()


func try_interact() -> bool:
	if Dialogue.active or target == null or not target.is_available():
		return false
	target.interact(self)
	return true


func face_now() -> void:
	show_pose(false, false, 0.0)


func _update_target() -> void:
	var best: Interactable = null
	var best_d := INF
	for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"interactables_2d"):
		var it := n as Interactable
		if it == null or not it.is_available() or not it.is_inside_tree():
			continue
		var d := position.distance_to(it.global_position)
		if d < it.radius + 14.0 and d < best_d:
			best_d = d
			best = it
	if best != target:
		target = best
		target_changed.emit(target)


==============================================================================
== render2d/pixel_art.gd
==============================================================================
class_name PixelArt
## The 2D art provider. Gameplay and map data only ever name things ("cobble",
## "lamp", "stall_rose", a character look); this class turns names into textures.
##
## Replacing the art: drop a PNG into res://art/pixel/ with the same name
## (tiles: art/pixel/tiles/<name>.png, 16x16; props: art/pixel/props/<name>.png;
## characters: art/pixel/chars/<id>.png, 16x32 frames in the sheet layout below) and it
## is used instead of the generated placeholder. No gameplay code changes.
##
## PLACEHOLDER ART: everything generated here is temporary programmer art in the
## Anjabi settlement palette (dusty rose, warm stone, ochre, muted teal, verdigris,
## indigo slate, aged brass, deep violet, warm amber light).

const T := 16
const OVERRIDE := "res://art/pixel/"

## Character sheet layout: 16x32 frames; columns idle, walk 1-4, run 1-4;
## rows facing down, up, left, right.
const FRAME := Vector2i(16, 32)
const COLS := 9
const DIRS := ["down", "up", "left", "right"]

# --- palette --------------------------------------------------------------------
const INK := Color("1c1622")
const STONE_HI := Color("a6948f")
const STONE := Color("85746f")
const STONE_LO := Color("62545a")
const MORTAR := Color("3e3340")
const COBBLE := Color("8b7a79")
const COBBLE_LO := Color("6d5f62")
const PAVE := Color("9e8c84")
const ROSE := Color("a8716c")
const ROSE_LO := Color("865a5c")
const OCHRE := Color("ae8a5a")
const OCHRE_LO := Color("8a6a46")
const TEAL := Color("5d8582")
const TEAL_LO := Color("466866")
const SLATE := Color("3d3754")
const SLATE_HI := Color("57507a")
const SLATE_LO := Color("2a253c")
const VERD := Color("4f8c7e")
const VERD_HI := Color("74b09e")
const BRASS := Color("aa7e3e")
const BRASS_HI := Color("dcb064")
const BRASS_LO := Color("5e4322")
const WOOD := Color("5c3a28")
const WOOD_HI := Color("7c5136")
const CANVAS_ROSE := Color("8e3542")
const CANVAS_TEAL := Color("2f6a6c")
const CREAM := Color("d6c29c")
const GLASS := Color("ffcf7a")
const GLASS_CORE := Color("fff0c0")
const GLASS_DARK := Color("2b2840")
const GRASS := Color("3f5f50")
const GRASS_HI := Color("547a63")
const GRASS_LO := Color("2e4a40")
const WATER := Color("1f2d46")
const WATER_HI := Color("35507a")
const LEAF := Color("3d6a48")
const LEAF_HI := Color("5a8a5a")
const BLOSSOM := Color("ece4d6")
const LAVENDER := Color("8d6bb6")
const POT := Color("9c5c3c")
const SCREEN := Color("5fe0ea")
const VOID := Color("0c0a12")

static var _tiles := {}
static var _props := {}
static var _sheets := {}


# --- public ---------------------------------------------------------------------

## A 16x16 tile image by name (override PNG if present, else generated).
static func tile(name: String) -> Image:
	if not _tiles.has(name):
		var img := _load_override("tiles/" + name)
		if img == null:
			img = _blank(T, T)
			_draw_tile(img, name)
		_tiles[name] = img
	return _tiles[name]


## The emissive part of a tile (windows, shopfronts) or null.
static func tile_glow(name: String) -> Image:
	return _glow_of(tile(name)) if name in GLOW_TILES else null


const GLOW_TILES := ["window_lit", "window_lit_rose", "window_lit_ochre", "window_lit_teal", "shop_window"]


## A prop texture by name, origin at the bottom-centre of its footprint.
static func prop(name: String) -> Texture2D:
	if not _props.has(name):
		var img := _load_override("props/" + name)
		if img == null:
			img = _draw_prop(name)
		_props[name] = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
	return _props[name]


## The emissive part of a prop (lantern glass, screens), or null.
static func prop_glow(name: String) -> Texture2D:
	var key := name + "#glow"
	if not _props.has(key):
		var src := (prop(name) as ImageTexture).get_image()
		var g := _glow_of(src)
		_props[key] = ImageTexture.create_from_image(g) if g != null else null
	return _props[key]


## A character sheet for a look: {coat, skin, hair, hair_color, long_coat, accent,
## hat, apron}. Colours may be Color or HTML strings. `id` names an override file.
static func character(look: Dictionary, id := "") -> Texture2D:
	var key := id if id != "" else str(look)
	if not _sheets.has(key):
		var img: Image = null
		if id != "":
			img = _load_override("chars/" + id)
		if img == null:
			img = _draw_character(look)
		_sheets[key] = ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)
	return _sheets[key]


# --- helpers --------------------------------------------------------------------

static func _load_override(rel: String) -> Image:
	var path := OVERRIDE + rel + ".png"
	if ResourceLoader.exists(path):
		var t := load(path) as Texture2D
		if t != null:
			return t.get_image()
	return null


static func _blank(w: int, h: int) -> Image:
	var img := Image.create(w, h, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	img.fill(Color(0, 0, 0, 0))
	return img


static func h2(x: int, y: int, s: int = 0) -> float:
	var n := x * 374761393 + y * 668265263 + s * 2246822519
	n = (n ^ (n >> 13)) * 1274126177
	return float((n ^ (n >> 16)) & 0xffff) / 65535.0


static func px(img: Image, x: int, y: int, c: Color) -> void:
	if x >= 0 and y >= 0 and x < img.get_width() and y < img.get_height():
		if c.a >= 0.999:
			img.set_pixel(x, y, c)
		elif c.a > 0.0:
			img.set_pixel(x, y, img.get_pixel(x, y).blend(c))


static func rect(img: Image, x: int, y: int, w: int, h: int, c: Color) -> void:
	for j in h:
		for i in w:
			px(img, x + i, y + j, c)


static func hline(img: Image, x: int, y: int, w: int, c: Color) -> void:
	rect(img, x, y, w, 1, c)


static func vline(img: Image, x: int, y: int, h: int, c: Color) -> void:
	rect(img, x, y, 1, h, c)


static func ellipse(img: Image, cx: float, cy: float, rx: float, ry: float, c: Color) -> void:
	for y in range(int(cy - ry) - 1, int(cy + ry) + 2):
		for x in range(int(cx - rx) - 1, int(cx + rx) + 2):
			var dx := (x + 0.5 - cx) / rx
			var dy := (y + 0.5 - cy) / ry
			if dx * dx + dy * dy <= 1.0:
				px(img, x, y, c)


static func ring(img: Image, cx: float, cy: float, rx: float, ry: float, w: float, c: Color) -> void:
	for y in range(int(cy - ry) - 2, int(cy + ry) + 3):
		for x in range(int(cx - rx) - 2, int(cx + rx) + 3):
			var dx := (x + 0.5 - cx) / rx
			var dy := (y + 0.5 - cy) / ry
			var d := sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
			if absf(d - 1.0) * minf(rx, ry) <= w * 0.5:
				px(img, x, y, c)


## A dark one-pixel outline around every opaque shape.
static func outline(img: Image, c: Color = INK) -> void:
	var w := img.get_width()
	var h := img.get_height()
	var src := img.duplicate() as Image
	for y in h:
		for x in w:
			if src.get_pixel(x, y).a > 0.5:
				continue
			for o: Vector2i in [Vector2i(1, 0), Vector2i(-1, 0), Vector2i(0, 1), Vector2i(0, -1)]:
				var nx := x + o.x
				var ny := y + o.y
				if nx >= 0 and ny >= 0 and nx < w and ny < h and src.get_pixel(nx, ny).a > 0.5:
					img.set_pixel(x, y, c)
					break


## Keeps only bright warm or cyan pixels (lamp glass, lit windows, screens).
static func _glow_of(src: Image) -> Image:
	var g := _blank(src.get_width(), src.get_height())
	var any := false
	for y in src.get_height():
		for x in src.get_width():
			var c := src.get_pixel(x, y)
			if c.a > 0.5 and c.get_luminance() > 0.68 and (c.r > 0.9 or c.b > 0.85) and c.s > 0.25:
				g.set_pixel(x, y, Color(c.r, c.g, c.b, 1.0))
				any = true
	return g if any else null


# --- tiles ----------------------------------------------------------------------

static func _draw_tile(img: Image, name: String) -> void:
	match name:
		"cobble":
			_cobbles(img, COBBLE, COBBLE_LO, MORTAR, 0)
		"cobble_warm":
			_cobbles(img, Color("94807a"), COBBLE_LO, MORTAR, 3)
		"plaza_band":
			_flags(img, PAVE, STONE_LO, 1)
		"terrace":
			_flags(img, Color("8f7d7a"), MORTAR, 2)
		"grass":
			img.fill(GRASS)
			for i in 26:
				var x := int(h2(i, 1, 7) * 16)
				var y := int(h2(i, 2, 7) * 16)
				px(img, x, y, GRASS_HI if i % 3 else GRASS_LO)
				if i % 5 == 0:
					px(img, x, y - 1, GRASS_HI)
		"flowers":
			_draw_tile(img, "grass")
			for i in 7:
				var x := 1 + int(h2(i, 3, 9) * 14)
				var y := 1 + int(h2(i, 4, 9) * 14)
				px(img, x, y, [LAVENDER, BLOSSOM, ROSE, LAVENDER][i % 4])
				px(img, x, y + 1, LEAF)
		"stairs":
			for y in 16:
				var k := y % 4
				var c := STONE_HI if k == 0 else (STONE if k < 3 else MORTAR)
				hline(img, 0, y, 16, c)
			for y in range(0, 16, 4):
				for x in 16:
					if h2(x, y, 5) < 0.15:
						px(img, x, y + 1, STONE_LO)
		"water":
			img.fill(WATER)
			for i in 10:
				var x := int(h2(i, 5, 11) * 14)
				var y := int(h2(i, 6, 11) * 16)
				hline(img, x, y, 2 + i % 3, WATER_HI)
		"floor_wood":
			for y in 16:
				var plank := y / 4
				var c := WOOD_HI if plank % 2 == 0 else WOOD
				hline(img, 0, y, 16, c)
				if y % 4 == 3:
					hline(img, 0, y, 16, Color("3e2618"))
			for p in 4:
				vline(img, int(h2(p, 8, 2) * 15), p * 4, 3, Color("3e2618"))
		"floor_tile":
			_flags(img, Color("6e6070"), Color("4a3e4c"), 4)
		"mat":
			img.fill(CANVAS_ROSE)
			rect(img, 1, 1, 14, 14, Color("a64454"))
			rect(img, 3, 3, 10, 10, CANVAS_ROSE)
		"void":
			img.fill(VOID)
		"wall_face":
			_ashlar(img, STONE, STONE_LO, MORTAR)
		"wall_face_dark":
			_ashlar(img, STONE_LO, Color("4a3e46"), MORTAR)
		"wall_top":
			img.fill(STONE_HI)
			hline(img, 0, 13, 16, STONE)
			hline(img, 0, 14, 16, STONE_LO)
			hline(img, 0, 15, 16, MORTAR)
			for x in range(0, 16, 8):
				vline(img, x + int(h2(x, 0, 3) * 3), 0, 13, STONE)
		"plaster_rose", "plaster_ochre", "plaster_teal":
			var base: Color = {"plaster_rose": ROSE, "plaster_ochre": OCHRE, "plaster_teal": TEAL}[name]
			var lo: Color = {"plaster_rose": ROSE_LO, "plaster_ochre": OCHRE_LO, "plaster_teal": TEAL_LO}[name]
			img.fill(base)
			for i in 14:
				px(img, int(h2(i, 1, 13) * 16), int(h2(i, 2, 13) * 16), lo)
			hline(img, 0, 15, 16, lo)
		"interior_wall":
			img.fill(Color("5a4a5e"))
			for y in range(0, 16, 4):
				hline(img, 0, y, 16, Color("4a3c50"))
			hline(img, 0, 13, 16, WOOD)
			hline(img, 0, 14, 16, WOOD_HI)
			hline(img, 0, 15, 16, Color("3e2618"))
		"roof_slate":
			img.fill(SLATE)
			for row in 4:
				var y := row * 4
				hline(img, 0, y + 3, 16, SLATE_LO)
				var off := (row % 2) * 4
				for x in range(off, 16, 8):
					vline(img, x, y, 3, SLATE_LO)
				hline(img, 0, y, 16, SLATE_HI if row % 2 == 0 else SLATE)
		"roof_ridge":
			_draw_tile(img, "roof_slate")
			rect(img, 0, 0, 16, 3, SLATE_HI)
			hline(img, 0, 3, 16, SLATE_LO)
			for x in range(1, 16, 4):
				px(img, x, 1, BRASS_LO)
		"roof_eave":
			img.fill(Color(0, 0, 0, 0))
			rect(img, 0, 0, 16, 6, SLATE)
			hline(img, 0, 0, 16, SLATE_HI)
			hline(img, 0, 5, 16, SLATE_LO)
			hline(img, 0, 6, 16, BRASS_LO)
			rect(img, 0, 7, 16, 3, Color(0.05, 0.03, 0.08, 0.45))
		"roof_verdigris":
			img.fill(VERD)
			for row in 4:
				hline(img, 0, row * 4 + 3, 16, Color("3e7064"))
				for x in range((row % 2) * 3, 16, 6):
					px(img, x, row * 4 + 1, VERD_HI)
		"window_lit", "window_lit_rose", "window_lit_ochre", "window_lit_teal", "window_dark", "window_dark_rose", "window_dark_ochre", "window_dark_teal":
			var lit := name.begins_with("window_lit")
			var wall := ""
			if name.ends_with("rose"):
				wall = "plaster_rose"
			elif name.ends_with("ochre"):
				wall = "plaster_ochre"
			elif name.ends_with("teal"):
				wall = "plaster_teal"
			if wall != "":
				img.blit_rect(tile(wall), Rect2i(0, 0, 16, 16), Vector2i.ZERO)
			_window(img, lit)
		"door":
			rect(img, 3, 2, 10, 14, MORTAR)
			ellipse(img, 8, 5, 5, 4, MORTAR)
			rect(img, 4, 4, 8, 12, WOOD)
			ellipse(img, 8, 5.5, 4, 3, WOOD)
			for x in [6, 9]:
				vline(img, x, 3, 13, Color("3e2618"))
			px(img, 10, 10, BRASS_HI)
			hline(img, 2, 15, 12, STONE_HI)
		"shop_window":
			rect(img, 1, 3, 14, 11, MORTAR)
			rect(img, 2, 4, 12, 9, GLASS)
			rect(img, 4, 6, 8, 5, GLASS_CORE)
			vline(img, 8, 4, 9, BRASS_LO)
			hline(img, 2, 8, 12, BRASS_LO)
			for i in 3:
				px(img, 4 + i * 3, 11, POT)
		"vines":
			for i in 22:
				var x := int(h2(i, 1, 21) * 16)
				var y := int(pow(h2(i, 2, 21), 1.6) * 16)
				px(img, x, y, LEAF if i % 3 else LEAF_HI)
				px(img, x + 1, y, LEAF)
				if i % 4 == 0:
					px(img, x, y + 1, BLOSSOM)
			for x in range(0, 16, 5):
				vline(img, x + 1, 0, 3 + int(h2(x, 3, 21) * 8), LEAF)
		"awning_rose", "awning_teal":
			var c := CANVAS_ROSE if name == "awning_rose" else CANVAS_TEAL
			for x in 16:
				var stripe := (x / 4) % 2 == 0
				vline(img, x, 0, 9, c if stripe else CREAM)
				px(img, x, 9, c if stripe else CREAM)
			for x in range(0, 16, 4):
				px(img, x + 2, 10, c)
			hline(img, 0, 0, 16, Color(1, 1, 1, 0.15))
			rect(img, 0, 11, 16, 3, Color(0.05, 0.03, 0.08, 0.35))
		"balustrade", "balustrade_v":
			# top view of a stone balustrade: a capped rail and its shadow
			if name == "balustrade":
				rect(img, 0, 5, 16, 5, STONE_HI)
				hline(img, 0, 5, 16, Color("bfaea8"))
				hline(img, 0, 9, 16, STONE_LO)
				hline(img, 0, 7, 16, BRASS)
				rect(img, 0, 10, 16, 3, STONE)
				for x in range(1, 16, 4):
					vline(img, x, 10, 3, STONE_LO)
				rect(img, 0, 13, 16, 2, Color(0.05, 0.03, 0.08, 0.4))
			else:
				rect(img, 5, 0, 5, 16, STONE_HI)
				vline(img, 7, 0, 16, BRASS)
				vline(img, 10, 0, 16, Color(0.05, 0.03, 0.08, 0.4))
		"counter":
			rect(img, 0, 2, 16, 4, WOOD_HI)
			hline(img, 0, 2, 16, Color("9a6a46"))
			rect(img, 0, 6, 16, 10, WOOD)
			for x in range(2, 16, 5):
				vline(img, x, 7, 8, Color("3e2618"))
			hline(img, 0, 15, 16, Color("3e2618"))
		"shelf":
			img.fill(WOOD)
			for y in [4, 9, 14]:
				hline(img, 0, y, 16, WOOD_HI)
			for i in 9:
				var x := 1 + (i * 5) % 14
				var y: int = [1, 6, 11][i % 3]
				rect(img, x, y, 2, 3, [POT, BRASS, TEAL, CREAM, LAVENDER][i % 5])
		_:
			img.fill(Color("ff00ff"))


static func _cobbles(img: Image, base: Color, lo: Color, mortar: Color, s: int) -> void:
	img.fill(mortar)
	# stones of 4-6 px in staggered rows, each with a lit top-left and a dark lip
	var y := 0
	var row := 0
	while y < 16:
		var hgt := 4 + int(h2(row, s, 1) * 2)
		var x := -int(h2(row, s, 2) * 4)
		while x < 16:
			var wid := 4 + int(h2(x + 20, row, s) * 3)
			var c := base.lerp(lo, h2(x + 7, row + 3, s) * 0.7)
			c = c.lerp(ROSE, 0.12 * h2(x, row, s + 9))
			rect(img, x + 1, y + 1, wid - 1, hgt - 1, c)
			hline(img, x + 1, y + 1, wid - 2, c.lightened(0.12))
			hline(img, x + 1, y + hgt - 1, wid - 1, c.darkened(0.18))
			x += wid
		y += hgt
		row += 1


static func _flags(img: Image, base: Color, joint: Color, s: int) -> void:
	img.fill(base)
	for i in 30:
		px(img, int(h2(i, 1, s) * 16), int(h2(i, 2, s) * 16), base.darkened(0.08 + 0.08 * h2(i, 3, s)))
	hline(img, 0, 15, 16, joint)
	vline(img, 15, 0, 8, joint)
	vline(img, 7, 8, 8, joint)
	hline(img, 0, 7, 16, joint.lerp(base, 0.4))


static func _ashlar(img: Image, base: Color, lo: Color, mortar: Color) -> void:
	img.fill(mortar)
	for row in 3:
		var y := row * 5 + 1
		var off := (row % 2) * 5
		var x := off - 10
		while x < 16:
			var wid := 9 + int(h2(x + 30, row, 4) * 2)
			var c := base.lerp(lo, h2(x, row, 6) * 0.6)
			rect(img, x + 1, y, wid - 1, 4, c)
			hline(img, x + 1, y, wid - 2, c.lightened(0.1))
			x += wid
	for i in 6:
		px(img, int(h2(i, 9, 4) * 16), int(h2(i, 8, 4) * 16), lo.darkened(0.2))


static func _window(img: Image, lit: bool) -> void:
	# arched window in a stone surround with a brass mullion and a sill
	ellipse(img, 8, 5, 4.5, 4, MORTAR)
	rect(img, 3, 5, 10, 8, MORTAR)
	var g := GLASS if lit else GLASS_DARK
	ellipse(img, 8, 5.5, 3.5, 3, g)
	rect(img, 4, 5, 8, 7, g)
	if lit:
		rect(img, 6, 6, 4, 4, GLASS_CORE)
	else:
		px(img, 5, 6, Color("4a4670"))
	vline(img, 8, 3, 9, BRASS_LO)
	hline(img, 4, 8, 8, BRASS_LO)
	hline(img, 2, 13, 12, STONE_HI)
	hline(img, 2, 14, 12, STONE_LO)


# --- props ----------------------------------------------------------------------

static func _draw_prop(name: String) -> Image:
	match name:
		"lamp":
			var img := _blank(16, 48)
			rect(img, 6, 40, 4, 6, BRASS_LO)
			rect(img, 5, 45, 6, 2, STONE_LO)
			vline(img, 7, 14, 28, BRASS_LO)
			vline(img, 8, 14, 28, BRASS)
			rect(img, 4, 4, 8, 10, BRASS_LO)
			rect(img, 5, 5, 6, 8, GLASS)
			rect(img, 6, 7, 4, 4, GLASS_CORE)
			rect(img, 3, 2, 10, 2, BRASS)
			px(img, 7, 1, BRASS_HI)
			px(img, 8, 1, BRASS_HI)
			outline(img)
			return img
		"stall_rose", "stall_teal":
			return _stall(CANVAS_ROSE if name == "stall_rose" else CANVAS_TEAL, name == "stall_rose")
		"armillary":
			var img := _blank(32, 56)
			# plinth and fluted column
			rect(img, 7, 48, 18, 6, STONE_LO)
			rect(img, 8, 47, 16, 2, STONE_HI)
			rect(img, 12, 26, 8, 22, STONE)
			for x in [13, 16, 19]:
				vline(img, x, 27, 20, STONE_LO)
			rect(img, 10, 24, 12, 3, BRASS)
			# brass rings round a glowing core
			ring(img, 16, 13, 11, 11, 1.4, BRASS)
			ring(img, 16, 13, 11, 4, 1.2, BRASS_HI)
			ring(img, 16, 13, 4, 11, 1.2, BRASS)
			ring(img, 16, 13, 8, 9, 1.0, BRASS_LO)
			ellipse(img, 16, 13, 3.2, 3.2, GLASS)
			ellipse(img, 16, 13, 1.8, 1.8, GLASS_CORE)
			outline(img)
			return img
		"compass":
			return _compass()
		"tree":
			var img := _blank(32, 44)
			rect(img, 14, 30, 4, 12, WOOD)
			ellipse(img, 16, 18, 13, 13, LEAF)
			for i in 40:
				var a := h2(i, 1, 31) * TAU
				var r := h2(i, 2, 31) * 11.0
				px(img, int(16 + cos(a) * r), int(18 + sin(a) * r * 0.95), LEAF_HI if sin(a) < 0.2 else GRASS_LO)
			for i in 6:
				px(img, int(8 + h2(i, 3, 31) * 16), int(9 + h2(i, 4, 31) * 16), BLOSSOM)
			ellipse(img, 16, 41, 7, 2.5, Color(0.05, 0.03, 0.08, 0.35))
			outline(img)
			return img
		"cypress":
			var img := _blank(16, 44)
			rect(img, 7, 36, 2, 6, WOOD)
			for y in 34:
				var w := sin(float(y) / 34.0 * PI) * 5.5 + 1.0
				hline(img, int(8 - w), y + 2, int(w * 2), LEAF if y % 3 else GRASS_LO)
				px(img, int(8 - w + 1), y + 2, LEAF_HI)
			outline(img)
			return img
		"barrel":
			var img := _blank(16, 20)
			rect(img, 3, 4, 10, 14, WOOD)
			for x in [5, 8, 11]:
				vline(img, x, 5, 12, WOOD_HI)
			for y in [6, 15]:
				hline(img, 3, y, 10, BRASS_LO)
			ellipse(img, 8, 4, 5, 2, Color("6e4630"))
			outline(img)
			return img
		"crates":
			var img := _blank(16, 22)
			rect(img, 1, 9, 14, 11, WOOD)
			rect(img, 4, 2, 9, 8, WOOD_HI)
			for c: Rect2i in [Rect2i(1, 9, 14, 11), Rect2i(4, 2, 9, 8)]:
				hline(img, c.position.x, c.position.y, c.size.x, Color("9a6a46"))
				vline(img, c.position.x + c.size.x / 2, c.position.y, c.size.y, Color("3e2618"))
			outline(img)
			return img
		"pot_lavender", "pot_leaf":
			var img := _blank(16, 24)
			rect(img, 4, 15, 8, 8, POT)
			hline(img, 3, 15, 10, Color("b8714a"))
			vline(img, 5, 16, 6, Color("b8714a"))
			if name == "pot_lavender":
				for i in 9:
					var x := 4 + i
					var hgt := 6 + int(h2(i, 1, 41) * 6)
					vline(img, x, 15 - hgt, hgt, LEAF)
					rect(img, x, 15 - hgt, 1, 3, LAVENDER)
			else:
				ellipse(img, 8, 10, 6, 5, LEAF)
				ellipse(img, 7, 9, 3, 2, LEAF_HI)
			outline(img)
			return img
		"bench":
			var img := _blank(32, 16)
			rect(img, 2, 10, 4, 5, STONE)
			rect(img, 26, 10, 4, 5, STONE)
			rect(img, 1, 6, 30, 4, WOOD_HI)
			for x in range(1, 31, 5):
				vline(img, x, 6, 4, WOOD)
			rect(img, 1, 2, 30, 3, WOOD)
			outline(img)
			return img
		"terminal":
			var img := _blank(16, 32)
			rect(img, 2, 8, 12, 22, STONE)
			rect(img, 1, 6, 14, 3, STONE_HI)
			rect(img, 3, 10, 10, 8, BRASS_LO)
			rect(img, 4, 11, 8, 6, SCREEN)
			for i in 5:
				px(img, 5 + int(h2(i, 1, 51) * 6), 12 + int(h2(i, 2, 51) * 4), Color("e0ffff"))
			rect(img, 4, 20, 8, 2, BRASS)
			outline(img)
			return img
		"arcade_bay":
			return _arcade_bay()
		"banner_rose", "banner_teal":
			var img := _blank(12, 30)
			var c := CANVAS_ROSE if name == "banner_rose" else CANVAS_TEAL
			hline(img, 0, 0, 12, BRASS)
			rect(img, 1, 1, 10, 25, c)
			for y in range(26, 29):
				hline(img, 1 + (y - 26), y, 10 - (y - 26) * 2, c)
			ring(img, 6, 11, 3, 3, 1.0, BRASS_HI)
			px(img, 6, 11, BRASS_HI)
			vline(img, 6, 16, 6, BRASS_HI)
			return img
		"dome":
			var img := _blank(64, 40)
			rect(img, 4, 32, 56, 6, STONE_HI)
			hline(img, 4, 37, 56, STONE_LO)
			for y in 30:
				var w := sqrt(maxf(0.0, 1.0 - pow((30.0 - y) / 30.0, 2.0))) * 26.0
				hline(img, int(32 - w), y + 2, int(w * 2), VERD)
				px(img, int(32 - w), y + 2, Color("3e7064"))
				px(img, int(32 - w + 3), y + 2, VERD_HI)
			for k in [-16, 0, 16]:
				for y in 30:
					var x := 32 + int(k * sqrt(maxf(0.0, 1.0 - pow((30.0 - y) / 30.0, 2.0))))
					px(img, x, y + 2, BRASS_LO)
			vline(img, 32, 0, 4, BRASS_HI)
			outline(img)
			return img
		"string_lights":
			var img := _blank(160, 24)
			for x in 160:
				var t := float(x) / 159.0
				var y := int(3 + 14.0 * 4.0 * t * (1.0 - t))
				px(img, x, y, BRASS_LO)
				if x % 10 == 5:
					px(img, x, y + 1, GLASS)
					px(img, x, y + 2, GLASS_CORE)
			return img
		"rug":
			var img := _blank(48, 32)
			rect(img, 0, 0, 48, 32, CANVAS_ROSE)
			rect(img, 2, 2, 44, 28, Color("a64454"))
			rect(img, 5, 5, 38, 22, CANVAS_ROSE)
			for i in 4:
				ring(img, 24, 16, 6 + i * 3, 4 + i * 2, 0.8, CREAM if i % 2 else BRASS)
			return img
		"notice":
			var img := _blank(16, 24)
			vline(img, 3, 8, 15, WOOD)
			vline(img, 12, 8, 15, WOOD)
			rect(img, 1, 2, 14, 10, WOOD_HI)
			rect(img, 2, 3, 12, 8, CREAM)
			for y in [5, 7, 9]:
				hline(img, 4, y, 8, STONE_LO)
			outline(img)
			return img
		"shadow_arch":
			var img := _blank(48, 16)
			rect(img, 0, 0, 48, 16, Color(0.05, 0.03, 0.1, 0.35))
			return img
	var bad := _blank(16, 16)
	bad.fill(Color("ff00ff"))
	return bad


static func _stall(c: Color, instruments: bool) -> Image:
	var img := _blank(48, 44)
	# counter
	rect(img, 3, 26, 42, 14, WOOD)
	rect(img, 2, 24, 44, 3, WOOD_HI)
	for x in range(6, 44, 8):
		vline(img, x, 27, 12, Color("3e2618"))
	# goods on the counter
	if instruments:
		ring(img, 12, 19, 5, 5, 1.0, BRASS_HI)
		ring(img, 12, 19, 5, 2, 1.0, BRASS)
		px(img, 12, 19, GLASS)
		rect(img, 21, 20, 8, 4, BRASS_LO)
		hline(img, 21, 20, 8, BRASS_HI)
		ellipse(img, 35, 22, 4, 2, BRASS)
	else:
		for i in 5:
			rect(img, 6 + i * 8, 18, 5, 6, [POT, TEAL, CREAM, POT, LAVENDER][i])
			hline(img, 6 + i * 8, 18, 5, Color(1, 1, 1, 0.25))
	# poles and striped canopy
	vline(img, 3, 6, 34, WOOD_HI)
	vline(img, 44, 6, 34, WOOD_HI)
	for x in 48:
		var stripe := (x / 6) % 2 == 0
		vline(img, x, 2, 9, c if stripe else CREAM)
		if x % 6 == 3:
			px(img, x, 11, c if stripe else CREAM)
	hline(img, 0, 2, 48, Color(1, 1, 1, 0.2))
	rect(img, 0, 12, 48, 3, Color(0.05, 0.03, 0.08, 0.35))
	# hanging lantern
	vline(img, 40, 11, 3, BRASS_LO)
	rect(img, 38, 14, 5, 6, BRASS_LO)
	rect(img, 39, 15, 3, 4, GLASS)
	outline(img)
	return img


static func _compass() -> Image:
	var img := _blank(80, 80)
	var c := Vector2(40, 40)
	for y in 80:
		for x in 80:
			var d := Vector2(x + 0.5, y + 0.5) - c
			var r := d.length() / 38.0
			if r > 1.0:
				continue
			var col := Color("6e6066")
			if r > 0.78 and r < 0.93:
				col = Color("3a3040")
				var a := atan2(d.y, d.x)
				if absf(fposmod(a / (TAU / 32.0), 1.0) - 0.5) > 0.38 and r > 0.82 and r < 0.9:
					col = BRASS
			for rr: float in [0.78, 0.93, 0.99, 0.36]:
				if absf(r - rr) < 0.018:
					col = BRASS_HI
			# eight-point star
			var ang := atan2(d.y, d.x)
			var k := TAU / 8.0
			var idx := int(floor((ang + k * 0.5) / k))
			var sa := fposmod(ang + k * 0.5, k) - k * 0.5
			var len := 0.76 if posmod(idx, 2) == 0 else 0.46
			var width := (1.0 - r / len) * 0.21
			if r < len and absf(sa) * r * 2.6 < width:
				col = BRASS if sa > 0.0 else BRASS_LO
			if r < 0.07:
				col = BRASS_HI
			img.set_pixel(x, y, col)
	return img


static func _arcade_bay() -> Image:
	# one bay of the terrace arcade, 48x32: pilasters, an arch, a warm shopfront
	var img := _blank(48, 32)
	# recess: dark warm interior
	rect(img, 9, 8, 30, 24, Color("2a1a18"))
	ellipse(img, 24, 10, 15, 9, Color("2a1a18"))
	# lit window, door
	rect(img, 12, 14, 11, 10, GLASS)
	rect(img, 14, 16, 7, 6, GLASS_CORE)
	vline(img, 17, 14, 10, BRASS_LO)
	rect(img, 27, 12, 9, 20, WOOD)
	ellipse(img, 31.5, 13, 4.5, 3, WOOD)
	px(img, 34, 22, BRASS_HI)
	# arch ring and keystone
	ring(img, 24, 10, 16, 10, 2.0, STONE_HI)
	rect(img, 22, 0, 4, 3, STONE_HI)
	# pilasters
	for x0 in [0, 41]:
		rect(img, x0, 0, 7, 32, STONE)
		vline(img, x0, 0, 32, STONE_HI)
		vline(img, x0 + 6, 0, 32, STONE_LO)
		for y in range(4, 32, 6):
			hline(img, x0, y, 7, MORTAR)
	# striped awning across the arch
	for x in range(8, 40):
		var stripe := (x / 4) % 2 == 0
		vline(img, x, 6, 5, CANVAS_ROSE if stripe else CREAM)
	rect(img, 8, 11, 32, 2, Color(0.05, 0.03, 0.08, 0.35))
	return img


# --- characters -----------------------------------------------------------------

static func _col(v: Variant, fallback: Color) -> Color:
	if v is Color:
		return v
	if v is String and str(v) != "":
		return Color.html(str(v))
	return fallback


## A 16x32 adult figure in 4 directions x (idle, walk 1-4, run 1-4).
static func _draw_character(look: Dictionary) -> Image:
	var sheet := _blank(FRAME.x * COLS, FRAME.y * 4)
	var spec := {
		"coat": _col(look.get("coat", null), Color("80211c")),
		"accent": _col(look.get("accent", null), BRASS),
		"skin": _col(look.get("skin", null), Color("c89a7a")),
		"hair_color": _col(look.get("hair_color", null), Color("1a0f0d")),
		"hair": str(look.get("hair", "bun")),
		"long": bool(look.get("long_coat", true)),
		"hat": str(look.get("hat", "")),
		"apron": bool(look.get("apron", false)),
	}
	for d in 4:
		for col in COLS:
			var f := _blank(FRAME.x, FRAME.y)
			var run := col >= 5
			var step := 0 if col == 0 else (col - 1) % 4
			_figure(f, spec, DIRS[d], col == 0, run, step)
			outline(f)
			sheet.blit_rect(f, Rect2i(Vector2i.ZERO, FRAME), Vector2i(col * FRAME.x, d * FRAME.y))
	return sheet


static func _figure(img: Image, s: Dictionary, dir: String, idle: bool, run: bool, step: int) -> void:
	var coat: Color = s["coat"]
	var coat_lo := coat.darkened(0.3)
	var coat_hi := coat.lightened(0.15)
	var skin: Color = s["skin"]
	var skin_lo := skin.darkened(0.18)
	var hair: Color = s["hair_color"]
	var accent: Color = s["accent"]
	var boot := Color("231a1c")
	var trouser := Color("2e2834")
	# gait: steps 0 and 2 are contact poses (legs apart), 1 and 3 are passing (bob up)
	var bob := 0 if idle else (1 if step % 2 == 1 else 0)
	var spread := 0 if idle or step % 2 == 1 else (2 if run else 1)
	var lead := 1 if step == 0 else -1
	var lean := 1 if run and not idle else 0
	var y0 := 2 - bob
	var side := dir == "left" or dir == "right"
	var flip := dir == "left"
	var X := func(x: int) -> int: return 15 - x if flip else x
	# shadow
	ellipse(img, 8, 30.5, 5, 1.6, Color(0.05, 0.03, 0.08, 0.4))
	# legs and boots
	if side:
		var fx := 7 + lean
		var a := fx + spread * lead
		var b := fx - spread * lead
		for lx: int in [b, a]:
			rect(img, X.call(lx), y0 + 20, 2, 7, trouser)
			rect(img, X.call(lx) - (1 if flip else 0), y0 + 26, 3, 2, boot)
	else:
		var l_off := spread * lead
		rect(img, 5, y0 + 20 + maxi(0, -l_off), 2, 7 - absi(l_off), trouser)
		rect(img, 9, y0 + 20 + maxi(0, l_off), 2, 7 - absi(l_off), trouser)
		rect(img, 5, y0 + 26, 2, 2, boot)
		rect(img, 9, y0 + 26, 2, 2, boot)
	# coat body and skirt
	var skirt := 7 if s["long"] else 4
	if side:
		rect(img, X.call(5 + lean) - (4 if flip else 0), y0 + 10, 5, 10, coat)
		rect(img, X.call(4 + lean) - (6 if flip else 0), y0 + 18, 7, skirt, coat)
		vline(img, X.call(4 + lean), y0 + 18, skirt, coat_lo)
		vline(img, X.call(8 + lean), y0 + 11, 7 + skirt, coat_hi)
	else:
		rect(img, 4, y0 + 10, 8, 10, coat)
		rect(img, 3, y0 + 18, 10, skirt, coat)
		vline(img, 3, y0 + 18, skirt, coat_lo)
		vline(img, 12, y0 + 18, skirt, coat_lo)
		vline(img, 4, y0 + 11, 7, coat_hi)
		if dir == "down":
			# lapels, belt, buttons
			vline(img, 7, y0 + 11, 7, coat_lo)
			vline(img, 8, y0 + 11, 7, coat_lo)
			hline(img, 4, y0 + 17, 8, accent.darkened(0.2))
			px(img, 8, y0 + 13, accent)
			px(img, 8, y0 + 15, accent)
			if s["apron"]:
				rect(img, 5, y0 + 17, 6, 7, CREAM)
		else:
			vline(img, 8, y0 + 18, skirt, coat_lo)
	# shoulders and arms
	var swing := 0 if idle else (2 if run else 1) * (1 if step < 2 else -1) * (0 if step % 2 == 1 else 1)
	if side:
		var ax: int = X.call(7 + lean + (swing if not flip else -swing))
		rect(img, ax, y0 + 11, 2, 7 if not run else 5, coat_lo)
		rect(img, ax, y0 + (18 if not run else 16), 2, 2, skin)
	else:
		rect(img, 2, y0 + 11 + maxi(0, swing), 2, 7, coat_lo)
		rect(img, 12, y0 + 11 + maxi(0, -swing), 2, 7, coat_lo)
		rect(img, 2, y0 + 18 + maxi(0, swing), 2, 2, skin)
		rect(img, 12, y0 + 18 + maxi(0, -swing), 2, 2, skin)
	# scarf / collar
	rect(img, 5 if not side else X.call(5 + lean) - (4 if flip else 0), y0 + 9, 6 if not side else 5, 2, accent)
	# head (adult proportions: about a fifth of the height)
	var hx := 5 + (lean if side else 0)
	if side and flip:
		hx = 16 - hx - 6
	rect(img, hx, y0 + 2, 6, 7, skin)
	vline(img, hx + (0 if flip else 5) if side else hx + 5, y0 + 3, 6, skin_lo)
	var style: String = s["hair"]
	if dir == "up":
		rect(img, hx, y0 + 1, 6, 7, hair)
		if style == "bun":
			rect(img, hx + 2, y0, 2, 2, hair)
		elif style == "long":
			rect(img, hx, y0 + 7, 6, 4, hair)
	else:
		rect(img, hx, y0 + 1, 6, 2, hair)
		if dir == "down":
			px(img, hx, y0 + 3, hair)
			px(img, hx + 5, y0 + 3, hair)
			px(img, hx + 1, y0 + 5, INK)
			px(img, hx + 4, y0 + 5, INK)
			px(img, hx + 2, y0 + 7, skin_lo)
			px(img, hx + 3, y0 + 7, skin_lo)
			if style == "long":
				vline(img, hx - 1, y0 + 2, 8, hair)
				vline(img, hx + 6, y0 + 2, 8, hair)
		else:
			var back := hx + (5 if flip else 0)
			vline(img, back, y0 + 2, 5, hair)
			rect(img, hx + (4 if flip else 0), y0 + 1, 2, 2, hair)
			var eye := hx + (1 if flip else 4)
			px(img, eye, y0 + 5, INK)
			px(img, hx + (-1 if flip else 6), y0 + 5, skin)
			if style == "long":
				vline(img, back, y0 + 7, 4, hair)
		if style == "bun" and dir != "up":
			rect(img, hx + (4 if flip else 0) if side else hx + 2, y0, 2, 2, hair)
	match s["hat"]:
		"hardhat":
			rect(img, hx - 1, y0, 8, 3, Color("d8a020"))
			hline(img, hx - 1, y0 + 3, 8, Color("a87818"))
			if dir != "up":
				px(img, hx + 3, y0 + 1, GLASS_CORE)
		"brim", "cap":
			rect(img, hx - (1 if s["hat"] == "brim" else 0), y0 + 1, 8 if s["hat"] == "brim" else 6, 1, accent.darkened(0.4))
			rect(img, hx, y0 - 1, 6, 2, accent.darkened(0.3))


==============================================================================
== tests/menu_test.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Headless check of the title screen, menu, options, character creation and the
## title <-> game flow.   Godot --headless --path . res://tests/menu_test.tscn
## Uses its own save folder and settings file, never the player's.

const DIR := "user://test_menu"

var _fails := 0
var _checks := 0
var app: Node
var title: TitleScreen


func _ready() -> void:
	DirAccess.make_dir_recursive_absolute(DIR)
	_wipe()
	SaveManager.dir = DIR
	SaveManager.autosave_enabled = true
	Settings.path = DIR.path_join("settings.cfg")
	await _boot()

	# --- the title at rest
	check(title.is_visible_in_tree() and not app.call("in_game") and app.get("main") == null, "boot shows the title and starts no game")
	check(WorldTime.is_paused(), "the world clock is paused at the title")
	var want := ["NEW GAME", "LOAD GAME", "OPTIONS"]
	if OS.has_feature("pc"):
		want.append("QUIT")
	check(Array(title.items()) == want, "menu offers %s (got %s)" % [str(want), str(title.items())])
	check(Audio.current_music == "title", "the title theme is requested (placeholder)")
	check(title.backdrop.planet.material is ShaderMaterial and title.ship != null, "the planet and the ship are in the scene")

	# --- keyboard and gamepad navigation
	var f := get_viewport().gui_get_focus_owner() as Button
	check(f != null and f.text == "NEW GAME", "the first entry has the focus")
	_action(&"ui_down")
	await _frames(2)
	f = get_viewport().gui_get_focus_owner() as Button
	check(f != null and f.text == "LOAD GAME" and Audio.last_ui == "move", "down moves the highlight and plays the move sound")
	var pad := InputEventJoypadButton.new()
	pad.button_index = JOY_BUTTON_DPAD_DOWN
	pad.pressed = true
	Input.parse_input_event(pad)
	await _frames(2)
	f = get_viewport().gui_get_focus_owner() as Button
	check(f != null and f.text == "OPTIONS", "the gamepad d-pad moves it too")
	pad = InputEventJoypadButton.new()
	pad.button_index = JOY_BUTTON_A
	pad.pressed = true
	Input.parse_input_event(pad)
	await _frames(2)
	pad = pad.duplicate()
	pad.pressed = false
	Input.parse_input_event(pad)
	await _frames(2)
	check(title.state() == "options" and Audio.last_ui in ["accept", "move"], "gamepad A opens Options")

	# --- options
	var music := title.slider("music")
	check(music != null and title.slider("master") != null and title.slider("sfx") != null, "Options has master, music and effects volume")
	music.value = 0.25
	var bus := AudioServer.get_bus_index("Music")
	check(bus >= 0 and absf(AudioServer.get_bus_volume_db(bus) - linear_to_db(0.25)) < 0.1, "the music slider sets the Music bus")
	title.slider("master").value = 0.0
	check(AudioServer.is_bus_mute(AudioServer.get_bus_index("Master")), "master at zero mutes")
	title.slider("master").value = 0.6
	check(not AudioServer.is_bus_mute(AudioServer.get_bus_index("Master")), "and unmutes when raised")
	_action(&"ui_cancel")
	await _frames(2)
	check(title.state() == "menu" and FileAccess.file_exists(Settings.path), "cancel returns to the menu and settings are saved")
	Settings.volume["music"] = 1.0
	Settings.load_settings()
	check(is_equal_approx(Settings.volume["music"], 0.25) and is_equal_approx(Settings.volume["master"], 0.6), "settings persist")

	# --- load with nothing valid to load
	var bad := FileAccess.open(DIR.path_join("save_broken.json"), FileAccess.WRITE)
	bad.store_string("{ not json")
	bad.close()
	var old := FileAccess.open(DIR.path_join("save_old.json"), FileAccess.WRITE)
	old.store_string(JSON.stringify({"version": 0, "world": {"map": "anjabi_town"}}))
	old.close()
	title.choose("LOAD GAME")
	await _frames(2)
	check(title.state() == "load" and title.listed_saves().is_empty(), "unreadable and old-version files are not offered")
	title.choose("BACK")
	await _frames(2)

	# --- new game leads to character creation, then the game
	title.choose("NEW GAME")
	await _frames(4)
	var cc: CharacterCreation = app.get("creation")
	check(cc != null and not app.call("in_game") and WorldTime.is_paused(), "NEW GAME opens character creation; still no game, clock still paused")
	cc.step("hair")
	cc.step("coat")
	cc.set_player_name("Tester")
	var prof := cc.profile()
	check(prof["player_name"] == "Tester" and prof["player_look"]["hair"] == "short" and prof["player_look"]["coat"] == "#1f3555", "creation builds a profile (%s)" % str(prof["player_look"]))
	cc.done.emit(prof)
	await _frames(8)
	var main: Node = app.get("main")
	check(app.call("in_game") and main != null and not title.is_visible_in_tree(), "BEGIN starts the game and hides the title")
	var player: Player3D = main.get("player")
	check(player != null and player.figure.hair == "short" and player.figure.coat.is_equal_approx(Color.html("#1f3555")), "the player wears the created look")
	check(str(GameState.get_flag("player_name", "")) == "Tester" and Journal.quests.has("quiet_mine"), "the name is kept and the opening quest begins")
	check(not WorldTime.is_paused() and WorldTime.time_text() == "6:40 PM" and Audio.current_music == "game", "the clock runs from the start of the game")

	# --- back to the title without losing the game
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0
	WorldTime.advance_minutes(45)
	player.global_position += Vector3(3, 0, 0)
	await _frames(4)
	var pos := player.global_position
	var ui: GameUi = main.get("ui")
	ui.toggle_menu()
	await _frames(2)
	check(ui.menu.title_enabled and ui.menu.find_children("*", "Button", true, false).any(func(b: Button) -> bool: return b.text == "Title screen" and b.visible), "the pause menu offers the title screen")
	ui.title_requested.emit()
	await _frames(6)
	check(not app.call("in_game") and app.get("main") == main and title.is_visible_in_tree() and not get_tree().paused, "returning shows the title and keeps the game loaded")
	check(Array(title.items())[0] == "CONTINUE" and not SaveManager.slot_info("auto").is_empty(), "the title now offers CONTINUE and the game was autosaved")
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 600.0
	var m0 := WorldTime.total_minutes()
	await _frames(20)
	check(WorldTime.total_minutes() == m0, "no time passes while at the title")
	title.choose("CONTINUE")
	await _frames(6)
	check(app.call("in_game") and app.get("main") == main and main.get("player") == player and player.global_position.distance_to(pos) < 0.2, "CONTINUE resumes the same game where it was left")
	check(WorldTime.time_text() == "7:25 PM" or WorldTime.total_minutes() > m0, "and the clock carries on from 7:25 PM")
	await _frames(10)
	check(WorldTime.total_minutes() > m0, "time runs again in the game")
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0
	check((player.camera as Camera3D).current, "the game camera is current again")

	# --- loading from the title bypasses creation
	check(SaveManager.save_game("manual"), "save in game")
	var saved_at := player.global_position
	var saved_clock := WorldTime.total_minutes()
	player.global_position += Vector3(-4, 0, 0)
	WorldTime.advance_minutes(200)
	app.call("return_to_title")
	await _frames(6)
	title.choose("LOAD GAME")
	await _frames(2)
	var listed := Array(title.listed_saves())
	check(listed.has("manual") and listed.has("auto") and not listed.has("broken") and not listed.has("old"), "LOAD GAME lists the valid slots (%s)" % str(listed))
	check(title.choose("MANUAL SAVE"), "the manual save can be chosen")
	await _frames(8)
	var main2: Node = app.get("main")
	check(app.call("in_game") and main2 != main and app.get("creation") == null, "loading starts the game directly, with no character creation")
	var p2: Player3D = main2.get("player")
	check(p2.global_position.distance_to(saved_at) < 0.3 and WorldTime.total_minutes() == saved_clock, "the save's position and clock are restored")
	check(p2.figure.hair == "short" and str(GameState.get_flag("player_name", "")) == "Tester", "the created character comes back with the save")

	# --- new game over a game in progress asks first
	app.call("return_to_title")
	await _frames(6)
	title.choose("NEW GAME")
	await _frames(4)
	check(app.get("creation") == null and Array(title.items()).any(func(t: String) -> bool: return t.contains("CONFIRM")), "NEW GAME over a game in progress asks for confirmation")
	title.choose("NEW GAME")
	await _frames(4)
	cc = app.get("creation")
	check(cc != null, "confirming opens character creation")
	cc.cancelled.emit()
	await _frames(4)
	check(app.get("creation") == null and title.state() == "menu" and app.get("main") == main2, "BACK returns to the title with the game untouched")

	# --- a fresh launch with an autosave offers CONTINUE
	app.queue_free()
	await _frames(3)
	await _boot()
	check(Array(title.items())[0] == "CONTINUE" and app.get("main") == null, "a fresh launch offers CONTINUE from the autosave")
	title.choose("CONTINUE")
	await _frames(8)
	check(app.call("in_game") and app.get("creation") == null, "which loads it without character creation")
	check((app.get("main").get("player") as Player3D).figure.hair == "short", "with the same character")

	app.queue_free()
	await _frames(2)
	_wipe()
	print("\n%d checks, %d failed" % [_checks, _fails])
	get_tree().quit(1 if _fails > 0 else 0)


func _boot() -> void:
	app = (load("res://game/app.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	# this test covers the title flow into the 3D town; tests/slice2d_test.gd covers the 2D slice
	app.set("start_map", "anjabi_town")
	app.set("animate", false)
	app.set("fade_time", 0.0)
	add_child(app)
	await _frames(4)
	title = app.get("title")


func _action(a: StringName) -> void:
	var e := InputEventAction.new()
	e.action = a
	e.pressed = true
	Input.parse_input_event(e)


func _wipe() -> void:
	var d := DirAccess.open(DIR)
	if d != null:
		for f in d.get_files():
			d.remove(f)


func _frames(n: int) -> void:
	for i in n:
		await get_tree().process_frame


func check(ok: bool, what: String) -> void:
	_checks += 1
	if not ok:
		_fails += 1
	print("%s  %s" % ["PASS" if ok else "FAIL", what])

== tests/menu_test.tscn

[gd_scene load_steps=2 format=3]

[ext_resource type="Script" path="res://tests/menu_test.gd" id="1"]

[node name="SliceTest" type="Node"]
script = ExtResource("1")


==============================================================================
== tests/overview.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Renders a top-down overview of the whole town for comparing with the concept map.
##   Godot --path . res://tests/overview.tscn -- /some/folder

func _ready() -> void:
	var dir := "user://"
	var args := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	if not args.is_empty():
		dir = args[0]
	SaveManager.autosave_enabled = false
	SaveManager.boot_from_autosave = false
	var main: Node = (load("res://game/main.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	add_child(main)
	await get_tree().create_timer(1.0).timeout
	main.get_node("UI").visible = false
	var cam := Camera3D.new()
	cam.projection = Camera3D.PROJECTION_ORTHOGONAL
	cam.size = 112.0
	cam.far = 400.0
	cam.position = Vector3(83, 150, 56)
	cam.rotation_degrees = Vector3(-90, 0, 0)
	add_child(cam)
	cam.current = true
	await get_tree().create_timer(1.5).timeout
	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
	get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join("overview_town.png"))
	get_tree().quit()

==============================================================================
== tests/perf_probe.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Windowed render-cost probe. Prints draw calls, primitives, objects and frame time
## at representative spots. A desktop proxy only; it is not an iPhone measurement.
##   Godot --path . res://tests/perf_probe.tscn

const O := preload("res://maps/anjabi/town_3d.gd").INSERT_AT


func _ready() -> void:
	# measure real frame cost: no vsync (Settings would otherwise turn it back on)
	Settings.vsync = false
	DisplayServer.window_set_vsync_mode(DisplayServer.VSYNC_DISABLED)
	SaveManager.autosave_enabled = false
	SaveManager.boot_from_autosave = false
	var main: Node = (load("res://game/main.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	add_child(main)
	# fixed night rain so runs are comparable; pass "day" after -- to measure a low evening sun
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0
	WorldTime.set_time(18 if "day" in OS.get_cmdline_user_args() else 21, 30)
	Weather.set_weather("rain")
	await get_tree().create_timer(1.0).timeout
	print("spot                 draws   prims(k)  objects  frame ms")
	var town_spots := {"steps": O + Vector3(2, 0.05, 5), "tavern terrace": O + Vector3(-6, 3.05, -11.5),
		"pods": Vector2(600, 150), "docks": Vector2(1170, 460), "beach": Vector2(650, 600), "estate": Vector2(240, 590)}
	for k: String in town_spots:
		await _measure(main, k, town_spots[k])
	main.call("load_map", "anjabi_mine", "default")
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.5).timeout
	for k: String in {"mine stairs": Vector3(8, 3.0, 7.5), "mine yard": Vector3(24, 0.05, 15.5), "mine spur": Vector3(39.5, 0.05, 21.5)}:
		await _measure(main, k, {"mine stairs": Vector3(8, 3.0, 7.5), "mine yard": Vector3(24, 0.05, 15.5), "mine spur": Vector3(39.5, 0.05, 21.5)}[k])
	get_tree().quit()


func _measure(main: Node, label: String, where: Variant) -> void:
	var p: Player3D = main.get("player")
	if where is Vector2:
		p.global_position = main.get("map").call("nearest_walk", where) + Vector3(0, 0.1, 0)
	else:
		p.global_position = where
	p.velocity = Vector3.ZERO
	p.camera.reset_smoothing()
	await get_tree().create_timer(1.0).timeout
	var t0 := Time.get_ticks_usec()
	var n := 60
	for i in n:
		await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
	var ms := (Time.get_ticks_usec() - t0) / 1000.0 / n
	var draws := RenderingServer.get_rendering_info(RenderingServer.RENDERING_INFO_TOTAL_DRAW_CALLS_IN_FRAME)
	var prims := RenderingServer.get_rendering_info(RenderingServer.RENDERING_INFO_TOTAL_PRIMITIVES_IN_FRAME)
	var objs := RenderingServer.get_rendering_info(RenderingServer.RENDERING_INFO_TOTAL_OBJECTS_IN_FRAME)
	print("%-20s %6d   %7.1f   %6d   %6.2f" % [label, draws, prims / 1000.0, objs, ms])

== tests/screenshot.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Windowed visual check: writes PNGs of a few moments to the folder given after "--".
##   Godot --path . res://tests/screenshot.tscn -- /some/folder

var main: Node
var dir := "user://"


func _ready() -> void:
	var args := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	if not args.is_empty():
		dir = args[0]
	SaveManager.autosave_enabled = false
	SaveManager.boot_from_autosave = false
	main = (load("res://game/main.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	add_child(main)
	await _wait(1.6)
	await _shot("01_spawn")
	main.get("player").global_position = Vector2(170, 270)
	await _wait(1.0)
	await _shot("01b_stair_foot")
	var player: Player = main.get("player")
	var ui: GameUi = main.get_node("UI")
	player.global_position = Vector2(516, 282)
	await _wait(1.0)
	player.try_interact()
	await _wait(1.2)
	Dialogue.advance()
	await _wait(1.2)
	await _shot("02_guard_dialogue")
	Dialogue.advance()
	await _wait(0.6)
	await _shot("03_choices")
	Dialogue.cancel()
	player.global_position = Vector2(300, 620)
	await _wait(1.0)
	await _shot("04_pods")
	player.global_position = Vector2(790, 470)
	await _wait(1.0)
	await _shot("05_spur")
	GameState.set_flag("anjabi.cart", "trench")
	main.call("restore_world", {"map": "anjabi_mine", "x": 500.0, "y": 150.0})
	await _wait(1.0)
	await _shot("06_yard")
	GameState.apply([{"clue": "closure_notice"}, {"clue": "early_recall"}, {"objective": "quiet_mine.notice"}, {"reveal": "quiet_mine.way_in"}])
	ui.toggle_journal()
	await _wait(0.4)
	await _shot("07_journal")
	ui.close_panels()
	ui.toggle_menu()
	await _wait(0.4)
	await _shot("08_menu")
	get_tree().quit()


func _wait(s: float) -> void:
	await get_tree().create_timer(s, true, false, true).timeout


func _shot(shot_name: String) -> void:
	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
	var img := get_viewport().get_texture().get_image()
	img.save_png(dir.path_join(shot_name + ".png"))

== tests/screenshot_2d.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Windowed shots of the 2D prototype (the Steps baked to 2D) at several times and
## weathers, plus walking and running.  Godot --path . res://tests/screenshot_2d.tscn -- /some/folder

func _ready() -> void:
	var dir := "user://"
	var args := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	if not args.is_empty():
		dir = args[0]
	SaveManager.autosave_enabled = false
	var main: Node = (load("res://game/main.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	main.set("boot", "proto2d")
	add_child(main)
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0
	await get_tree().create_timer(1.0).timeout
	var p: Player2D = main.get("player")
	var S := 16.0
	var looks := [["p2d_01_steps_day", Vector2(2, 5), 13, "clear"], ["p2d_02_terrace_golden", Vector2(-6, -12), 18, "clear"],
		["p2d_03_steps_night_rain", Vector2(2, 5), 22, "rain"], ["p2d_04_pods_morning", Vector2(4, -34), 9, "cloudy"],
		["p2d_05_mine_fog", Vector2(-30, 2), 8, "fog"], ["p2d_06_steps_snow", Vector2(4, 2), 21, "snow"]]
	for l: Array in looks:
		WorldTime.set_time(l[2], 0)
		Weather.set_weather(l[3])
		for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"environment_controller"):
			n.call(&"snap")
		p.position = main.get("map").call("nearest_standable", (l[1] as Vector2) * S)
		await get_tree().create_timer(1.0).timeout
		await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
		get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join(str(l[0]) + ".png"))
	WorldTime.set_time(16, 0)
	Weather.set_weather("clear")
	p.position = Vector2(-4, 6) * S
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.5).timeout
	Input.action_press(&"move_right")
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.5).timeout
	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
	get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join("p2d_07_walk.png"))
	Input.action_press(&"sprint")
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.5).timeout
	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
	get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join("p2d_08_run.png"))
	Input.action_release(&"move_right")
	Input.action_release(&"sprint")
	get_tree().quit()

== tests/screenshot_3d.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Windowed visual check of the 3D district. Usage as tests/screenshot.gd:
##   Godot --path . res://tests/screenshot_3d.tscn -- /some/folder

func _ready() -> void:
	var dir := "user://"
	var args := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	if not args.is_empty():
		dir = args[0]
	SaveManager.autosave_enabled = false
	SaveManager.boot_from_autosave = false
	var main: Node = (load("res://game/main.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	add_child(main)
	# these shots are the fixed night-rain reference; time-of-day shots are in screenshot_time
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0
	WorldTime.set_time(21, 30)
	Weather.set_weather("rain")
	await get_tree().create_timer(1.0).timeout
	var o: Vector3 = preload("res://maps/anjabi/town_3d.gd").INSERT_AT  # the Steps insert in the town
	var shots := {
		"3d_01_steps": o + Vector3(2, 0.05, 5),
		"3d_02_tavern": o + Vector3(-6, 3.05, -11.5),
		"3d_03_steps_east": o + Vector3(21, 0.05, 1),
		"3d_04_terrace_west": o + Vector3(-22, 3.05, -18),
	}
	for k: String in shots:
		var player: Player3D = main.get("player")
		player.global_position = shots[k]
		player.velocity = Vector3.ZERO
		player.camera.reset_smoothing()
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.8).timeout
		await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
		get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join(k + ".png"))
	# walking pose and a conversation
	var p: Player3D = main.get("player")
	p.global_position = o + Vector3(-5.6, 3.05, -10.9)
	p.camera.reset_smoothing()
	Input.action_press(&"move_left")
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.3).timeout
	Input.action_release(&"move_left")
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.5).timeout
	p.try_interact()
	await get_tree().create_timer(1.2).timeout
	Dialogue.advance()
	await get_tree().create_timer(1.2).timeout
	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
	get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join("3d_07_dialogue.png"))
	Dialogue.cancel()
	# the mine yard
	main.call("load_map", "anjabi_mine", "default")
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.5).timeout
	var mine := {
		"3d_10_mine_stairs": Vector3(8, 3.0, 7.5),
		"3d_11_mine_gate": Vector3(24, 0.05, 15.5),
		"3d_12_mine_washhouse": Vector3(16, 0.05, 31.5),
		"3d_13_mine_spur": Vector3(39.5, 0.05, 21.5),
		"3d_14_mine_door": Vector3(24, 0.05, 8.5),
	}
	for k: String in mine:
		var mp: Player3D = main.get("player")
		mp.global_position = mine[k]
		mp.velocity = Vector3.ZERO
		mp.camera.reset_smoothing()
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.8).timeout
		await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
		get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join(k + ".png"))
	GameState.set_flag("anjabi.cart", "trench")
	main.call("restore_world", {"map": "anjabi_mine", "dim": 3, "x": 40.0, "y": 0.05, "z": 18.6, "fx": 0.0, "fz": -1.0})
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.8).timeout
	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
	get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join("3d_15_mine_bridge.png"))
	# the whole town
	main.call("load_map", "anjabi_town", "default")
	await get_tree().create_timer(0.5).timeout
	var town := {
		"3d_20_town_steps": Vector2(880, 415), "3d_21_town_tavern": Vector2(760, 350),
		"3d_22_town_pods": Vector2(600, 150), "3d_23_town_tion": Vector2(515, 465),
		"3d_24_town_beach": Vector2(650, 600), "3d_25_town_docks": Vector2(1170, 460),
		"3d_26_town_estate": Vector2(240, 590), "3d_27_town_guard": Vector2(945, 160), "3d_28_town_stairs_beach": Vector2(600, 545), "3d_29_town_stairs_pods": Vector2(650, 245),
	}
	for k: String in town:
		var tp: Player3D = main.get("player")
		tp.global_position = main.get("map").call("nearest_walk", town[k]) + Vector3(0, 0.1, 0)
		tp.velocity = Vector3.ZERO
		tp.camera.reset_smoothing()
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.8).timeout
		await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
		get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join(k + ".png"))
	get_tree().quit()

== tests/screenshot_overhaul.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Windowed shots of the market quarter of the Steps and the arrival shot.
##   Godot --path . res://tests/screenshot_overhaul.tscn -- /some/folder

const O: Vector3 = preload("res://maps/anjabi/town_3d.gd").INSERT_AT


func _ready() -> void:
	var dir := "user://"
	var args := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	if not args.is_empty():
		dir = args[0]
	SaveManager.autosave_enabled = false
	var main: Node = (load("res://game/main.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	add_child(main)
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0
	await get_tree().create_timer(1.2).timeout
	var p: Player3D = main.get("player")
	# arrival shot at dusk, clear
	WorldTime.set_time(18, 50)
	Weather.set_weather("clear")
	_snap()
	p.global_position = O + Vector3(2, 0.05, 6)
	p.camera.play_intro(8.0)
	await get_tree().create_timer(1.5).timeout
	await _shot(dir, "ov_01_arrival")
	await get_tree().create_timer(3.4).timeout
	await _shot(dir, "ov_02_arrival_sweep")
	await get_tree().create_timer(3.5).timeout
	var spots := [["ov_03_plaza_dusk", Vector3(2, 0.05, 5), 18, 50, "clear"], ["ov_04_market_night", Vector3(-4, 0.05, -2), 21, 30, "clear"],
		["ov_05_plaza_day", Vector3(2, 0.05, 4), 11, 0, "clear"], ["ov_06_terrace_houses", Vector3(4, 3.05, -19), 19, 30, "clear"],
		["ov_07_garden", Vector3(-17, 3.05, -18), 17, 30, "clear"], ["ov_08_plaza_rain_night", Vector3(2, 0.05, 5), 22, 0, "rain"]]
	for s: Array in spots:
		WorldTime.set_time(s[2], s[3])
		Weather.set_weather(s[4])
		_snap()
		p.global_position = O + (s[1] as Vector3)
		p.velocity = Vector3.ZERO
		p.camera.reset_smoothing()
		await get_tree().create_timer(1.0).timeout
		await _shot(dir, s[0])
	get_tree().quit()


func _snap() -> void:
	for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"environment_controller"):
		n.call(&"snap")


func _shot(dir: String, name: String) -> void:
	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
	get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join(name + ".png"))

== tests/screenshot_time.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Windowed shots of the same spots through the day and in each weather, plus the
## run pose. Usage:  Godot --path . res://tests/screenshot_time.tscn -- /some/folder

func _ready() -> void:
	var dir := "user://"
	var args := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	if not args.is_empty():
		dir = args[0]
	SaveManager.autosave_enabled = false
	SaveManager.boot_from_autosave = false
	var main: Node = (load("res://game/main.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	add_child(main)
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0
	await get_tree().create_timer(1.0).timeout
	var o: Vector3 = preload("res://maps/anjabi/town_3d.gd").INSERT_AT
	var p: Player3D = main.get("player")
	var looks := [
		["t_01_dawn_clear", 5, 50, "clear"], ["t_02_morning_clear", 9, 0, "clear"], ["t_03_midday_clear", 13, 0, "clear"],
		["t_04_golden_clear", 18, 40, "clear"], ["t_05_dusk_clear", 19, 45, "clear"], ["t_06_night_clear", 23, 0, "clear"],
		["t_07_midday_cloudy", 13, 0, "cloudy"], ["t_08_midday_rain", 13, 0, "rain"], ["t_09_evening_heavy", 18, 40, "heavy_rain"],
		["t_10_morning_fog", 8, 0, "fog"], ["t_11_midday_snow", 13, 0, "snow"], ["t_12_night_snow", 22, 0, "snow"],
	]
	for l: Array in looks:
		WorldTime.set_time(l[1], l[2])
		Weather.set_weather(l[3])
		for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"environment_controller"):
			n.call(&"snap")
		p.global_position = o + Vector3(2, 0.05, 5)
		p.velocity = Vector3.ZERO
		p.camera.reset_smoothing()
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.9).timeout
		await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
		get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join(str(l[0]) + ".png"))
	# other places at midday and golden hour
	for l: Array in [["t_20_docks_midday", Vector2(1170, 460), 13, 0], ["t_21_pods_golden", Vector2(600, 150), 18, 30], ["t_22_beach_dawn", Vector2(650, 600), 6, 10]]:
		WorldTime.set_time(l[2], l[3])
		Weather.set_weather("clear")
		for n in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"environment_controller"):
			n.call(&"snap")
		p.global_position = main.get("map").call("nearest_walk", l[1]) + Vector3(0, 0.1, 0)
		p.velocity = Vector3.ZERO
		p.camera.reset_smoothing()
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.9).timeout
		await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
		get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join(str(l[0]) + ".png"))
	# walk and run poses
	WorldTime.set_time(16, 30)
	Weather.set_weather("clear")
	for l: Array in [["t_30_walk", false], ["t_31_run", true]]:
		p.global_position = o + Vector3(-8, 0.05, 6)
		p.camera.reset_smoothing()
		Input.action_press(&"move_right")
		if l[1]:
			Input.action_press(&"sprint")
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.62).timeout
		await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
		get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join(str(l[0]) + ".png"))
		Input.action_release(&"move_right")
		Input.action_release(&"sprint")
		await get_tree().create_timer(0.4).timeout
	get_tree().quit()

== tests/screenshot_title.gd

extends Node
## Windowed shots of the title screen, its panels and character creation.
##   Godot --path . res://tests/screenshot_title.tscn -- /some/folder

func _ready() -> void:
	var dir := "user://"
	var args := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	if not args.is_empty():
		dir = args[0]
	SaveManager.autosave_enabled = false
	var app: Node = (load("res://game/app.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	app.set("fade_time", 0.0)
	add_child(app)
	var title: TitleScreen = app.get("title")
	await _shot(dir, "title_01_menu", 6.0)
	await _shot(dir, "title_02_later", 8.0)
	title.show_options()
	await _shot(dir, "title_03_options", 0.6)
	title.show_load()
	await _shot(dir, "title_04_load", 0.6)
	title.show_menu()
	title.choose("NEW GAME")
	await _shot(dir, "title_05_character", 1.5)
	get_tree().quit()


func _shot(dir: String, name: String, wait: float) -> void:
	await get_tree().create_timer(wait).timeout
	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
	get_viewport().get_texture().get_image().save_png(dir.path_join(name + ".png"))

## Windowed visual check of the town greybox. Usage as tests/screenshot.gd.

func _ready() -> void:
	var dir := "user://"

		dir = args[0]
