==============================================================================
== tests/slice2d_test.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Headless test of the 2D tile-based vertical slice (branch 2d): the Steps and the
## shop. Grid movement, turning, running, collision, depth sorting, the NPC and its
## dialogue, the quest step, doors in and out, lighting, saves, and the title flow.
##   Godot --headless --path . res://tests/slice2d_test.tscn

const DIR := "user://test_slice2d"

var _fails := 0
var _checks := 0
var main: Node
var p: GridPlayer
var world: TileWorld


func _ready() -> void:
	DirAccess.make_dir_recursive_absolute(DIR)
	SaveManager.dir = DIR
	SaveManager.autosave_enabled = false
	SaveManager.boot_from_autosave = false
	main = (load("res://game/main.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	main.set("start_map", "anjabi_steps")
	add_child(main)
	await _frames(6)
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0
	world = main.get("map")
	p = main.get("player")
	check(world is TileWorld and p is GridPlayer and str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_steps", "a new game starts on the 2D Steps with a grid player")
	check(world.size == Vector2i(40, 30), "the Steps map is 40 x 30 tiles")
	check(p.tile == Vector2i(19, 22) and p.dir_name() == "up", "the player spawns south of the compass facing it")
	var vp := world.get_viewport() as SubViewport
	var box := vp.get_parent() as SubViewportContainer
	check(vp != null and vp.size.x == 240 and box.stretch_shrink == 2, "the world renders 240 px (15 tiles) wide and is scaled up 2x (%s)" % str(vp.size if vp else ""))
	check(Journal.quests.has("quiet_mine"), "the opening quest begins")

	# a tap turns without moving; holding walks one tile per step, smoothly
	await _tap(&"move_left", 2)
	check(p.tile == Vector2i(19, 22) and p.dir_name() == "left", "a tap turns in place")
	var mid := false
	Input.action_press(&"move_left")
	var t_end := Time.get_ticks_msec() + 600
	while Time.get_ticks_msec() < t_end:
		await get_tree().process_frame
		if p.is_moving() and fposmod(p.position.x - 8.0, 16.0) > 2.0 and fposmod(p.position.x - 8.0, 16.0) < 14.0:
			mid = true
	Input.action_release(&"move_left")
	await _settle()
	check(mid, "a step glides between tiles")
	check(p.tile.y == 22 and p.tile.x <= 17 and p.tile.x >= 15, "holding walks tile by tile (now at %s)" % str(p.tile))
	check(fposmod(p.position.x - 8.0, 16.0) < 0.01, "the player stops exactly on a tile")
	# running covers more ground in the same time
	p.place(Vector2i(10, 21), "right")
	await _frames(2)
	var x0 := p.tile.x
	await _hold(&"move_right", 0.5)
	var walked := p.tile.x - x0
	p.place(Vector2i(10, 21), "right")
	await _frames(2)
	Input.action_press(&"sprint")
	await _hold(&"move_right", 0.5)
	Input.action_release(&"sprint")
	var ran := p.tile.x - 10
	check(ran > walked, "running covers more tiles than walking (%d vs %d)" % [ran, walked])

	# collision: the terrace wall, the balustrade, props, the map edge
	p.place(Vector2i(10, 13), "up")
	await _hold(&"move_up", 0.6)
	check(p.tile == Vector2i(10, 13), "the terrace wall blocks")
	p.place(Vector2i(20, 21), "up")
	await _hold(&"move_up", 0.4)
	check(p.tile == Vector2i(20, 20) or p.tile == Vector2i(20, 21), "the armillary's plinth blocks (at %s)" % str(p.tile))
	check(world.is_solid(Vector2i(19, 19)) and world.is_solid(Vector2i(8, 17)), "landmark and lamps are solid")
	p.place(Vector2i(10, 24), "down")
	await _hold(&"move_down", 0.4)
	check(p.tile == Vector2i(10, 24), "the south balustrade blocks")
	# the stairs climb to the terrace under the arch
	p.place(Vector2i(19, 13), "up")
	await _hold(&"move_up", 1.6)
	check(p.tile.y <= 9 and p.tile.x == 19, "the stairs lead up to the terrace (at %s)" % str(p.tile))
	var arch: TileMapLayer = world.get_node("Over")
	check(arch.get_cell_source_id(Vector2i(19, 10)) >= 0 and arch.z_index > p.z_index, "an arch over the stairs is drawn above the player")

	# depth sorting: north of a lamp the player is drawn behind it, south in front
	var lamp: Node2D = null
	for n in world.entities.get_children():
		if n.name.begins_with("Prop_lamp") and world.tile_of(n.position) == Vector2i(8, 17):
			lamp = n
	check(lamp != null and world.entities.y_sort_enabled, "props and people share a y-sorted layer")
	p.place(Vector2i(8, 16), "down")
	check(p.position.y < lamp.position.y, "standing north of the lamp sorts behind it")
	p.place(Vector2i(8, 18), "up")
	check(p.position.y > lamp.position.y, "standing south of the lamp sorts in front of it")

	# the tavern keeper: talk, dialogue, the quest step
	var keeper: GridNpc = world.entities.get_node("Npc_tavern_keeper")
	check(keeper != null and keeper.tile == Vector2i(9, 8), "the tavern keeper stands on the terrace")
	check(not world.can_enter(Vector2i(9, 8)), "people block their tile")
	p.place(Vector2i(9, 9), "up")
	await _frames(3)
	check(p.target == keeper.interactable and main.get("ui").hud.prompt_text().contains("Tavern keeper"), "facing the keeper offers Talk")
	p.try_interact()
	await _frames(2)
	check(Dialogue.active and keeper.dir_name() == "down", "talking opens the dialogue and the keeper turns to the player")
	await _talk(["Where"])
	check(Journal.has_clue("mine_directions") and GameState.get_flag("tavern_keeper.met") == true, "asking the way records a clue in the journal (quest step)")

	# the shop door: in, look, out
	p.place(Vector2i(35, 18), "up")
	await _frames(3)
	check(p.target != null and p.target.label == "Shop", "the shop door offers Enter")
	await _hold(&"move_up", 0.4)
	await _frames(6)
	p = main.get("player")
	world = main.get("map")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_steps_shop" and p.tile == Vector2i(5, 8), "stepping into the door enters the shop")
	check(world.indoor and world.lighting.indoor, "the shop is lit as an interior")
	p.place(Vector2i(2, 3), "up")
	await _frames(3)
	check(p.target != null and p.target.label == "Shelves", "the shelves can be examined")
	p.place(Vector2i(5, 8), "down")
	await _hold(&"move_down", 0.4)
	await _frames(6)
	p = main.get("player")
	world = main.get("map")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_steps" and p.tile == Vector2i(35, 18) and p.dir_name() == "down", "the mat leads back out in front of the shop door")

	# lighting follows the clock
	var tint: CanvasModulate = world.find_children("*", "CanvasModulate", true, false)[0]
	Weather.set_weather("clear")
	WorldTime.set_time(13, 0)
	await _frames(3)
	var day := tint.color.get_luminance()
	WorldTime.set_time(23, 0)
	await _frames(3)
	var lamps := world.find_children("*", "PointLight2D", true, false)
	check(tint.color.get_luminance() < day * 0.6 and lamps.size() >= 6 and (lamps[0] as PointLight2D).visible, "night darkens the Steps and the lamps light")

	# save and load
	p.place(Vector2i(12, 20), "left")
	await _frames(2)
	check(SaveManager.save_game("s2"), "save on the 2D Steps")
	main.call("load_map", "anjabi_steps_shop", "default")
	await _frames(4)
	check(SaveManager.load_game("s2"), "load the save")
	await _frames(4)
	p = main.get("player")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_steps" and p.tile == Vector2i(12, 20) and Journal.has_clue("mine_directions"), "load restores map, tile and journal")

	# the title starts the 2D slice
	main.queue_free()
	await _frames(3)
	var app: Node = (load("res://game/app.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	app.set("animate", false)
	app.set("fade_time", 0.0)
	add_child(app)
	await _frames(4)
	var title: TitleScreen = app.get("title")
	title.choose("NEW GAME")
	await _frames(4)
	(app.get("creation") as CharacterCreation).done.emit({"player_name": "", "player_look": {"hair": "long", "coat": "#1f3555"}})
	await _frames(8)
	var m2: Node = app.get("main")
	check(m2 != null and str(m2.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_steps" and m2.get("player") is GridPlayer, "NEW GAME from the title starts the 2D slice")
	app.queue_free()
	await _frames(2)
	SaveManager.delete_save("s2")
	print("\n%d checks, %d failed" % [_checks, _fails])
	get_tree().quit(1 if _fails > 0 else 0)


func _tap(a: StringName, frames: int) -> void:
	Input.action_press(a)
	await _frames(frames)
	Input.action_release(a)
	await _settle()


func _hold(a: StringName, seconds: float) -> void:
	Input.action_press(a)
	await get_tree().create_timer(seconds).timeout
	Input.action_release(a)
	await _settle()


func _settle() -> void:
	for i in 60:
		await get_tree().process_frame
		if not is_instance_valid(p) or not p.is_moving():
			break
	await _frames(2)


func _talk(prefer: Array) -> void:
	var want := prefer.duplicate()
	for i in 200:
		if not Dialogue.active:
			break
		var opts := Dialogue.choice_texts()
		if opts.is_empty():
			Dialogue.advance()
		else:
			var pick := opts.size() - 1
			for j in opts.size():
				if not want.is_empty() and opts[j].contains(str(want[0])):
					pick = j
					want.pop_front()
					break
			Dialogue.choose(pick)
		await get_tree().process_frame


func _frames(n: int) -> void:
	for i in n:
		await get_tree().process_frame


func check(ok: bool, what: String) -> void:
	_checks += 1
	if not ok:
		_fails += 1
	print("%s  %s" % ["PASS" if ok else "FAIL", what])

==============================================================================
== tests/slice_test.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Headless end-to-end check of the Anjabi slice.
##   Godot --headless --path . res://tests/slice_test.tscn

var _fails := 0
var _checks := 0
var main: Node
var player


func _ready() -> void:
	SaveManager.autosave_enabled = false
	SaveManager.boot_from_autosave = false
	main = (load("res://game/main.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	add_child(main)
	await _frames(3)
	await _district()
	main.call("load_map", "anjabi_mine", "default")
	await _frames(3)
	await _run()
	await _town()
	await _world()
	await _interiors()
	await _proto2d()
	SaveManager.delete_save("test")
	print("\n%d checks, %d failed" % [_checks, _fails])
	get_tree().quit(1 if _fails > 0 else 0)


func _run() -> void:
	player = main.get("player")
	var map = main.get("map")
	check(player != null and map is GameMap3D and player is Player3D, "the mine yard is a 3D map with a 3D player")
	check(Journal.quests.has("quiet_mine"), "new game opens the investigation")
	check(player.global_position.distance_to(Vector3(8, 6.05, 1.2)) < 0.3, "player starts at the top of the Estate stairs")

	# movement, and the real 6 m stair down to the yard
	var z0: float = player.global_position.z
	Input.action_press(&"move_down")
	await _frames(30)
	Input.action_release(&"move_down")
	check(player.global_position.z > z0 + 1.0, "keyboard movement moves the player (%.2f m)" % (player.global_position.z - z0))
	Input.action_press(&"move_up")
	await _frames(120)
	Input.action_release(&"move_up")
	check(player.global_position.z > 0.0 and player.global_position.y > 5.8, "the top of the stairs is closed off (z=%.2f)" % player.global_position.z)
	Input.action_press(&"move_down")
	await _frames(240)
	Input.action_release(&"move_down")
	await _frames(10)
	check(player.global_position.y < 0.2 and player.global_position.z > 12.6 and player.is_on_floor(), "the stairs lead down into the yard (y=%.2f z=%.2f)" % [player.global_position.y, player.global_position.z])

	# touch stick
	var ui: GameUi = main.get_node("UI")
	var x0: float = player.global_position.x
	ui.touch.vector = Vector2.RIGHT
	await _frames(20)
	ui.touch.vector = Vector2.ZERO
	check(player.global_position.x > x0 + 0.5, "touch stick vector moves the player")

	# collision with the gate
	await _place3(Vector3(24, 0.05, 14.5))
	Input.action_press(&"move_up")
	await _frames(90)
	Input.action_release(&"move_up")
	check(player.global_position.z > 12.5, "the gate blocks the road (z=%.2f)" % player.global_position.z)

	# notice
	await _place3(Vector3(21.25, 0.05, 13.1))
	check(player.target != null and player.target.label == "Notice", "notice is targeted")
	check(ui.hud.prompt_text().contains("Notice"), "HUD prompt shows '%s'" % ui.hud.prompt_text())
	check(player.try_interact() and Dialogue.active, "interacting opens dialogue")
	var at: Vector3 = player.global_position
	Input.action_press(&"move_down")
	await _frames(10)
	Input.action_release(&"move_down")
	check(player.global_position.distance_to(at) < 0.05, "player cannot walk during dialogue")
	await _talk([])
	check(Journal.has_clue("closure_notice"), "notice records a clue")
	check(Journal.objective_state("quiet_mine", "notice") == Journal.DONE, "notice objective done")

	# guard before the driller: no way to press
	await _place3(Vector3(26.4, 0.05, 14.3))
	check(player.target != null and player.target.label == "Gate guard", "gate guard is targeted")
	player.try_interact()
	check(Dialogue.active, "guard dialogue starts")
	await _talk(["The crew was pulled"])
	check(not Journal.has_clue("power_cut"), "guard cannot be pressed without the driller's account")

	# driller
	await _place3(Vector3(16.8, 0.05, 31.0))
	check(player.target != null and player.target.label == "Driller", "driller is targeted")
	player.try_interact()
	await _talk(["What happened at the mine", "Who gave"])
	check(Journal.has_clue("early_recall"), "driller records a clue")

	# guard again
	await _place3(Vector3(26.4, 0.05, 14.3))
	player.try_interact()
	await _talk(["The crew was pulled", "another way"])
	check(Journal.has_clue("power_cut"), "pressing the guard records a clue")
	check(Journal.objective_state("quiet_mine", "way_in") == Journal.ACTIVE, "guard reveals the hidden objective")

	# puzzle: wrong branch first
	var cart: OreCart3D = map.get_node("Entities/OreCart")
	await _place3(Vector3(40, 0.05, 30.2))
	check(player.target != null and player.target.label == "Ore cart", "cart is targeted")
	player.try_interact()
	await _until(func() -> bool: return not cart.moving)
	check(cart.state() == "buffer", "points at A: cart rolls to the buffer")
	await _talk([])
	await _place3(Vector3(40, 0.05, 18.4))
	Input.action_press(&"move_up")
	await _frames(60)
	Input.action_release(&"move_up")
	check(player.global_position.z > 17.0 and player.global_position.y > -0.2, "washout blocks the spur (z=%.2f)" % player.global_position.z)
	await _place3(Vector3(43.4, 0.05, 19.3))
	check(player.target != null and player.target.label == "Ore cart", "cart at the buffer is targeted")
	player.try_interact()
	await _until(func() -> bool: return not cart.moving)
	check(cart.state() == "start", "cart can be hauled back")
	# lever, then the right branch
	await _place3(Vector3(38.6, 0.05, 23.4))
	check(player.target != null and player.target.label == "Points lever", "lever is targeted")
	player.try_interact()
	check(str(GameState.get_flag("anjabi.spur_switch")) == "b", "lever sets the points to B")
	await _place3(Vector3(40, 0.05, 30.2))
	player.try_interact()
	await _until(func() -> bool: return not cart.moving)
	check(cart.state() == "trench", "points at B: cart drops into the washout")
	await _talk([])
	check(Journal.objective_state("quiet_mine", "way_in") == Journal.DONE, "bridging completes the objective")
	await _place3(Vector3(40, 0.05, 18.4))
	Input.action_press(&"move_up")
	await _frames(80)
	Input.action_release(&"move_up")
	check(player.global_position.z < 14.8 and player.global_position.y > -0.2, "player can cross the cart bridge (z=%.2f y=%.2f)" % [player.global_position.z, player.global_position.y])
	Input.action_press(&"move_up")
	Input.action_press(&"move_left")
	await _frames(60)
	Input.action_release(&"move_up")
	Input.action_release(&"move_left")
	check(player.global_position.z < 12.4, "the corridor behind the washout opens into the inner yard (z=%.2f)" % player.global_position.z)

	# mine door
	await _place3(Vector3(24, 0.05, 6.6))
	check(player.target != null and player.target.label == "Mine door", "mine door is targeted")
	player.try_interact()
	await _talk([])
	check(Journal.has_clue("sealed_outside"), "mine door records the final clue")
	check(Journal.is_complete("quiet_mine"), "investigation is marked complete")

	# journal panel
	ui.toggle_journal()
	check(ui.journal.visible and get_tree().paused, "journal opens and pauses the world")
	ui.close_panels()
	check(not get_tree().paused, "closing the journal resumes")

	# save / load
	await _place3(Vector3(30, 0.05, 9))
	check(SaveManager.save_game("test"), "save writes a file")
	main.call("new_game")
	await _frames(3)
	player = main.get("player")
	check(Journal.clues.is_empty() and not GameState.flags.has("anjabi.cart"), "new game clears progress")
	check(SaveManager.load_game("test"), "load reads the file")
	await _frames(3)
	player = main.get("player")
	map = main.get("map")
	cart = map.get_node("Entities/OreCart")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_mine" and player.global_position.distance_to(Vector3(30, 0.05, 9)) < 0.3, "load restores the player position")
	check(Journal.has_clue("closure_notice") and Journal.has_clue("early_recall") and Journal.has_clue("power_cut") and Journal.has_clue("sealed_outside") and Journal.is_complete("quiet_mine"), "load restores the journal")
	check(cart.state() == "trench" and cart.global_position.distance_to(Vector3(40, 0, 16)) < 0.1, "load restores the puzzle state")
	await _place3(Vector3(40, 0.05, 14.0))
	Input.action_press(&"move_down")
	await _frames(80)
	Input.action_release(&"move_down")
	check(player.global_position.z > 17.5 and player.global_position.y > -0.2, "bridge is still walkable after load (z=%.2f)" % player.global_position.z)
	# follow-up dialogue reacts to state
	await _place3(Vector3(16.8, 0.05, 31.0))
	player.try_interact()
	await _talk([])
	check(GameState.get_flag("driller.told_seal") == true, "driller reacts to the sealed door")


func _town() -> void:
	# travel: mine -> town -> mine
	await _place3(Vector3(8, 6.05, 1.0))
	check(player.target is MapExit3D, "the Estate stairs are an exit")
	player.try_interact()
	await _frames(4)
	player = main.get("player")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_town" and player is Player3D, "the mine stairs lead up into the town")
	await _frames(10)
	player = main.get("player")
	var town = main.get("map")
	check(town is GameMap3D and player is Player3D, "the town is a 3D map with a 3D player")
	var meshes: int = town.find_children("*", "GeometryInstance3D", true, false).size()
	check(meshes > 300, "the town is built from real meshes (%d)" % meshes)
	await _frames(20)
	check(player.is_on_floor(), "player arrives standing on the town ground (y=%.2f)" % player.global_position.y)
	check(player.target is MapExit3D, "arrives beside the way back to the mine")
	# every landmark from the greybox is on solid, walkable ground
	var landmarks := {"Steps": Vector2(880, 415), "Forest": Vector2(230, 110), "Garden": Vector2(200, 345),
		"Estate": Vector2(240, 578), "Mine": Vector2(212, 700), "Airpad": Vector2(450, 800),
		"Beach": Vector2(650, 640), "Tion Tower": Vector2(515, 465), "Tavern": Vector2(760, 350),
		"Pods": Vector2(580, 70), "Guard Tower": Vector2(945, 130), "Docks": Vector2(1170, 460),
		"Shop": Vector2(930, 385), "Tram": Vector2(970, 465), "Rainforest": Vector2(1380, 325)}
	var bad := []
	for k: String in landmarks:
		var at: Vector3 = town.nearest_walk(landmarks[k])
		await _place3(at + Vector3(0, 0.3, 0))
		await _frames(10)
		if not player.is_on_floor() or absf(player.global_position.y - at.y) > 0.6:
			bad.append(k)
	check(bad.is_empty(), "the player stands at all 15 landmarks %s" % ("" if bad.is_empty() else str(bad)))
	# the Steps: district title, walking, real height differences across town
	await _place3(town.nearest_walk(Vector2(880, 415)) + Vector3(0, 0.1, 0))
	await _frames(20)
	check(main.get_node("UI").hud._title.text.contains("THE STEPS"), "district title shows '%s'" % main.get_node("UI").hud._title.text)
	var x0: float = player.global_position.x
	Input.action_press(&"move_left")
	await _frames(40)
	Input.action_release(&"move_left")
	check(player.global_position.x < x0 - 1.0, "player walks in town")
	var hp: float = town.nearest_walk(Vector2(580, 70)).y
	var hb: float = town.nearest_walk(Vector2(650, 640)).y
	check(hp - hb > 10.0, "the pods stand %.1f m above the beach" % (hp - hb))
	# leave the Steps west along the walkway toward Tion Tower: the ground rises
	var zw: float = _walk_z(town, O.x - 14.5, -9, 9)
	await _place3(Vector3(O.x - 14.5, town.ground(O.x - 14.5, O.z + zw) + 0.1, O.z + zw))
	await _frames(10)
	var y0: float = player.global_position.y
	Input.action_press(&"move_left")
	await _frames(240)
	Input.action_release(&"move_left")
	await _frames(10)
	check(player.global_position.y > y0 + 0.5 and player.is_on_floor(), "walkways climb from the Steps (%.2f -> %.2f m)" % [y0, player.global_position.y])
	# the sea stops the player on the beach
	await _place3(town.nearest_walk(Vector2(650, 640)) + Vector3(0, 0.1, 0))
	Input.action_press(&"move_right")
	Input.action_press(&"move_down")
	await _frames(600)
	Input.action_release(&"move_right")
	Input.action_release(&"move_down")
	check(player.global_position.y > -6.6 and player.is_on_floor(), "the sea stops the player on the beach (y=%.2f)" % player.global_position.y)
	await _place3(town.nearest_walk(Vector2(880, 415)) + Vector3(0, 0.1, 0))
	check(SaveManager.save_game("test"), "save in town")
	var at_save: Vector3 = player.global_position
	main.call("new_game")
	await _frames(3)
	check(SaveManager.load_game("test"), "load in town")
	await _frames(3)
	player = main.get("player")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_town" and player.global_position.distance_to(at_save) < 0.3, "load restores map and position")
	town = main.get("map")
	await _place3(town.nearest_walk(Vector2(212, 692)) + Vector3(0, 0.1, 0))
	player.try_interact()
	await _frames(4)
	player = main.get("player")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_mine", "the mine yard in town leads back to the mine map")
	await _place3(Vector3(43.6, 0.05, 32.2))
	check(player.target is MapExit3D, "the airpad path is an exit")
	player.try_interact()
	await _frames(10)
	player = main.get("player")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_town" and player.is_on_floor(), "the airpad path arrives on the ground in town")
	check(player.target is MapExit3D, "arrives beside the way back")
	player.try_interact()
	await _frames(4)
	player = main.get("player")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_mine" and player.global_position.distance_to(Vector3(43.3, 0.05, 32.2)) < 0.3, "and returns to the east side of the mine")


## The Steps: the detailed insert (plaza, stairs, tavern terrace) inside the town map.
const O := preload("res://maps/anjabi/town_3d.gd").INSERT_AT


func _district() -> void:
	player = main.get("player")
	var map: Node = main.get("map")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_town", "new game starts in the town")
	check(map is GameMap3D and player is Player3D, "the town is a 3D map with a 3D player")
	check(player.global_position.distance_to(O + Vector3(2, 0.05, 5)) < 0.5, "new game starts on the Steps plaza")
	var meshes: int = map.find_children("*", "GeometryInstance3D", true, false).size()
	var bodies: int = map.find_children("*", "StaticBody3D", true, false).size()
	check(meshes > 300 and bodies > 50, "the town is built from real meshes and colliders (%d meshes, %d bodies)" % [meshes, bodies])
	check(map.find_children("*", "Npc3D", true, false).size() >= 1, "the Steps insert is part of the town")
	var cam: Camera3D = player.camera
	check(cam.current and cam.projection == Camera3D.PROJECTION_PERSPECTIVE and absf(cam.rotation_degrees.y) < 0.01, "fixed-orientation follow camera is current")
	await _frames(20)
	check(player.is_on_floor() and absf(player.global_position.y) < 0.2, "player stands on the plaza (y=%.2f)" % player.global_position.y)
	# walk east, camera follows
	var x0: float = player.global_position.x
	var c0: float = cam.global_position.x
	Input.action_press(&"move_right")
	await _frames(40)
	Input.action_release(&"move_right")
	await _frames(30)
	check(player.global_position.x > x0 + 1.5, "keyboard moves the 3D player (%.2f m)" % (player.global_position.x - x0))
	check(cam.global_position.x > c0 + 1.0, "camera follows the player")
	check(player.facing.x > 0.9, "player faces the way it walked")
	var p0: Vector3 = player.global_position
	Input.action_press(&"move_right")
	Input.action_press(&"move_down")
	await _frames(20)
	Input.action_release(&"move_right")
	Input.action_release(&"move_down")
	var d: Vector3 = player.global_position - p0
	check(d.x > 0.3 and d.z > 0.3, "diagonal movement")
	x0 = player.global_position.x
	var ui: GameUi = main.get_node("UI")
	ui.touch.vector = Vector2.LEFT
	await _frames(30)
	ui.touch.vector = Vector2.ZERO
	check(player.global_position.x < x0 - 0.8, "touch stick moves the 3D player")
	# the terrace wall blocks, the stairs climb
	await _place3(O + Vector3(-6, 0.05, -7))
	Input.action_press(&"move_up")
	await _frames(60)
	Input.action_release(&"move_up")
	check(player.global_position.z > O.z - 10.2 and player.global_position.y < 0.5, "terrace wall blocks the plaza")
	await _place3(O + Vector3(0, 0.05, -2))
	Input.action_press(&"move_up")
	await _frames(150)
	Input.action_release(&"move_up")
	await _frames(10)
	check(player.global_position.y > 2.8 and player.is_on_floor(), "stairs carry the player up to the tavern terrace (y=%.2f)" % player.global_position.y)
	# tavern keeper
	await _place3(O + Vector3(-7.2, 3.05, -10.9))
	check(player.target != null and player.target.label == "Tavern keeper", "tavern keeper is targeted")
	await _frames(2)
	check(ui.hud.prompt_text().contains("Tavern keeper"), "HUD prompt shows '%s' (dialogue %s, hud %s, target %s)" % [ui.hud.prompt_text(), Dialogue.active, ui.hud.visible, ui.hud.player.get("target")])
	check(player.try_interact() and Dialogue.active, "talking opens dialogue")
	var at: Vector3 = player.global_position
	Input.action_press(&"move_down")
	await _frames(10)
	Input.action_release(&"move_down")
	check(player.global_position.distance_to(at) < 0.05, "player cannot walk during dialogue")
	await _talk(["Where's the mine"])
	check(GameState.get_flag("tavern_keeper.met") == true, "dialogue effects apply in 3D")
	# the insert joins the town seamlessly: walk off the plaza east and west
	for side: float in [1.0, -1.0]:
		var edge_x: float = O.x + (20.5 if side > 0 else -12.5)
		var zl: float = _walk_z(map, edge_x + side * 2.0, -9, 9)
		check(not is_nan(zl), "a town walkway meets the plaza's %s edge" % ("east" if side > 0 else "west"))
		if is_nan(zl):
			continue
		await _place3(Vector3(edge_x - side * 3.0, 0.05, O.z + zl))
		Input.action_press(&"move_right" if side > 0 else &"move_left")
		await _frames(150)
		Input.action_release(&"move_right" if side > 0 else &"move_left")
		await _frames(10)
		var past: float = (player.global_position.x - edge_x) * side
		check(past > 4.0 and player.is_on_floor(), "the plaza runs into the town's walkway (%s, %.1f m past the edge)" % ["east" if side > 0 else "west", past])
	# from the terrace west onto the town's paths at the same level
	await _place3(O + Vector3(-20, 3.05, -20))
	Input.action_press(&"move_left")
	await _frames(150)
	Input.action_release(&"move_left")
	await _frames(10)
	check(player.global_position.x < O.x - 26.0 and player.is_on_floor(), "the terrace runs into the town's paths (x=%.1f)" % (player.global_position.x - O.x))
	# B-17 among the town's pods, and the sign by the mine
	var b17: Node3D = map.get_node("PodB17Door")
	await _place3(Vector3(b17.global_position.x, map.ground(b17.global_position.x, b17.global_position.z + 1.0) + 0.1, b17.global_position.z + 0.6))
	await _frames(10)
	check(player.target != null and player.target.label == "Pod B-17", "B-17's door is in the town")
	player.try_interact()
	await _talk([])
	# save / load
	await _place3(O + Vector3(10, 0.05, 6))
	check(SaveManager.save_game("test"), "save on the Steps")
	main.call("load_map", "anjabi_mine", "default")
	await _frames(3)
	check(SaveManager.load_game("test"), "load on the Steps")
	await _frames(3)
	player = main.get("player")
	check(player is Player3D and player.global_position.distance_to(O + Vector3(10, 0.05, 6)) < 0.2, "load restores map and position")
	# a save from the retired district map resumes on the Steps
	main.call("restore_world", {"map": "anjabi_district", "dim": 3, "x": -30.0, "y": -2.95, "z": 4.0})
	await _frames(3)
	player = main.get("player")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_town" and player.global_position.distance_to(O + Vector3(2, 0.05, 5)) < 0.5, "old district saves resume on the Steps")
	# to the mine and back
	var exit: Node3D = map.get_node("ToMineRoad") if is_instance_valid(map) else null
	map = main.get("map")
	exit = map.get_node("ToMineRoad")
	await _place3(exit.global_position - Vector3(0, 0.7, 0))
	await _frames(6)
	check(player.target is MapExit3D, "the mine path is an exit")
	player.try_interact()
	await _frames(4)
	player = main.get("player")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_mine" and player is Player3D, "the path leads into the 3D mine yard")


## Local z (relative to O) of the town walk cell at world x nearest the plaza centre line, or NAN.
func _walk_z(map: Node, x: float, z0: int, z1: int) -> float:
	var best := NAN
	for z in range(z0, z1 + 1):
		var cx := int(x)
		var cz := int(O.z + z)
		var t: int = map.cls[cz * map.w + cx]
		if t == 1 or t == 2 or t == 3:
			var local := (cz + 0.5) - O.z
			if is_nan(best) or absf(local) < absf(best):
				best = local
	return best


func _place3(p: Vector3) -> void:
	player.global_position = p
	player.velocity = Vector3.ZERO
	await _frames(6)


func check(ok: bool, what: String) -> void:
	_checks += 1
	if not ok:
		_fails += 1
	print("%s  %s" % ["PASS" if ok else "FAIL", what])


func _frames(n: int) -> void:
	for i in n:
		await get_tree().physics_frame
	await get_tree().process_frame


func _place(p: Vector2) -> void:
	player.global_position = p
	await _frames(4)


func _until(cond: Callable) -> void:
	for i in 1200:
		if cond.call():
			break
		await get_tree().physics_frame
	await _frames(2)


## Plays the open dialogue: picks the first choice containing any of `prefer`
## (each used once), otherwise the last choice, until it ends.
func _talk(prefer: Array) -> void:
	var want := prefer.duplicate()
	for i in 200:
		if not Dialogue.active:
			break
		var opts := Dialogue.choice_texts()
		if opts.is_empty():
			Dialogue.advance()
		else:
			var pick := opts.size() - 1
			for w: String in want:
				var found := false
				for j in opts.size():
					if opts[j].contains(w):
						pick = j
						want.erase(w)
						found = true
						break
				if found:
					break
			Dialogue.choose(pick)
		await get_tree().process_frame
	await _frames(2)


## World clock, calendar, weather, lighting, persistence and sprint.
func _world() -> void:
	main.call("new_game")
	await _frames(5)
	player = main.get("player")
	var hud: Hud = main.get("ui").hud
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0
	check(WorldTime.date_text() == "Autumn 12 · Thursday" and WorldTime.time_text() == "6:40 PM", "new game starts on Autumn 12, Thursday, 6:40 PM (%s, %s)" % [WorldTime.date_text(), WorldTime.time_text()])
	check(hud.clock_text().contains("Autumn 12") and hud.clock_text().contains("6:40 PM"), "the clock HUD shows date and 12-hour time")
	check(WorldTime.period() == "evening" and Weather.current == "rain", "it is a rainy evening")

	# midnight rollover
	var got := {"days": 0, "periods": [], "seasons": 0, "years": 0, "jumps": 0}
	var on_day := func(_c: WorldCalendar) -> void: got["days"] += 1
	var on_period := func(p: String, _prev: String) -> void: (got["periods"] as Array).append(p)
	var on_season := func(_s: int) -> void: got["seasons"] += 1
	var on_year := func(_y: int) -> void: got["years"] += 1
	WorldTime.day_changed.connect(on_day)
	WorldTime.period_changed.connect(on_period)
	WorldTime.season_changed.connect(on_season)
	WorldTime.year_changed.connect(on_year)
	WorldTime.set_time(23, 58)
	WorldTime.advance_minutes(3)
	check(WorldTime.hour == 0 and WorldTime.minute == 1 and WorldTime.calendar.day == 13 and got["days"] == 1, "midnight rolls the clock over and advances one day")
	check(WorldTime.calendar.weekday_name() == "Friday" and WorldTime.time_text() == "12:01 AM", "the weekday follows and midnight reads 12:01 AM")
	(got["periods"] as Array).clear()
	WorldTime.set_time(4, 59)
	WorldTime.advance_minutes(1)
	check(got["periods"] == ["dawn"] and WorldTime.period() == "dawn", "dawn begins at 05:00 and announces itself once")
	WorldTime.set_time(12, 0)
	check(WorldTime.time_text() == "12:00 PM" and WorldTime.period() == "midday" and WorldTime.is_between(9, 17) and not WorldTime.is_between(22, 6), "noon reads 12:00 PM; opening-hours query works")
	# season and year rollover
	WorldTime.set_date(1, 2, 28)
	WorldTime.set_time(23, 59)
	WorldTime.advance_minutes(1)
	check(WorldTime.calendar.season_name() == "Winter" and WorldTime.calendar.day == 1 and got["seasons"] == 1, "day 28 of Autumn rolls into Winter 1")
	WorldTime.set_date(1, 3, 28)
	WorldTime.set_time(23, 59)
	WorldTime.advance_minutes(1)
	check(WorldTime.calendar.year == 2 and WorldTime.calendar.season_name() == "Spring" and got["years"] == 1, "the end of Winter starts year 2 in Spring")
	# long skips and sleeping
	var d0 := WorldTime.calendar.total_days()
	WorldTime.advance_minutes(3 * 1440 + 30)
	check(WorldTime.calendar.total_days() == d0 + 3 and WorldTime.minute == 30, "skipping three days and thirty minutes lands exactly")
	WorldTime.set_time(22, 15)
	d0 = WorldTime.calendar.total_days()
	WorldTime.sleep_until(6)
	check(WorldTime.hour == 6 and WorldTime.minute == 0 and WorldTime.calendar.total_days() == d0 + 1, "sleeping until 6 wakes the next morning")
	WorldTime.calendar.add_event("test_event", WorldTime.calendar.season, WorldTime.calendar.day + 1)
	var due: Array = []
	var on_event := func(e: Dictionary) -> void: due.append(e["id"])
	WorldTime.event_due.connect(on_event)
	WorldTime.sleep_until(0, 5)
	check(due == ["test_event"], "a scheduled calendar event fires on its day")
	WorldTime.event_due.disconnect(on_event)
	WorldTime.calendar.events.clear()
	for pair: Array in [[WorldTime.day_changed, on_day], [WorldTime.period_changed, on_period], [WorldTime.season_changed, on_season], [WorldTime.year_changed, on_year]]:
		(pair[0] as Signal).disconnect(pair[1])

	# running and pausing
	WorldTime.set_time(10, 0)
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 120.0
	await _frames(30)
	var ran := WorldTime.minute_of_day() - 600
	check(ran > 10, "time passes during play (%d minutes in 30 frames at test speed)" % ran)
	WorldTime.pause("test")
	var m0 := WorldTime.total_minutes()
	await _frames(20)
	check(WorldTime.total_minutes() == m0 and WorldTime.is_paused(), "a registered pause stops the clock")
	WorldTime.resume("test")
	get_tree().paused = true
	m0 = WorldTime.total_minutes()
	await _frames(20)
	get_tree().paused = false
	check(WorldTime.total_minutes() == m0, "menus (paused tree) stop the clock")
	await _frames(10)
	check(WorldTime.total_minutes() > m0, "and it runs again afterwards")
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0

	# weather
	WorldTime.set_date(1, 2, 12)
	WorldTime.set_time(8, 0)
	Weather.reset(42)
	var f1 := str(Weather.forecast(24))
	Weather.reset(42)
	check(str(Weather.forecast(24)) == f1 and Weather.forecast(24).size() >= 2, "the same seed plans the same forecast")
	var fc := Weather.forecast(24)
	check(int(fc[fc.size() - 1]["until"]) - WorldTime.total_minutes() == 24 * 60, "the forecast covers the next 24 hours")
	var changes := {"n": 0}
	var on_weather := func(_t: String, _p: String) -> void: changes["n"] += 1
	Weather.weather_changed.connect(on_weather)
	for i in 24:
		WorldTime.advance_minutes(60)
	check(changes["n"] >= 2 and changes["n"] <= 9, "weather changes a few times a day, not every minute (%d in 24 h)" % changes["n"])
	Weather.set_weather("fog", 60)
	check(Weather.current == "fog", "weather can be overridden")
	WorldTime.advance_minutes(61)
	check(Weather.override == "" and Weather.current == str(Weather.schedule[0]["type"]), "a timed override hands back to the forecast")
	WorldTime.set_date(1, 3, 2)
	Weather.reset(7)
	var seen := {}
	for i in 24 * 20:
		WorldTime.advance_minutes(60)
		seen[Weather.current] = true
	check(seen.has("snow") and not seen.has("heavy_rain"), "winter brings snow and no storms (%s)" % str(seen.keys()))
	WorldTime.set_date(1, 1, 2)
	Weather.reset(7)
	seen.clear()
	for i in 24 * 20:
		WorldTime.advance_minutes(60)
		seen[Weather.current] = true
	check(seen.has("clear") and not seen.has("snow"), "summer is mostly clear and never snows (%s)" % str(seen.keys()))
	Weather.weather_changed.disconnect(on_weather)

	# lighting follows the clock
	var ctl: EnvironmentController = main.get("map").environment
	check(ctl != null and ctl.env != null, "the map has an environment controller")
	Weather.set_weather("clear")
	WorldTime.set_time(13, 0)
	await _frames(3)
	var day_sun := ctl.sun.light_energy
	var day_bg := ctl.env.background_color.get_luminance()
	var lamp: Light3D = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"lamp_lights")[0]
	check(not lamp.visible, "street lamps are off at midday")
	var e0 := ctl.env.ambient_light_energy
	WorldTime.advance_minutes(1)
	await _frames(2)
	check(absf(ctl.env.ambient_light_energy - e0) < 0.02, "light changes smoothly from minute to minute")
	WorldTime.set_time(23, 0)
	await _frames(3)
	check(ctl.env.background_color.get_luminance() < day_bg * 0.4 and lamp.visible and lamp.light_energy > 1.0, "night is dark and the lamps are lit")
	check(ctl.sun.light_energy < day_sun and ctl.sun.light_color.b > ctl.sun.light_color.r, "the moon is dimmer and bluer than the sun")
	WorldTime.set_time(18, 40)
	await _frames(3)
	check(ctl.sun.light_color.r > ctl.sun.light_color.b * 1.3, "golden hour is warm")
	# weather presentation: blends, then indoor override
	WorldTime.set_time(13, 0)
	await _frames(3)
	var clear_sun := ctl.sun.light_energy
	Weather.set_weather("heavy_rain")
	await _frames(5)
	check(float(ctl.w["rain"]) > 0.0 and float(ctl.w["rain"]) < 0.5, "weather blends in gradually (rain %.2f after 5 frames)" % float(ctl.w["rain"]))
	ctl.snap()
	check(is_equal_approx(float(ctl.w["rain"]), 1.0) and ctl.sun.light_energy < clear_sun * 0.6, "heavy rain darkens the day")
	var rain: Rain3D = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group(&"precipitation").filter(func(n: Node) -> bool: return n is Rain3D)[0]
	check(rain.emitting, "rain falls")
	Weather.set_weather("snow")
	ctl.snap()
	check(not rain.emitting and (rain.get_child(1) as GPUParticles3D).emitting, "snow replaces rain")
	ctl.indoor = true
	ctl.snap()
	check(ctl.sun.light_energy == 0.0 and not (rain.get_child(1) as GPUParticles3D).emitting and lamp.visible, "indoors ignores sun and weather")
	ctl.indoor = false
	ctl.snap()
	check(ctl.sun.light_energy > 0.0, "back outdoors the sun returns")

	# persistence
	WorldTime.set_date(3, 3, 17)
	WorldTime.set_time(21, 7)
	Weather.reset(99)
	Weather.set_weather("snow", 300)
	var sched := str(Weather.schedule)
	check(SaveManager.save_game("test"), "save with clock and weather")
	main.call("new_game")
	await _frames(3)
	check(WorldTime.calendar.year == 1 and Weather.current == "rain", "a new game resets the clock and weather")
	check(SaveManager.load_game("test"), "load with clock and weather")
	check(WorldTime.calendar.year == 3 and WorldTime.calendar.season_name() == "Winter" and WorldTime.calendar.day == 17 and WorldTime.hour == 21 and WorldTime.minute == 7, "load restores year, season, day, hour and minute")
	check(Weather.current == "snow" and Weather.override == "snow" and str(Weather.schedule) == sched, "load restores the weather, its override and the forecast")
	await _frames(3)
	ctl = main.get("map").environment
	check(is_equal_approx(float(ctl.w["snow"]), 1.0) and is_equal_approx(float(ctl.w["cover"]), 1.0), "the loaded world is lit for its time and weather at once")
	WorldTime.advance_minutes(301)
	check(Weather.override == "", "the restored override still expires on time")

	# sprint
	main.call("new_game")
	await _frames(10)
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0
	player = main.get("player")
	await _place3(O + Vector3(-8, 0.05, 6))
	Input.action_press(&"move_right")
	await _frames(40)
	var x0: float = player.global_position.x
	await _frames(40)
	var walked: float = player.global_position.x - x0
	check(not player.sprinting, "walking is not sprinting")
	Input.action_press(&"sprint")
	await _frames(25)
	x0 = player.global_position.x
	await _frames(40)
	var ran_d: float = player.global_position.x - x0
	check(player.sprinting and ran_d > walked * 1.5, "holding sprint runs faster (%.2f m vs %.2f m)" % [ran_d, walked])
	Input.action_release(&"sprint")
	await _frames(25)
	check(not player.sprinting and absf(Vector3(player.velocity.x, 0, player.velocity.z).length() - player.walk_speed) < 0.3, "releasing sprint returns to a walk")
	Input.action_release(&"move_right")
	await _frames(20)
	player.touch.sprint = true
	Input.action_press(&"move_left")
	await _frames(30)
	check(player.sprinting, "the RUN toggle sprints without the key")
	Input.action_release(&"move_left")
	player.touch.sprint = false
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 1.0


## The Golden Sun-style 2D prototype (maps/anjabi/steps_2d.tscn, baked by tools/bake_2d.gd).
func _proto2d() -> void:
	main.call("new_game", "anjabi_steps_2d")
	await _frames(5)
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0
	var map: Map2D = main.get("map")
	player = main.get("player")
	check(map is Map2D and player is Player2D and str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_steps_2d", "the 2D prototype loads as a 2D map with a 2D player")
	var S := float(map.S)
	check(absf(map.height_at(Vector2(2, 5) * S)) < 0.2 and absf(map.height_at(Vector2(-6, -15) * S) - 3.0) < 0.2, "the baked heights put the plaza at 0 m and the terrace at 3 m")
	check(map.entities.get_child_count() > 30, "buildings, lamps and trees are y-sorted sprites (%d)" % map.entities.get_child_count())
	player.position = Vector2(2, 5) * S
	await _frames(2)
	var x0: float = player.position.x
	Input.action_press(&"move_right")
	await _frames(30)
	Input.action_release(&"move_right")
	check(player.position.x - x0 > 20.0, "the player walks on the plaza")
	# the terrace wall: walking north from the plaza beside the stairs is blocked
	player.position = Vector2(-6, -8) * S
	await _frames(2)
	Input.action_press(&"move_up")
	await _frames(60)
	Input.action_release(&"move_up")
	check(player.position.y > -10.5 * S and absf(map.height_at(player.position)) < 0.3, "the terrace wall stops the player (y = %.1f m)" % (player.position.y / S))
	# the stairs climb to the terrace
	player.position = Vector2(0, -3.5) * S
	await _frames(2)
	Input.action_press(&"move_up")
	await _frames(90)
	Input.action_release(&"move_up")
	check(map.height_at(player.position) > 2.8, "the stairs lead up to the terrace (h = %.2f m)" % map.height_at(player.position))
	var keeper: Person2D = null
	for n in map.entities.get_children():
		if n is Person2D and not n is Player2D and (n as Person2D).interactable != null:
			keeper = n
	check(keeper != null, "the tavern keeper stands on the terrace")
	player.position = keeper.position + Vector2(0, 14)
	await _frames(4)
	check(player.target == keeper.interactable and player.try_interact() and Dialogue.active, "walking up to the keeper offers Talk, and talking opens the dialogue")
	Dialogue.cancel()
	await _frames(2)
	# lighting follows the clock
	var tint: CanvasModulate = map.find_children("*", "CanvasModulate", true, false)[0]
	Weather.set_weather("clear")
	WorldTime.set_time(13, 0)
	await _frames(2)
	var day := tint.color.get_luminance()
	WorldTime.set_time(23, 0)
	await _frames(2)
	check(tint.color.get_luminance() < day * 0.6 and tint.color.b > tint.color.r, "night tints the 2D map dark blue")
	# save and load in 2D
	var at: Vector2 = player.position
	check(SaveManager.save_game("test"), "save in the 2D prototype")
	main.call("new_game")
	await _frames(3)
	check(SaveManager.load_game("test"), "load the 2D prototype")
	await _frames(3)
	player = main.get("player")
	check(player is Player2D and player.position.distance_to(at) < 2.0, "load restores the 2D map and position")
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 1.0


## Pokemon-style interiors: the shop door on the Steps leads into a room and back.
func _interiors() -> void:
	main.call("new_game")
	await _frames(8)
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 0.0
	player = main.get("player")
	var door: MapExit3D = main.get("map").find_child("ShopDoor", true, false)
	check(door != null and door.target_map == "anjabi_shop", "the shop on the Steps has a door")
	await _place3(door.global_position - Vector3(0, 0.85, -0.9))
	await _frames(4)
	check(player.target == door, "standing at the shop door offers Enter")
	var door_at: Vector3 = door.global_position
	player.try_interact()
	await _frames(8)
	player = main.get("player")
	var shop = main.get("map")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_shop" and shop is InteriorMap3D and player.is_on_floor(), "entering puts the player inside the shop, standing")
	check((shop as MapBuilder3D).environment.indoor, "the shop is lit as an interior")
	Weather.set_weather("heavy_rain")
	(shop as MapBuilder3D).environment.snap()
	var rain: Rain3D = player.find_children("*", "Rain3D", true, false)[0]
	check(not rain.emitting, "it does not rain indoors")
	var shelf: Interactable3D = null
	for n in shop.find_children("*", "Interactable3D", true, false):
		if (n as Interactable3D).dialogue_id == "anjabi/shop_shelves":
			shelf = n
	check(shelf != null and Dialogue.has_method("start"), "the shelves can be examined")
	var mat: MapExit3D = shop.find_child("ExitMat", true, false)
	await _place3(Vector3(mat.global_position.x, 0.05, mat.global_position.z - 0.2))
	await _frames(4)
	check(player.target == mat, "the door mat offers Leave")
	player.try_interact()
	await _frames(8)
	player = main.get("player")
	check(str(main.get("map_id")) == "anjabi_town" and player.global_position.distance_to(door_at) < 4.0, "leaving returns to the Steps, at the shop door")
	Weather.set_weather("rain")
	WorldTime.minutes_per_second = 1.0


==============================================================================
== tests/slice_test.tscn

[gd_scene load_steps=2 format=3]

[node name="SliceTest" type="Node"]
script = ExtResource("1")


==============================================================================
== tools/bake_2d.gd
==============================================================================
extends Node
## Bakes the 3D Steps district into 2D art for the Golden Sun-style prototype
## (maps/anjabi/steps_2d.tscn). Windowed, because it renders:
##   Godot --path . res://tools/bake_2d.tscn
##
## Projection: an orthographic camera pitched 45 degrees, image stretched vertically by
## sqrt(2), so a point (x, y, z) lands at screen (x, z - y) metres: ground squares stay
## square and walls stand straight up, the classic 3/4 oblique view. S pixels per metre.
##
## Writes res://art/baked2d/steps/:
##   ground.png        everything flat (paving, walls, stairs, sea), with the shadows of
##                     the tall things falling on it; lit for a neutral clear day
##   ground_glow.png   emissive surfaces only (signs, windows), added at night
##   objects/NNN.png   each model (building, lamp, tree, barrel...) alone, plus _glow
##   heights.res       Image (FORMAT_RF): top surface height per 1/4 m, -1000 = nothing
##   layout.json       where everything goes, lamps, interactables, people, spawns
## and res://art/baked2d/chars/<id>.png: 8 directions x (idle, 8 walk, 8 run) sheets.

const OUT := "res://art/baked2d/steps/"
const CHARS := "res://art/baked2d/chars/"
const S := 16
const SS := 2
const X0 := -46.0
const X1 := 46.0
const V0 := -58.0
const V1 := 46.0
const Z0 := -46.0
const Z1 := 40.0
const GRID := 4
const CELL := Vector2i(32, 42)
const CELL_FEET := 35
const DIRS := 8

var district: Node3D
var vp: SubViewport
var cam: Camera3D
var sun: DirectionalLight3D
var env: Environment


func _ready() -> void:
	MapBuilder3D.bake_mode = true
	RenderingServer.directional_shadow_atlas_set_size(8192, true)
	RenderingServer.global_shader_parameter_set(&"weather_wet", 0.35)
	RenderingServer.global_shader_parameter_set(&"weather_snow", 0.0)
	DirAccess.make_dir_recursive_absolute(OUT + "objects")
	DirAccess.make_dir_recursive_absolute(CHARS)
	for d: String in [OUT + "objects", CHARS]:
		var da := DirAccess.open(d)
		for f in da.get_files():
			da.remove(f)

	vp = SubViewport.new()
	vp.own_world_3d = true
	vp.transparent_bg = true
	vp.msaa_3d = Viewport.MSAA_4X
	vp.size = Vector2i(int((X1 - X0) * S * SS), int((V1 - V0) * S * SS / sqrt(2.0)))
	vp.render_target_update_mode = SubViewport.UPDATE_DISABLED
	add_child(vp)
	district = (load("res://maps/anjabi/district.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate()
	vp.add_child(district)
	await get_tree().process_frame
	await get_tree().process_frame

	var layout := {"S": S, "grid": GRID, "x0": X0, "v0": V0, "z0": Z0, "sea": -7.0,
		"width": int((X1 - X0) * S), "height": int((V1 - V0) * S),
		"objects": [], "lamps": [], "interactables": [], "people": [], "spawns": {}}
	_collect_and_strip(layout)
	_setup_view()

	# classify direct children: models become sprites, signs ride with their model
	var objects: Array[Node3D] = []
	var boxes: Array[AABB] = []
	var labels: Array[Label3D] = []
	for c in district.get_children():
		if c is Label3D:
			labels.append(c)
		elif c is Node3D and (c as Node3D).scene_file_path.ends_with(".glb"):
			var b := _aabb(c)
			if b.size.length() > 0.01 and b.get_center().x > X0 - 2 and b.get_center().x < X1 + 2 and b.get_center().z > Z0 - 6 and b.get_center().z < Z1 + 4:
				objects.append(c)
				boxes.append(b)
	var owner_of := {}
	for l in labels:
		for i in objects.size():
			if boxes[i].grow(1.6).has_point(l.global_position):
				owner_of[l] = i
				break
	print("bake: %d objects, %d labels" % [objects.size(), labels.size()])

	# ground: tall things cast shadows but are not drawn
	for o in objects:
		_shadow_only(o, true)
	for l: Label3D in owner_of:
		l.visible = false
	district.entities.visible = false
	var lit := await _render(false)
	lit.save_png(OUT + "ground.png")
	var glow := await _render(true)
	if _bright(glow):
		glow.save_png(OUT + "ground_glow.png")
	for o in objects:
		_shadow_only(o, false)

	# each object alone
	var keep: Array[Node] = []
	for c in district.get_children():
		if c.visible:
			keep.append(c)
	for i in objects.size():
		for c in keep:
			(c as Node3D).visible = c == objects[i] or owner_of.get(c, -1) == i
		var img := await _render(false)
		var used := img.get_used_rect()
		if used.size.x < 1:
			continue
		var name := "%03d" % i
		img.get_region(used).save_png(OUT + "objects/" + name + ".png")
		var entry := {"file": name, "rect": [used.position.x, used.position.y, used.size.x, used.size.y],
			"sort_z": boxes[i].get_center().z, "model": objects[i].scene_file_path.get_file().get_basename()}
		var g := await _render(true)
		var gr := g.get_region(used)
		if _bright(gr):
			gr.save_png(OUT + "objects/" + name + "_glow.png")
			entry["glow"] = true
		(layout["objects"] as Array).append(entry)
	for c in keep:
		(c as Node3D).visible = true

	# heights
	await get_tree().physics_frame
	var heights := _heights()
	ResourceSaver.save(heights, OUT + "heights.res")

	var f := FileAccess.open(OUT + "layout.json", FileAccess.WRITE)
	f.store_string(JSON.stringify(layout, "\t"))
	f.close()

	# character sheets
	await _bake_people(layout)
	f = FileAccess.open(OUT + "layout.json", FileAccess.WRITE)
	f.store_string(JSON.stringify(layout, "\t"))
	f.close()
	print("bake: done")
	get_tree().quit()


# --- scene preparation -----------------------------------------------------------

## Records lamps, interactables, people and spawns, then removes the district's own
## lights, environment and probes so the bake controls the light.
func _collect_and_strip(layout: Dictionary) -> void:
	for n in district.find_children("*", "OmniLight3D", true, false):
		var l := n as OmniLight3D
		var p := l.global_position
		(layout["lamps"] as Array).append({"x": p.x, "y": p.y, "z": p.z, "energy": float(l.get_meta(&"lit_energy", l.light_energy)), "range": l.omni_range})
	for n in district.find_children("*", "Interactable3D", true, false):
		var it := n as Interactable3D
		if it.get_parent() is Npc3D:
			continue
		var p := it.global_position
		var e := {"prompt": it.prompt, "label": it.label, "dialogue_id": it.dialogue_id, "radius": it.radius, "x": p.x, "y": p.y, "z": p.z}
		if it is MapExit3D:
			e["target_map"] = (it as MapExit3D).target_map
			e["target_spawn"] = (it as MapExit3D).target_spawn
		(layout["interactables"] as Array).append(e)
	var people := 0
	for n in district.entities.get_children():
		var d := {}
		if n is Npc3D:
			var npc := n as Npc3D
			d = {"name": npc.display_name, "dialogue_id": npc.dialogue_id, "coat": npc.coat.to_html(), "accent": npc.accent.to_html(),
				"skin": npc.skin.to_html(), "hood": npc.hood, "look": _look_json(npc.look), "yaw": npc.yaw_deg,
				"points": [[npc.global_position.x, npc.global_position.y, npc.global_position.z]]}
		elif n is Townsfolk:
			var t := n as Townsfolk
			var pts := []
			for p in t.points:
				pts.append([p.x, p.y, p.z])
			d = {"name": "", "dialogue_id": "", "coat": t.coat.to_html(), "accent": t.accent.to_html(), "skin": t.skin.to_html(),
				"hood": t.hood, "look": _look_json(t.look), "yaw": t.yaw_deg, "points": pts}
		else:
			continue
		d["sheet"] = "person_%02d" % people
		people += 1
		(layout["people"] as Array).append(d)
	for m in district.get_node("Spawns").get_children():
		var p := (m as Node3D).global_position
		layout["spawns"][str(m.name)] = [p.x, p.y, p.z]
	for t: String in ["OmniLight3D", "DirectionalLight3D", "WorldEnvironment", "ReflectionProbe", "EnvironmentController"]:
		for n in district.find_children("*", t, true, false):
			n.get_parent().remove_child(n)
			n.free()


static func _look_json(look: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var out := {}
	for k: String in look:
		var v: Variant = look[k]
		out[k] = (v as Color).to_html() if v is Color else v
	return out


func _setup_view() -> void:
	cam = Camera3D.new()
	cam.projection = Camera3D.PROJECTION_ORTHOGONAL
	cam.keep_aspect = Camera3D.KEEP_HEIGHT
	cam.size = (V1 - V0) / sqrt(2.0)
	cam.rotation_degrees = Vector3(-45, 0, 0)
	cam.near = 1.0
	cam.far = 500.0
	var vc := (V0 + V1) * 0.5
	cam.position = Vector3((X0 + X1) * 0.5, 0, vc) + Vector3(0, sin(PI / 4), cos(PI / 4)) * 200.0
	vp.add_child(cam)
	cam.current = true
	sun = _sun()
	vp.add_child(sun)
	env = _env()
	var we := WorldEnvironment.new()
	we.environment = env
	vp.add_child(we)


static func _sun() -> DirectionalLight3D:
	var l := DirectionalLight3D.new()
	l.rotation_degrees = Vector3(-52, 205, 0)
	l.light_color = Color(1.0, 0.95, 0.86)
	l.light_energy = 1.45
	l.shadow_enabled = true
	l.directional_shadow_mode = DirectionalLight3D.SHADOW_ORTHOGONAL
	l.directional_shadow_max_distance = 600.0
	l.shadow_blur = 1.5
	return l


static func _env() -> Environment:
	var e := Environment.new()
	e.background_mode = Environment.BG_CLEAR_COLOR
	e.ambient_light_source = Environment.AMBIENT_SOURCE_COLOR
	e.ambient_light_color = Color(0.6, 0.66, 0.78)
	e.ambient_light_energy = 0.55
	e.reflected_light_source = Environment.REFLECTION_SOURCE_DISABLED
	e.tonemap_mode = Environment.TONE_MAPPER_FILMIC
	e.tonemap_exposure = 1.05
	e.adjustment_enabled = true
	e.adjustment_saturation = 1.15
	e.adjustment_contrast = 1.05
	return e


static func _aabb(n: Node) -> AABB:
	var out := AABB()
	var first := true
	for v in n.find_children("*", "VisualInstance3D", true, false):
		var vi := v as VisualInstance3D
		var b := vi.global_transform * vi.get_aabb()
		out = b if first else out.merge(b)
		first = false
	if n is VisualInstance3D:
		var b := (n as VisualInstance3D).global_transform * (n as VisualInstance3D).get_aabb()
		out = b if first else out.merge(b)
	return out


static func _shadow_only(n: Node, on: bool) -> void:
	for g in n.find_children("*", "GeometryInstance3D", true, false):
		(g as GeometryInstance3D).cast_shadow = GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_SHADOWS_ONLY if on else GeometryInstance3D.SHADOW_CASTING_SETTING_ON


# --- rendering -------------------------------------------------------------------

## One frame of the map viewport at final size. glow = emissive surfaces only.
func _render(glow: bool) -> Image:
	sun.visible = not glow
	env.ambient_light_energy = 0.0 if glow else 0.55
	vp.render_target_update_mode = SubViewport.UPDATE_ONCE
	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
	await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
	var img := vp.get_texture().get_image()
	img.convert(Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	img.resize(int((X1 - X0) * S), int((V1 - V0) * S), Image.INTERPOLATE_LANCZOS)
	return img


static func _bright(img: Image) -> bool:
	var w := img.get_width()
	var h := img.get_height()
	for y in range(0, h, 2):
		for x in range(0, w, 2):
			var c := img.get_pixel(x, y)
			if c.a > 0.3 and c.get_luminance() > 0.12:
				return true
	return false


# --- heights ---------------------------------------------------------------------

## Top surface height every 1/4 m: the highest of five rays (centre and +-0.1 m), so
## thin railings are not missed.
func _heights() -> Image:
	var space := vp.find_world_3d().direct_space_state
	var w := int((X1 - X0) * GRID)
	var h := int((Z1 - Z0) * GRID)
	var img := Image.create(w, h, false, Image.FORMAT_RF)
	var q := PhysicsRayQueryParameters3D.new()
	q.collision_mask = 1
	q.collide_with_areas = false
	var offs: Array[Vector2] = [Vector2.ZERO, Vector2(0.1, 0), Vector2(-0.1, 0), Vector2(0, 0.1), Vector2(0, -0.1)]
	for j in h:
		for i in w:
			var x := X0 + (i + 0.5) / GRID
			var z := Z0 + (j + 0.5) / GRID
			var top := -1000.0
			for o in offs:
				q.from = Vector3(x + o.x, 40.0, z + o.y)
				q.to = Vector3(x + o.x, -20.0, z + o.y)
				var r := space.intersect_ray(q)
				if not r.is_empty():
					top = maxf(top, (r["position"] as Vector3).y)
			img.set_pixel(i, j, Color(top, 0, 0))
	return img


# --- characters ------------------------------------------------------------------

func _bake_people(layout: Dictionary) -> void:
	var cvp := SubViewport.new()
	cvp.own_world_3d = true
	cvp.transparent_bg = true
	cvp.msaa_3d = Viewport.MSAA_4X
	var k := 4
	cvp.size = Vector2i(CELL.x * k, int(CELL.y * k / sqrt(2.0)))
	cvp.render_target_update_mode = SubViewport.UPDATE_DISABLED
	add_child(cvp)
	var c := Camera3D.new()
	c.projection = Camera3D.PROJECTION_ORTHOGONAL
	c.keep_aspect = Camera3D.KEEP_HEIGHT
	var top_v := -float(CELL_FEET) / S
	var bot_v := float(CELL.y - CELL_FEET) / S
	c.size = (bot_v - top_v) / sqrt(2.0)
	c.rotation_degrees = Vector3(-45, 0, 0)
	c.position = Vector3(0, 0, (top_v + bot_v) * 0.5) + Vector3(0, sin(PI / 4), cos(PI / 4)) * 20.0
	c.near = 1.0
	c.far = 60.0
	cvp.add_child(c)
	c.current = true
	var l := _sun()
	l.shadow_enabled = false
	cvp.add_child(l)
	var we := WorldEnvironment.new()
	we.environment = _env()
	cvp.add_child(we)
	var specs: Array[Dictionary] = [{"sheet": "player", "coat": Color(0.5, 0.13, 0.11).to_html(), "accent": Color(0.62, 0.42, 0.2).to_html(),
		"skin": Color(0.78, 0.6, 0.48).to_html(), "hood": false, "look": {"hair": "bun", "hair_color": Color(0.1, 0.06, 0.05).to_html(), "long_coat": true}}]
	for p: Dictionary in layout["people"]:
		specs.append(p)
	for spec in specs:
		var f := Figure3D.new()
		f.coat = Color.html(str(spec["coat"]))
		f.accent = Color.html(str(spec["accent"]))
		f.skin = Color.html(str(spec["skin"]))
		f.hood = bool(spec["hood"])
		var look := {}
		for key: String in spec["look"]:
			var v: Variant = spec["look"][key]
			look[key] = Color.html(str(v)) if key == "hair_color" else v
		f.configure(look)
		cvp.add_child(f)
		f.set_process(false)
		await get_tree().process_frame
		var sheet := Image.create(CELL.x * 17, CELL.y * DIRS, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
		for d in DIRS:
			var a := TAU * d / DIRS
			var dir := Vector2(cos(a), sin(a))
			f.rotation.y = atan2(-dir.x, -dir.y)
			for col in 17:
				_pose(f, col)
				cvp.render_target_update_mode = SubViewport.UPDATE_ONCE
				await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
				await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
				var img := cvp.get_texture().get_image()
				img.convert(Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
				img.resize(CELL.x, CELL.y, Image.INTERPOLATE_LANCZOS)
				_crisp(img)
				sheet.blit_rect(img, Rect2i(Vector2i.ZERO, CELL), Vector2i(col * CELL.x, d * CELL.y))
		sheet.save_png(CHARS + str(spec["sheet"]) + ".png")
		f.queue_free()
		await get_tree().process_frame
	cvp.queue_free()


## Column 0 idle, 1-8 walk, 9-16 run (phase steps of 1/8 cycle).
static func _pose(f: Figure3D, col: int) -> void:
	f._t = 0.0
	if col == 0:
		f._amount = 0.0
		f._run = 0.0
		f._phase = 0.0
		f._speed = 0.0
	else:
		var run := col >= 9
		f._amount = 1.0
		f._run = 1.0 if run else 0.0
		f._speed = 1.8 if run else 1.0
		f._phase = TAU * float((col - 1) % 8) / 8.0
	f._process(0.0)


## Hard alpha edge and a one-pixel dark outline, so sprites read as pixel art.
static func _crisp(img: Image) -> void:
	var w := img.get_width()
	var h := img.get_height()
	var solid := PackedByteArray()
	solid.resize(w * h)
	for y in h:
		for x in w:
			var c := img.get_pixel(x, y)
			if c.a >= 0.45:
				c.a = 1.0
				img.set_pixel(x, y, Color(c.r, c.g, c.b, 1.0))
				solid[y * w + x] = 1
			else:
				img.set_pixel(x, y, Color(0, 0, 0, 0))
	var ink := Color(0.05, 0.04, 0.07, 1.0)
	for y in h:
		for x in w:
			if solid[y * w + x] == 1:
				continue
			for o: Vector2i in [Vector2i(1, 0), Vector2i(-1, 0), Vector2i(0, 1), Vector2i(0, -1)]:
				var nx := x + o.x
				var ny := y + o.y
				if nx >= 0 and ny >= 0 and nx < w and ny < h and solid[ny * w + nx] == 1:
					img.set_pixel(x, y, ink)
					break

== tools/build_town_3d.gd
==============================================================================
extends SceneTree
## Builds the cell grid for the 3D town from the director's greybox and its walk mask.
##   Godot --headless --path . -s tools/build_town_3d.gd
## One cell per metre (0.12 m per greybox pixel). Each cell gets a type (water, path,
## grass, sand, slab, cliff), a visual height and a collision height. Walkable heights
## are interpolated (Laplace) between district anchor heights, so walkways become
## gentle slopes between levels. Cliffs and slabs are raised walls in the collider.
## Writes maps/anjabi/town_3d_cells.res (Image, RGBF: r = visual h, g = type,
## b = collision h) and docs/reference/town_3d_heights_debug.png.
## Re-run after changing the greybox, the mask, or ANCHORS.

const SRC := "res://maps/anjabi/town_greybox.png"
const MASK := "res://maps/anjabi/town_walk.res"
const OUT := "res://maps/anjabi/town_3d_cells.res"
const DEBUG := "res://docs/reference/town_3d_heights_debug.png"
const ORIGIN := Vector2(34, 34)
const PX := 0.12
const W := 166
const H := 109
const SEA := -7.0
const WATER := 0
const PATH := 1
const GRASS := 2
const SAND := 3
const SLAB := 4
const CLIFF := 5
## Cells covered by the detailed Steps insert (maps/anjabi/district.gd placed at
## INSERT_AT): drawn and collided by the insert, not by the grid.
const INSERT := 6
## World position (m) of the insert's origin: the Steps, greybox (880, 415).
const INSERT_AT := Vector2(101.52, 45.72)
## Insert walk areas in insert-local metres (x0, z0, x1, z1) and their heights.
const INSERT_AREAS := [
	[Rect2(-12, -10, 32, 20), 0.0],   # the Steps plaza
	[Rect2(-24, -28, 36, 18), 3.0],   # tavern terrace
]
## Image px -> height (m). Walk cells within ANCHOR_R of each are fixed.
const ANCHORS := [
	[Vector2(880, 415), 0.0],    # the Steps
	[Vector2(930, 385), 0.0],    # shop front
	[Vector2(650, 250), 4.0],    # pods approach
	[Vector2(580, 70), 6.0],     # pods
	[Vector2(945, 130), 6.0],    # guard tower
	[Vector2(230, 110), 6.0],    # forest
	[Vector2(200, 345), 3.0],    # garden
	[Vector2(240, 578), 3.0],    # estate
	[Vector2(515, 465), 2.0],    # Tion Tower walk
	[Vector2(212, 700), -3.0],   # mine
	[Vector2(450, 800), -3.0],   # airpad
	[Vector2(650, 640), -6.0],   # beach
	[Vector2(1170, 460), -1.0],  # docks
	[Vector2(970, 465), -1.0],   # tram
	[Vector2(1380, 325), 0.0],   # rainforest approach
]
const ANCHOR_R := 4.0

var cls := PackedByteArray()
var hv := PackedFloat32Array()
var hc := PackedFloat32Array()
var insert_h := PackedFloat32Array()
var insert_fix := PackedByteArray()


func _init() -> void:
	var img := Image.load_from_file(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(SRC))
	img.convert(Image.FORMAT_RGB8)
	var mask: BitMap = load(MASK)
	cls.resize(W * H)
	hv.resize(W * H)
	hc.resize(W * H)
	var counts := [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]
	for z in H:
		for x in W:
			var p := ORIGIN + (Vector2(x, z) + Vector2(0.5, 0.5)) / PX
			var votes := 0
			for o: Vector2 in [Vector2.ZERO, Vector2(2.5, 0), Vector2(-2.5, 0), Vector2(0, 2.5), Vector2(0, -2.5)]:
				var q := Vector2i(p + o)
				if mask.get_bitv(q):
					votes += 1
			var c := img.get_pixelv(Vector2i(p))
			var t := CLIFF
			if votes >= 3:
				t = GRASS if (c.g8 > c.r8 + 12 and c.g8 > c.b8 + 25) else PATH
			elif c.b8 > c.r8 + 30 and c.b8 > 170:
				t = WATER
			elif absi(c.r8 - c.g8) < 14 and absi(c.g8 - c.b8) < 16 and c.r8 > 168:
				t = SLAB
			cls[z * W + x] = t
			counts[t] += 1
	print("cells: water %d path %d grass %d sand %d slab %d cliff %d" % counts)
	# reserve the insert: cells inside it, and a one-cell rim pinned to its height
	insert_h.resize(W * H)
	insert_fix.resize(W * H)
	for a: Array in INSERT_AREAS:
		var r: Rect2 = a[0]
		r.position += INSERT_AT
		for z in range(int(r.position.y) - 1, int(r.end.y) + 2):
			for x in range(int(r.position.x) - 1, int(r.end.x) + 2):
				if x < 0 or z < 0 or x >= W or z >= H:
					continue
				var c := Vector2(x + 0.5, z + 0.5)
				var i := z * W + x
				if r.grow(-0.5).has_point(c):
					cls[i] = INSERT
					insert_h[i] = a[1]
					insert_fix[i] = 1
				elif r.grow(0.6).has_point(c) and cls[i] != INSERT and cls[i] != WATER and insert_fix[i] == 0:
					# a walkable rim so the insert always meets the town at its own height
					cls[i] = PATH
					insert_h[i] = a[1] - 0.03
					insert_fix[i] = 2
	_heights()
	_report()
	var out := Image.create(W, H, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBF)
	var dbg := Image.create(W, H, false, Image.FORMAT_RGB8)
	for z in H:
		for x in W:
			var i := z * W + x
			out.set_pixel(x, z, Color(hv[i], float(cls[i]), hc[i]))
			var k := clampf((hv[i] + 7.0) / 20.0, 0.0, 1.0)
			var col := Color(0.1, 0.25, 0.6) if cls[i] == WATER else Color(k, k, k)
			if _walk(cls[i]):
				col = Color(0.2 + k * 0.8, 0.6 * k + 0.2, 0.2)
			dbg.set_pixel(x, z, col)
	ResourceSaver.save(out, OUT)
	dbg.resize(W * 6, H * 6, Image.INTERPOLATE_NEAREST)
	dbg.save_png(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(DEBUG))
	print("wrote %s and %s" % [OUT, DEBUG])
	quit()


static func _walk(t: int) -> bool:
	return t == PATH or t == GRASS or t == SAND or t == INSERT


func _heights() -> void:
	var fixed := PackedByteArray()
	fixed.resize(W * H)
	for a: Array in ANCHORS:
		var c := (a[0] as Vector2 - ORIGIN) * PX
		for z in range(int(c.y - ANCHOR_R), int(c.y + ANCHOR_R) + 1):
			for x in range(int(c.x - ANCHOR_R), int(c.x + ANCHOR_R) + 1):
				if x < 0 or z < 0 or x >= W or z >= H:
					continue
				var i := z * W + x
				if _walk(cls[i]) and Vector2(x + 0.5, z + 0.5).distance_to(c) <= ANCHOR_R:
					fixed[i] = 1
					hv[i] = a[1]
	for i in W * H:
		if insert_fix[i] > 0:
			fixed[i] = 1
			hv[i] = insert_h[i]
	# successive over-relaxation over walk cells only
	for it in 4000:
		var worst := 0.0
		for z in H:
			for x in W:
				var i := z * W + x
				if fixed[i] == 1 or not _walk(cls[i]):
					continue
				var s := 0.0
				var n := 0
				if x > 0 and _walk(cls[i - 1]):
					s += hv[i - 1]; n += 1
				if x < W - 1 and _walk(cls[i + 1]):
					s += hv[i + 1]; n += 1
				if z > 0 and _walk(cls[i - W]):
					s += hv[i - W]; n += 1
				if z < H - 1 and _walk(cls[i + W]):
					s += hv[i + W]; n += 1
				if n == 0:
					continue
				var nv: float = hv[i] + 1.9 * (s / n - hv[i])
				worst = maxf(worst, absf(nv - hv[i]))
				hv[i] = nv
		if worst < 0.0005:
			print("relaxed in %d iterations" % it)
			break
	for i in W * H:
		if cls[i] == PATH and hv[i] < -4.6:
			cls[i] = SAND
	# non-walk cells: BFS from walk cells carrying the source height
	var base := PackedFloat32Array()
	base.resize(W * H)
	var dist := PackedInt32Array()
	dist.resize(W * H)
	dist.fill(-1)
	var q := PackedInt32Array()
	for i in W * H:
		if _walk(cls[i]):
			dist[i] = 0
			base[i] = hv[i]
			hc[i] = hv[i]
			q.append(i)
	var head := 0
	while head < q.size():
		var i := q[head]
		head += 1
		var x := i % W
		for n: int in [i - 1, i + 1, i - W, i + W]:
			if n < 0 or n >= W * H or dist[n] != -1:
				continue
			if (n == i - 1 and x == 0) or (n == i + 1 and x == W - 1):
				continue
			dist[n] = dist[i] + 1
			base[n] = base[i]
			q.append(n)
	for i in W * H:
		var t := cls[i]
		if t == INSERT:
			hc[i] = -30.0
			continue
		if _walk(t):
			continue
		var b := base[i] if dist[i] >= 0 else 0.0
		hc[i] = b + 4.0
		match t:
			WATER:
				hv[i] = SEA
			SLAB:
				hv[i] = b + 0.15
			_:
				hv[i] = b + minf(2.2 + dist[i] * 0.5, 5.0)


func _report() -> void:
	var worst := 0.0
	for z in H:
		for x in W - 1:
			var i := z * W + x
			if cls[i] == INSERT:
				continue
			if _walk(cls[i]) and _walk(cls[i + 1]) and cls[i + 1] != INSERT:
				worst = maxf(worst, absf(hv[i] - hv[i + 1]))
			if z < H - 1 and _walk(cls[i]) and _walk(cls[i + W]) and cls[i + W] != INSERT:
				worst = maxf(worst, absf(hv[i] - hv[i + W]))
	print("steepest walkable step between neighbouring cells: %.2f m (%.0f deg)" % [worst, rad_to_deg(atan(worst))])


==============================================================================
== tools/build_town_mask.gd
==============================================================================
extends SceneTree
## Builds the walkable mask for the Anjabi town greybox from the layout image itself.
##   Godot --headless --path . -s tools/build_town_mask.gd
## Walkways, beach, docks (beige) and vegetation (green) are walkable; cliffs,
## buildings and water are not. Light-grey slabs that are meant to be walked on
## cannot be told apart from buildings by colour, so they are listed in SLABS.
## Re-run after replacing maps/anjabi/town_greybox.png.

const SRC := "res://maps/anjabi/town_greybox.png"
const OUT := "res://maps/anjabi/town_walk.res"
const DEBUG := "res://docs/reference/town_walkable_debug.png"
const FRAME := Rect2i(34, 34, 1380, 902)
const GREEN_WALKABLE := true
const CLOSE_RADIUS := 3
## Image-pixel polygons forced walkable (mine yard, airpad).
const SLABS := [
	[Vector2(132, 655), Vector2(215, 642), Vector2(295, 668), Vector2(300, 715), Vector2(240, 768), Vector2(150, 755)],
	[Vector2(327, 790), Vector2(357, 750), Vector2(508, 750), Vector2(508, 826), Vector2(425, 850), Vector2(327, 800)],
]
## Landmarks (image pixels) checked for reachability from the first one.
const LANDMARKS := {
	"Steps": Vector2(880, 415), "Forest": Vector2(230, 110), "Garden": Vector2(200, 345),
	"Estate": Vector2(240, 578), "Mine": Vector2(212, 700), "Airpad": Vector2(450, 800),
	"Beach": Vector2(650, 640), "Tion Tower": Vector2(515, 465), "Tavern": Vector2(760, 350),
	"Pods": Vector2(580, 70), "Guard Tower": Vector2(945, 130), "Docks": Vector2(1170, 460),
	"Shop": Vector2(930, 385), "Tram": Vector2(970, 465), "Rainforest": Vector2(1380, 325),
}

var w := 0
var h := 0


func _init() -> void:
	var img := Image.load_from_file(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(SRC))
	img.convert(Image.FORMAT_RGB8)
	w = img.get_width()
	h = img.get_height()
	var px := img.get_data()
	var m := PackedByteArray()
	m.resize(w * h)
	for y in range(FRAME.position.y, FRAME.end.y):
		for x in range(FRAME.position.x, FRAME.end.x):
			var i := (y * w + x) * 3
			var r := px[i]
			var g := px[i + 1]
			var b := px[i + 2]
			var beige := r > 205 and g > 185 and b < 228 and r - b > 22 and r >= g
			var green := g > r + 12 and g > b + 25
			if beige or (GREEN_WALKABLE and green):
				m[y * w + x] = 1
	for poly: Array in SLABS:
		var pts := PackedVector2Array(poly)
		var box := Rect2(pts[0], Vector2.ZERO)
		for p in pts:
			box = box.expand(p)
		for y in range(int(box.position.y), int(box.end.y) + 1):
			for x in range(int(box.position.x), int(box.end.x) + 1):
				if Geometry2D.is_point_in_polygon(Vector2(x, y), pts):
					m[y * w + x] = 1
	# close small gaps: label text, stair treads, outlines
	m = _spread(m, CLOSE_RADIUS, 1)
	m = _spread(m, CLOSE_RADIUS, 0)
	for y in h:
		for x in w:
			if not FRAME.has_point(Vector2i(x, y)):
				m[y * w + x] = 0

	var bm := BitMap.new()
	bm.create(Vector2i(w, h))
	var dbg := Image.load_from_file(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(SRC))
	dbg.convert(Image.FORMAT_RGB8)
	var reach := _flood(m, _nearest(m, LANDMARKS["Steps"]))
	for y in h:
		for x in w:
			var i := y * w + x
			if m[i] == 1:
				bm.set_bit(x, y, true)
				dbg.set_pixel(x, y, Color(0.2, 0.9, 0.4) if reach[i] == 1 else Color(0.95, 0.2, 0.7))
			else:
				dbg.set_pixel(x, y, dbg.get_pixel(x, y).darkened(0.55))
	ResourceSaver.save(bm, OUT)
	dbg.save_png(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(DEBUG))
	for k: String in LANDMARKS:
		var p := _nearest(m, LANDMARKS[k])
		var ok := p.x >= 0 and reach[p.y * w + p.x] == 1
		print("%-12s %s  (snapped %.0f px)" % [k, "reachable" if ok else "NOT REACHABLE", Vector2(p).distance_to(LANDMARKS[k])])
	quit()


## Separable box dilation of cells equal to `value`.
func _spread(src: PackedByteArray, r: int, value: int) -> PackedByteArray:
	var tmp := src.duplicate()
	for y in h:
		var run := 0
		for x in range(-r, w):
			var add := x + r
			if add < w and src[y * w + add] == value:
				run += 1
			var drop := x - r - 1
			if drop >= 0 and src[y * w + drop] == value:
				run -= 1
			if x >= 0 and run > 0:
				tmp[y * w + x] = value
	var out := tmp.duplicate()
	for x in w:
		var run := 0
		for y in range(-r, h):
			var add := y + r
			if add < h and tmp[add * w + x] == value:
				run += 1
			var drop := y - r - 1
			if drop >= 0 and tmp[drop * w + x] == value:
				run -= 1
			if y >= 0 and run > 0:
				out[y * w + x] = value
	return out


func _nearest(m: PackedByteArray, at: Vector2) -> Vector2i:
	var c := Vector2i(at)
	for r in range(0, 60):
		for dy in range(-r, r + 1):
			for dx in range(-r, r + 1):
				if maxi(absi(dx), absi(dy)) != r:
					continue
				var p := c + Vector2i(dx, dy)
				if p.x >= 0 and p.y >= 0 and p.x < w and p.y < h and m[p.y * w + p.x] == 1:
					return p
	return Vector2i(-1, -1)


func _flood(m: PackedByteArray, start: Vector2i) -> PackedByteArray:
	var seen := PackedByteArray()
	seen.resize(w * h)
	if start.x < 0:
		return seen
	var stack := PackedInt32Array([start.y * w + start.x])
	seen[stack[0]] = 1
	while not stack.is_empty():
		var i := stack[stack.size() - 1]
		stack.remove_at(stack.size() - 1)
		var x := i % w
		for n: int in [i - 1, i + 1, i - w, i + w]:
			if n < 0 or n >= w * h or seen[n] == 1 or m[n] == 0:
				continue
			if (n == i - 1 and x == 0) or (n == i + 1 and x == w - 1):
				continue
			seen[n] = 1
			stack.append(n)
	return seen


==============================================================================
== tools/bundle_source.py
==============================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Concatenate every text source file into docs/ALL_SOURCE.txt for reviewers and
tools that cannot browse the repo. Run from the project root:
    python3 tools/bundle_source.py
"""
import os

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
OUT = os.path.join(ROOT, "docs", "ALL_SOURCE.txt")
EXTS = (".gd", ".gdshader", ".json", ".tscn", ".godot", ".py", ".sh")
SKIP_DIRS = {".git", ".godot", "export", "docs"}

paths = []
for base, dirs, files in os.walk(ROOT):
    dirs[:] = sorted(d for d in dirs if d not in SKIP_DIRS)
    for f in sorted(files):
        if f.endswith(EXTS):
            paths.append(os.path.relpath(os.path.join(base, f), ROOT))

with open(OUT, "w") as out:
    out.write("COMETO - all source files. Index:\n")
    for p in paths:
        out.write("  %s\n" % p)
    for p in paths:
        out.write("\n\n" + "=" * 78 + "\n== %s\n" % p + "=" * 78 + "\n")
        with open(os.path.join(ROOT, p)) as f:
            out.write(f.read())
print("wrote %s (%d files)" % (os.path.relpath(OUT, ROOT), len(paths)))

== tools/package_review.sh
==============================================================================
#!/bin/sh
# Builds export/COMETO_review_<date>.zip: the whole project minus caches and git
# history, plus docs/ALL_SOURCE.txt. Run from the project root:
#     sh tools/package_review.sh
set -e
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
python3 tools/bundle_source.py
mkdir -p export
touch export/.gdignore
OUT="export/COMETO_review_$(date +%Y-%m-%d).zip"
rm -f "$OUT"
zip -r -q "$OUT" . -x ".git/*" ".godot/*" "export/*" "*.DS_Store" "*.tmp"
echo "$OUT  $(du -h "$OUT" | cut -f1)"
