== ui/compass.gd
==============================================================================
class_name Compass
extends Control
## Compass, bottom-left: a hairline ring with tick marks and a north pointer. The
## camera never rotates, so north is always up.

func _ready() -> void:
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	custom_minimum_size = Vector2(40, 40)


func _draw() -> void:
	var c := size * 0.5
	var r := minf(size.x, size.y) * 0.5 - 3.0
	var line := Color(UiTheme.ACCENT, 0.55)
	draw_arc(c, r, 0.0, TAU, 48, Color(UiTheme.ACCENT, 0.3), 1.0, true)
	# open inner arcs, like an instrument bezel
	for i in 4:
		var a0 := TAU * i / 4.0 + 0.3
		draw_arc(c, r * 0.62, a0, a0 + PI * 0.5 - 0.6, 10, Color(UiTheme.ACCENT, 0.22), 1.0, true)
	for i in 16:
		var d := Vector2.from_angle(TAU * i / 16.0)
		var inner := r - (4.0 if i % 4 == 0 else 2.0)
		draw_line(c + d * inner, c + d * r, line if i % 4 == 0 else Color(line, 0.3), 1.0, true)
	# north pointer and centre mark
	var tip := c + Vector2(0, -r + 5.0)
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([tip, tip + Vector2(2.5, 5), tip + Vector2(-2.5, 5)]), UiTheme.BONE)
	draw_line(c + Vector2(-2, 0), c + Vector2(2, 0), line, 1.0)
	draw_line(c + Vector2(0, -2), c + Vector2(0, 2), line, 1.0)
	var f := UiTheme.caps_font()
	draw_string(f, c + Vector2(-2.5, -r - 1.0), "N", HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT, -1, 6, Color(UiTheme.BONE, 0.85))


==============================================================================
== ui/dialogue_box.gd
==============================================================================
class_name DialogueBox
extends Control
## Renders the Dialogue autoload. Tap / interact to finish a line, then to advance.

@export var chars_per_second := 70.0

var _panel: PanelContainer
var _speaker: Label
var _body: RichTextLabel
var _choices: VBoxContainer
var _more: Label
var _shown := 0.0
var _typing := false


func _ready() -> void:
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	visible = false
	_panel = PanelContainer.new()
	add_child(_panel)
	_panel.anchor_left = 0.14
	_panel.anchor_right = 0.86
	_panel.anchor_top = 1.0
	_panel.anchor_bottom = 1.0
	_panel.offset_bottom = -10
	_panel.grow_vertical = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BEGIN
	_panel.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	var box := VBoxContainer.new()
	box.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	_panel.add_child(box)
	_speaker = Label.new()
	_speaker.add_theme_color_override("font_color", UiTheme.ACCENT)
	_speaker.add_theme_font_override("font", UiTheme.caps_font())
	_speaker.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 7)
	box.add_child(_speaker)
	_body = RichTextLabel.new()
	_body.fit_content = true
	_body.scroll_active = false
	_body.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 30)
	_body.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	box.add_child(_body)
	_choices = VBoxContainer.new()
	box.add_child(_choices)
	_more = Label.new()
	_more.text = "▾"
	_more.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	_more.add_theme_color_override("font_color", UiTheme.ACCENT)
	_more.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 8)
	box.add_child(_more)

	Dialogue.started.connect(func(_id: String) -> void: visible = true)
	Dialogue.ended.connect(_on_ended)
	Dialogue.line_shown.connect(_on_line)
	Dialogue.choices_shown.connect(_on_choices)


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if not _typing:
		return
	_shown += delta * chars_per_second
	if _shown >= _body.text.length():
		_finish_typing()
	else:
		_body.visible_characters = int(_shown)


func _input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if not visible or not event.is_action_pressed(&"interact"):
		return
	get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
	if _choices.get_child_count() > 0:
		var f := get_viewport().gui_get_focus_owner() as Button
		if f != null and f.get_parent() == _choices:
			f.pressed.emit()
	else:
		_step()


func _gui_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	var mb := event as InputEventMouseButton
	if mb != null and mb.pressed and mb.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT:
		accept_event()
		if _choices.get_child_count() == 0:
			_step()


func _step() -> void:
	if _typing:
		_finish_typing()
	else:
		Dialogue.advance()


func _finish_typing() -> void:
	_typing = false
	_body.visible_characters = -1
	_more.visible = _choices.get_child_count() == 0


func _on_line(speaker: String, text: String) -> void:
	_clear_choices()
	_speaker.text = speaker.to_upper()
	_speaker.visible = speaker != ""
	_body.add_theme_color_override("default_color", UiTheme.BONE if speaker != "" else Color(0.68, 0.78, 0.84))
	_body.text = text
	_body.visible_characters = 0
	_shown = 0.0
	_typing = true
	_more.visible = false


func _on_choices(options: Array[String]) -> void:
	_finish_typing()
	_clear_choices()
	_more.visible = false
	for i in options.size():
		var b := Button.new()
		b.text = "›  " + options[i]
		b.alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT
		b.pressed.connect(Dialogue.choose.bind(i))
		_choices.add_child(b)
	if not options.is_empty():
		(_choices.get_child(0) as Button).grab_focus.call_deferred()


func _on_ended(_id: String) -> void:
	_clear_choices()
	_typing = false
	visible = false


func _clear_choices() -> void:
	for c in _choices.get_children():
		_choices.remove_child(c)
		c.queue_free()


==============================================================================
== ui/game_ui.gd
==============================================================================
class_name GameUi
extends CanvasLayer
## Builds and owns every screen-space control. Panels pause the world.

signal new_game_requested
## The player chose "Title screen" in the pause menu.
signal title_requested

var touch: TouchControls
var hud: Hud
var dialogue_box: DialogueBox
var journal: JournalPanel
var menu: PauseMenu


func _ready() -> void:
	process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS
	var root := Control.new()
	root.theme = UiTheme.build()
	root.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	add_child(root)
	root.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)

	touch = TouchControls.new()
	touch.process_mode = Node.PROCESS_MODE_PAUSABLE
	hud = Hud.new()
	dialogue_box = DialogueBox.new()
	journal = JournalPanel.new()
	menu = PauseMenu.new()
	for c: Control in [touch, hud, dialogue_box, journal, menu]:
		root.add_child(c)
		c.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)

	hud.journal_pressed.connect(toggle_journal)
	hud.menu_pressed.connect(toggle_menu)
	journal.closed.connect(close_panels)
	menu.closed.connect(close_panels)
	menu.title_requested.connect(func() -> void: title_requested.emit())
	menu.new_game_requested.connect(func() -> void: new_game_requested.emit())


func bind_player(p: Node) -> void:
	hud.bind_player(p)


func toggle_journal() -> void:
	if journal.visible:
		close_panels()
	elif not Dialogue.active:
		close_panels()
		journal.refresh()
		journal.visible = true
		_set_paused(true)


func toggle_menu() -> void:
	if menu.visible or journal.visible:
		close_panels()
	elif not Dialogue.active:
		menu.visible = true
		menu.open()
		_set_paused(true)


func close_panels() -> void:
	journal.visible = false
	menu.visible = false
	_set_paused(false)


func _set_paused(v: bool) -> void:
	hud.visible = not v
	get_tree().paused = v


func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event.is_action_pressed(&"journal"):
		toggle_journal()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()
	elif event.is_action_pressed(&"menu"):
		toggle_menu()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()


==============================================================================
== ui/hud.gd
==============================================================================
class_name Hud
extends Control
## Minimal in-world HUD: one contextual prompt chip, two small corner chips,
## transient journal toasts and the location title card.

signal journal_pressed
signal menu_pressed

## Player or Player3D.
var player

var _safe: Control
var _prompt: Button
var _run: Button
var _clock: WorldClockHUD
var _toast: Label
var _title: Label
var _queue: Array[String] = []
var _toasting := false
var _title_tween: Tween


func _ready() -> void:
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	_safe = Control.new()
	_safe.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	add_child(_safe)
	_safe.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)

	var corner := HBoxContainer.new()
	_safe.add_child(corner)
	corner.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_TOP_RIGHT)
	corner.grow_horizontal = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BEGIN
	corner.position += Vector2(-6, 6)
	corner.add_child(_chip("JOURNAL", func() -> void: journal_pressed.emit()))
	corner.add_child(_chip("MENU", func() -> void: menu_pressed.emit()))

	_prompt = _chip("", func() -> void:
		if player != null:
			player.call("try_interact"))
	_prompt.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 8)
	_safe.add_child(_prompt)
	_prompt.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_CENTER_BOTTOM)
	_prompt.grow_horizontal = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BOTH
	_prompt.grow_vertical = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BEGIN
	_prompt.offset_bottom = -12
	_prompt.offset_top = -12
	_prompt.visible = false

	_clock = WorldClockHUD.new()
	_safe.add_child(_clock)
	_clock.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_TOP_RIGHT)
	_clock.grow_horizontal = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BEGIN
	_clock.position += Vector2(-8, 26)

	# run toggle for touch play; Shift (or pad B) does the same while held
	_run = _chip("RUN", func() -> void: pass)
	_run.toggle_mode = true
	_run.toggled.connect(func(on: bool) -> void:
		if player != null and player.get("touch") != null:
			player.touch.sprint = on)
	_safe.add_child(_run)
	_run.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_BOTTOM_RIGHT)
	_run.grow_horizontal = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BEGIN
	_run.grow_vertical = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BEGIN
	_run.position += Vector2(-8, -10)

	var tracker := QuestTracker.new()
	_safe.add_child(tracker)
	tracker.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_TOP_LEFT)
	tracker.position = Vector2(10, 8)
	var compass := Compass.new()
	_safe.add_child(compass)
	compass.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_BOTTOM_LEFT)
	compass.size = Vector2(40, 40)
	compass.position += Vector2(10, -50)

	_toast = Label.new()
	_toast.add_theme_color_override("font_color", UiTheme.ACCENT)
	_toast.add_theme_color_override("font_shadow_color", UiTheme.SHADOW)
	_toast.add_theme_font_override("font", UiTheme.caps_font())
	_toast.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 7)
	_toast.modulate.a = 0.0
	_toast.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	_safe.add_child(_toast)
	_toast.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_CENTER_TOP)
	_toast.grow_horizontal = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BOTH
	_toast.offset_top = 70

	_title = Label.new()
	_title.add_theme_font_override("font", UiTheme.caps_font())
	_title.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 13)
	_title.add_theme_color_override("font_shadow_color", UiTheme.SHADOW)
	_title.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	_title.modulate.a = 0.0
	_safe.add_child(_title)
	_title.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_CENTER_TOP)
	_title.grow_horizontal = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BOTH
	_title.offset_top = 46

	Journal.updated.connect(_on_journal_updated)
	GameState.notified.connect(toast)
	GameState.location_entered.connect(show_location)
	get_viewport().size_changed.connect(_apply_safe_area)
	_apply_safe_area()


func bind_player(p: Node) -> void:
	player = p
	if p.get("touch") != null:
		p.touch.sprint = _run.button_pressed


func clock_text() -> String:
	return _clock.text()


func show_location(text: String) -> void:
	_title.text = text
	if _title_tween != null:
		_title_tween.kill()
	_title.modulate.a = 0.0
	_title_tween = create_tween()
	_title_tween.tween_property(_title, "modulate:a", 1.0, 0.8).set_delay(0.3)
	_title_tween.tween_interval(2.2)
	_title_tween.tween_property(_title, "modulate:a", 0.0, 1.2)


func toast(text: String) -> void:
	_queue.append(text)
	if not _toasting:
		_next_toast()


func prompt_text() -> String:
	return _prompt.text if _prompt.visible else ""


func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
	var t = player.get("target") if is_instance_valid(player) else null
	var show := t != null and not Dialogue.active
	_prompt.visible = show
	_run.visible = not Dialogue.active
	if show:
		var text: String = str(t.prompt).to_upper()
		if t.label != "":
			text += "  ·  " + t.label
		if _prompt.text != text:
			_prompt.text = text


func _next_toast() -> void:
	if _queue.is_empty():
		_toasting = false
		return
	_toasting = true
	_toast.text = _queue.pop_front()
	var tw := create_tween()
	tw.tween_property(_toast, "modulate:a", 1.0, 0.25)
	tw.tween_interval(2.4)
	tw.tween_property(_toast, "modulate:a", 0.0, 0.5)
	tw.tween_callback(_next_toast)


func _on_journal_updated(kind: String, text: String) -> void:
	match kind:
		"quest":
			toast("INVESTIGATION OPENED  ·  " + text)
		"objective_new":
			toast("NEW LEAD  ·  " + text)
		"objective":
			toast("LEAD FOLLOWED  ·  " + text)
		"clue":
			toast("CLUE RECORDED  ·  " + text)
		"complete":
			toast("JOURNAL UPDATED  ·  " + text)


func _chip(text: String, on_press: Callable) -> Button:
	var b := Button.new()
	b.text = text
	b.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 7)
	UiTheme.chip(b)
	b.pressed.connect(on_press)
	return b


## Keeps HUD chips clear of the notch and home indicator. Untested on device.
func _apply_safe_area() -> void:
	if not OS.has_feature("mobile"):
		return
	var win := Vector2(DisplayServer.window_get_size())
	if win.x <= 0.0 or win.y <= 0.0:
		return
	var safe := Rect2(DisplayServer.get_display_safe_area())
	var k := get_viewport_rect().size / win
	_safe.offset_left = safe.position.x * k.x
	_safe.offset_top = safe.position.y * k.y
	_safe.offset_right = -(win.x - safe.end.x) * k.x
	_safe.offset_bottom = -(win.y - safe.end.y) * k.y


==============================================================================
== ui/journal_panel.gd
==============================================================================
class_name JournalPanel
extends Control
## Investigations and clues, read straight from the Journal autoload.

signal closed

var _quests: RichTextLabel
var _clues: RichTextLabel


func _ready() -> void:
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	visible = false
	var shade := ColorRect.new()
	shade.color = Color(0.0, 0.04, 0.08, 0.55)
	shade.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	add_child(shade)
	shade.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	var panel := PanelContainer.new()
	add_child(panel)
	panel.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT, Control.PRESET_MODE_MINSIZE, 18)
	var root := VBoxContainer.new()
	panel.add_child(root)
	var cols := HBoxContainer.new()
	cols.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	cols.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 14)
	root.add_child(cols)
	_quests = _column(cols, "INVESTIGATIONS")
	_clues = _column(cols, "CLUES")
	var close := Button.new()
	close.text = "CLOSE"
	close.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_END
	close.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 8)
	UiTheme.chip(close)
	close.pressed.connect(func() -> void: closed.emit())
	root.add_child(close)


func refresh() -> void:
	var amber := UiTheme.ACCENT.to_html(false)
	var dim := UiTheme.DIM.to_html(false)
	var q := ""
	for id: String in Journal.quests:
		var def: Dictionary = Journal.quest_defs.get(id, {})
		q += "[color=#%s]%s[/color]" % [amber, Journal.quest_title(id)]
		if Journal.is_complete(id):
			q += "  [color=#%s](to be continued)[/color]" % dim
		q += "\n[color=#%s]%s[/color]\n\n" % [dim, str(def.get("summary", ""))]
		for o: Dictionary in def.get("objectives", []):
			var st := Journal.objective_state(id, str(o["id"]))
			if st == Journal.DONE:
				q += "[color=#%s]✓  %s[/color]\n" % [dim, str(o["text"])]
			elif st == Journal.ACTIVE:
				q += "◦  %s\n" % str(o["text"])
		q += "\n"
	_quests.text = q if q != "" else "[color=#%s]Nothing yet.[/color]" % dim
	var c := ""
	for id in Journal.clues:
		var def: Dictionary = Journal.clue_defs.get(id, {})
		c += "[color=#%s]%s[/color]\n%s\n\n" % [amber, str(def.get("title", id)), str(def.get("text", ""))]
	_clues.text = c if c != "" else "[color=#%s]Nothing recorded.[/color]" % dim


func _column(parent: Control, title: String) -> RichTextLabel:
	var col := VBoxContainer.new()
	col.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	col.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	parent.add_child(col)
	var head := Label.new()
	head.text = title
	head.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 8)
	head.add_theme_color_override("font_color", UiTheme.DIM)
	col.add_child(head)
	var body := RichTextLabel.new()
	body.bbcode_enabled = true
	body.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	col.add_child(body)
	return body


==============================================================================
== ui/pause_menu.gd
==============================================================================
class_name PauseMenu
extends Control

signal closed
signal new_game_requested
signal title_requested

## Set by game/app.gd when a title screen exists to return to.
var title_enabled := false
var _title: Button

var _status: Label
var _load: Button
var _new: Button
var _first: Button
var _confirm_new := false


func _ready() -> void:
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	visible = false
	var shade := ColorRect.new()
	shade.color = Color(0.0, 0.04, 0.08, 0.55)
	shade.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	add_child(shade)
	shade.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	var panel := PanelContainer.new()
	panel.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(150, 0)
	add_child(panel)
	panel.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_CENTER)
	panel.grow_horizontal = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BOTH
	panel.grow_vertical = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BOTH
	var box := VBoxContainer.new()
	panel.add_child(box)
	var title := Label.new()
	title.text = "COMETO"
	title.add_theme_color_override("font_color", UiTheme.ACCENT)
	title.add_theme_font_override("font", UiTheme.caps_font())
	title.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 10)
	box.add_child(title)
	_first = _button(box, "Resume", func() -> void: closed.emit())
	_button(box, "Save", _on_save)
	_load = _button(box, "Load", _on_load)
	_new = _button(box, "New game", _on_new)
	_title = _button(box, "Title screen", func() -> void: title_requested.emit())
	_status = Label.new()
	_status.add_theme_color_override("font_color", UiTheme.DIM)
	_status.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 8)
	box.add_child(_status)


func open() -> void:
	_confirm_new = false
	_title.visible = title_enabled
	_new.text = "New game"
	_status.text = "The game also saves itself when you leave."
	_load.disabled = not SaveManager.has_save("manual")
	_first.grab_focus.call_deferred()


func _on_save() -> void:
	var ok := SaveManager.save_game("manual")
	_status.text = "Saved." if ok else "Could not save."
	_load.disabled = not SaveManager.has_save("manual")


func _on_load() -> void:
	if SaveManager.load_game("manual"):
		closed.emit()
	else:
		_status.text = "Could not load."


func _on_new() -> void:
	if not _confirm_new:
		_confirm_new = true
		_new.text = "New game - press again to confirm"
		return
	new_game_requested.emit()
	closed.emit()


func _button(parent: Control, text: String, on_press: Callable) -> Button:
	var b := Button.new()
	b.text = text
	b.alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT
	b.pressed.connect(on_press)
	parent.add_child(b)
	return b


==============================================================================
== ui/quest_tracker.gd
==============================================================================
class_name QuestTracker
extends VBoxContainer
## Top-left tracker in the style of the director's key art: the game's name, then the
## open investigation with its active objectives as checkboxes. Reads Journal; draws
## nothing of its own state.

var _name: Label
var _rule: ColorRect
var _quest: Label
var _list: VBoxContainer


func _ready() -> void:
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	add_theme_constant_override("separation", 2)
	_name = _label("COMETOGRAPHIA", 6, UiTheme.DIM)
	_name.add_theme_font_override("font", UiTheme.caps_font())
	_rule = ColorRect.new()
	_rule.color = Color(UiTheme.ACCENT, 0.6)
	_rule.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(110, 1)
	_rule.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	add_child(_rule)
	_quest = _label("", 8, UiTheme.ACCENT)
	_quest.add_theme_font_override("font", UiTheme.caps_font())
	_list = VBoxContainer.new()
	_list.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	_list.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 0)
	add_child(_list)
	Journal.updated.connect(func(_k: String, _t: String) -> void: refresh())
	refresh()


func refresh() -> void:
	for c in _list.get_children():
		c.queue_free()
	var qid := ""
	for id: String in Journal.quests:
		if not Journal.is_complete(id):
			qid = id
			break
	if qid == "":
		_quest.text = ""
		return
	_quest.text = Journal.quest_title(qid).to_upper()
	for o: Dictionary in Journal.quest_defs.get(qid, {}).get("objectives", []):
		var st := Journal.objective_state(qid, str(o["id"]))
		if st == Journal.ACTIVE:
			_list.add_child(_label_node("–  " + str(o["text"]), 7, UiTheme.BONE))
		elif st == Journal.DONE:
			_list.add_child(_label_node("✓  " + str(o["text"]), 7, Color(UiTheme.DIM, 0.8)))


func _label(text: String, size: int, col: Color) -> Label:
	var l := _label_node(text, size, col)
	add_child(l)
	return l


func _label_node(text: String, size: int, col: Color) -> Label:
	var l := Label.new()
	l.text = text
	l.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", size)
	l.add_theme_color_override("font_color", col)
	l.add_theme_color_override("font_shadow_color", UiTheme.SHADOW)
	l.add_theme_constant_override("shadow_offset_x", 1)
	l.add_theme_constant_override("shadow_offset_y", 1)
	l.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	return l

== ui/tech_panel_style.gd
==============================================================================
class_name TechPanelStyle
extends StyleBox
## Panel look for the interface: a dark blue translucent field, a hairline outline,
## brighter corner brackets and a short header tick. Drawn, not textured, so it is
## crisp at any size.

var bg := Color(0.02, 0.06, 0.1, 0.86)
var line := Color(0.56, 0.82, 0.94, 0.22)
var bracket := Color(0.56, 0.82, 0.94, 0.9)
var bracket_len := 6.0
var tick := true


func _init(pad: float = 8.0) -> void:
	set_content_margin_all(pad)


func _draw(to_canvas_item: RID, rect: Rect2) -> void:
	var rs := RenderingServer
	rs.canvas_item_add_rect(to_canvas_item, rect, bg)
	var a := rect.position
	var b := rect.end
	# hairline outline
	for r: Rect2 in [Rect2(a.x, a.y, rect.size.x, 1), Rect2(a.x, b.y - 1, rect.size.x, 1),
			Rect2(a.x, a.y, 1, rect.size.y), Rect2(b.x - 1, a.y, 1, rect.size.y)]:
		rs.canvas_item_add_rect(to_canvas_item, r, line)
	# corner brackets
	var n := minf(bracket_len, minf(rect.size.x, rect.size.y) * 0.4)
	for c: Array in [[a.x, a.y, 1, 1], [b.x, a.y, -1, 1], [a.x, b.y, 1, -1], [b.x, b.y, -1, -1]]:
		var x: float = c[0]
		var y: float = c[1]
		var sx: float = c[2]
		var sy: float = c[3]
		rs.canvas_item_add_rect(to_canvas_item, Rect2(minf(x, x + sx * n), y if sy > 0 else y - 1, n, 1), bracket)
		rs.canvas_item_add_rect(to_canvas_item, Rect2(x if sx > 0 else x - 1, minf(y, y + sy * n), 1, n), bracket)
	if tick and rect.size.x > 40.0:
		rs.canvas_item_add_rect(to_canvas_item, Rect2(a.x + n + 4, a.y, 14, 1), Color(bracket, 0.5))

== ui/touch_controls.gd
==============================================================================
class_name TouchControls
extends Control
## Touch (and mouse) movement without on-screen buttons: drag anywhere to steer,
## tap to interact. Sits underneath every other control, so HUD chips and panels
## take their own touches first.

signal tapped

@export var deadzone := 5.0
@export var stick_radius := 22.0
@export var tap_ms := 350

var vector := Vector2.ZERO
## Run toggle for touch play (the HUD's RUN chip sets it); Shift does the same on keys.
var sprint := false

var _down := false
var _dragged := false
var _origin := Vector2.ZERO
var _knob := Vector2.ZERO
var _t0 := 0


func _ready() -> void:
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	Dialogue.started.connect(func(_id: String) -> void: release())


func release() -> void:
	_down = false
	_dragged = false
	vector = Vector2.ZERO
	queue_redraw()


func _notification(what: int) -> void:
	if what == NOTIFICATION_PAUSED or what == NOTIFICATION_VISIBILITY_CHANGED:
		release()


func _gui_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	var mb := event as InputEventMouseButton
	if mb != null and mb.button_index == MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT:
		if mb.pressed:
			_down = true
			_dragged = false
			_origin = mb.position
			_knob = mb.position
			_t0 = Time.get_ticks_msec()
		else:
			var was_tap := _down and not _dragged and Time.get_ticks_msec() - _t0 <= tap_ms
			release()
			if was_tap:
				tapped.emit()
		return
	var mm := event as InputEventMouseMotion
	if mm != null and _down:
		var d := mm.position - _origin
		if d.length() > deadzone:
			_dragged = true
		if _dragged:
			# floating stick: the origin trails the finger once it leaves the ring
			if d.length() > stick_radius:
				_origin = mm.position - d.normalized() * stick_radius
				d = mm.position - _origin
			vector = d / stick_radius
			_knob = mm.position
			queue_redraw()


func _draw() -> void:
	if not _dragged:
		return
	draw_arc(_origin, stick_radius, 0.0, TAU, 40, Color(1, 1, 1, 0.14), 1.0)
	draw_circle(_knob, 4.0, Color(1, 1, 1, 0.22))

== ui/ui_theme.gd
==============================================================================
class_name UiTheme
## Interface look, built in code so nothing falls back to default Godot styling.
## Direction: calm, minimal, technical. Deep blue translucent fields, hairlines and
## corner brackets, pale cyan accents, small spaced capitals for labels.
## PROVISIONAL: system typeface. A licensed display/body face should replace it.

const INK := Color(0.02, 0.06, 0.1, 0.86)
const INK_SOFT := Color(0.02, 0.06, 0.1, 0.5)
## Primary accent: pale cyan.
const ACCENT := Color(0.56, 0.82, 0.94)
const ACCENT_DEEP := Color(0.24, 0.5, 0.68)
const BONE := Color(0.9, 0.95, 0.98)
const DIM := Color(0.5, 0.62, 0.7)
const SHADOW := Color(0.0, 0.03, 0.06, 0.75)

static var _caps: FontVariation


static func box(bg: Color, border: Color, border_w: int = 1, pad: float = 6.0) -> StyleBoxFlat:
	var s := StyleBoxFlat.new()
	s.bg_color = bg
	s.border_color = border
	s.set_border_width_all(border_w)
	s.set_content_margin_all(pad)
	s.anti_aliasing = false
	return s


static func base_font() -> SystemFont:
	var f := SystemFont.new()
	f.font_names = PackedStringArray(["DIN Alternate", "Avenir Next", "Helvetica Neue", "Roboto", "sans-serif"])
	f.font_weight = 400
	return f


## Letter-spaced face for labels and headers set in capitals.
static func caps_font() -> FontVariation:
	if _caps == null:
		_caps = FontVariation.new()
		_caps.base_font = base_font()
		_caps.spacing_glyph = 1
	return _caps


## Style a label as a small spaced-capitals header.
static func caps(l: Control, size: int, col: Color) -> void:
	l.add_theme_font_override("font", caps_font())
	l.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", size)
	l.add_theme_color_override("font_color", col)


static func build() -> Theme:
	var t := Theme.new()
	t.default_font = base_font()
	t.default_font_size = 10

	t.set_stylebox("panel", "PanelContainer", TechPanelStyle.new(8.0))

	# buttons: bare text, a left accent bar when focused, a faint wash on hover
	var flat := box(Color(0, 0, 0, 0), Color(0, 0, 0, 0), 0, 3.0)
	flat.content_margin_left = 7.0
	var hover := box(Color(ACCENT, 0.1), Color(0, 0, 0, 0), 0, 3.0)
	hover.content_margin_left = 7.0
	var focus := box(Color(ACCENT, 0.08), ACCENT, 0, 3.0)
	focus.border_width_left = 2
	focus.content_margin_left = 7.0
	t.set_stylebox("normal", "Button", flat)
	t.set_stylebox("hover", "Button", hover)
	t.set_stylebox("pressed", "Button", hover)
	t.set_stylebox("focus", "Button", focus)
	t.set_stylebox("disabled", "Button", flat)
	t.set_color("font_color", "Button", BONE)
	t.set_color("font_hover_color", "Button", ACCENT)
	t.set_color("font_pressed_color", "Button", ACCENT)
	t.set_color("font_focus_color", "Button", ACCENT)
	t.set_color("font_disabled_color", "Button", Color(DIM, 0.5))

	# sliders: a hairline track, accent fill, a small bar for the grabber
	var track := StyleBoxLine.new()
	track.color = Color(DIM, 0.5)
	track.thickness = 1
	var fill := StyleBoxLine.new()
	fill.color = ACCENT
	fill.thickness = 1
	t.set_stylebox("slider", "HSlider", track)
	t.set_stylebox("grabber_area", "HSlider", fill)
	t.set_stylebox("grabber_area_highlight", "HSlider", fill)
	t.set_icon("grabber", "HSlider", _bar(BONE))
	t.set_icon("grabber_highlight", "HSlider", _bar(ACCENT))
	t.set_icon("grabber_disabled", "HSlider", _bar(DIM))
	# text entry: an underline, nothing else
	var edit := box(Color(ACCENT, 0.05), Color(ACCENT, 0.45), 0, 3.0)
	edit.border_width_bottom = 1
	var edit_focus := box(Color(ACCENT, 0.1), ACCENT, 0, 3.0)
	edit_focus.border_width_bottom = 1
	t.set_stylebox("normal", "LineEdit", edit)
	t.set_stylebox("focus", "LineEdit", edit_focus)
	t.set_color("font_color", "LineEdit", BONE)
	t.set_color("font_placeholder_color", "LineEdit", Color(DIM, 0.7))
	t.set_color("caret_color", "LineEdit", ACCENT)

	t.set_color("font_color", "Label", BONE)
	t.set_color("default_color", "RichTextLabel", BONE)
	t.set_font_size("normal_font_size", "RichTextLabel", 10)
	t.set_constant("line_separation", "RichTextLabel", 2)
	t.set_constant("separation", "VBoxContainer", 2)
	t.set_constant("separation", "HBoxContainer", 6)
	return t


static func _bar(c: Color) -> ImageTexture:
	var img := Image.create(3, 9, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	img.fill(c)
	return ImageTexture.create_from_image(img)


## Insets (left, top, right, bottom) in the control's own units that keep content clear
## of the notch and home indicator. Zero off mobile. Untested on device.
static func safe_insets(c: Control) -> Vector4:
	if not OS.has_feature("mobile"):
		return Vector4.ZERO
	var win := Vector2(DisplayServer.window_get_size())
	if win.x <= 0.0 or win.y <= 0.0:
		return Vector4.ZERO
	var safe := Rect2(DisplayServer.get_display_safe_area())
	var k := c.get_viewport_rect().size / win
	return Vector4(safe.position.x * k.x, safe.position.y * k.y, (win.x - safe.end.x) * k.x, (win.y - safe.end.y) * k.y)


## A small HUD chip: spaced capitals over a faint field with an underline.
static func chip(button: Button) -> void:
	var states := {"normal": [INK_SOFT, Color(ACCENT, 0.45)], "hover": [INK, ACCENT], "pressed": [Color(ACCENT_DEEP, 0.7), ACCENT],
		"focus": [INK_SOFT, Color(ACCENT, 0.45)]}
	for k: String in states:
		var s := box(states[k][0], states[k][1], 0, 4.0)
		s.border_width_bottom = 1
		s.content_margin_left = 6.0
		s.content_margin_right = 6.0
		button.add_theme_stylebox_override(k, s)
	button.add_theme_font_override("font", caps_font())
	button.focus_mode = Control.FOCUS_NONE

== ui/world_clock_hud.gd

class_name WorldClockHUD
extends HBoxContainer
## Compact clock: date and weekday over the 12-hour time with a small drawn weather
## glyph, right-aligned behind a hairline. Reads WorldTime and Weather through their
## signals; holds no state of its own. Hidden during dialogue and when
## WorldTime.hud_hidden is set (cinematics).

var _date: Label
var _time: Label
var _icon: WeatherGlyph


func _ready() -> void:
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	add_theme_constant_override("separation", 5)
	var col := VBoxContainer.new()
	col.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	col.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 0)
	add_child(col)
	_date = Label.new()
	UiTheme.caps(_date, 6, UiTheme.DIM)
	_date.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	col.add_child(_date)
	var row := HBoxContainer.new()
	row.alignment = BoxContainer.ALIGNMENT_END
	row.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 4)
	row.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	col.add_child(row)
	_icon = WeatherGlyph.new()
	_icon.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER
	row.add_child(_icon)
	_time = Label.new()
	_time.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 11)
	_time.add_theme_color_override("font_color", UiTheme.BONE)
	row.add_child(_time)
	for l: Label in [_date, _time]:
		l.add_theme_color_override("font_shadow_color", UiTheme.SHADOW)
		l.add_theme_constant_override("shadow_offset_x", 1)
		l.add_theme_constant_override("shadow_offset_y", 1)
		l.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	var bar := ColorRect.new()
	bar.color = Color(UiTheme.ACCENT, 0.7)
	bar.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(1, 0)
	bar.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	add_child(bar)
	WorldTime.minute_changed.connect(func(_h: int, _m: int) -> void: refresh())
	WorldTime.day_changed.connect(func(_c: WorldCalendar) -> void: refresh())
	WorldTime.time_jumped.connect(refresh)
	Weather.weather_changed.connect(func(_t: String, _p: String) -> void: refresh())
	refresh()


func refresh() -> void:
	_date.text = WorldTime.date_text().to_upper()
	_time.text = WorldTime.time_text()
	_icon.set_state(Weather.current, WorldTime.is_between(6, 19.5))


func text() -> String:
	return "%s\n%s" % [WorldTime.date_text(), WorldTime.time_text()]


func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
	visible = not Dialogue.active and not WorldTime.hud_hidden


## Small line-drawn weather symbol.
class WeatherGlyph:
	extends Control

	var weather := "clear"
	var day := true

	func _init() -> void:
		custom_minimum_size = Vector2(11, 11)
		mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE

	func set_state(w: String, is_day: bool) -> void:
		if w != weather or is_day != day:
			weather = w
			day = is_day
			queue_redraw()

	func _draw() -> void:
		var c := UiTheme.ACCENT
		var o := size * 0.5
		match weather:
			"clear":
				if day:
					draw_arc(o, 2.4, 0, TAU, 16, c, 1.0, true)
					for i in 8:
						var d := Vector2.from_angle(TAU * i / 8.0)
						draw_line(o + d * 3.9, o + d * 5.2, c, 1.0, true)
				else:
					draw_arc(o, 3.6, PI * 0.35, PI * 1.65, 16, c, 1.0, true)
					draw_arc(o + Vector2(2.2, 0), 3.0, PI * 0.62, PI * 1.38, 12, c, 1.0, true)
			"cloudy":
				_cloud(o + Vector2(0, 0.5), c)
			"rain", "heavy_rain":
				_cloud(o + Vector2(0, -1.5), c)
				var n := 4 if weather == "heavy_rain" else 2
				for i in n:
					var x := o.x - 3.0 + 6.0 * (float(i) + 0.5) / float(n)
					draw_line(Vector2(x + 0.6, o.y + 2.0), Vector2(x - 0.6, o.y + 4.8), c, 1.0, true)
			"fog":
				for i in 3:
					var y := o.y - 3.0 + i * 3.0
					draw_line(Vector2(o.x - 4.5 + i, y), Vector2(o.x + 4.5 - (2 - i), y), c, 1.0)
			"snow":
				for i in 3:
					var d := Vector2.from_angle(PI * i / 3.0 + PI * 0.5) * 4.2
					draw_line(o - d, o + d, c, 1.0, true)

	func _cloud(at: Vector2, c: Color) -> void:
		draw_arc(at + Vector2(-2.0, 0.6), 2.0, PI * 0.5, PI * 1.5, 10, c, 1.0, true)
		draw_arc(at + Vector2(0.4, -0.6), 2.6, PI, TAU, 12, c, 1.0, true)
		draw_arc(at + Vector2(2.6, 0.9), 1.7, -PI * 0.5, PI * 0.5, 10, c, 1.0, true)
		draw_line(at + Vector2(-2.0, 2.6), at + Vector2(2.6, 2.6), c, 1.0)

== ui/title/character_creation.gd

class_name CharacterCreation
extends Control
## Shown after NEW GAME: a name and the player's look, with a turning preview of the
## in-game figure. Only appearance exists so far (no stats, classes or backgrounds are
## in the game). Emits done(profile); the profile is the set of GameState flags a new
## game starts with: player_name and player_look (colours as HTML strings, so the save
## file can hold them).
## Each option is one focusable row: press or tap to step forward, left/right to step
## either way, so it works with touch, mouse, keys and gamepad alike.

signal done(profile: Dictionary)
signal cancelled

const OPTIONS := [
	{"key": "hair", "label": "HAIR", "values": [["Bun", "bun"], ["Short", "short"], ["Long", "long"], ["Shaved", "bald"]]},
	{"key": "hair_color", "label": "HAIR COLOUR", "values": [["Black", "#1a0f0d"], ["Brown", "#4a2e1c"], ["Auburn", "#7a3320"], ["Fair", "#c9a66b"], ["Grey", "#9a9a9a"]]},
	{"key": "skin", "label": "SKIN", "values": [["Tone 1", "#f0d2b8"], ["Tone 2", "#d9a981"], ["Tone 3", "#b5835a"], ["Tone 4", "#8a5a3c"], ["Tone 5", "#5a3a28"]]},
	{"key": "coat", "label": "COAT", "values": [["Burgundy", "#80211c"], ["Navy", "#1f3555"], ["Forest", "#27452f"], ["Slate", "#454b55"], ["Ochre", "#8a6a25"]]},
	{"key": "long_coat", "label": "COAT LENGTH", "values": [["Long", true], ["Short", false]]},
]

var _pick := {}
var _rows := {}
var _name: LineEdit
var _begin: Button
var _stage: Node3D
var _figure: Figure3D


func _ready() -> void:
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	var shade := ColorRect.new()
	shade.color = Color(0.01, 0.02, 0.06, 0.62)
	shade.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	add_child(shade)
	shade.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	var safe := Control.new()
	safe.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	add_child(safe)
	safe.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	var s := UiTheme.safe_insets(self)
	safe.offset_left = s.x
	safe.offset_top = s.y
	safe.offset_right = -s.z
	safe.offset_bottom = -s.w

	# preview: the real figure in its own small 3D world
	var vp := SubViewport.new()
	vp.own_world_3d = true
	vp.transparent_bg = true
	vp.size = Vector2i(420, 620)
	vp.render_target_update_mode = SubViewport.UPDATE_ALWAYS
	add_child(vp)
	_stage = Node3D.new()
	vp.add_child(_stage)
	var cam := Camera3D.new()
	cam.position = Vector3(0, 1.0, 3.6)
	cam.fov = 30.0
	vp.add_child(cam)
	var key := DirectionalLight3D.new()
	key.rotation_degrees = Vector3(-35, -35, 0)
	key.light_color = Color(1.0, 0.86, 0.72)
	key.light_energy = 1.3
	vp.add_child(key)
	var rim := DirectionalLight3D.new()
	rim.rotation_degrees = Vector3(-10, 150, 0)
	rim.light_color = Color(0.5, 0.62, 1.0)
	rim.light_energy = 0.8
	vp.add_child(rim)
	var env := Environment.new()
	env.background_mode = Environment.BG_COLOR
	env.background_color = Color(0, 0, 0, 0)
	env.ambient_light_source = Environment.AMBIENT_SOURCE_COLOR
	env.ambient_light_color = Color(0.4, 0.44, 0.6)
	env.ambient_light_energy = 0.8
	var we := WorldEnvironment.new()
	we.environment = env
	vp.add_child(we)
	var view := TextureRect.new()
	view.texture = vp.get_texture()
	view.expand_mode = TextureRect.EXPAND_IGNORE_SIZE
	view.stretch_mode = TextureRect.STRETCH_KEEP_ASPECT_CENTERED
	view.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	safe.add_child(view)
	view.anchor_left = 0.1
	view.anchor_right = 0.42
	view.anchor_top = 0.08
	view.anchor_bottom = 0.94

	var panel := PanelContainer.new()
	panel.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(200, 0)
	safe.add_child(panel)
	panel.anchor_left = 0.7
	panel.anchor_right = 0.7
	panel.anchor_top = 0.5
	panel.anchor_bottom = 0.5
	panel.grow_horizontal = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BOTH
	panel.grow_vertical = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BOTH
	var box := VBoxContainer.new()
	box.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 3)
	panel.add_child(box)
	var h := Label.new()
	h.text = "NEW GAME  ·  CHARACTER"
	UiTheme.caps(h, 7, UiTheme.ACCENT)
	box.add_child(h)
	var rule := ColorRect.new()
	rule.color = Color(UiTheme.ACCENT, 0.3)
	rule.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 1)
	box.add_child(rule)
	_name = LineEdit.new()
	_name.placeholder_text = "Name"
	_name.max_length = 16
	_name.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 9)
	_name.text_submitted.connect(func(_t: String) -> void: _begin.grab_focus())
	box.add_child(_name)
	for o: Dictionary in OPTIONS:
		_pick[o["key"]] = 0
		var b := Button.new()
		b.alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT
		b.add_theme_font_override("font", UiTheme.caps_font())
		b.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 7)
		b.pressed.connect(_step.bind(o, 1))
		b.gui_input.connect(func(e: InputEvent) -> void:
			if e.is_action_pressed(&"ui_right"):
				_step(o, 1)
				b.accept_event()
			elif e.is_action_pressed(&"ui_left"):
				_step(o, -1)
				b.accept_event())
		b.mouse_entered.connect(b.grab_focus)
		box.add_child(b)
		_rows[o["key"]] = b
		_label(o)
	_begin = Button.new()
	_begin.text = "BEGIN"
	_begin.add_theme_font_override("font", UiTheme.caps_font())
	_begin.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 8)
	_begin.add_theme_color_override("font_color", UiTheme.ACCENT)
	_begin.pressed.connect(func() -> void:
		Audio.ui("accept")
		done.emit(profile()))
	box.add_child(_begin)
	var back := Button.new()
	back.text = "BACK"
	back.add_theme_font_override("font", UiTheme.caps_font())
	back.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 8)
	back.pressed.connect(_cancel)
	box.add_child(back)
	for b: Button in [_begin, back]:
		b.alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT
		b.mouse_entered.connect(b.grab_focus)
	_rebuild()
	(_rows[OPTIONS[0]["key"]] as Button).grab_focus.call_deferred()


## The flags a new game starts with.
func profile() -> Dictionary:
	var look := {}
	for o: Dictionary in OPTIONS:
		look[o["key"]] = (o["values"] as Array)[_pick[o["key"]]][1]
	return {"player_name": _name.text.strip_edges(), "player_look": look}


func set_player_name(text: String) -> void:
	_name.text = text


## Step an option by key (tests and the rows themselves).
func step(key: String, by: int = 1) -> void:
	for o: Dictionary in OPTIONS:
		if o["key"] == key:
			_step(o, by)


func _step(o: Dictionary, by: int) -> void:
	_pick[o["key"]] = posmod(int(_pick[o["key"]]) + by, (o["values"] as Array).size())
	_label(o)
	_rebuild()
	Audio.ui("adjust")


func _label(o: Dictionary) -> void:
	(_rows[o["key"]] as Button).text = "%s    ‹  %s  ›" % [o["label"], (o["values"] as Array)[_pick[o["key"]]][0]]


func _rebuild() -> void:
	var yaw := _figure.rotation.y if _figure != null else PI + 0.5
	if _figure != null:
		_figure.queue_free()
	_figure = Figure3D.new()
	_figure.harmonize_clothes = false
	_figure.accent = Color(0.62, 0.42, 0.2)
	_figure.configure({"hair": "bun", "long_coat": true})
	Player3D.apply_look(_figure, profile()["player_look"])
	_figure.rotation.y = yaw
	_stage.add_child(_figure)


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if _figure != null:
		_figure.rotation.y += delta * 0.5


func _cancel() -> void:
	Audio.ui("back")
	cancelled.emit()


func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if is_visible_in_tree() and event.is_action_pressed(&"ui_cancel"):
		_cancel()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()


==============================================================================
== ui/title/title_backdrop.gd
==============================================================================
class_name TitleBackdrop
extends Control
## The planet behind the title: a shader-drawn limb, ground and cloud layers that scroll
## at different speeds, and (added by TitleScreen in front of the title lettering) the
## ship and a layer of near cloud. Nothing here is simulated; it is three flat layers.

const PLANET := preload("res://art/shaders/title_planet.gdshader")
const WISPS := preload("res://art/shaders/title_wisps.gdshader")

var planet: ColorRect
var _mats: Array[ShaderMaterial] = []
static var _noise: NoiseTexture2D


## Seamless cloud noise shared by the layers.
static func noise() -> NoiseTexture2D:
	if _noise == null:
		var n := FastNoiseLite.new()
		n.noise_type = FastNoiseLite.TYPE_SIMPLEX_SMOOTH
		n.frequency = 0.012
		n.fractal_octaves = 5
		n.fractal_gain = 0.52
		_noise = NoiseTexture2D.new()
		_noise.width = 256 if OS.has_feature("web") else 512
		_noise.height = _noise.width
		_noise.seamless = true
		_noise.generate_mipmaps = true
		_noise.noise = n
	return _noise


func _ready() -> void:
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	planet = _layer(PLANET)
	(planet.material as ShaderMaterial).set_shader_parameter("lite", 1.0 if is_lite() else 0.0)
	resized.connect(_fit)
	_fit()


## Near cloud, to be placed in front of the ship.
func make_wisps() -> ColorRect:
	var w := _layer(WISPS, false)
	_fit()
	return w


## Web and other constrained targets get the lighter planet shader.
static func is_lite() -> bool:
	return OS.has_feature("web")


func _layer(shader: Shader, add := true) -> ColorRect:
	var r := ColorRect.new()
	r.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	var m := ShaderMaterial.new()
	m.shader = shader
	m.set_shader_parameter("noise_tex", noise())
	r.material = m
	_mats.append(m)
	if add:
		add_child(r)
		r.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	return r


func _fit() -> void:
	var a := size.x / maxf(size.y, 1.0)
	for m in _mats:
		m.set_shader_parameter("aspect", a)


==============================================================================
== ui/title/title_screen.gd
==============================================================================
class_name TitleScreen
extends Control
## The opening screen: the planet (TitleBackdrop), the title lettering with the ship
## drifting across it, and the main menu with its Load and Options panels.
## It owns no game state. game/app.gd listens to its signals and tells it whether a game
## is in progress (`session_live`) so it can offer CONTINUE.
## Works with mouse, touch and the built-in ui_* actions (keyboard and gamepad).

signal continue_requested
signal new_game_requested
signal load_requested(slot: String)
signal quit_requested
## PROTOTYPE: the 2D test area. Remove with the entry in show_menu().
signal prototype_requested

const TITLE := "COMETOGRAPHIA"
const SLOT_NAMES := {"auto": "Autosave", "manual": "Manual save"}

## The shelved 2D prototype (entry 15) stays in the code but is off the menu.
var show_prototype := false
## A game is loaded behind the title (returned from the pause menu).
var session_live := false
## False in tests: no fades or delays.
var animate := true

var backdrop: TitleBackdrop
var ship: TitleShip
var _wisps: ColorRect
var _title: Label
var _safe: Control
var _menu: VBoxContainer
var _panel: PanelContainer
var _panel_box: VBoxContainer
var _state := "menu"
var _quiet := true
var _confirm_new := false
var _new: Button


func _ready() -> void:
	mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_STOP
	backdrop = TitleBackdrop.new()
	add_child(backdrop)
	backdrop.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)

	# the lettering sits in the scene: the ship and near cloud pass in front of it
	_title = Label.new()
	_title.text = TITLE
	var thin := SystemFont.new()
	thin.font_names = PackedStringArray(["Helvetica Neue", "Avenir Next", "Roboto", "sans-serif"])
	thin.font_weight = 100
	var spaced := FontVariation.new()
	spaced.base_font = thin
	spaced.spacing_glyph = 9
	_title.add_theme_font_override("font", spaced)
	_title.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 30)
	_title.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(0.98, 0.94, 0.9, 0.94))
	_title.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_CENTER
	_title.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	add_child(_title)
	_title.anchor_left = 0.0
	_title.anchor_right = 1.0
	_title.anchor_top = 0.4
	_title.anchor_bottom = 0.4
	_title.offset_left = 9
	_title.grow_vertical = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BOTH

	ship = TitleShip.new()
	add_child(ship)
	_wisps = backdrop.make_wisps()
	add_child(_wisps)
	_wisps.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)

	_safe = Control.new()
	_safe.mouse_filter = Control.MOUSE_FILTER_IGNORE
	add_child(_safe)
	_safe.set_anchors_and_offsets_preset(Control.PRESET_FULL_RECT)
	_menu = VBoxContainer.new()
	_menu.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 1)
	_safe.add_child(_menu)
	_menu.anchor_left = 0.5
	_menu.anchor_right = 0.5
	_menu.anchor_top = 0.62
	_menu.anchor_bottom = 0.62
	_menu.grow_horizontal = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BOTH
	_panel = PanelContainer.new()
	_panel.visible = false
	_panel.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(190, 0)
	_safe.add_child(_panel)
	_panel.anchor_left = 0.5
	_panel.anchor_right = 0.5
	_panel.anchor_top = 0.74
	_panel.anchor_bottom = 0.74
	_panel.grow_horizontal = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BOTH
	_panel.grow_vertical = Control.GROW_DIRECTION_BOTH
	_panel_box = VBoxContainer.new()
	_panel_box.add_theme_constant_override("separation", 3)
	_panel.add_child(_panel_box)

	resized.connect(_layout)
	get_viewport().size_changed.connect(_layout)
	_layout()
	show_menu()
	if animate:
		_title.modulate.a = 0.0
		_menu.modulate.a = 0.0
		var tw := create_tween()
		tw.tween_interval(0.8)
		tw.tween_property(_title, "modulate:a", 1.0, 2.2).set_trans(Tween.TRANS_SINE)
		tw.parallel().tween_property(_menu, "modulate:a", 1.0, 1.6).set_delay(1.2)


func _layout() -> void:
	ship.set_home(Vector2(size.x * 0.3, size.y * 0.31))
	ship.scale = Vector2.ONE * 1.5
	var s := UiTheme.safe_insets(self)
	_safe.offset_left = s.x
	_safe.offset_top = s.y
	_safe.offset_right = -s.z
	_safe.offset_bottom = -s.w


# --- main menu ----------------------------------------------------------------------

## Labels of the entries currently offered, top to bottom.
func items() -> PackedStringArray:
	var out := PackedStringArray()
	for c in _menu.get_children():
		if not c.is_queued_for_deletion():
			out.append((c as Button).text)
	return out


func can_continue() -> bool:
	return session_live or not SaveManager.slot_info("auto").is_empty()


func show_menu() -> void:
	_state = "menu"
	_confirm_new = false
	_panel.visible = false
	_menu.visible = true
	_clear(_menu)
	if can_continue():
		_item(_menu, "CONTINUE", func() -> void: continue_requested.emit())
	_new = _item(_menu, "NEW GAME", _on_new)
	_item(_menu, "LOAD GAME", show_load)
	_item(_menu, "OPTIONS", show_options)
	if show_prototype:
		_item(_menu, "2D PROTOTYPE", func() -> void: prototype_requested.emit())
	if OS.has_feature("pc"):
		_item(_menu, "QUIT", func() -> void: quit_requested.emit())
	_focus_first(_menu)


## Hide the lettering and menu while another screen (character creation) is in front.
func set_covered(covered: bool) -> void:
	_title.visible = not covered
	_safe.visible = not covered


## Press the entry with this label (tests, and nothing else).
func choose(label: String) -> bool:
	for box: Control in [_menu, _panel_box]:
		if not box.is_visible_in_tree():
			continue
		for c in box.get_children():
			var b := c as Button
			if b != null and not b.is_queued_for_deletion() and b.text.begins_with(label) and not b.disabled:
				b.pressed.emit()
				return true
	return false


func state() -> String:
	return _state


func _on_new() -> void:
	# starting over discards the game in progress: ask once
	if session_live and not _confirm_new:
		_confirm_new = true
		_new.text = "NEW GAME  ·  CONFIRM"
		return
	new_game_requested.emit()


# --- load ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

func show_load() -> void:
	_open_panel("load", "LOAD GAME")
	var saves := SaveManager.list_saves()
	if saves.is_empty():
		_note("No saved games.")
	for s in saves:
		var slot := str(s["slot"])
		var text := "%s  ·  %s" % [str(SLOT_NAMES.get(slot, slot.capitalize())).to_upper(), _place(str(s["map"]))]
		if str(s["when"]) != "":
			text += "  ·  " + str(s["when"])
		var b := _item(_panel_box, text, func() -> void: load_requested.emit(slot))
		b.alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT
		b.tooltip_text = "Saved " + str(s["saved"]).replace("T", " ")
	_item(_panel_box, "BACK", _back)
	_focus_first(_panel_box)


## Slots listed in the load panel.
func listed_saves() -> PackedStringArray:
	var out := PackedStringArray()
	for s in SaveManager.list_saves():
		out.append(str(s["slot"]))
	return out


static func _place(map_id: String) -> String:
	return map_id.replace("_", " ").capitalize()


# --- options ------------------------------------------------------------------------

func show_options() -> void:
	_open_panel("options", "OPTIONS")
	for row: Array in [["master", "MASTER VOLUME"], ["music", "MUSIC"], ["sfx", "EFFECTS"]]:
		_slider(row[0], row[1])
	if Settings.has_display_options():
		_toggle("FULLSCREEN", func() -> bool: return Settings.fullscreen, Settings.set_fullscreen)
		_toggle("VSYNC", func() -> bool: return Settings.vsync, Settings.set_vsync)
	_item(_panel_box, "BACK", _back)
	_focus_first(_panel_box)


func _slider(key: String, text: String) -> void:
	var row := HBoxContainer.new()
	_panel_box.add_child(row)
	var l := Label.new()
	l.text = text
	UiTheme.caps(l, 6, UiTheme.DIM)
	l.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(74, 0)
	row.add_child(l)
	var s := HSlider.new()
	s.name = key
	s.min_value = 0.0
	s.max_value = 1.0
	s.step = 0.05
	s.value = Settings.volume[key]
	s.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(90, 12)
	s.size_flags_horizontal = Control.SIZE_EXPAND_FILL
	s.size_flags_vertical = Control.SIZE_SHRINK_CENTER
	row.add_child(s)
	var pct := Label.new()
	UiTheme.caps(pct, 6, UiTheme.BONE)
	pct.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(18, 0)
	pct.horizontal_alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_RIGHT
	pct.text = str(roundi(s.value * 100.0))
	row.add_child(pct)
	s.value_changed.connect(func(v: float) -> void:
		Settings.set_volume(key, v)
		pct.text = str(roundi(v * 100.0))
		_sound("adjust"))
	s.focus_entered.connect(func() -> void:
		l.add_theme_color_override("font_color", UiTheme.ACCENT)
		_sound("move"))
	s.focus_exited.connect(func() -> void: l.add_theme_color_override("font_color", UiTheme.DIM))


func _toggle(text: String, getter: Callable, setter: Callable) -> void:
	var label := func() -> String: return "%s  ·  %s" % [text, "ON" if getter.call() else "OFF"]
	var b := _item(_panel_box, label.call(), func() -> void: pass)
	b.alignment = HORIZONTAL_ALIGNMENT_LEFT
	b.pressed.connect(func() -> void:
		setter.call(not getter.call())
		b.text = label.call())


## The volume slider for "master", "music" or "sfx" while Options is open.
func slider(key: String) -> HSlider:
	return _panel_box.find_child(key, true, false) as HSlider


# --- shared -------------------------------------------------------------------------

func _open_panel(state_name: String, heading: String) -> void:
	_state = state_name
	_menu.visible = false
	_panel.visible = true
	_clear(_panel_box)
	var h := Label.new()
	h.text = heading
	UiTheme.caps(h, 7, UiTheme.ACCENT)
	_panel_box.add_child(h)
	var rule := ColorRect.new()
	rule.color = Color(UiTheme.ACCENT, 0.3)
	rule.custom_minimum_size = Vector2(0, 1)
	_panel_box.add_child(rule)


func _note(text: String) -> void:
	var l := Label.new()
	l.text = text
	l.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 8)
	l.add_theme_color_override("font_color", UiTheme.DIM)
	_panel_box.add_child(l)


func _back() -> void:
	if _state == "options":
		Settings.save_settings()
	_sound("back")
	show_menu()


func _item(parent: Control, text: String, on_press: Callable) -> Button:
	var b := Button.new()
	b.text = text
	b.add_theme_font_override("font", UiTheme.caps_font())
	b.add_theme_font_size_override("font_size", 8)
	b.add_theme_color_override("font_color", Color(UiTheme.BONE, 0.72))
	var plain := UiTheme.box(Color(0, 0, 0, 0), Color(0, 0, 0, 0), 0, 3.0)
	plain.content_margin_left = 14.0
	plain.content_margin_right = 14.0
	var lit := UiTheme.box(Color(UiTheme.ACCENT, 0.06), Color(UiTheme.ACCENT, 0.9), 0, 3.0)
	lit.border_width_bottom = 1
	lit.content_margin_left = 14.0
	lit.content_margin_right = 14.0
	for k: String in ["normal", "hover", "pressed", "disabled"]:
		b.add_theme_stylebox_override(k, plain)
	b.add_theme_stylebox_override("focus", lit)
	b.pressed.connect(func() -> void:
		if on_press != _back:
			_sound("accept")
		on_press.call())
	# one highlight only: the pointer moves the focus
	b.mouse_entered.connect(b.grab_focus)
	b.focus_entered.connect(func() -> void:
		_sound("move")
		if animate:
			b.modulate.a = 0.6
			create_tween().tween_property(b, "modulate:a", 1.0, 0.25))
	parent.add_child(b)
	return b


func _focus_first(box: Control) -> void:
	_quiet = true
	for c in box.find_children("*", "Control", true, false):
		var ctl := c as Control
		if ctl.focus_mode != Control.FOCUS_NONE and not ctl.is_queued_for_deletion():
			(func() -> void:
				if is_instance_valid(ctl) and ctl.is_inside_tree():
					ctl.grab_focus()).call_deferred()
			break
	(func() -> void: _quiet = false).call_deferred()


func _sound(id: String) -> void:
	if not _quiet and is_visible_in_tree():
		Audio.ui(id)


static func _clear(box: Control) -> void:
	for c in box.get_children():
		box.remove_child(c)
		c.queue_free()


func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if is_visible_in_tree() and _state != "menu" and event.is_action_pressed(&"ui_cancel"):
		_back()
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()


==============================================================================
== ui/title/title_ship.gd
==============================================================================
class_name TitleShip
extends Node2D
## PROVISIONAL ART. The lone ship on the title screen, drawn as a dark silhouette with a
## lit core. It drifts and turns by fractions of a degree. Replace with painted art by
## swapping _draw() for a sprite; keep the drift.

var _t := 0.0
var _home := Vector2.ZERO
var _glow: Sprite2D


func _ready() -> void:
	_home = position
	# warm bloom around the core, additive
	var g := Gradient.new()
	g.offsets = PackedFloat32Array([0.0, 0.35, 1.0])
	g.colors = PackedColorArray([Color(1.0, 0.55, 0.25, 0.9), Color(1.0, 0.35, 0.12, 0.28), Color(1.0, 0.3, 0.1, 0.0)])
	var tex := GradientTexture2D.new()
	tex.gradient = g
	tex.width = 64
	tex.height = 64
	tex.fill = GradientTexture2D.FILL_RADIAL
	tex.fill_from = Vector2(0.5, 0.5)
	tex.fill_to = Vector2(1.0, 0.5)
	_glow = Sprite2D.new()
	_glow.texture = tex
	var m := CanvasItemMaterial.new()
	m.blend_mode = CanvasItemMaterial.BLEND_MODE_ADD
	_glow.material = m
	_glow.position = Vector2(-2, 2)
	add_child(_glow)


func set_home(p: Vector2) -> void:
	_home = p


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	_t += delta
	position = _home + Vector2(sin(_t * 0.11) * 5.0 + sin(_t * 0.043) * 3.0, sin(_t * 0.083 + 1.0) * 2.5)
	rotation = sin(_t * 0.07) * 0.03 + sin(_t * 0.031 + 2.0) * 0.015
	var pulse := 0.85 + 0.15 * sin(_t * 0.9) + 0.05 * sin(_t * 2.3)
	_glow.modulate.a = pulse
	_glow.scale = Vector2.ONE * (0.55 + 0.06 * pulse)


func _draw() -> void:
	var hull := Color(0.035, 0.03, 0.055)
	var rim := Color(0.95, 0.62, 0.38, 0.75)
	# long axis: nose to the right, tail left
	_poly([Vector2(-34, 1), Vector2(-20, -3), Vector2(8, -4), Vector2(40, 0), Vector2(8, 5), Vector2(-18, 6)], hull)
	# spire and fins rising from the core
	_poly([Vector2(-4, -4), Vector2(0, -44), Vector2(3, -4)], hull)
	_poly([Vector2(-11, -3), Vector2(-9, -24), Vector2(-6, -3)], hull)
	_poly([Vector2(5, -4), Vector2(9, -27), Vector2(11, -3)], hull)
	_poly([Vector2(-17, -2), Vector2(-16, -14), Vector2(-13, -2)], hull)
	# keel blades
	_poly([Vector2(-6, 5), Vector2(-5, 40), Vector2(-2, 5)], hull)
	_poly([Vector2(-13, 5), Vector2(-13, 22), Vector2(-10, 5)], hull)
	_poly([Vector2(3, 5), Vector2(5, 20), Vector2(7, 5)], hull)
	# swept wing reaching forward and up
	_poly([Vector2(6, -3), Vector2(38, -19), Vector2(41, -17), Vector2(16, 0)], hull)
	_poly([Vector2(-22, 2), Vector2(-40, 7), Vector2(-39, 9), Vector2(-20, 5)], hull)
	# sunlit edges
	draw_polyline(PackedVector2Array([Vector2(-20, -3), Vector2(8, -4), Vector2(40, 0)]), rim, 0.6, true)
	draw_line(Vector2(6, -3), Vector2(38, -19), rim, 0.6, true)
	draw_line(Vector2(0, -44), Vector2(3, -4), Color(rim, 0.5), 0.5, true)
	draw_line(Vector2(9, -27), Vector2(11, -3), Color(rim, 0.4), 0.5, true)
	# core
	draw_circle(Vector2(-2, 2), 7.2, Color(0.1, 0.03, 0.03))
	draw_circle(Vector2(-2, 2), 6.2, Color(0.75, 0.2, 0.08))
	draw_circle(Vector2(-3, 1), 4.2, Color(1.0, 0.45, 0.18))
	draw_circle(Vector2(-4, 0), 1.8, Color(1.0, 0.8, 0.55))
	draw_arc(Vector2(-2, 2), 7.2, 0, TAU, 24, Color(0.02, 0.02, 0.03), 1.0, true)
	draw_line(Vector2(-9, 2), Vector2(5, 2), Color(0.05, 0.02, 0.03, 0.7), 0.6)


func _poly(pts: Array, c: Color) -> void:
	draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array(pts), c)


==============================================================================
== world2d/grid_actor.gd
==============================================================================
class_name GridActor
extends Node2D
## Someone who moves tile by tile on a TileWorld, FireRed style: a step glides from
## one tile to the next over `walk_time` (or `run_time`), the sheet's walk or run frames
## alternate feet on successive steps, and turning in place is instant.
## Sheets come from PixelArt.character(look): columns idle, walk 1-4, run 1-4; rows
## down, up, left, right; 16x32 frames standing on the tile's bottom centre.

signal stepped(tile: Vector2i)

const DIR := {"down": Vector2i(0, 1), "up": Vector2i(0, -1), "left": Vector2i(-1, 0), "right": Vector2i(1, 0)}

var world: TileWorld
var look: Dictionary = {}
var sheet_id := ""
var tile := Vector2i.ZERO
## Facing as a unit vector (Vector2 so saves and the HUD treat it like the 2D player).
var facing := Vector2(0, 1)
var walk_time := 0.24
var run_time := 0.13

var sprite: Sprite2D
var _moving := false
var _from := Vector2.ZERO
var _to := Vector2.ZERO
var _t := 0.0
var _dur := 0.24
var _running := false
var _foot := 0
var _bump := 0.0


func _ready() -> void:
	sprite = Sprite2D.new()
	sprite.texture = PixelArt.character(look, sheet_id)
	sprite.hframes = PixelArt.COLS
	sprite.vframes = 4
	sprite.centered = false
	sprite.offset = Vector2(-8, -31)
	add_child(sprite)
	if world != null:
		tile = world.tile_of(position)
		position = world.foot(tile)
		world.claim(tile, self)
	_pose()


func place(t: Vector2i, face: String = "down") -> void:
	if world != null and is_inside_tree():
		world.release(tile, self)
	tile = t
	facing = Vector2(DIR.get(face, Vector2i(0, 1)))
	_moving = false
	if world != null:
		position = world.foot(t)
		if is_inside_tree():
			world.claim(t, self)
	if sprite != null:
		_pose()


func is_moving() -> bool:
	return _moving


func face(d: Vector2i) -> void:
	facing = Vector2(d)
	_pose()


func dir_name() -> String:
	var d := Vector2i(roundi(facing.x), roundi(facing.y))
	for k: String in DIR:
		if DIR[k] == d:
			return k
	return "down"


## Begin a step in direction d. Returns false (and turns to face it) if blocked.
func try_step(d: Vector2i, run := false) -> bool:
	facing = Vector2(d)
	var next := tile + d
	if world == null or not world.can_enter(next, self):
		_pose()
		return false
	world.claim(next, self)
	_from = position
	_to = world.foot(next)
	_t = 0.0
	_dur = run_time if run else walk_time
	_running = run
	_moving = true
	_foot = (_foot + 1) % 2
	return true


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	if _bump > 0.0:
		_bump -= delta
	if not _moving:
		return
	_t += delta / _dur
	if _t >= 1.0:
		world.release(tile, self)
		tile = world.tile_of(_to)
		position = _to
		_moving = false
		_pose()
		stepped.emit(tile)
		_on_arrived()
		return
	position = _from.lerp(_to, _t)
	_pose()


## Override: called when a step completes (doors, chaining).
func _on_arrived() -> void:
	pass


func _pose() -> void:
	if sprite == null:
		return
	var row: int = ["down", "up", "left", "right"].find(dir_name())
	var col := 0
	if _moving:
		# two frames per step, alternating feet: contact then passing
		var half := 0 if _t < 0.5 else 1
		col = (5 if _running else 1) + _foot * 2 + half
	elif _bump > 0.0:
		col = 1 + _foot * 2
	sprite.frame = row * PixelArt.COLS + col


==============================================================================
== world2d/grid_npc.gd
==============================================================================
class_name GridNpc
extends GridActor
## A person on a TileWorld. Talkable NPCs come from data/npcs2d.json (name, dialogue,
## look) and turn to face whoever speaks to them. Walkers wander a rectangle a step at
## a time. Schedules (WorldTime) and factions attach here later; nothing in this class
## draws except through GridActor and PixelArt.

var npc_id := ""
var display_name := ""
var dialogue_id := ""
var interactable: Interactable
## Tiles a walker may wander (empty = stands still).
var wander := Rect2i()
var _wait := 0.0


func _ready() -> void:
	walk_time = 0.32
	super._ready()
	if dialogue_id != "":
		interactable = Interactable.new()
		interactable.prompt = "Talk"
		interactable.label = display_name
		interactable.dialogue_id = dialogue_id
		add_child(interactable)
		interactable.interacted.connect(_on_talk)
	_wait = randf_range(0.5, 3.0)


func _on_talk(by: Node2D) -> void:
	var d := (by.position - position)
	if absf(d.x) > absf(d.y):
		face(Vector2i(signi(int(signf(d.x))), 0))
	else:
		face(Vector2i(0, signi(int(signf(d.y)))))


func _process(delta: float) -> void:
	super._process(delta)
	if wander.size == Vector2i.ZERO or _moving or Dialogue.active:
		return
	_wait -= delta
	if _wait > 0.0:
		return
	_wait = randf_range(1.2, 4.0)
	var dirs: Array[Vector2i] = [Vector2i(1, 0), Vector2i(-1, 0), Vector2i(0, 1), Vector2i(0, -1)]
	var d: Vector2i = dirs[randi() % 4]
	if wander.has_point(tile + d):
		try_step(d)
	else:
		face(d)


==============================================================================
== world2d/grid_player.gd
==============================================================================
class_name GridPlayer
extends GridActor
## The player on a TileWorld. Same outward interface as the other players (target,
## facing, try_interact, setup, camera, sprinting, touch) so main, the HUD and saves
## drive it alike. FireRed controls: tap a direction to turn, hold to walk, hold run
## (Shift / pad B / RUN) to run; walking into a wall plays a bump; steps chain with no
## pause while a direction is held. Doors trigger on arrival.

signal target_changed(target: Interactable)

## A tap shorter than this only turns the player.
const TURN_TIME := 0.09

var touch: TouchControls
var target: Interactable
var sprinting := false
var camera: Camera2D
var _held := Vector2i.ZERO
var _held_t := 0.0
var _arrived_warp := false


func setup(touch_controls: TouchControls, m: TileWorld) -> void:
	touch = touch_controls
	world = m
	camera = Camera2D.new()
	camera.limit_left = 0
	camera.limit_top = 0
	camera.limit_right = int(m.bounds.size.x)
	camera.limit_bottom = int(m.bounds.size.y)
	camera.offset = Vector2(0, -8)
	add_child(camera)
	camera.make_current()
	tile = m.tile_of(position)
	position = m.foot(tile)
	m.claim(tile, self)


func _input_dir() -> Vector2i:
	if Dialogue.active:
		return Vector2i.ZERO
	var v := Input.get_vector(&"move_left", &"move_right", &"move_up", &"move_down")
	if v.length() < 0.3 and touch != null:
		v = touch.vector
	if v.length() < 0.3:
		return Vector2i.ZERO
	if absf(v.x) > absf(v.y):
		return Vector2i(signi(int(signf(v.x))), 0)
	return Vector2i(0, signi(int(signf(v.y))))


func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
	if world == null:
		return
	# a position set from outside (load, spawn) snaps to its tile
	if not _moving and position != world.foot(tile):
		world.release(tile, self)
		tile = world.tile_of(position)
		position = world.foot(tile)
		world.claim(tile, self)
	var d := _input_dir()
	sprinting = d != Vector2i.ZERO and (Input.is_action_pressed(&"sprint") or (touch != null and touch.sprint))
	if d != _held:
		_held = d
		_held_t = 0.0
	else:
		_held_t += delta
	if not _moving and d != Vector2i.ZERO:
		var facing_i := Vector2i(roundi(facing.x), roundi(facing.y))
		if d != facing_i and _held_t < TURN_TIME:
			face(d)
		elif not try_step(d, sprinting):
			if _bump <= 0.0:
				_bump = 0.25
			_pose()
	_update_target()


func _on_arrived() -> void:
	var w := world.warp_at(tile)
	if not w.is_empty():
		GameState.travel_requested.emit(str(w["map"]), str(w["spawn"]))
		return
	# keep walking with no pause while the direction is still held
	var d := _input_dir()
	if d != Vector2i.ZERO and d == Vector2i(roundi(facing.x), roundi(facing.y)):
		try_step(d, sprinting)


func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
	if event.is_action_pressed(&"interact") and try_interact():
		get_viewport().set_input_as_handled()


func try_interact() -> bool:
	if Dialogue.active or _moving or target == null or not target.is_available():
		return false
	target.interact(self)
	return true


func face_now() -> void:
	_pose()


func _update_target() -> void:
	var best: Interactable = null
	if not _moving:
		best = world.interactable_at(tile + Vector2i(roundi(facing.x), roundi(facing.y)))
	if best != target:
		target = best
		target_changed.emit(target)


==============================================================================
== world2d/tile_world.gd
==============================================================================
class_name TileWorld
extends GameMap
## A 2D tile map built from a data file (data/maps2d/<name>.json). The file names
## tiles and objects; PixelArt turns the names into pictures; this class builds the
## layers and answers the gameplay questions (can I step here, what is in that tile,
## where does this door go). Nothing here knows how anything is drawn.
##
## Map file:
##   legend   char -> tile name (shared by all layers; " " = empty)
##   ground, build, detail, over   rows of chars, one char per 16 px tile
##     ground: floor; build: walls, roofs, rails (solid per data/maps2d/tiles.json);
##     detail: windows, doors, vines, awnings (drawn on build, never solid);
##     over: drawn above people (eaves, arches you walk under)
##   objects  props, decor, npcs, walkers, warps, signs, lights (see _object())
##   spawns   name -> [x, y, facing]
##   indoor   true for interiors (fixed lamplight, no weather)
##
## Coordinates: tile (x, y); a person or prop stands on its tile's bottom centre, so
## y-sorting by node position puts whoever is further south in front.

const T := 16
const LAYERS := ["ground", "build", "detail", "over"]

@export_file("*.json") var map_file := ""

var data: Dictionary = {}
var size := Vector2i.ZERO
var indoor := false
var lighting: EnvironmentController2D

var _solid := {}
var _blocked := {}
var _actors := {}
var _signs := {}
var _warps := {}
var _layers := {}
static var _catalogue := {}


func _ready() -> void:
	data = Data.load_dict(map_file)
	if _catalogue.is_empty():
		_catalogue = Data.load_dict("res://data/maps2d/tiles.json")
	indoor = bool(data.get("indoor", false))
	if display_name == "":
		display_name = str(data.get("name", ""))
	var rows: Array = data["ground"]
	size = Vector2i(str(rows[0]).length(), rows.size())
	bounds = Rect2(Vector2.ZERO, Vector2(size * T))
	var bg := ColorRect.new()
	bg.color = PixelArt.VOID
	bg.position = Vector2(-T * 20, -T * 20)
	bg.size = Vector2(size * T) + Vector2(T * 40, T * 40)
	bg.z_index = -30
	add_child(bg)
	_build_layers()
	entities.y_sort_enabled = true
	entities.z_index = 0
	for o: Dictionary in data.get("objects", []):
		_object(o)
	var spawns := Node2D.new()
	spawns.name = "Spawns"
	add_child(spawns)
	for k: String in data.get("spawns", {}):
		var s: Array = data["spawns"][k]
		var m := Marker2D.new()
		m.name = k
		m.position = foot(Vector2i(int(s[0]), int(s[1])))
		m.set_meta(&"facing", str(s[2]) if s.size() > 2 else "down")
		spawns.add_child(m)
	lighting = EnvironmentController2D.new()
	lighting.name = "Lighting"
	lighting.indoor = indoor
	add_child(lighting)
	for o: Dictionary in data.get("objects", []):
		if o.has("light"):
			var l: Dictionary = o["light"]
			var at := _tile(o["at"])
			lighting.add_lamp(foot(at) + Vector2(0, -float(l.get("height", 30))), float(l.get("energy", 1.0)), float(l.get("range", 5.0)) * T)


# --- building ---------------------------------------------------------------------

func _build_layers() -> void:
	var legend: Dictionary = data["legend"]
	# one atlas holding every tile the map uses
	var names: Array[String] = []
	for layer: String in LAYERS:
		for row: String in data.get(layer, []):
			for ch in row:
				if ch != " " and legend.has(ch) and not str(legend[ch]) in names:
					names.append(str(legend[ch]))
	var atlas_img := Image.create(T * names.size(), T * 2, false, Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
	atlas_img.fill(Color(0, 0, 0, 0))
	var has_glow := false
	for i in names.size():
		atlas_img.blit_rect(PixelArt.tile(names[i]), Rect2i(0, 0, T, T), Vector2i(i * T, 0))
		var g := PixelArt.tile_glow(names[i])
		if g != null:
			atlas_img.blit_rect(g, Rect2i(0, 0, T, T), Vector2i(i * T, T))
			has_glow = true
	var ts := TileSet.new()
	ts.tile_size = Vector2i(T, T)
	var src := TileSetAtlasSource.new()
	src.texture = ImageTexture.create_from_image(atlas_img)
	src.texture_region_size = Vector2i(T, T)
	for i in names.size():
		src.create_tile(Vector2i(i, 0))
		src.create_tile(Vector2i(i, 1))
	ts.add_source(src, 0)
	var z := {"ground": -12, "build": -11, "detail": -10, "over": 20}
	for layer: String in LAYERS:
		var tl := TileMapLayer.new()
		tl.name = layer.capitalize()
		tl.tile_set = ts
		tl.z_index = z[layer]
		add_child(tl)
		_layers[layer] = tl
		var rows: Array = data.get(layer, [])
		for y in rows.size():
			var row: String = rows[y]
			for x in row.length():
				var ch := row[x]
				if ch == " " or not legend.has(ch):
					continue
				var name := str(legend[ch])
				tl.set_cell(Vector2i(x, y), 0, Vector2i(names.find(name), 0))
				if layer != "over" and layer != "detail" and bool((_catalogue.get(name, {}) as Dictionary).get("solid", false)):
					_solid[Vector2i(x, y)] = true
	if has_glow:
		# lit windows again, additively, shining against the night tint
		var glow := TileMapLayer.new()
		glow.name = "Glow"
		glow.tile_set = ts
		glow.z_index = -9
		var m := CanvasItemMaterial.new()
		m.blend_mode = CanvasItemMaterial.BLEND_MODE_ADD
		m.light_mode = CanvasItemMaterial.LIGHT_MODE_UNSHADED
		glow.material = m
		glow.add_to_group(&"night_glow")
		add_child(glow)
		for layer: String in ["build", "detail"]:
			var rows: Array = data.get(layer, [])
			for y in rows.size():
				var row: String = rows[y]
				for x in row.length():
					var ch := row[x]
					if legend.has(ch) and PixelArt.tile_glow(str(legend[ch])) != null:
						glow.set_cell(Vector2i(x, y), 0, Vector2i(names.find(str(legend[ch])), 1))


## Props stand in Entities (y-sorted); decor lies flat under or over everyone.
func _object(o: Dictionary) -> void:
	var at := _tile(o["at"])
	match str(o.get("type", "prop")):
		"prop":
			var foot_size := _tile(o.get("size", [1, 1]))
			var node := Node2D.new()
			node.name = "Prop_" + str(o["sprite"])
			node.position = Vector2(at.x * T + foot_size.x * T * 0.5, (at.y + foot_size.y) * T)
			var tex := PixelArt.prop(str(o["sprite"]))
			var sp := Sprite2D.new()
			sp.texture = tex
			sp.centered = false
			sp.offset = Vector2(-tex.get_width() * 0.5, -tex.get_height()) + _vec(o.get("offset", [0, 0]))
			node.add_child(sp)
			_add_glow(node, str(o["sprite"]), sp.offset)
			entities.add_child(node)
			if bool(o.get("solid", true)):
				for j in foot_size.y:
					for i in foot_size.x:
						_blocked[at + Vector2i(i, j)] = true
		"decor":
			var tex := PixelArt.prop(str(o["sprite"]))
			var sp := Sprite2D.new()
			sp.name = "Decor_" + str(o["sprite"])
			sp.texture = tex
			sp.centered = false
			sp.position = Vector2(at * T) + _vec(o.get("offset", [0, 0]))
			sp.z_index = 21 if str(o.get("layer", "under")) == "over" else -8
			add_child(sp)
			_add_glow(sp, str(o["sprite"]), Vector2.ZERO)
		"npc":
			var def: Dictionary = (Data.load_dict("res://data/npcs2d.json") as Dictionary).get(str(o["id"]), {})
			var npc := GridNpc.new()
			npc.name = "Npc_" + str(o["id"])
			npc.npc_id = str(o["id"])
			npc.display_name = str(def.get("name", "Someone"))
			npc.dialogue_id = str(def.get("dialogue", ""))
			npc.look = def.get("look", {})
			npc.world = self
			npc.place(at, str(o.get("facing", "down")))
			entities.add_child(npc)
		"walker":
			var w := GridNpc.new()
			w.name = "Walker"
			w.look = o.get("look", {})
			w.world = self
			var a: Array = o["area"]
			w.wander = Rect2i(int(a[0]), int(a[1]), int(a[2]), int(a[3]))
			w.place(at, str(o.get("facing", "down")))
			entities.add_child(w)
		"warp":
			_warps[at] = {"map": str(o["to"]), "spawn": str(o.get("spawn", "default"))}
			if o.has("label"):
				_sign(at, str(o.get("prompt", "Enter")), str(o["label"]), "", str(o["to"]), str(o.get("spawn", "default")))
		"sign":
			_sign(at, str(o.get("prompt", "Look")), str(o.get("label", "")), str(o.get("dialogue", "")), "", "")
		"light":
			pass


func _sign(at: Vector2i, prompt: String, label: String, dialogue: String, to_map: String, to_spawn: String) -> void:
	var it := Interactable.new()
	it.name = "Sign_%d_%d" % [at.x, at.y]
	it.prompt = prompt
	it.label = label
	it.dialogue_id = dialogue
	it.position = foot(at) - Vector2(0, 8)
	if to_map != "":
		it.interacted.connect(func(_by: Node2D) -> void: GameState.travel_requested.emit(to_map, to_spawn))
	add_child(it)
	_signs[at] = it


func _add_glow(parent: Node2D, sprite: String, offset: Vector2) -> void:
	var g := PixelArt.prop_glow(sprite)
	if g == null:
		return
	var gs := Sprite2D.new()
	gs.texture = g
	gs.centered = false
	gs.offset = offset
	var m := CanvasItemMaterial.new()
	m.blend_mode = CanvasItemMaterial.BLEND_MODE_ADD
	m.light_mode = CanvasItemMaterial.LIGHT_MODE_UNSHADED
	gs.material = m
	gs.add_to_group(&"night_glow")
	parent.add_child(gs)


static func _tile(v: Variant) -> Vector2i:
	var a: Array = v
	return Vector2i(int(a[0]), int(a[1]))


static func _vec(v: Variant) -> Vector2:
	var a: Array = v
	return Vector2(float(a[0]), float(a[1]))


# --- gameplay queries -------------------------------------------------------------

## Where a person standing on `t` is drawn from: the tile's bottom centre.
func foot(t: Vector2i) -> Vector2:
	return Vector2(t.x * T + T * 0.5, t.y * T + T - 1)


func tile_of(p: Vector2) -> Vector2i:
	return Vector2i(floori(p.x / T), floori((p.y - 1) / T))


func in_bounds(t: Vector2i) -> bool:
	return t.x >= 0 and t.y >= 0 and t.x < size.x and t.y < size.y


## True if nothing stops a step onto `t` (walls, props, people, the map edge).
func can_enter(t: Vector2i, who: Node = null) -> bool:
	if not in_bounds(t) or _solid.has(t) or _blocked.has(t):
		return false
	var a: Node = _actors.get(t, null)
	return a == null or a == who or not is_instance_valid(a)


func is_solid(t: Vector2i) -> bool:
	return _solid.has(t) or _blocked.has(t)


## People claim tiles as they step so two never meet in one.
func claim(t: Vector2i, who: Node) -> void:
	_actors[t] = who


func release(t: Vector2i, who: Node) -> void:
	if _actors.get(t, null) == who:
		_actors.erase(t)


func actor_at(t: Vector2i) -> Node:
	var a: Node = _actors.get(t, null)
	return a if is_instance_valid(a) else null


## Whatever can be used on tile `t`: a person's or a sign's Interactable.
func interactable_at(t: Vector2i) -> Interactable:
	var a := actor_at(t)
	if a is GridNpc and (a as GridNpc).interactable != null:
		return (a as GridNpc).interactable
	return _signs.get(t, null)


func warp_at(t: Vector2i) -> Dictionary:
	return _warps.get(t, {})


func spawn_position(spawn: String) -> Vector2:
	var m := get_node_or_null("Spawns/" + spawn) as Node2D
	if m == null:
		m = get_node_or_null("Spawns/default") as Node2D
	return m.position if m != null else foot(Vector2i(1, 1))


func spawn_facing(spawn: String) -> String:
	var m := get_node_or_null("Spawns/" + spawn) as Node2D
	return str(m.get_meta(&"facing", "down")) if m != null else "down"

script = ExtResource("1")
map_id = "anjabi_steps_shop"
map_file = "res://data/maps2d/shop.json"

==============================================================================
== world2d/maps/steps.tscn
==============================================================================
[gd_scene load_steps=2 format=3]

[ext_resource type="Script" path="res://world2d/tile_world.gd" id="1"]
